# --- Set 1: Candidate LR genes (TEMPORARY; replace after Module 2) ---
lr_df <- data.frame(
  ligand   = c("TGFB1", "PDGFA", "TNF", "CCL2", "IL6"),
  receptor = c("TGFBR1", "PDGFRA", "TNFRSF1A", "CCR2", "IL6R"),
  stringsAsFactors = FALSE
)
lr_genes <- unique(unlist(strsplit(c(lr_df$ligand, lr_df$receptor), "_")))
lr_genes <- toupper(lr_genes)

# --- Set 2: HSC markers ---
hsc_markers <- toupper(c(
  "LRAT", "GFAP", "DES", "RELN", "HAND2",
  "ACTA2", "COL1A1", "COL1A2", "COL3A1", "COL4A1",
  "TIMP1", "TIMP2", "LOX", "LOXL2",
  "PDGFRB", "PDGFRA", "NGFR", "CD248", "GFRA2"
))

# --- Set 3: Fibrosis / ECM / TGF-beta genes ---
fibrosis_genes <- toupper(c(
  "TGFB1", "TGFB2", "TGFB3", "TGFBR1", "TGFBR2",
  "SMAD2", "SMAD3", "SMAD4", "SMAD7",
  "MMP2", "MMP9", "MMP14", "TIMP1", "TIMP3",
  "FN1", "SPARC", "POSTN", "LUM", "DCN",

  "IL6", "IL1B", "CCL2", "CXCL9", "CXCL10", "TNF"
))

# --- Set 4: Module 1 core markers (placeholder) ---
module1_core <- toupper(c("GENE_A", "GENE_B", "GENE_C", "GENE_D", "GENE_E"))

# ============================================================
# Step 4: Coverage function
# ============================================================

compute_coverage <- function(gene_set, panel, set_name) {
  gene_set <- unique(toupper(gene_set))
  gene_set <- gene_set[gene_set != "" & !is.na(gene_set)]
  
  covered <- intersect(gene_set, panel)
  missing <- setdiff(gene_set, panel)
  rate <- length(covered) / length(gene_set) * 100
  
  summary_df <- data.frame(
    GeneSet         = set_name,
    Total           = length(gene_set),
    Covered         = length(covered),
    Missing         = length(missing),
    CoveragePercent = round(rate, 1),
    stringsAsFactors = FALSE
  )
  
  list(summary = summary_df, covered = covered, missing = missing)
}

# ============================================================
# Step 5: Run coverage for all gene sets
# ============================================================

res_lr       <- compute_coverage(lr_genes,       panel_genes, "Candidate_LR")
res_hsc      <- compute_coverage(hsc_markers,    panel_genes, "HSC_Markers")
res_fibrosis <- compute_coverage(fibrosis_genes, panel_genes, "Fibrosis_ECM")
res_m1       <- compute_coverage(module1_core,   panel_genes, "Module1_Core")

coverage_summary <- rbind(
  res_lr$summary,
  res_hsc$summary,
  res_fibrosis$summary,
  res_m1$summary
)

cat("\n========== Coverage Summary ==========\n")
print(coverage_summary)

# ============================================================
# Step 6: Detailed covered/missing lists
# ============================================================

cat("\n--- HSC markers: covered ---\n");    print(res_hsc$covered)
cat("\n--- HSC markers: missing ---\n");    print(res_hsc$missing)

cat("\n--- Fibrosis genes: covered ---\n"); print(res_fibrosis$covered)
cat("\n--- Fibrosis genes: missing ---\n"); print(res_fibrosis$missing)

cat("\n--- Candidate LR: covered ---\n");   print(res_lr$covered)
cat("\n--- Candidate LR: missing ---\n");   print(res_lr$missing)

# ============================================================
# Step 7: Save outputs

write.csv(coverage_summary, "panel_coverage_summary.csv", row.names = FALSE)
write.csv(data.frame(gene = res_hsc$covered), "covered_HSC_markers.csv", row.names = FALSE)
write.csv(data.frame(gene = res_hsc$missing), "missing_HSC_markers.csv", row.names = FALSE)
write.csv(data.frame(gene = res_fibrosis$covered), "covered_fibrosis_genes.csv", row.names = FALSE)
write.csv(data.frame(gene = res_fibrosis$missing), "missing_fibrosis_genes.csv", row.names = FALSE)

saveRDS(list(
  coverage_summary = coverage_summary,

# Also search any column whose values contain "Stellate"
search_celltype <- function(df) {
  hits <- sapply(colnames(df), function(col) {
    vals <- df[[col]]
    if (is.character(vals) || is.factor(vals)) {
      any(grepl("Stellate|Hep\\.|Macrophage|Cholangiocyte",
                unique(vals), ignore.case = TRUE))
    } else FALSE
  })
  names(hits)[hits]
}

celltype_cols <- search_celltype(cosmx_obj@meta.data)
cat("\nColumns containing cell type names:\n")
print(celltype_cols)

# Inspect each hit
for (col in celltype_cols) {
  cat("\n===== Column:", col, "=====\n")
  print(table(cosmx_obj@meta.data[[col]], useNA = "ifany"))
}

celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"

# 2f.1: Extract Stellate cells and check HSC marker expression

# ------------------------------------------------------------

celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]

avg_by_type <- sapply(markers_to_plot, function(g) {
  tapply(expr_mat[g, ], celltype_vec, mean, na.rm = TRUE)
})

  cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]] == "Stellate.cells",

]

# ============================================================
# Step 2g: Define fibrotic region and test HSC spatial enrichment
# ============================================================

library(ggplot2)
library(dplyr)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2g.1: Define fibrotic score per cell
# ------------------------------------------------------------

# Fibrosis signature genes present in the panel
fibro_sig <- c("COL1A1", "COL1A2", "COL3A1", "COL4A1", "FN1",
               "TGFB1", "TGFBR1", "TGFBR2", "MMP2", "TIMP1", "DCN", "LUM")
fibro_sig <- fibro_sig[fibro_sig %in% rownames(cosmx_obj[["RNA"]])]
cat("Fibrosis signature genes used:\n"); print(fibro_sig)

# Compute per-cell fibrosis score (mean expression of signature)
expr_mat <- as.matrix(cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data[fibro_sig, , drop = FALSE])
fibro_score <- colMeans(expr_mat)

# Add to metadata
cosmx_obj@meta.data$fibro_score <- fibro_score

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2g.2: Compare fibrosis score between Stellate and other cells
# ------------------------------------------------------------

celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]

score_by_type <- tapply(fibro_score, celltype_vec, mean, na.rm = TRUE)
score_by_type <- sort(score_by_type, decreasing = TRUE)
cat("\nMean fibrosis score by cell type (top 10):\n")
print(round(head(score_by_type, 10), 3))

# Statistical test: Stellate vs all others
stellate_score <- fibro_score[celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells"]
other_score    <- fibro_score[celltype_vec != "Stellate.cells"]

wilcox_res <- wilcox.test(stellate_score, other_score)
cat(sprintf("\nWilcoxon test (Stellate vs others): p = %.3e\n", wilcox_res$p.value))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2g.3: Define fibrotic region spatially
# ------------------------------------------------------------

# Strategy: use high fibrosis score cells to define "fibrotic region"
# Use top 25% of cells by fibro_score as threshold
fibro_threshold <- quantile(fibro_score, 0.75, na.rm = TRUE)
cat(sprintf("\nFibrosis score threshold (top 25%%): %.3f\n", fibro_threshold))

cosmx_obj@meta.data$is_fibrotic <- fibro_score >= fibro_threshold

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2g.4: Test whether Stellate cells are enriched in fibrotic regions
# ------------------------------------------------------------

# Contingency table
ct <- table(
  celltype = ifelse(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells", "Stellate", "Other"),
  region   = ifelse(cosmx_obj@meta.data$is_fibrotic, "Fibrotic", "NonFibrotic")
)
print(ct)

# Fisher's exact test
fisher_res <- fisher.test(ct)
cat(sprintf("\nFisher's exact test: OR = %.3f, p = %.3e\n",
            fisher_res$estimate, fisher_res$p.value))

# Proportion of Stellate cells in fibrotic vs non-fibrotic region
prop_stellate_fibro <- mean(celltype_vec[cosmx_obj@meta.data$is_fibrotic] == "Stellate.cells")
prop_stellate_nonfibro <- mean(celltype_vec[!cosmx_obj@meta.data$is_fibrotic] == "Stellate.cells")
cat(sprintf("Stellate proportion in fibrotic region: %.2f%%\n", prop_stellate_fibro * 100))
cat(sprintf("Stellate proportion in non-fibrotic region: %.2f%%\n", prop_stellate_nonfibro * 100))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2g.5: Visualize spatial overlay
# ------------------------------------------------------------

plot_df <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[, c("x_slide_mm", "y_slide_mm")]
plot_df$is_stellate <- celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells"
plot_df$is_fibrotic <- cosmx_obj@meta.data$is_fibrotic

p_overlay <- ggplot() +
  geom_point(data = plot_df[!plot_df$is_fibrotic, ],
             aes(x = x_slide_mm, y = y_slide_mm),
             size = 0.1, alpha = 0.15, color = "grey85") +
  geom_point(data = plot_df[plot_df$is_fibrotic & !plot_df$is_stellate, ],
             aes(x = x_slide_mm, y = y_slide_mm),
             size = 0.2, alpha = 0.4, color = "#F18F01") +
  geom_point(data = plot_df[plot_df$is_stellate, ],
             aes(x = x_slide_mm, y = y_slide_mm),
             size = 0.4, alpha = 0.7, color = "#C73E1D") +
  coord_fixed() +
  labs(title = "Stellate cells (red) vs fibrotic region (orange)",
       subtitle = sprintf("Stellate: %d | Fibrotic region: %d cells (top 25%%)",
                          sum(plot_df$is_stellate), sum(plot_df$is_fibrotic)),
       x = "x_slide_mm", y = "y_slide_mm") +
  theme_classic()
print(p_overlay)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2g.6: Save results
# ------------------------------------------------------------

saveRDS(list(
  fibro_score       = fibro_score,
  fibro_threshold   = fibro_threshold,
  score_by_type     = score_by_type,
  wilcox_p          = wilcox_res$p.value,
  fisher_OR         = fisher_res$estimate,
  fisher_p          = fisher_res$p.value,
  prop_stellate_fibro    = prop_stellate_fibro,
  prop_stellate_nonfibro = prop_stellate_nonfibro
), "GSE292268_Slide1_HSC_fibrosis_enrichment.rds")

write.csv(data.frame(
  cell_type = names(score_by_type),
  mean_fibro_score = as.numeric(score_by_type)
), "GSE292268_Slide1_fibro_score_by_type.csv", row.names = FALSE)

cat("\n=== Step 2g completed ===\n")
# ============================================================
# Step 2h: Correct OR for Stellate enrichment in fibrotic region
# ============================================================

# Extract contingency table values
a <- ct["Stellate", "Fibrotic"]      # Stellate in fibrotic
b <- ct["Stellate", "NonFibrotic"]   # Stellate in non-fibrotic
c <- ct["Other", "Fibrotic"]         # Other in fibrotic
d <- ct["Other", "NonFibrotic"]      # Other in non-fibrotic

# Correct OR: Stellate enrichment in fibrotic region
OR_stellate <- (a / b) / (c / d)

cat(sprintf("Stellate enrichment OR (fibrotic vs non-fibrotic): %.3f\n", OR_stellate))
cat(sprintf("95%% CI: [%.3f, %.3f]\n",
            exp(log(OR_stellate) - 1.96 * sqrt(1/a + 1/b + 1/c + 1/d)),
            exp(log(OR_stellate) + 1.96 * sqrt(1/a + 1/b + 1/c + 1/d))))

# Save corrected result
saveRDS(list(
  OR_stellate = OR_stellate,
  ct = ct,
  prop_fibro = prop_stellate_fibro,
  prop_nonfibro = prop_stellate_nonfibro
), "GSE292268_Slide1_Stellate_enrichment_corrected.rds")

cat("\n=== Module 0 completed ===\n")

# ============================================================
# Module 0 final summary
# ============================================================

module0_summary <- data.frame(
  Item = c(
    "CosMx panel size",
    "Candidate LR coverage (temp)",
    "HSC marker coverage",
    "Fibrosis gene coverage",
    "Stellate cells detected",
    "Stellate cells percentage",
    "Stellate cells FOV coverage",
    "Stellate enrichment OR",
    "Stellate enrichment p-value",
    "Spatial coordinates available"
  ),
  Value = c(
    "1000 genes",
    "100% (10/10, temporary)",
    "47.4% (9/19, core markers present)",
    "75.9% (22/29)",
    "3559",
    "6.5%",
    "64 FOVs",
    sprintf("%.2f", OR_stellate),
    "< 0.001",
    "Yes (x_slide_mm, y_slide_mm)"
  )
)

print(module0_summary)
write.csv(module0_summary, "Module0_summary.csv", row.names = FALSE)

# ============================================================
# Supplementary 1: Independent definition of fibrotic region
# ============================================================

library(ggplot2)
library(dplyr)

celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Method A: Non-HSC-derived fibrotic signature
# ------------------------------------------------------------

# Inflammatory / injury-driven fibrotic genes, mainly from macrophages
# and hepatocytes, NOT from HSC
fibro_sig_indep <- c(
  "IL6", "IL1B", "CCL2", "CXCL9", "CXCL10", "TNF",   # inflammation
  "TGFB1", "TGFBR1", "TGFBR2",                       # signaling
  "MMP2", "MMP14"                                    # matrix turnover
)
fibro_sig_indep <- fibro_sig_indep[
  fibro_sig_indep %in% rownames(cosmx_obj[["RNA"]])
]
cat("Independent fibrosis signature:\n"); print(fibro_sig_indep)

expr_mat_indep <- as.matrix(
  cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data[fibro_sig_indep, , drop = FALSE]
)
fibro_score_indep <- colMeans(expr_mat_indep)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Method B: Compute region score using non-HSC cells only
# ------------------------------------------------------------

non_hsc_idx <- which(celltype_vec != "Stellate.cells")
fibro_score_nonhsc <- colMeans(expr_mat_indep[, non_hsc_idx, drop = FALSE])

# Assign region labels based on non-HSC-derived threshold
thr_nonhsc <- quantile(fibro_score_nonhsc, 0.75, na.rm = TRUE)
is_fibrotic_nonhsc <- fibro_score_indep >= thr_nonhsc

cat(sprintf("Threshold (non-HSC, top 25%%): %.3f\n", thr_nonhsc))
cat(sprintf("Fibrotic cells: %d (%.1f%%)\n",
            sum(is_fibrotic_nonhsc),
            100 * mean(is_fibrotic_nonhsc)))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Recompute enrichment OR
# ------------------------------------------------------------

ct2 <- table(
  celltype = ifelse(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells", "Stellate", "Other"),
  region   = ifelse(is_fibrotic_nonhsc, "Fibrotic", "NonFibrotic")
)
print(ct2)

a2 <- ct2["Stellate", "Fibrotic"]
b2 <- ct2["Stellate", "NonFibrotic"]
c2 <- ct2["Other", "Fibrotic"]
d2 <- ct2["Other", "NonFibrotic"]

OR_indep <- (a2 / b2) / (c2 / d2)
se_logOR <- sqrt(1/a2 + 1/b2 + 1/c2 + 1/d2)
CI_low  <- exp(log(OR_indep) - 1.96 * se_logOR)
CI_high <- exp(log(OR_indep) + 1.96 * se_logOR)

cat(sprintf("\n=== Independent fibrotic region ===\n"))
cat(sprintf("OR = %.3f, 95%% CI [%.3f, %.3f]\n", OR_indep, CI_low, CI_high))
cat(sprintf("Original OR (collagen-based) = 7.438\n"))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Save
# ------------------------------------------------------------

saveRDS(list(
  OR_indep = OR_indep,
  CI = c(CI_low, CI_high),
  ct = ct2,
  fibro_sig_indep = fibro_sig_indep,
  threshold = thr_nonhsc
), "GSE292268_Stellate_enrichment_independent.rds")

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Compare: original vs independent
# ------------------------------------------------------------

compare_df <- data.frame(
  Definition = c("Original (collagen-based)", "Independent (non-HSC)"),
  OR = c(7.438, OR_indep),
  CI_low = c(6.902, CI_low),
  CI_high = c(8.016, CI_high)
)
print(compare_df)
write.csv(compare_df, "Stellate_enrichment_comparison.csv", row.names = FALSE)

# ============================================================
# Supplementary 2: Sample-level OR distribution
# ============================================================

# Identify the sample column
# Candidates: Run_Tissue_name, slide_ID_numeric, fov
cat("Candidate sample columns:\n")
print(table(cosmx_obj@meta.data$Run_Tissue_name, useNA = "ifany"))
print(table(cosmx_obj@meta.data$slide_ID_numeric, useNA = "ifany"))

# Assuming Run_Tissue_name identifies the sample
sample_col <- "Run_Tissue_name"
samples <- unique(cosmx_obj@meta.data[[sample_col]])
cat(sprintf("\nSamples: %d\n", length(samples)))
print(samples)

# Compute OR per sample
or_by_sample <- data.frame(
  Sample = character(),
  N_stellate = integer(),
  N_other = integer(),
  OR = numeric(),
  CI_low = numeric(),
  CI_high = numeric(),
  stringsAsFactors = FALSE
)

for (s in samples) {
  idx_s <- which(cosmx_obj@meta.data[[sample_col]] == s)
  
  ct_s <- table(
    celltype = ifelse(celltype_vec[idx_s] == "Stellate.cells",
                      "Stellate", "Other"),
    region   = ifelse(is_fibrotic_nonhsc[idx_s],
                      "Fibrotic", "NonFibrotic")
  )
  
  # Handle potential 0 cells
  if (nrow(ct_s) < 2 || ncol(ct_s) < 2) next
  
  a <- ct_s["Stellate", "Fibrotic"]
  b <- ct_s["Stellate", "NonFibrotic"]
  c <- ct_s["Other", "Fibrotic"]
  d <- ct_s["Other", "NonFibrotic"]
  
  if (any(c(a, b, c, d) == 0)) next
  
  or_s <- (a / b) / (c / d)
  se_s <- sqrt(1/a + 1/b + 1/c + 1/d)
  
  or_by_sample <- rbind(or_by_sample, data.frame(
    Sample = s,
    N_stellate = a + b,
    N_other = c + d,
    OR = or_s,
    CI_low = exp(log(or_s) - 1.96 * se_s),
    CI_high = exp(log(or_s) + 1.96 * se_s),
    stringsAsFactors = FALSE
  ))
}

print(or_by_sample)
write.csv(or_by_sample, "Stellate_OR_by_sample.csv", row.names = FALSE)

# Forest plot
p_forest <- ggplot(or_by_sample, aes(x = OR, y = Sample)) +
  geom_point(size = 3, color = "#C73E1D") +
  geom_errorbarh(aes(xmin = CI_low, xmax = CI_high),
                 height = 0.2, color = "#C73E1D") +
  geom_vline(xintercept = 1, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey40") +
  scale_x_log10() +
  labs(title = "Stellate cell enrichment OR by sample",
       x = "Odds Ratio (log scale)", y = NULL) +
  theme_classic()
print(p_forest)
ggsave("Stellate_OR_by_sample_forest.pdf", p_forest, width = 6, height = 4)

# Supplementary 3 (fixed): Verify Stellate cell annotation quality

# Panel-available HSC markers
hsc_check2 <- c("PDGFRB", "COL1A1", "COL1A2", "COL3A1",
                "TGFBR1", "TGFBR2", "PDGFRA", "NGFR")
hsc_check2 <- hsc_check2[hsc_check2 %in% rownames(cosmx_obj[["RNA"]])]

# Average expression and positive rate in Stellate cells
stellate_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
expr_stellate <- as.matrix(
  cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data[hsc_check2, stellate_idx, drop = FALSE]
)

avg_stellate <- rowMeans(expr_stellate)
frac_stellate <- rowMeans(expr_stellate > 0)

# Build data frame WITHOUT rounding the character column
df_stellate <- data.frame(
  Marker   = hsc_check2,
  AvgExpr  = round(avg_stellate, 3),
  PosFrac  = round(frac_stellate, 3),
  stringsAsFactors = FALSE
)
cat("HSC marker expression in Stellate cells:\n")
print(df_stellate)

# Compare with non-Stellate cells
other_idx <- setdiff(seq_len(ncol(cosmx_obj)), stellate_idx)
expr_other <- as.matrix(
  cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data[hsc_check2, other_idx, drop = FALSE]
)
avg_other  <- rowMeans(expr_other)
frac_other <- rowMeans(expr_other > 0)

comparison <- data.frame(
  Marker            = hsc_check2,
  Avg_Stellate      = round(avg_stellate, 3),
  Avg_Other         = round(avg_other, 3),
  FoldChange        = round(avg_stellate / (avg_other + 1e-6), 3),
  PosFrac_Stellate  = round(frac_stellate, 3),
  PosFrac_Other     = round(frac_other, 3),
  stringsAsFactors  = FALSE
)
cat("\nStellate vs Other comparison:\n")
print(comparison)
write.csv(comparison, "Stellate_marker_comparison.csv", row.names = FALSE)

  Stellate   = celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells",
  COL1A1_pos = col1a1_pos,
  Fibrotic   = is_fibrotic_nonhsc

  FOV = character(), N_stellate = integer(), N_other = integer(),

  stringsAsFactors = FALSE
)

  idx_f <- which(fov_vec == f)
  
  ct_f <- table(
    celltype = ifelse(celltype_vec[idx_f] == "Stellate.cells", "Stellate", "Other"),
    region   = ifelse(is_fibrotic_nonhsc[idx_f], "Fibrotic", "NonFibrotic")

  b <- ct_f["Stellate", "NonFibrotic"]
  c <- ct_f["Other", "Fibrotic"]
  d <- ct_f["Other", "NonFibrotic"]
  
  if (any(c(a, b, c, d) == 0)) next
  
  or_f <- (a / b) / (c / d)
  se_f <- sqrt(1/a + 1/b + 1/c + 1/d)
  
  or_by_fov <- rbind(or_by_fov, data.frame(
    FOV = as.character(f),
    N_stellate = a + b, N_other = c + d,
    OR = or_f,
    CI_low  = exp(log(or_f) - 1.96 * se_f),
    CI_high = exp(log(or_f) + 1.96 * se_f),
    stringsAsFactors = FALSE
  ))
}

            sum(or_by_fov$OR > 1), nrow(or_by_fov),
            100 * mean(or_by_fov$OR > 1)))

# Check panel coverage for each LR pair
check_in_panel <- function(x, panel) {
  genes <- unique(unlist(strsplit(toupper(as.character(x)), "_")))
  all(genes %in% panel)
}

lr_sig$ligand_in_panel   <- sapply(lr_sig$ligand,   check_in_panel, panel_genes)
lr_sig$receptor_in_panel <- sapply(lr_sig$receptor, check_in_panel, panel_genes)
lr_sig$both_in_panel     <- lr_sig$ligand_in_panel & lr_sig$receptor_in_panel

# Global coverage
global_cov <- mean(lr_sig$both_in_panel) * 100
cat(sprintf("\nGlobal LR coverage: %.1f%% (%d/%d)\n",
            global_cov, sum(lr_sig$both_in_panel), nrow(lr_sig)))

# Target pathway coverage
target_pathways <- c("TGFb","SPP1","IL1","HGF","WNT",
                     "CXCL","CCL","TNF","PDGF","IGF")
cat("\nTarget pathway coverage:\n")
for (pw in target_pathways) {
  pw_lr <- lr_sig[grepl(pw, lr_sig$pathway_name, ignore.case = TRUE), ]
  if (nrow(pw_lr) > 0) {
    cat(sprintf("  %-6s: %.1f%% (%d/%d)\n",
                pw, mean(pw_lr$both_in_panel) * 100,
                sum(pw_lr$both_in_panel), nrow(pw_lr)))
  }
}

# Save
write.csv(lr_sig, "CellChat_significant_LR_pairs.csv", row.names = FALSE)
saveRDS(list(global_coverage = global_cov, lr_sig = lr_sig),
        "Module2_LR_coverage.rds")

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Quick fix for TGFb check
# ------------------------------------------------------------

# Manually normalize CellChat complex names
normalize_receptor <- function(x) {
  x <- as.character(x)
  x <- gsub("TGFbR1_R2", "TGFBR1_TGFBR2", x, ignore.case = TRUE)
  x <- gsub("ACVR1B_TGFbR2", "ACVR1B_TGFBR2", x, ignore.case = TRUE)
  x <- toupper(x)
  unique(unlist(strsplit(x, "_")))
}

check_in_panel_fixed <- function(x, panel) {
  genes <- normalize_receptor(x)
  all(genes %in% panel)
}

# Recheck TGFb with fixed function
tgf_lr$receptor_fixed <- sapply(tgf_lr$receptor, function(x) {
  paste(normalize_receptor(x), collapse = "_")
})
tgf_lr$both_in_panel_fixed <- sapply(tgf_lr$ligand, check_in_panel_fixed, panel_genes) &
                              sapply(tgf_lr$receptor, check_in_panel_fixed, panel_genes)

cat("\n=== TGFb coverage after fix ===\n")
print(tgf_lr[, c("source", "target", "ligand", "receptor",
                 "receptor_fixed", "both_in_panel", "both_in_panel_fixed")])

cat(sprintf("\nTGFb coverage (original): %.1f%%\n", 100 * mean(tgf_lr$both_in_panel)))
cat(sprintf("TGFb coverage (fixed): %.1f%%\n", 100 * mean(tgf_lr$both_in_panel_fixed)))

# Also check whether ACVR1B is in panel

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Universal complex expansion for CellChat LR pairs
# ------------------------------------------------------------

# Legacy CellChat names that need manual correction
legacy_map <- list(
  "TGFbR1_R2"     = c("TGFBR1", "TGFBR2"),
  "ACVR1B_TGFbR2" = c("ACVR1B", "TGFBR2"),
  "TGFbR1"        = c("TGFBR1"),
  "TGFbR2"        = c("TGFBR2"),
  "TGFbR1_R2"     = c("TGFBR1", "TGFBR2")
)

# Universal expansion function
expand_complex <- function(x) {
  x <- as.character(x)
  
  # Case 1: Known legacy name
  if (x %in% names(legacy_map)) {
    return(legacy_map[[x]])
  }
  
  # Case 2: Split by underscore, then uppercase each part
  parts <- unlist(strsplit(x, "_"))
  
  # For each part, check legacy map first
  result <- unlist(lapply(parts, function(p) {
    # Check if p needs legacy correction
    if (p == "R2") return("TGFBR2")
    if (p == "R1") return("TGFBR1")
    # Otherwise uppercase
    toupper(p)
  }))
  
  unique(result)
}

# Test on all problem cases
cat("=== Test expansion ===\n")
test_names <- c("TGFbR1_R2", "ACVR1B_TGFbR2", "PDGFRA", "FLT1_KDR",
                "ITGAV_ITGB1", "ITGAM_ITGB2", "ACVR1_ACVR2A")
for (n in test_names) {
  cat(sprintf("%-20s -> %s\n", n, paste(expand_complex(n), collapse = ", ")))
}
# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Recompute all LR pair coverage with corrected expansion
# ------------------------------------------------------------

# Check ligand and receptor coverage
lr_sig$ligand_in_panel_fixed <- sapply(lr_sig$ligand, function(x) {
  genes <- expand_complex(x)
  all(genes %in% panel_genes)
})

lr_sig$receptor_in_panel_fixed <- sapply(lr_sig$receptor, function(x) {
  genes <- expand_complex(x)
  all(genes %in% panel_genes)
})

lr_sig$both_in_panel_fixed <- lr_sig$ligand_in_panel_fixed & 
  lr_sig$receptor_in_panel_fixed

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Compare original vs fixed
# ------------------------------------------------------------

cat("=== Coverage comparison ===\n")
cat(sprintf("Global coverage (original): %.1f%% (%d/%d)\n",
            100 * mean(lr_sig$both_in_panel),
            sum(lr_sig$both_in_panel), nrow(lr_sig)))
cat(sprintf("Global coverage (fixed):    %.1f%% (%d/%d)\n",
            100 * mean(lr_sig$both_in_panel_fixed),
            sum(lr_sig$both_in_panel_fixed), nrow(lr_sig)))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Target pathway coverage (fixed)
# ------------------------------------------------------------

cat("\n=== Target pathway coverage (fixed) ===\n")
target_pathways <- c("TGFb","SPP1","IL1","HGF","WNT",
                     "CXCL","CCL","TNF","PDGF","IGF")
for (pw in target_pathways) {
  pw_lr <- lr_sig[grepl(pw, lr_sig$pathway_name, ignore.case = TRUE), ]
  if (nrow(pw_lr) > 0) {
    cat(sprintf("  %-6s: %.1f%% (%d/%d)\n",
                pw,
                100 * mean(pw_lr$both_in_panel_fixed),
                sum(pw_lr$both_in_panel_fixed),
                nrow(pw_lr)))
  }
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Check whether SPP1/IL1B/TNF/CCL2 axes are in panel
# ------------------------------------------------------------

cat("\n=== Non-RTK candidate axes ===\n")
check_axes <- list(
  c("SPP1", "ITGAV"), c("SPP1", "ITGB1"), c("SPP1", "CD44"),
  c("IL1B", "IL1R1"), c("TNF", "TNFRSF1A"), c("CCL2", "CCR2"),
  c("PDGFB", "PDGFRB"), c("PDGFC", "PDGFRB"), c("PDGFD", "PDGFRB"),
  c("HGF", "MET"), c("IGF1", "IGF1R"), c("IGF2", "IGF1R")
)
for (ax in check_axes) {
  ligand_ok <- toupper(ax[1]) %in% panel_genes
  receptor_ok <- toupper(ax[2]) %in% panel_genes
  cat(sprintf("%-15s: ligand=%s, receptor=%s, both=%s\n",
              paste(ax, collapse = "-"),
              ligand_ok, receptor_ok, ligand_ok & receptor_ok))
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Save corrected results
# ------------------------------------------------------------

write.csv(lr_sig, "CellChat_significant_LR_pairs_corrected.csv", row.names = FALSE)
saveRDS(list(global_coverage_original = 100 * mean(lr_sig$both_in_panel),
             global_coverage_fixed = 100 * mean(lr_sig$both_in_panel_fixed),
             lr_sig = lr_sig),
        "Module2_LR_coverage_corrected.rds")

cat("\n=== Corrected coverage saved ===\n")

if (!exists("cosmx_obj")) {
  setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
  cosmx_obj <- readRDS("GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")

celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Define 4 candidate axes with both ends

axes <- list(
  HGF_MET = list(
    name = "HGF-MET",
    ligand = "HGF",
    ligand_source = c("Stellate.cells", "Inflammatory.macrophages",
                      "Non.inflammatory.macrophages"),
    receptor = "MET",
    receptor_target = c("Hep.1", "Hep.2", "Hep.3", "Hep.4", "Hep.5", "Hep.6",

# ------------------------------------------------------------

all_genes <- unique(c(
  "HGF", "MET", "TGFB1", "TGFBR1", "TGFBR2",
  "PDGFC", "PDGFRB", "SPP1", "ITGAV", "ITGB1"
))

  gene = all_genes,
  in_panel = all_genes %in% rownames(cosmx_obj[["RNA"]])
)
print(panel_check)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Expression matrix
# ------------------------------------------------------------

genes_in_panel <- all_genes[all_genes %in% rownames(cosmx_obj[["RNA"]])]
expr_mat <- as.matrix(cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data[genes_in_panel, , drop = FALSE])

# Positive rate per cell type
pos_by_type <- sapply(genes_in_panel, function(g) {
  tapply(expr_mat[g, ] > 0, celltype_vec, mean, na.rm = TRUE)
})
avg_by_type <- sapply(genes_in_panel, function(g) {
  tapply(expr_mat[g, ], celltype_vec, mean, na.rm = TRUE)
})

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Dual-end positive rate table for each axis
# ------------------------------------------------------------

cat("\n\n========== DUAL-END POSITIVE RATE TABLE ==========\n")

axis_table <- data.frame(
  Axis = character(), End = character(), Gene = character(),
  CellType = character(), PosRate = numeric(), AvgExpr = numeric(),
  stringsAsFactors = FALSE
)

for (ax_name in names(axes)) {
  ax <- axes[[ax_name]]
  cat(sprintf("\n===== %s =====\n", ax$name))
  
  # Ligand end
  cat("\n--- Ligand: ", ax$ligand, " ---\n", sep = "")
  lig_src <- intersect(ax$ligand_source, rownames(pos_by_type))
  for (ct in lig_src) {
    pr <- pos_by_type[ct, ax$ligand]
    av <- avg_by_type[ct, ax$ligand]
    cat(sprintf("  %-30s PosRate = %.3f, AvgExpr = %.3f\n", ct, pr, av))
    axis_table <- rbind(axis_table, data.frame(
      Axis = ax$name, End = "Ligand", Gene = ax$ligand,
      CellType = ct, PosRate = round(pr, 3), AvgExpr = round(av, 3)
    ))
  }
  
  # Receptor end
  rec_genes <- if (length(ax$receptor) > 1) ax$receptor else ax$receptor
  cat("\n--- Receptor: ", paste(rec_genes, collapse = " + "), " ---\n", sep = "")
  rec_tgt <- intersect(ax$receptor_target, rownames(pos_by_type))
  for (ct in rec_tgt) {
    for (rg in rec_genes) {
      if (rg %in% colnames(pos_by_type)) {
        pr <- pos_by_type[ct, rg]
        av <- avg_by_type[ct, rg]
        cat(sprintf("  %-30s %s PosRate = %.3f, AvgExpr = %.3f\n",
                    ct, rg, pr, av))
        axis_table <- rbind(axis_table, data.frame(
          Axis = ax$name, End = "Receptor", Gene = rg,
          CellType = ct, PosRate = round(pr, 3), AvgExpr = round(av, 3)
        ))
      }
    }
  }
}

write.csv(axis_table, "Candidate_axes_dual_end_pos_rate.csv", row.names = FALSE)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. MET cell type distribution
# ------------------------------------------------------------

cat("\n\n========== MET CELL TYPE DISTRIBUTION ==========\n")
if ("MET" %in% colnames(pos_by_type)) {
  met_dist <- data.frame(
    CellType = rownames(pos_by_type),
    PosRate = round(pos_by_type[, "MET"], 3),
    AvgExpr = round(avg_by_type[, "MET"], 3)
  )
  met_dist <- met_dist[order(-met_dist$PosRate), ]
  print(met_dist)
  write.csv(met_dist, "MET_cell_type_distribution.csv", row.names = FALSE)
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. TGFBR1 and TGFBR2 status
# ------------------------------------------------------------

cat("\n\n========== TGFBR1 / TGFBR2 STATUS ==========\n")
for (g in c("TGFBR1", "TGFBR2")) {
  cat(sprintf("\n--- %s ---\n", g))
  if (g %in% rownames(cosmx_obj[["RNA"]])) {
    cat(sprintf("  In panel: TRUE\n"))
    stellate_pr <- pos_by_type["Stellate.cells", g]
    stellate_avg <- avg_by_type["Stellate.cells", g]
    cat(sprintf("  Stellate cells: PosRate = %.3f, AvgExpr = %.3f\n",
                stellate_pr, stellate_avg))
  } else {
    cat(sprintf("  In panel: FALSE\n"))
  }
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. Automatic pass/fail decision
# ------------------------------------------------------------

cat("\n\n========== AXIS PASS/FAIL DECISION ==========\n")
cat("Standard: BOTH ends must have PosRate >= 0.10\n\n")

for (ax_name in names(axes)) {
  ax <- axes[[ax_name]]
  sub <- axis_table[axis_table$Axis == ax$name, ]
  
  # Best positive rate at each end
  lig_max <- max(sub$PosRate[sub$End == "Ligand"], na.rm = TRUE)
  rec_max <- max(sub$PosRate[sub$End == "Receptor"], na.rm = TRUE)
  
  # TGFb special case: both receptors needed
  if (ax$name == "TGFB1-TGFBR1/2") {
    tgfbr1_pr <- pos_by_type["Stellate.cells", "TGFBR1"]
    tgfbr2_pr <- pos_by_type["Stellate.cells", "TGFBR2"]
    cat(sprintf("%s:\n", ax$name))
    cat(sprintf("  Ligand max PosRate:   %.3f\n", lig_max))
    cat(sprintf("  TGFBR1 (Stellate):    %.3f\n", tgfbr1_pr))
    cat(sprintf("  TGFBR2 (Stellate):    %.3f\n", tgfbr2_pr))
    if (tgfbr1_pr >= 0.10 && tgfbr2_pr >= 0.10) {
      cat("  --> PASS (both receptors >= 10%)\n\n")
    } else if (tgfbr1_pr < 0.10 && tgfbr2_pr >= 0.10) {
      cat("  --> PARTIAL (TGFBR1 < 10%, functional complex incomplete)\n\n")
    } else {
      cat("  --> FAIL\n\n")
    }
  } else {
    cat(sprintf("%s:\n", ax$name))
    cat(sprintf("  Ligand max PosRate:   %.3f\n", lig_max))
    cat(sprintf("  Receptor max PosRate: %.3f\n", rec_max))
    if (lig_max >= 0.10 && rec_max >= 0.10) {
      cat("  --> PASS\n\n")
    } else {
      cat("  --> FAIL (at least one end < 10%)\n\n")
    }
  }
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. Check what Hep.4 is (to confirm it's a hepatocyte subtype)
# ------------------------------------------------------------

cat("\n========== Hep.4 identity check ==========\n")
hep_markers <- c("ALB", "APOA1", "TTR", "TF", "HP", "APOB", "FGA", "FGB")
hep_markers <- hep_markers[hep_markers %in% rownames(cosmx_obj[["RNA"]])]

if (length(hep_markers) > 0) {
  hep_expr <- as.matrix(cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data[hep_markers, , drop = FALSE])
  hep_by_type <- sapply(hep_markers, function(g) {
    tapply(hep_expr[g, ], celltype_vec, mean, na.rm = TRUE)
  })
  hep_types <- c("Hep.1", "Hep.2", "Hep.3", "Hep.4", "Hep.5", "Hep.6")
  hep_types <- intersect(hep_types, rownames(hep_by_type))
  cat("\nHepatocyte markers in Hep subtypes:\n")
  print(round(hep_by_type[hep_types, , drop = FALSE], 2))
}

cat("\n=== Analysis completed ===\n")


# ============================================================
# Hep.4 spatial distribution and adjacency with HGF+ HSC
# ============================================================

library(Seurat)
library(tidyverse)
library(ggplot2)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 0. Reload CosMx if needed
# ------------------------------------------------------------

if (!exists("cosmx_obj")) {
  setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
  cosmx_obj <- readRDS("GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
}

celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Extract Hep.4 and HGF+ HSC coordinates
# ------------------------------------------------------------

# Hep.4 cells
hep4_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.4")
cat(sprintf("Hep.4 cells: %d\n", length(hep4_idx)))

# HGF+ Stellate cells
hgf_expr <- as.numeric(cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data["HGF", ])
stellate_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
hgf_pos_stellate <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]
cat(sprintf("HGF+ Stellate cells: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\n",
            length(hgf_pos_stellate), length(stellate_idx),
            100 * length(hgf_pos_stellate) / length(stellate_idx)))

# Extract coordinates
coords_all <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[, c("x_slide_mm", "y_slide_mm", "fov")]
coords_hep4 <- coords_all[hep4_idx, ]
coords_hgf_hsc <- coords_all[hgf_pos_stellate, ]

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Hep.4 spatial distribution across FOVs
# ------------------------------------------------------------

fov_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data$fov
all_fovs <- sort(unique(fov_vec))
cat(sprintf("Total FOVs: %d\n", length(all_fovs)))

# Count Hep.4 per FOV
hep4_by_fov <- table(fov_vec[hep4_idx])
hsc_by_fov <- table(fov_vec[stellate_idx])
hgf_hsc_by_fov <- table(fov_vec[hgf_pos_stellate])

# Build summary
fov_summary <- data.frame(
  FOV = all_fovs,
  N_cells_total = as.numeric(table(fov_vec)[all_fovs]),
  N_Hep4 = as.numeric(hep4_by_fov[all_fovs]),
  N_Stellate = as.numeric(hsc_by_fov[all_fovs]),
  N_HGF_HSC = as.numeric(hgf_hsc_by_fov[all_fovs])
)
fov_summary[is.na(fov_summary)] <- 0

# Add proportions
fov_summary$Hep4_prop <- fov_summary$N_Hep4 / fov_summary$N_cells_total
fov_summary$HGF_HSC_prop <- fov_summary$N_HGF_HSC / fov_summary$N_cells_total

cat("\nHep.4 distribution across FOVs:\n")
cat(sprintf("  Median Hep.4 per FOV: %.1f\n", median(fov_summary$N_Hep4)))
cat(sprintf("  Range: %d - %d\n", min(fov_summary$N_Hep4), max(fov_summary$N_Hep4)))
cat(sprintf("  Coefficient of variation: %.2f\n",
            sd(fov_summary$N_Hep4) / mean(fov_summary$N_Hep4)))

write.csv(fov_summary, "Hep4_spatial_distribution_by_FOV.csv", row.names = FALSE)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Spatial clustering test: is Hep.4 spatially clustered?
# ------------------------------------------------------------

# Ripley's K or nearest neighbor analysis
# Simple approach: compare observed Hep.4 co-localization vs random

# For each FOV, compute mean nearest-neighbor distance from Hep.4 to HGF+ HSC
compute_nn_distance <- function(src_coords, tgt_coords) {
  if (nrow(src_coords) == 0 || nrow(tgt_coords) == 0) return(NA)
  dists <- sapply(seq_len(nrow(src_coords)), function(i) {
    min(sqrt((tgt_coords$x_slide_mm - src_coords$x_slide_mm[i])^2 +
               (tgt_coords$y_slide_mm - src_coords$y_slide_mm[i])^2))
  })
  mean(dists)
}

# Compute per FOV: Hep.4 to HGF+ HSC mean nearest-neighbor distance
observed_nn <- data.frame(
  FOV = all_fovs,
  Mean_NN_Hep4_to_HGF_HSC = NA_real_,
  Mean_NN_Hep4_to_HSC = NA_real_,
  Mean_NN_Hep4_to_other = NA_real_
)

for (i in seq_along(all_fovs)) {
  f <- all_fovs[i]
  
  hep4_in_fov <- coords_hep4[coords_hep4$fov == f, ]
  hgf_hsc_in_fov <- coords_hgf_hsc[coords_hgf_hsc$fov == f, ]
  hsc_in_fov <- coords_all[coords_all$fov == f & 
                             celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells", ]
  other_in_fov <- coords_all[coords_all$fov == f & 
                               celltype_vec != "Hep.4", ]
  
  observed_nn$Mean_NN_Hep4_to_HGF_HSC[i] <- 
    compute_nn_distance(hep4_in_fov, hgf_hsc_in_fov)
  observed_nn$Mean_NN_Hep4_to_HSC[i] <- 
    compute_nn_distance(hep4_in_fov, hsc_in_fov)
  observed_nn$Mean_NN_Hep4_to_other[i] <- 
    compute_nn_distance(hep4_in_fov, other_in_fov)
}

cat("\nObserved nearest-neighbor distances (µm):\n")
cat(sprintf("  Hep.4 -> HGF+ HSC:     %.1f ± %.1f\n",
            mean(observed_nn$Mean_NN_Hep4_to_HGF_HSC, na.rm = TRUE),
            sd(observed_nn$Mean_NN_Hep4_to_HGF_HSC, na.rm = TRUE)))
cat(sprintf("  Hep.4 -> all HSC:      %.1f ± %.1f\n",
            mean(observed_nn$Mean_NN_Hep4_to_HSC, na.rm = TRUE),
            sd(observed_nn$Mean_NN_Hep4_to_HSC, na.rm = TRUE)))
cat(sprintf("  Hep.4 -> other cells:  %.1f ± %.1f\n",
            mean(observed_nn$Mean_NN_Hep4_to_other, na.rm = TRUE),
            sd(observed_nn$Mean_NN_Hep4_to_other, na.rm = TRUE)))

write.csv(observed_nn, "Hep4_NN_distances_by_FOV.csv", row.names = FALSE)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Permutation test: is Hep.4-HGF+HSC adjacency significant?
# ------------------------------------------------------------

set.seed(123)
n_perm <- 1000
perm_results <- numeric(n_perm)

for (p in 1:n_perm) {
  # Shuffle Hep.4 labels within each FOV
  shuffled_hep4 <- coords_hep4
  for (f in all_fovs) {
    idx_f <- which(shuffled_hep4$fov == f)
    if (length(idx_f) > 1) {
      cells_in_fov <- which(coords_all$fov == f)
      sampled <- sample(cells_in_fov, length(idx_f))
      shuffled_hep4[idx_f, c("x_slide_mm", "y_slide_mm")] <- 
        coords_all[sampled, c("x_slide_mm", "y_slide_mm")]
    }
  }
  
  # Compute mean NN distance
  nn_dist <- numeric(length(all_fovs))
  for (i in seq_along(all_fovs)) {
    f <- all_fovs[i]
    hep4_in_fov <- shuffled_hep4[shuffled_hep4$fov == f, ]
    hgf_hsc_in_fov <- coords_hgf_hsc[coords_hgf_hsc$fov == f, ]
    nn_dist[i] <- compute_nn_distance(hep4_in_fov, hgf_hsc_in_fov)
  }
  perm_results[p] <- mean(nn_dist, na.rm = TRUE)
}

observed_mean_nn <- mean(observed_nn$Mean_NN_Hep4_to_HGF_HSC, na.rm = TRUE)
perm_pvalue <- mean(perm_results <= observed_mean_nn)
cat(sprintf("\n=== Permutation test ===\n"))
cat(sprintf("Observed mean NN distance: %.2f µm\n", observed_mean_nn))
cat(sprintf("Null mean NN distance: %.2f µm (SD %.2f)\n",
            mean(perm_results), sd(perm_results)))
cat(sprintf("Permutation p-value (one-sided, closer than random): %.4f\n",
            perm_pvalue))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Visualization
# ------------------------------------------------------------

# 5a. Spatial distribution of Hep.4
plot_df <- coords_all
plot_df$is_hep4 <- seq_len(nrow(plot_df)) %in% hep4_idx
plot_df$is_hgf_hsc <- seq_len(nrow(plot_df)) %in% hgf_pos_stellate

p_spatial <- ggplot() +
  geom_point(data = plot_df[!plot_df$is_hep4 & !plot_df$is_hgf_hsc, ],
             aes(x = x_slide_mm, y = y_slide_mm),
             size = 0.1, alpha = 0.15, color = "grey80") +
  geom_point(data = plot_df[plot_df$is_hep4, ],
             aes(x = x_slide_mm, y = y_slide_mm),
             size = 0.3, alpha = 0.6, color = "#2E86AB") +
  geom_point(data = plot_df[plot_df$is_hgf_hsc, ],
             aes(x = x_slide_mm, y = y_slide_mm),
             size = 0.5, alpha = 0.9, color = "#C73E1D") +
  coord_fixed() +
  labs(title = "Hep.4 (blue) vs HGF+ HSC (red) spatial distribution",
       subtitle = sprintf("Hep.4: %d | HGF+ HSC: %d",
                          sum(plot_df$is_hep4), sum(plot_df$is_hgf_hsc)),
       x = "x_slide_mm", y = "y_slide_mm") +
  theme_classic()
print(p_spatial)
ggsave("Hep4_HGF_HSC_spatial.pdf", p_spatial, width = 10, height = 8)

# 5b. NN distance distribution
p_nn <- ggplot(observed_nn, aes(x = Mean_NN_Hep4_to_HGF_HSC)) +
  geom_histogram(bins = 20, fill = "#C73E1D", alpha = 0.7, color = "white") +
  geom_vline(xintercept = mean(perm_results), linetype = "dashed",
             color = "black", size = 1) +
  geom_vline(xintercept = observed_mean_nn, linetype = "solid",
             color = "blue", size = 1) +
  labs(title = "Hep.4 to HGF+ HSC nearest-neighbor distance",
       subtitle = sprintf("Observed mean: %.1f µm | Null mean: %.1f µm | p = %.4f",
                          observed_mean_nn, mean(perm_results), perm_pvalue),
       x = "Mean nearest-neighbor distance (µm)", y = "FOV count") +
  theme_classic()
print(p_nn)
ggsave("Hep4_NN_distance_distribution.pdf", p_nn, width = 8, height = 5)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Save results
# ------------------------------------------------------------

saveRDS(list(
  hep4_idx = hep4_idx,
  hgf_pos_stellate = hgf_pos_stellate,
  fov_summary = fov_summary,
  observed_nn = observed_nn,
  perm_results = perm_results,
  observed_mean_nn = observed_mean_nn,
  perm_pvalue = perm_pvalue
), "Hep4_HGF_HSC_adjacency_analysis.rds")

cat("\n=== Hep.4 spatial analysis completed ===\n")


# ============================================================
# Corrected spatial adjacency analysis
# ============================================================

library(Seurat)
library(tidyverse)
library(ggplot2)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Define cell groups
# ------------------------------------------------------------

celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]

# HGF expression in Stellate cells
hgf_expr <- as.numeric(cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data["HGF", ])
stellate_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
hgf_pos_stellate <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]
hgf_neg_stellate <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] == 0]

cat(sprintf("HGF+ HSC: %d\n", length(hgf_pos_stellate)))
cat(sprintf("HGF- HSC: %d\n", length(hgf_neg_stellate)))

hep4_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.4")

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Define radius-based adjacency (30 µm)
# ------------------------------------------------------------

# Convert µm to mm: 30 µm = 0.03 mm
radius_mm <- 0.03

coords_all <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[, c("x_slide_mm", "y_slide_mm", "fov")]
coords_hep4 <- coords_all[hep4_idx, ]
coords_hgf_pos <- coords_all[hgf_pos_stellate, ]
coords_hgf_neg <- coords_all[hgf_neg_stellate, ]

# Count neighbors within radius per Hep.4 cell
count_neighbors <- function(src_coords, tgt_coords, radius) {
  sapply(seq_len(nrow(src_coords)), function(i) {
    dx <- tgt_coords$x_slide_mm - src_coords$x_slide_mm[i]
    dy <- tgt_coords$y_slide_mm - src_coords$y_slide_mm[i]
    sum(sqrt(dx^2 + dy^2) <= radius)
  })
}

# For each FOV, compute mean neighbor count
fov_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data$fov
all_fovs <- sort(unique(fov_vec))

adj_results <- data.frame(
  FOV = all_fovs,
  N_Hep4 = NA_integer_,
  Mean_N_HGFpos_HSC = NA_real_,
  Mean_N_HGFneg_HSC = NA_real_,
  Mean_N_other = NA_real_
)

for (i in seq_along(all_fovs)) {
  f <- all_fovs[i]
  
  hep4_in_fov <- coords_hep4[coords_hep4$fov == f, ]
  hgfpos_in_fov <- coords_hgf_pos[coords_hgf_pos$fov == f, ]
  hgfneg_in_fov <- coords_hgf_neg[coords_hgf_neg$fov == f, ]
  other_in_fov <- coords_all[coords_all$fov == f &
                               !(seq_len(nrow(coords_all)) %in%
                                   c(hep4_idx, stellate_idx)), ]
  
  if (nrow(hep4_in_fov) == 0) next
  
  adj_results$N_Hep4[i] <- nrow(hep4_in_fov)
  adj_results$Mean_N_HGFpos_HSC[i] <- mean(
    count_neighbors(hep4_in_fov, hgfpos_in_fov, radius_mm))
  adj_results$Mean_N_HGFneg_HSC[i] <- mean(
    count_neighbors(hep4_in_fov, hgfneg_in_fov, radius_mm))
  adj_results$Mean_N_other[i] <- mean(
    count_neighbors(hep4_in_fov, other_in_fov, radius_mm))
}

cat("\n=== Adjacency analysis (30 µm radius) ===\n")
cat(sprintf("Hep.4 -> HGF+ HSC:     %.2f neighbors\n",
            mean(adj_results$Mean_N_HGFpos_HSC, na.rm = TRUE)))
cat(sprintf("Hep.4 -> HGF- HSC:     %.2f neighbors\n",
            mean(adj_results$Mean_N_HGFneg_HSC, na.rm = TRUE)))
cat(sprintf("Hep.4 -> other cells:  %.2f neighbors\n",
            mean(adj_results$Mean_N_other, na.rm = TRUE)))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Permutation test
# ------------------------------------------------------------

set.seed(123)
n_perm <- 1000
perm_HGFpos <- numeric(n_perm)
perm_HGFneg <- numeric(n_perm)

for (p in 1:n_perm) {
  shuffled_hep4 <- coords_hep4
  for (f in all_fovs) {
    idx_f <- which(shuffled_hep4$fov == f)
    if (length(idx_f) > 1) {
      cells_in_fov <- which(coords_all$fov == f)
      sampled <- sample(cells_in_fov, length(idx_f))
      shuffled_hep4[idx_f, c("x_slide_mm", "y_slide_mm")] <- 
        coords_all[sampled, c("x_slide_mm", "y_slide_mm")]
    }
  }
  
  nn_pos <- numeric(length(all_fovs))
  nn_neg <- numeric(length(all_fovs))
  for (i in seq_along(all_fovs)) {
    f <- all_fovs[i]
    hep4_in_fov <- shuffled_hep4[shuffled_hep4$fov == f, ]
    hgfpos_in_fov <- coords_hgf_pos[coords_hgf_pos$fov == f, ]
    hgfneg_in_fov <- coords_hgf_neg[coords_hgf_neg$fov == f, ]
    if (nrow(hep4_in_fov) > 0) {
      nn_pos[i] <- mean(count_neighbors(hep4_in_fov, hgfpos_in_fov, radius_mm))
      nn_neg[i] <- mean(count_neighbors(hep4_in_fov, hgfneg_in_fov, radius_mm))
    }
  }
  perm_HGFpos[p] <- mean(nn_pos, na.rm = TRUE)
  perm_HGFneg[p] <- mean(nn_neg, na.rm = TRUE)
}

obs_pos <- mean(adj_results$Mean_N_HGFpos_HSC, na.rm = TRUE)
obs_neg <- mean(adj_results$Mean_N_HGFneg_HSC, na.rm = TRUE)

cat("\n=== Permutation test ===\n")
cat(sprintf("Hep.4 -> HGF+ HSC: Observed = %.2f, Null = %.2f, p = %.4f\n",
            obs_pos, mean(perm_HGFpos),
            mean(perm_HGFpos >= obs_pos)))
cat(sprintf("Hep.4 -> HGF- HSC: Observed = %.2f, Null = %.2f, p = %.4f\n",
            obs_neg, mean(perm_HGFneg),
            mean(perm_HGFneg >= obs_neg)))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Visualization
# ------------------------------------------------------------

# Neighbor count distribution
adj_long <- adj_results %>%
  select(FOV, Mean_N_HGFpos_HSC, Mean_N_HGFneg_HSC, Mean_N_other) %>%
  pivot_longer(-FOV, names_to = "Target", values_to = "Mean_Neighbors")
adj_long$Target <- factor(adj_long$Target,
                          levels = c("Mean_N_HGFpos_HSC", "Mean_N_HGFneg_HSC", "Mean_N_other"),
                          labels = c("HGF+ HSC", "HGF- HSC", "Other cells"))

p_adj <- ggplot(adj_long, aes(x = Target, y = Mean_Neighbors, fill = Target)) +
  geom_boxplot(alpha = 0.7) +
  geom_jitter(width = 0.2, size = 0.5, alpha = 0.3) +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c("#C73E1D", "#F18F01", "grey70")) +
  labs(title = "Hep.4 spatial adjacency (30 µm radius)",
       subtitle = "Per FOV neighbor count",
       x = NULL, y = "Mean neighbors per Hep.4 cell") +
  theme_classic() +
  theme(legend.position = "none")
print(p_adj)
ggsave("Hep4_adjacency_corrected.pdf", p_adj, width = 7, height = 5)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Save
# ------------------------------------------------------------

write.csv(adj_results, "Hep4_adjacency_by_FOV_corrected.csv", row.names = FALSE)
saveRDS(list(
  adj_results = adj_results,
  obs_pos = obs_pos, obs_neg = obs_neg,
  perm_HGFpos = perm_HGFpos, perm_HGFneg = perm_HGFneg
), "Hep4_adjacency_corrected.rds")

cat("\n=== Corrected spatial analysis completed ===\n")


# ============================================================
# Module 4 前置检查：距离分布 + 邻接半径适用性 + 重叠比例
# ============================================================

library(Seurat)
library(tidyverse)
library(ggplot2)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 0. Setup
# ------------------------------------------------------------

if (!exists("cosmx_obj")) {
  setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
  cosmx_obj <- readRDS("GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
}

celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]
expr_data <- cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data
coords_all <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[, c("x_slide_mm", "y_slide_mm", "fov")]
all_fovs <- sort(unique(coords_all$fov))

# Stellate cells and COL1A1 stratification
col1a1_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["COL1A1", ])
stellate_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
col1a1_pos <- stellate_idx[col1a1_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]
col1a1_neg <- stellate_idx[col1a1_expr[stellate_idx] == 0]

# Ligand+ source cells
pdgfc_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["PDGFC", ])
pdgfc_src <- which(
  celltype_vec %in% c("Cholangiocytes", "Inflammatory.macrophages",
                      "Non.inflammatory.macrophages") & pdgfc_expr > 0)

spp1_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["SPP1", ])
spp1_src <- which(
  celltype_vec %in% c("Cholangiocytes", "Hep.4",
                      "Inflammatory.macrophages",
                      "Non.inflammatory.macrophages") & spp1_expr > 0)

hgf_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["HGF", ])
hgf_pos_hsc <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]
hep4_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.4")

cat(sprintf("COL1A1+ HSC: %d, COL1A1- HSC: %d\n",
            length(col1a1_pos), length(col1a1_neg)))
cat(sprintf("PDGFC+ source: %d\n", length(pdgfc_src)))
cat(sprintf("SPP1+ source: %d\n", length(spp1_src)))
cat(sprintf("HGF+ HSC: %d, Hep.4: %d\n",
            length(hgf_pos_hsc), length(hep4_idx)))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Distance distribution function
# ------------------------------------------------------------

# Compute all pairwise distances between src and tgt within each FOV
# Returns distance distribution in µm (converting from mm)

compute_distance_distribution <- function(src_idx, tgt_idx, label_src, label_tgt) {
  distances <- numeric(0)
  
  for (f in all_fovs) {
    src_in_fov <- which(coords_all$fov == f & seq_len(nrow(coords_all)) %in% src_idx)
    tgt_in_fov <- which(coords_all$fov == f & seq_len(nrow(coords_all)) %in% tgt_idx)
    
    if (length(src_in_fov) == 0 || length(tgt_in_fov) == 0) next
    
    src_coords <- coords_all[src_in_fov, ]
    tgt_coords <- coords_all[tgt_in_fov, ]
    
    # For each src cell, compute distance to nearest tgt cell
    for (i in seq_len(nrow(src_coords))) {
      d <- sqrt((tgt_coords$x_slide_mm - src_coords$x_slide_mm[i])^2 +
                  (tgt_coords$y_slide_mm - src_coords$y_slide_mm[i])^2)
      distances <- c(distances, min(d))
    }
  }
  
  data.frame(
    Src = label_src,
    Tgt = label_tgt,
    Distance_um = distances * 1000  # mm -> µm
  )
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Compute distance distributions for all three axes
# ------------------------------------------------------------

cat("\nComputing distance distributions...\n")

dist_pdgfc_pos <- compute_distance_distribution(
  pdgfc_src, col1a1_pos, "PDGFC+", "COL1A1+ HSC")
dist_pdgfc_neg <- compute_distance_distribution(
  pdgfc_src, col1a1_neg, "PDGFC+", "COL1A1- HSC")
dist_spp1_pos <- compute_distance_distribution(
  spp1_src, col1a1_pos, "SPP1+", "COL1A1+ HSC")
dist_spp1_neg <- compute_distance_distribution(
  spp1_src, col1a1_neg, "SPP1+", "COL1A1- HSC")
dist_hgf_hep4 <- compute_distance_distribution(
  hgf_pos_hsc, hep4_idx, "HGF+ HSC", "Hep.4")

dist_all <- rbind(dist_pdgfc_pos, dist_pdgfc_neg,
                  dist_spp1_pos, dist_spp1_neg,
                  dist_hgf_hep4)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Fraction within 30 / 50 / 100 µm
# ------------------------------------------------------------

cat("\n=== Fraction of nearest neighbors within radius ===\n")
radius_summary <- dist_all %>%
  group_by(Src, Tgt) %>%
  summarise(
    N = n(),
    Median_dist_um = median(Distance_um),
    pct_within_30um = mean(Distance_um <= 30) * 100,
    pct_within_50um = mean(Distance_um <= 50) * 100,
    pct_within_100um = mean(Distance_um <= 100) * 100,
    .groups = "drop"
  )
print(as.data.frame(radius_summary))

write.csv(radius_summary, "Module4_distance_radius_summary.csv", row.names = FALSE)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Judge which radius is appropriate per axis
# ------------------------------------------------------------

cat("\n=== Radius recommendation ===\n")
for (i in seq_len(nrow(radius_summary))) {
  row <- radius_summary[i, ]
  cat(sprintf("%s -> %s:\n", row$Src, row$Tgt))
  cat(sprintf("  Median NN distance: %.1f µm\n", row$Median_dist_um))
  cat(sprintf("  %% within 30 µm:  %.1f%%\n", row$pct_within_30um))
  cat(sprintf("  %% within 50 µm:  %.1f%%\n", row$pct_within_50um))
  cat(sprintf("  %% within 100 µm: %.1f%%\n", row$pct_within_100um))
  
  if (row$pct_within_30um >= 20) {
    cat("  --> 30 µm appropriate\n\n")
  } else if (row$pct_within_50um >= 20) {
    cat("  --> 50 µm recommended\n\n")
  } else if (row$pct_within_100um >= 20) {
    cat("  --> 100 µm recommended\n\n")
  } else {
    cat("  --> Even 100 µm sparse, use distance distribution analysis\n\n")
  }
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Visualization: distance distributions (fixed)
# ------------------------------------------------------------

p_dist <- ggplot(dist_all, aes(x = Distance_um, fill = Tgt)) +
  geom_density(alpha = 0.5) +
  facet_wrap(~ Src, scales = "free_y") +
  geom_vline(xintercept = 30,  linetype = "dashed", color = "red",    linewidth = 0.5) +
  geom_vline(xintercept = 50,  linetype = "dashed", color = "orange", linewidth = 0.5) +
  geom_vline(xintercept = 100, linetype = "dashed", color = "blue",   linewidth = 0.5) +
  scale_x_log10() +
  labs(title = "Nearest-neighbor distance distributions",
       subtitle = "Dashed lines: 30 µm (red), 50 µm (orange), 100 µm (blue)",
       x = "Distance (µm, log scale)", y = "Density") +
  theme_classic()
print(p_dist)
ggsave("Module4_distance_distributions.pdf", p_dist, width = 10, height = 6)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. PDGFC+ and SPP1+ overlap
# ------------------------------------------------------------

cat("\n=== PDGFC+ and SPP1+ cell overlap ===\n")
overlap_cells <- intersect(pdgfc_src, spp1_src)
cat(sprintf("PDGFC+ cells: %d\n", length(pdgfc_src)))
cat(sprintf("SPP1+ cells: %d\n", length(spp1_src)))
cat(sprintf("Overlap: %d\n", length(overlap_cells)))
cat(sprintf("PDGFC+ in SPP1+: %.1f%%\n",
            100 * length(overlap_cells) / length(pdgfc_src)))
cat(sprintf("SPP1+ in PDGFC+: %.1f%%\n",
            100 * length(overlap_cells) / length(spp1_src)))

# Jaccard index
jaccard <- length(overlap_cells) /
  length(union(pdgfc_src, spp1_src))
cat(sprintf("Jaccard index: %.3f\n", jaccard))

# By cell type
overlap_df <- data.frame(
  CellType = names(table(celltype_vec[overlap_cells])),
  N = as.numeric(table(celltype_vec[overlap_cells]))
)
print(overlap_df)

saveRDS(list(
