# ============================================================
# Correct statistical framework for COL1A1 selectivity
# ============================================================

library(Seurat)
library(tidyverse)

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
if (!exists("cosmx_obj")) {
  cosmx_obj <- readRDS("GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
}

celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]
expr_data <- cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data
coords_all <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[, c("x_slide_mm", "y_slide_mm", "fov")]
all_fovs <- sort(unique(coords_all$fov))
radius_mm <- 0.03

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Baseline COL1A1+ proportion among all HSCs
# ------------------------------------------------------------

col1a1_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["COL1A1", ])
stellate_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
col1a1_pos <- stellate_idx[col1a1_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]
col1a1_neg <- stellate_idx[col1a1_expr[stellate_idx] == 0]

baseline_pos_prop <- length(col1a1_pos) /
  (length(col1a1_pos) + length(col1a1_neg))

cat(sprintf("=== Baseline HSC composition ===\n"))
cat(sprintf("COL1A1+ HSC: %d\n", length(col1a1_pos)))
cat(sprintf("COL1A1- HSC: %d\n", length(col1a1_neg)))
cat(sprintf("Baseline COL1A1+ proportion: %.1f%%\n", baseline_pos_prop * 100))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Define a proper selectivity metric per ligand+ cell
# ------------------------------------------------------------

# Ligand+ source cells
pdgfc_src <- which(celltype_vec %in% c("Cholangiocytes",
                                       "Inflammatory.macrophages",
                                       "Non.inflammatory.macrophages") &
                     as.numeric(expr_data["PDGFC", ]) > 0)
spp1_src <- which(celltype_vec %in% c("Cholangiocytes", "Hep.4",
                                      "Inflammatory.macrophages",
                                      "Non.inflammatory.macrophages") &
                    as.numeric(expr_data["SPP1", ]) > 0)

# For each ligand+ cell, compute the proportion of COL1A1+ HSC
# among HSCs within 30 µm
compute_col1a1_prop <- function(src_coords, pos_coords, neg_coords, radius) {
  props <- numeric(nrow(src_coords))
  for (i in seq_len(nrow(src_coords))) {
    dx_pos <- pos_coords$x_slide_mm - src_coords$x_slide_mm[i]
    dy_pos <- pos_coords$y_slide_mm - src_coords$y_slide_mm[i]
    n_pos <- sum(sqrt(dx_pos^2 + dy_pos^2) <= radius)
    
    dx_neg <- neg_coords$x_slide_mm - src_coords$x_slide_mm[i]
    dy_neg <- neg_coords$y_slide_mm - src_coords$y_slide_mm[i]
    n_neg <- sum(sqrt(dx_neg^2 + dy_neg^2) <= radius)
    
    total <- n_pos + n_neg
    props[i] <- if (total == 0) NA else n_pos / total
  }
  props
}

# Run per FOV, aggregate
run_axis_selectivity <- function(src_idx, src_label) {
  set.seed(123)
  coords_src <- coords_all[src_idx, ]
  coords_pos <- coords_all[col1a1_pos, ]
  coords_neg <- coords_all[col1a1_neg, ]
  
  # Observed
  all_props <- numeric(0)
  for (f in all_fovs) {
    src_f <- coords_src[coords_src$fov == f, ]
    pos_f <- coords_pos[coords_pos$fov == f, ]
    neg_f <- coords_neg[coords_neg$fov == f, ]
    if (nrow(src_f) == 0) next
    props <- compute_col1a1_prop(src_f, pos_f, neg_f, radius_mm)
    all_props <- c(all_props, props)
  }
  all_props <- all_props[!is.na(all_props)]
  
  obs_mean_prop <- mean(all_props)
  obs_n_cells <- length(all_props)
  
  cat(sprintf("\n=== %s -> HSC composition within 30 um ===\n", src_label))
  cat(sprintf("Ligand+ cells with >=1 HSC neighbor: %d\n", obs_n_cells))
  cat(sprintf("Mean COL1A1+ proportion: %.3f\n", obs_mean_prop))
  cat(sprintf("Baseline COL1A1+ proportion: %.3f\n", baseline_pos_prop))
  cat(sprintf("Direction: %s baseline\n",
              ifelse(obs_mean_prop > baseline_pos_prop, "ABOVE", "BELOW")))
  
  # Permutation: shuffle ligand+ positions within FOV
  n_perm <- 1000
  perm_means <- numeric(n_perm)
  for (p in 1:n_perm) {
    shuffled <- coords_src
    for (f in all_fovs) {
      idx_f <- which(shuffled$fov == f)
      if (length(idx_f) > 1) {
        cells_in_fov <- which(coords_all$fov == f)
        sampled <- sample(cells_in_fov, length(idx_f))
        shuffled[idx_f, c("x_slide_mm", "y_slide_mm")] <-
          coords_all[sampled, c("x_slide_mm", "y_slide_mm")]
      }
    }
    perm_props <- numeric(0)
    for (f in all_fovs) {
      src_f <- shuffled[shuffled$fov == f, ]
      pos_f <- coords_pos[coords_pos$fov == f, ]
      neg_f <- coords_neg[coords_neg$fov == f, ]
      if (nrow(src_f) == 0) next
      props <- compute_col1a1_prop(src_f, pos_f, neg_f, radius_mm)
      perm_props <- c(perm_props, props)
    }
    perm_props <- perm_props[!is.na(perm_props)]
    perm_means[p] <- mean(perm_props)
  }
  
  p_selectivity <- mean(perm_means >= obs_mean_prop)
  cat(sprintf("Null mean: %.3f (SD %.3f)\n",
              mean(perm_means), sd(perm_means)))
  cat(sprintf("Permutation p-value (observed >= null): %.4f\n",
              p_selectivity))
  
  list(
    src = src_label,
    n_cells = obs_n_cells,
    obs_mean_prop = obs_mean_prop,
    baseline = baseline_pos_prop,
    direction = ifelse(obs_mean_prop > baseline_pos_prop, "above", "below"),
    null_mean = mean(perm_means),
    null_sd = sd(perm_means),
    p_value = p_selectivity
  )
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Run for both axes
# ------------------------------------------------------------

res_pdgfc <- run_axis_selectivity(pdgfc_src, "PDGFC+")
res_spp1  <- run_axis_selectivity(spp1_src, "SPP1+")

# Summary
summary_df <- data.frame(
  Axis = c("PDGFC-PDGFRB", "SPP1-ITGAV/ITGB1"),
  N_ligand_cells = c(res_pdgfc$n_cells, res_spp1$n_cells),
  Obs_COL1A1pos_prop = round(c(res_pdgfc$obs_mean_prop,
                               res_spp1$obs_mean_prop), 3),
  Baseline = round(baseline_pos_prop, 3),
  Direction = c(res_pdgfc$direction, res_spp1$direction),
  Null_mean = round(c(res_pdgfc$null_mean, res_spp1$null_mean), 3),
  P_value = c(res_pdgfc$p_value, res_spp1$p_value)
)
print(summary_df)
write.csv(summary_df, "Module4_corrected_selectivity.csv", row.names = FALSE)

saveRDS(list(pdgfc = res_pdgfc, spp1 = res_spp1, summary = summary_df),
        "Module4_corrected_results.rds")

cat("\n=== Corrected analysis completed ===\n")



# ============================================================
# 探索性分析：三个潜在方向（无 ape 依赖）
# ============================================================

library(Seurat)
library(tidyverse)
library(ggplot2)

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
if (!exists("cosmx_obj")) {
  cosmx_obj <- readRDS("GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
}

celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]
expr_data <- cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data
coords_all <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[, c("x_slide_mm", "y_slide_mm", "fov")]
all_fovs <- sort(unique(coords_all$fov))

# HSC and COL1A1
col1a1_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["COL1A1", ])
stellate_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
col1a1_pos <- stellate_idx[col1a1_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]
col1a1_neg <- stellate_idx[col1a1_expr[stellate_idx] == 0]

coords_hsc <- coords_all[stellate_idx, ]
coords_hsc$COL1A1_expr <- col1a1_expr[stellate_idx]
coords_hsc$COL1A1_pos <- coords_hsc$COL1A1_expr > 0

cat(sprintf("HSC: %d, COL1A1+: %d (%.1f%%), COL1A1-: %d\n",
            length(stellate_idx), length(col1a1_pos),
            100 * length(col1a1_pos) / length(stellate_idx),
            length(col1a1_neg)))

# ============================================================
# 方向一：COL1A1+/- HSC 的空间自相关
# ============================================================

cat("\n========== Direction 1: Spatial autocorrelation ==========\n")

moran_results <- data.frame(
  FOV = all_fovs,
  N_HSC = NA_integer_,
  Moran_I_COL1A1_pos = NA_real_
)

compute_moran <- function(coords_f, values) {
  n <- length(values)
  if (n < 10) return(NA)
  dist_mat <- as.matrix(dist(coords_f[, c("x_slide_mm", "y_slide_mm")]))
  W <- 1 / dist_mat
  diag(W) <- 0
  W[is.infinite(W)] <- 0
  W <- W / rowSums(W)
  x <- values - mean(values)
  I <- (n / sum(W)) * (t(x) %*% W %*% x) / sum(x^2)
  as.numeric(I)
}

for (i in seq_along(all_fovs)) {
  f <- all_fovs[i]
  idx_f <- which(coords_hsc$fov == f)
  if (length(idx_f) < 10) next
  coords_f <- coords_hsc[idx_f, ]
  moran_results$N_HSC[i] <- length(idx_f)
  moran_results$Moran_I_COL1A1_pos[i] <- compute_moran(
    coords_f, as.numeric(coords_f$COL1A1_pos))
}

cat("\nMoran's I for COL1A1+ HSC (per FOV):\n")
cat(sprintf("  Mean Moran's I: %.3f\n",
            mean(moran_results$Moran_I_COL1A1_pos, na.rm = TRUE)))
cat(sprintf("  Median: %.3f\n",
            median(moran_results$Moran_I_COL1A1_pos, na.rm = TRUE)))
cat(sprintf("  SD: %.3f\n",
            sd(moran_results$Moran_I_COL1A1_pos, na.rm = TRUE)))
cat(sprintf("  FOVs with I > 0: %d / %d\n",
            sum(moran_results$Moran_I_COL1A1_pos > 0, na.rm = TRUE),
            sum(!is.na(moran_results$Moran_I_COL1A1_pos))))

# ============================================================
# 方向二：COL1A1 连续表达 vs 配体邻近
# ============================================================

cat("\n========== Direction 2: Continuous COL1A1 vs ligand proximity ==========\n")

pdgfc_src <- which(celltype_vec %in% c("Cholangiocytes",
                                       "Inflammatory.macrophages",
                                       "Non.inflammatory.macrophages") &
                     as.numeric(expr_data["PDGFC", ]) > 0)
spp1_src <- which(celltype_vec %in% c("Cholangiocytes", "Hep.4",
                                      "Inflammatory.macrophages",
                                      "Non.inflammatory.macrophages") &
                    as.numeric(expr_data["SPP1", ]) > 0)

coords_pdgfc <- coords_all[pdgfc_src, ]
coords_spp1 <- coords_all[spp1_src, ]

radius_mm <- 0.03

count_ligand_neighbors <- function(hsc_coords, ligand_coords, radius) {
  n_hsc <- nrow(hsc_coords)
  counts <- numeric(n_hsc)
  for (i in seq_len(n_hsc)) {
    dx <- ligand_coords$x_slide_mm - hsc_coords$x_slide_mm[i]
    dy <- ligand_coords$y_slide_mm - hsc_coords$y_slide_mm[i]
    counts[i] <- sum(sqrt(dx^2 + dy^2) <= radius)
  }
  counts
}

coords_hsc$n_PDGFC_30um <- 0
coords_hsc$n_SPP1_30um <- 0

for (f in all_fovs) {
  idx_hsc <- which(coords_hsc$fov == f)
  if (length(idx_hsc) == 0) next
  idx_pdgfc <- which(coords_pdgfc$fov == f)
  idx_spp1 <- which(coords_spp1$fov == f)
  if (length(idx_pdgfc) > 0) {
    coords_hsc$n_PDGFC_30um[idx_hsc] <- count_ligand_neighbors(
      coords_hsc[idx_hsc, ], coords_pdgfc[idx_pdgfc, ], radius_mm)
  }
  if (length(idx_spp1) > 0) {
    coords_hsc$n_SPP1_30um[idx_hsc] <- count_ligand_neighbors(
      coords_hsc[idx_hsc, ], coords_spp1[idx_spp1, ], radius_mm)
  }
}

cor_pdgfc <- cor.test(coords_hsc$COL1A1_expr, coords_hsc$n_PDGFC_30um,
                      method = "spearman")
cor_spp1 <- cor.test(coords_hsc$COL1A1_expr, coords_hsc$n_SPP1_30um,
                     method = "spearman")

cat("\nCOL1A1 continuous expression vs ligand neighbors (Spearman):\n")
cat(sprintf("  vs PDGFC+ neighbors: rho = %.3f, p = %.3e\n",
            cor_pdgfc$estimate, cor_pdgfc$p.value))
cat(sprintf("  vs SPP1+ neighbors:  rho = %.3f, p = %.3e\n",
            cor_spp1$estimate, cor_spp1$p.value))

hsc_with_pdgfc <- coords_hsc$n_PDGFC_30um > 0
hsc_with_spp1 <- coords_hsc$n_SPP1_30um > 0

wilcox_pdgfc <- wilcox.test(coords_hsc$COL1A1_expr[hsc_with_pdgfc],
                            coords_hsc$COL1A1_expr[!hsc_with_pdgfc])
wilcox_spp1 <- wilcox.test(coords_hsc$COL1A1_expr[hsc_with_spp1],
                           coords_hsc$COL1A1_expr[!hsc_with_spp1])

cat("\nCOL1A1 expression in HSC with vs without ligand neighbors:\n")
cat(sprintf("  PDGFC+: yes = %.3f, no = %.3f, p = %.3e\n",
            mean(coords_hsc$COL1A1_expr[hsc_with_pdgfc]),
            mean(coords_hsc$COL1A1_expr[!hsc_with_pdgfc]),
            wilcox_pdgfc$p.value))
cat(sprintf("  SPP1+:  yes = %.3f, no = %.3f, p = %.3e\n",
            mean(coords_hsc$COL1A1_expr[hsc_with_spp1]),
            mean(coords_hsc$COL1A1_expr[!hsc_with_spp1]),
            wilcox_spp1$p.value))

# ============================================================
# 方向三：HGF+ vs HGF- HSC 到 Hep.4 的距离分布
# ============================================================

cat("\n========== Direction 3: HGF+ vs HGF- HSC -> Hep.4 ==========\n")

hgf_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["HGF", ])
hgf_pos_hsc <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]
hgf_neg_hsc <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] == 0]
hep4_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.4")

coords_hgf_pos <- coords_all[hgf_pos_hsc, ]
coords_hgf_neg <- coords_all[hgf_neg_hsc, ]
coords_hep4 <- coords_all[hep4_idx, ]

compute_nn_to_target <- function(hsc_coords, target_coords) {
  nn <- numeric(nrow(hsc_coords))
  for (i in seq_len(nrow(hsc_coords))) {
    f <- hsc_coords$fov[i]
    tgt_f <- target_coords[target_coords$fov == f, ]
    if (nrow(tgt_f) == 0) {
      nn[i] <- NA
      next
    }
    d <- sqrt((tgt_f$x_slide_mm - hsc_coords$x_slide_mm[i])^2 +
                (tgt_f$y_slide_mm - hsc_coords$y_slide_mm[i])^2)
    nn[i] <- min(d) * 1000
  }
  nn
}

dist_hgf_pos <- compute_nn_to_target(coords_hgf_pos, coords_hep4)
dist_hgf_neg <- compute_nn_to_target(coords_hgf_neg, coords_hep4)

cat("\nHGF+ HSC -> Hep.4:\n")
cat(sprintf("  Median: %.1f µm\n", median(dist_hgf_pos, na.rm = TRUE)))
cat(sprintf("  %% within 50 µm:  %.1f%%\n",
            100 * mean(dist_hgf_pos <= 50, na.rm = TRUE)))
cat(sprintf("  %% within 100 µm: %.1f%%\n",
            100 * mean(dist_hgf_pos <= 100, na.rm = TRUE)))

cat("\nHGF- HSC -> Hep.4:\n")
cat(sprintf("  Median: %.1f µm\n", median(dist_hgf_neg, na.rm = TRUE)))
cat(sprintf("  %% within 50 µm:  %.1f%%\n",
            100 * mean(dist_hgf_neg <= 50, na.rm = TRUE)))
cat(sprintf("  %% within 100 µm: %.1f%%\n",
            100 * mean(dist_hgf_neg <= 100, na.rm = TRUE)))

wilcox_hgf <- wilcox.test(dist_hgf_pos, dist_hgf_neg)
cat(sprintf("\nWilcoxon test (HGF+ vs HGF-): p = %.4f\n", wilcox_hgf$p.value))

# ============================================================
# Summary
# ============================================================

cat("\n\n========== Summary ==========\n")
cat(sprintf("Direction 1 (COL1A1+ HSC clustering): Mean Moran's I = %.3f\n",
            mean(moran_results$Moran_I_COL1A1_pos, na.rm = TRUE)))
cat(sprintf("Direction 2 (COL1A1 vs PDGFC): rho = %.3f, p = %.3e\n",
            cor_pdgfc$estimate, cor_pdgfc$p.value))
cat(sprintf("Direction 2 (COL1A1 vs SPP1):  rho = %.3f, p = %.3e\n",
            cor_spp1$estimate, cor_spp1$p.value))
cat(sprintf("Direction 3 (HGF+ vs HGF- HSC -> Hep.4): p = %.4f\n",
            wilcox_hgf$p.value))

saveRDS(list(
  moran_results = moran_results,
  coords_hsc = coords_hsc,
  cor_pdgfc = cor_pdgfc, cor_spp1 = cor_spp1,
  wilcox_pdgfc = wilcox_pdgfc, wilcox_spp1 = wilcox_spp1,
  dist_hgf_pos = dist_hgf_pos, dist_hgf_neg = dist_hgf_neg,
  wilcox_hgf = wilcox_hgf
), "Exploratory_analysis_3_directions.rds")

cat("\n=== Exploratory analysis completed ===\n")


# ============================================================
# 方向三混杂控制 + 方向 A/B/C 并行探索
# ============================================================

library(Seurat)
library(tidyverse)
library(ggplot2)

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
if (!exists("cosmx_obj")) {
  cosmx_obj <- readRDS("GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
}

celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]
expr_data <- cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data
coords_all <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[, c("x_slide_mm", "y_slide_mm", "fov")]
all_fovs <- sort(unique(coords_all$fov))

# Basal definitions
stellate_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
col1a1_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["COL1A1", ])
hgf_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["HGF", ])

hgf_pos <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]
hgf_neg <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] == 0]
hep4_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.4")

# ============================================================
# 1. 纤维化区定义 (非HSC来源的独立定义)
# ============================================================

cat("\n========== Defining fibrotic region ==========\n")

fibro_sig_indep <- c("IL6", "IL1B", "CCL2", "CXCL9", "CXCL10", "TNF",
                     "TGFB1", "TGFBR1", "TGFBR2", "MMP2", "MMP14")
fibro_sig_indep <- fibro_sig_indep[fibro_sig_indep %in% rownames(expr_data)]

expr_indep <- as.matrix(expr_data[fibro_sig_indep, , drop = FALSE])
fibro_score_indep <- colMeans(expr_indep)

non_hsc_idx <- which(celltype_vec != "Stellate.cells")
fibro_score_nonhsc <- colMeans(expr_indep[, non_hsc_idx, drop = FALSE])
thr_nonhsc <- quantile(fibro_score_nonhsc, 0.75, na.rm = TRUE)
is_fibrotic <- fibro_score_indep >= thr_nonhsc

cat(sprintf("Fibrotic cells: %d (%.1f%%)\n",
            sum(is_fibrotic), 100 * mean(is_fibrotic)))

# ============================================================
# 2. 方向三混杂控制
# ============================================================

cat("\n\n========== Direction 3 confound control ==========\n")

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2.1 按纤维化区 vs 非纤维化区分层
# ------------------------------------------------------------

compute_nn_to_hep4 <- function(hsc_idx, fovs = all_fovs) {
  nn <- numeric(length(hsc_idx))
  for (i in seq_along(hsc_idx)) {
    cell_i <- hsc_idx[i]
    f <- coords_all$fov[cell_i]
    if (!(f %in% fovs)) { nn[i] <- NA; next }
    hep4_in_fov <- which(coords_all$fov == f & 
                           seq_len(nrow(coords_all)) %in% hep4_idx)
    if (length(hep4_in_fov) == 0) { nn[i] <- NA; next }
    tgt <- coords_all[hep4_in_fov, ]
    d <- sqrt((tgt$x_slide_mm - coords_all$x_slide_mm[cell_i])^2 +
                (tgt$y_slide_mm - coords_all$y_slide_mm[cell_i])^2)
    nn[i] <- min(d) * 1000
  }
  nn
}

# Stratified by fibrotic region
hgf_pos_fibro <- hgf_pos[is_fibrotic[hgf_pos]]
hgf_pos_nonfibro <- hgf_pos[!is_fibrotic[hgf_pos]]
hgf_neg_fibro <- hgf_neg[is_fibrotic[hgf_neg]]
hgf_neg_nonfibro <- hgf_neg[!is_fibrotic[hgf_neg]]

cat("\n=== Stratified by fibrotic region ===\n")
cat(sprintf("HGF+ HSC in fibrotic region: %d\n", length(hgf_pos_fibro)))
cat(sprintf("HGF+ HSC in non-fibrotic:    %d\n", length(hgf_pos_nonfibro)))
cat(sprintf("HGF- HSC in fibrotic region: %d\n", length(hgf_neg_fibro)))
cat(sprintf("HGF- HSC in non-fibrotic:    %d\n", length(hgf_neg_nonfibro)))

# Distance within fibrotic region
dist_hgf_pos_fibro <- compute_nn_to_hep4(hgf_pos_fibro)
dist_hgf_neg_fibro <- compute_nn_to_hep4(hgf_neg_fibro)
# Distance within non-fibrotic region
dist_hgf_pos_nonfibro <- compute_nn_to_hep4(hgf_pos_nonfibro)
dist_hgf_neg_nonfibro <- compute_nn_to_hep4(hgf_neg_nonfibro)

cat("\n=== HGF+ vs HGF- HSC -> Hep.4 (fibrotic region only) ===\n")
cat(sprintf("HGF+ median: %.1f µm\n", median(dist_hgf_pos_fibro, na.rm = TRUE)))
cat(sprintf("HGF- median: %.1f µm\n", median(dist_hgf_neg_fibro, na.rm = TRUE)))
w1 <- wilcox.test(dist_hgf_pos_fibro, dist_hgf_neg_fibro)
cat(sprintf("Wilcoxon p = %.4f\n", w1$p.value))

cat("\n=== HGF+ vs HGF- HSC -> Hep.4 (non-fibrotic region only) ===\n")
cat(sprintf("HGF+ median: %.1f µm\n", median(dist_hgf_pos_nonfibro, na.rm = TRUE)))
cat(sprintf("HGF- median: %.1f µm\n", median(dist_hgf_neg_nonfibro, na.rm = TRUE)))
w2 <- wilcox.test(dist_hgf_pos_nonfibro, dist_hgf_neg_nonfibro)
cat(sprintf("Wilcoxon p = %.4f\n", w2$p.value))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2.2 其他肝细胞亚型对照
# ------------------------------------------------------------

cat("\n=== HGF+ HSC -> other hepatocyte subtypes ===\n")
for (hep_label in c("Hep.1", "Hep.2", "Hep.3", "Hep.4", "Hep.5", "Hep.6")) {
  hep_idx <- which(celltype_vec == hep_label)
  if (length(hep_idx) < 50) next
  
  compute_nn <- function(hsc_idx) {
    nn <- numeric(length(hsc_idx))
    for (i in seq_along(hsc_idx)) {
      cell_i <- hsc_idx[i]
      f <- coords_all$fov[cell_i]
      hep_in_fov <- which(coords_all$fov == f & 
                            seq_len(nrow(coords_all)) %in% hep_idx)
      if (length(hep_in_fov) == 0) { nn[i] <- NA; next }
      tgt <- coords_all[hep_in_fov, ]
      d <- sqrt((tgt$x_slide_mm - coords_all$x_slide_mm[cell_i])^2 +
                  (tgt$y_slide_mm - coords_all$y_slide_mm[cell_i])^2)
      nn[i] <- min(d) * 1000
    }
    nn
  }
  
  d_pos <- compute_nn(hgf_pos)
  d_neg <- compute_nn(hgf_neg)
  w <- wilcox.test(d_pos, d_neg)
  cat(sprintf("%s (n=%d): HGF+ = %.1f, HGF- = %.1f µm, p = %.4f\n",
              hep_label, length(hep_idx),
              median(d_pos, na.rm = TRUE),
              median(d_neg, na.rm = TRUE),
              w$p.value))
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2.3 其他 HSC 亚群对照
# ------------------------------------------------------------

cat("\n=== Other HSC subsets -> Hep.4 ===\n")
pdgfrb_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["PDGFRB", ])
pdgfrb_pos <- stellate_idx[pdgfrb_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]
pdgfrb_neg <- stellate_idx[pdgfrb_expr[stellate_idx] == 0]

d_pdgfrb_pos <- compute_nn_to_hep4(pdgfrb_pos)
d_pdgfrb_neg <- compute_nn_to_hep4(pdgfrb_neg)
w3 <- wilcox.test(d_pdgfrb_pos, d_pdgfrb_neg)
cat(sprintf("PDGFRB+ HSC -> Hep.4: median %.1f µm (n=%d)\n",
            median(d_pdgfrb_pos, na.rm = TRUE), length(pdgfrb_pos)))
cat(sprintf("PDGFRB- HSC -> Hep.4: median %.1f µm (n=%d)\n",
            median(d_pdgfrb_neg, na.rm = TRUE), length(pdgfrb_neg)))
cat(sprintf("Wilcoxon p = %.4f\n", w3$p.value))

# ============================================================
# 3. 方向 A: HGF+ vs HGF- HSC 转录谱
# ============================================================

cat("\n\n========== Direction A: HGF+ vs HGF- HSC transcriptome ==========\n")

# Use FindMarkers
hsc_obj <- subset(cosmx_obj, cells = colnames(cosmx_obj)[stellate_idx])
hsc_obj$hgf_status <- ifelse(colnames(hsc_obj) %in% 
                               colnames(cosmx_obj)[hgf_pos], "HGF_pos", "HGF_neg")

Idents(hsc_obj) <- hsc_obj$hgf_status
deg_hsc <- FindMarkers(hsc_obj, ident.1 = "HGF_pos", ident.2 = "HGF_neg",
                       logfc.threshold = 0.1, min.pct = 0.05)
deg_hsc$gene <- rownames(deg_hsc)
cat(sprintf("DEGs between HGF+ and HGF- HSC: %d\n", nrow(deg_hsc)))
cat(sprintf("Significant (p < 0.05): %d\n", sum(deg_hsc$p_val_adj < 0.05)))

cat("\nTop 20 DEGs by p-value:\n")
top_degs <- head(deg_hsc[order(deg_hsc$p_val_adj), 
                         c("gene", "avg_log2FC", "p_val_adj")], 20)
print(top_degs)

write.csv(deg_hsc, "DirectionA_HGFpos_vs_neg_DEGs.csv", row.names = FALSE)

# ============================================================
# 4. 方向 B: Hep.4 其他受体表达
# ============================================================

cat("\n\n========== Direction B: Hep.4 receptor profile ==========\n")

receptors_to_check <- c("EGFR", "FGFR1", "FGFR2", "FGFR3", "FGFR4",
                        "IGF1R", "INSR", "MET", "TGFBR1", "TGFBR2",
                        "PDGFRA", "PDGFRB", "KDR", "FLT1", "AXL",
                        "NOTCH1", "NOTCH2", "LRP1", "LRP6", "FZD1")
receptors_to_check <- receptors_to_check[
  receptors_to_check %in% rownames(expr_data)]

hep4_expr <- as.matrix(expr_data[receptors_to_check, hep4_idx, drop = FALSE])
hep4_avg <- rowMeans(hep4_expr)
hep4_pos_rate <- rowMeans(hep4_expr > 0)

cat("\nHep.4 receptor expression:\n")
hep4_df <- data.frame(
  Receptor = receptors_to_check,
  AvgExpr = round(hep4_avg, 3),
  PosRate = round(hep4_pos_rate, 3)
)
hep4_df <- hep4_df[order(-hep4_df$PosRate), ]
print(hep4_df)

# Compare Hep.4 vs other Hep subtypes
cat("\n=== Hep.4 vs Hep.5 receptor comparison ===\n")
hep5_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.5")
if (length(hep5_idx) >= 50) {
  hep5_expr <- as.matrix(expr_data[receptors_to_check, hep5_idx, drop = FALSE])
  hep5_pos_rate <- rowMeans(hep5_expr > 0)
  compare_df <- data.frame(
    Receptor = receptors_to_check,
    Hep4 = round(hep4_pos_rate, 3),
    Hep5 = round(hep5_pos_rate, 3),
    FC = round(hep4_pos_rate / (hep5_pos_rate + 0.001), 2)
  )
  compare_df <- compare_df[order(-compare_df$FC), ]
  print(compare_df)
}

write.csv(hep4_df, "DirectionB_Hep4_receptors.csv", row.names = FALSE)

library(Seurat)

celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"

expr_data <- cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data

stellate_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
hgf_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["HGF", ])
hgf_pos <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]
hgf_neg <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] == 0]
hep4_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.4")
hep5_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.5")

# ============================================================
# 1. COL3A1 阳性率和真实 FC 核查
# ============================================================

cat("========== 1. COL3A1 sanity check ==========\n")

check_gene <- function(gene, idx1, idx2, label1, label2) {
  if (!(gene %in% rownames(expr_data))) {
    cat(sprintf("%s not in panel\n", gene)); return(NULL)
  }
  x <- as.numeric(expr_data[gene, ])
  pos_rate1 <- mean(x[idx1] > 0)
  pos_rate2 <- mean(x[idx2] > 0)
  mean_all_1 <- mean(x[idx1])
  mean_all_2 <- mean(x[idx2])
  mean_pos_1 <- mean(x[idx1][x[idx1] > 0])
  mean_pos_2 <- mean(x[idx2][x[idx2] > 0])
  fc_all <- mean_all_1 / (mean_all_2 + 1e-6)
  fc_pos <- mean_pos_1 / (mean_pos_2 + 1e-6)
  
  cat(sprintf("\n--- %s ---\n", gene))
  cat(sprintf("  %s: PosRate = %.3f, Mean(all) = %.3f, Mean(pos) = %.3f\n",
              label1, pos_rate1, mean_all_1, mean_pos_1))
  cat(sprintf("  %s: PosRate = %.3f, Mean(all) = %.3f, Mean(pos) = %.3f\n",
              label2, pos_rate2, mean_all_2, mean_pos_2))
  cat(sprintf("  FC (all): %.2f, FC (pos only): %.2f\n", fc_all, fc_pos))
  
  data.frame(
    Gene = gene,
    PosRate_HGFpos = round(pos_rate1, 3),
    PosRate_HGFneg = round(pos_rate2, 3),
    Mean_all_HGFpos = round(mean_all_1, 3),
    Mean_all_HGFneg = round(mean_all_2, 3),
    Mean_pos_HGFpos = round(mean_pos_1, 3),
    Mean_pos_HGFneg = round(mean_pos_2, 3),
    FC_all = round(fc_all, 2),
    FC_pos = round(fc_pos, 2)
  )
}

genes_to_check <- c("COL3A1", "COL1A1", "COL6A1", "COL6A2",
                    "DCN", "BGN", "THBS1", "CXCL12", "IGFBP3")
sanity_results <- do.call(rbind, lapply(genes_to_check, function(g) {
  check_gene(g, hgf_pos, hgf_neg, "HGF+", "HGF-")
}))
print(sanity_results)
write.csv(sanity_results, "HGFpos_HSC_top_genes_sanity_check.csv", row.names = FALSE)

# ============================================================
# 2. 严格校正的 DEG 分析
# ============================================================

cat("\n\n========== 2. Strictly filtered DEGs ==========\n")

hsc_obj <- subset(cosmx_obj, cells = colnames(cosmx_obj)[stellate_idx])
hsc_obj$hgf_status <- ifelse(
  colnames(hsc_obj) %in% colnames(cosmx_obj)[hgf_pos],
  "HGF_pos", "HGF_neg")

Idents(hsc_obj) <- hsc_obj$hgf_status

# Re-run FindMarkers with strict criteria
deg_strict <- FindMarkers(
  hsc_obj,
  ident.1 = "HGF_pos",
  ident.2 = "HGF_neg",
  logfc.threshold = 0.5,
  min.pct = 0.1,
  test.use = "wilcox"
)
deg_strict$gene <- rownames(deg_strict)

# Additional filters
deg_final <- deg_strict[
  abs(deg_strict$avg_log2FC) > 1 &
    deg_strict$p_val_adj < 0.01 &
    (deg_strict$pct.1 - deg_strict$pct.2) > 0.2,
]

cat(sprintf("DEGs after strict filter: %d\n", nrow(deg_final)))
cat(sprintf("Up in HGF+: %d\n", sum(deg_final$avg_log2FC > 0)))
cat(sprintf("Down in HGF+: %d\n", sum(deg_final$avg_log2FC < 0)))

cat("\nTop 30 up-regulated genes:\n")
top_up <- deg_final[order(-deg_final$avg_log2FC), ][1:min(30, nrow(deg_final)), ]
print(top_up[, c("gene", "avg_log2FC", "pct.1", "pct.2", "p_val_adj")])

write.csv(deg_final, "HGFpos_HSC_DEGs_strict.csv", row.names = FALSE)

cat("\n\n========== 3. Pathway enrichment ==========\n")

if (!requireNamespace("clusterProfiler", quietly = TRUE)) {
  BiocManager::install("clusterProfiler")

  BiocManager::install("org.Hs.eg.db")

cat(sprintf("Genes for enrichment: %d\n", length(sig_genes)))

if (length(sig_genes) >= 10) {
  gene_ids <- tryCatch({
    bitr(sig_genes, fromType = "SYMBOL", toType = "ENTREZID",
         OrgDb = org.Hs.eg.db)
  }, error = function(e) NULL)
  
  if (!is.null(gene_ids) && nrow(gene_ids) >= 10) {
    # GO BP
    ego <- enrichGO(gene = gene_ids$ENTREZID,
                    OrgDb = org.Hs.eg.db, ont = "BP",
                    pAdjustMethod = "BH",
                    pvalueCutoff = 0.05, qvalueCutoff = 0.05,
                    readable = TRUE)
    cat("\n=== Top 15 GO BP terms ===\n")
    if (!is.null(ego) && nrow(as.data.frame(ego)) > 0) {
      print(head(as.data.frame(ego)[, c("Description", "GeneRatio",
                                        "pvalue", "p.adjust")], 15))
      write.csv(as.data.frame(ego), "HGFpos_HSC_GO_BP.csv", row.names = FALSE)
    }
    
    # KEGG
    ekegg <- enrichKEGG(gene = gene_ids$ENTREZID, organism = "hsa",
                        pvalueCutoff = 0.05)
    cat("\n=== Top 15 KEGG pathways ===\n")
    if (!is.null(ekegg) && nrow(as.data.frame(ekegg)) > 0) {
      print(head(as.data.frame(ekegg)[, c("Description", "GeneRatio",
                                          "pvalue", "p.adjust")], 15))
      write.csv(as.data.frame(ekegg), "HGFpos_HSC_KEGG.csv", row.names = FALSE)
    }
  }
}

  }

# 富集分析：用 CosMx panel 作为背景

library(tidyverse)

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")

# 1. 载入 panel 基因和 DEGs
panel_genes <- toupper(read.csv("GSE292268_Slide1_panel_genes.csv")$gene)

cat(sprintf("Up-regulated DEGs: %d\n", length(up_genes)))

# 2. 手工定义 KEGG 通路基因集
# 直接从 WebGestalt 输出的 enriched KEGG 通路取基因
ecm_receptor_genes <- toupper(c(
  "COL6A1", "ITGA9", "COL6A2", "THBS1", "ITGA1", "COL6A3", "COL1A1", "VTN",
  # KEGG hsa04512 完整成员（panel 内）
  "COL4A1", "COL4A2", "COL4A4", "FN1", "LAMA2", "LAMB1", "LAMC1",
  "TNXB", "ITGAV", "ITGB1", "ITGB5", "ITGB8", "CD44", "ITGA4",
  "ITGAM", "ITGB2", "ITGAL", "ITGAX"
))

focal_adhesion_genes <- toupper(c(
  "HGF", "COL6A1", "ITGA9", "COL6A2", "THBS1", "ITGA1", "COL6A3",
  "PDGFRA", "COL1A1", "VTN",
  "COL4A1", "COL4A2", "COL4A4", "FN1", "LAMA2", "LAMB1", "LAMC1",
  "TNXB", "ITGAV", "ITGB1", "ITGB5", "ITGB8", "PDGFRB", "IGF1R",
  "MET", "EGFR", "VEGFA", "KDR", "FLT1", "FLT4", "CSF1R", "AXL",
  "PIK3CA", "PIK3CB", "PIK3CD", "PIK3CG", "AKT1", "AKT2", "AKT3"
))

pi3k_akt_genes <- toupper(c(
  "HGF", "COL6A1", "ITGA9", "COL6A2", "FGFR2", "THBS1", "NGFR",
  "ITGA1", "CSF1", "COL6A3", "PDGFRA", "COL1A1", "VTN",
  "EGFR", "INSR", "IGF1R", "MET", "PDGFRB", "KDR", "FLT1", "FLT4",
  "PIK3CA", "PIK3CB", "PIK3CD", "PIK3CG", "AKT1", "AKT2", "AKT3",
  "CCND1", "CDK2", "CDK4", "CDK6", "BCL2", "BCL2L1", "MCL1"
))

ecm_organization_genes <- toupper(c(
  "COL6A1", "COL3A1", "BMP1", "APP", "COL27A1", "PDGFRA", "ENG",
  "COL1A1", "COL5A1", "PXDN", "TNFRSF1A", "VTN",
  "COL1A2", "COL4A1", "COL4A2", "COL4A4", "FN1", "LAMA2", "LAMB1",
  "LAMC1", "TNXB", "DCN", "BGN", "LUM", "SPARC", "POSTN", "MMP2", "MMP9"
))

# 3. 计算每个通路的富集（panel 背景）
compute_enrichment <- function(deg_genes, pathway_genes, panel_genes, pathway_name) {
  pathway_in_panel <- intersect(pathway_genes, panel_genes)
  deg_in_pathway <- intersect(deg_genes, pathway_in_panel)
  
  n_panel <- length(panel_genes)
  n_deg <- length(deg_genes)
  n_pathway <- length(pathway_in_panel)
  n_overlap <- length(deg_in_pathway)
  
  # 期望值 = 背景中的通路基因比例 × DEG 数量
  expect <- (n_pathway / n_panel) * n_deg
  # 富集比 = 观测值 / 期望值
  enrich_ratio <- n_overlap / expect
  
  # Fisher 检验
  fisher_res <- fisher.test(matrix(c(
    n_overlap, n_pathway - n_overlap,
    n_deg - n_overlap, n_panel - n_deg - n_pathway + n_overlap
  ), nrow = 2))
  
  data.frame(
    Pathway = pathway_name,
    Panel_Genes_in_Pathway = n_pathway,
    DEGs_in_Pathway = n_overlap,
    Expected = round(expect, 2),
    EnrichmentRatio = round(enrich_ratio, 2),
    P_value = fisher_res$p.value,
    FDR = NA_real_,
    GeneList = paste(deg_in_pathway, collapse = ";")
  )
}

# 4. 四个核心通路
results <- rbind(
  compute_enrichment(up_genes, ecm_receptor_genes,
                     panel_genes, "ECM-receptor interaction"),
  compute_enrichment(up_genes, focal_adhesion_genes,
                     panel_genes, "Focal adhesion"),
  compute_enrichment(up_genes, pi3k_akt_genes,
                     panel_genes, "PI3K-Akt signaling"),
  compute_enrichment(up_genes, ecm_organization_genes,
                     panel_genes, "Extracellular structure organization")
)

# 5. BH 校正
results$FDR <- p.adjust(results$P_value, method = "BH")

cat("\n=== Panel-background enrichment results ===\n")
print(results[, c("Pathway", "Panel_Genes_in_Pathway", "DEGs_in_Pathway",
                  "Expected", "EnrichmentRatio", "P_value", "FDR")])

write.csv(results, "HGFpos_HSC_enrichment_panel_background.csv", row.names = FALSE)

library(Seurat)

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
if (!exists("cosmx_obj")) {
  cosmx_obj <- readRDS("GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
}

celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]
expr_data <- cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data

# HGF+ HSC proportion
stellate_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
hgf_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["HGF", ])
hgf_pos <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]

cat("=== HGF+ HSC ===\n")
cat(sprintf("Total HSC: %d\n", length(stellate_idx)))
cat(sprintf("HGF+ HSC: %d (%.1f%%)\n",
            length(hgf_pos),
            100 * length(hgf_pos) / length(stellate_idx)))

# Hepatocyte subtypes and Hep.4 proportion
hep_types <- c("Hep.1", "Hep.2", "Hep.3", "Hep.4", "Hep.5", "Hep.6")
hep_counts <- sapply(hep_types, function(h) sum(celltype_vec == h))
total_hep <- sum(hep_counts)

cat("\n=== Hepatocyte subtypes ===\n")
print(hep_counts)
cat(sprintf("Total hepatocytes: %d\n", total_hep))
cat(sprintf("Hep.4 proportion: %.1f%%\n",
            100 * hep_counts["Hep.4"] / total_hep))

# Overall proportion among all cells
cat("\n=== Overall ===\n")
cat(sprintf("All cells: %d\n", ncol(cosmx_obj)))
cat(sprintf("HGF+ HSC / all cells: %.2f%%\n",
            100 * length(hgf_pos) / ncol(cosmx_obj)))
cat(sprintf("Hep.4 / all cells: %.2f%%\n",
            100 * hep_counts["Hep.4"] / ncol(cosmx_obj)))

# HGF+ HSC and Hep.4 spatial co-occurrence
cat("\n=== Spatial co-occurrence ===\n")
cat(sprintf("FOVs containing both HGF+ HSC and Hep.4: %d / 64\n",
            sum(sapply(unique(cosmx_obj@meta.data$fov), function(f) {
              idx_f <- which(cosmx_obj@meta.data$fov == f)
              any(idx_f %in% hgf_pos) && any(idx_f %in% which(celltype_vec == "Hep.4"))
            }))))


# ============================================================
# Clarify: HGF+/- HSC to Hep.4 vs Hep.5 distance distribution
# ============================================================

library(Seurat)
library(tidyverse)
library(ggplot2)

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
if (!exists("cosmx_obj")) {
  cosmx_obj <- readRDS("GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
}

celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]
expr_data <- cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data
coords_all <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[, c("x_slide_mm", "y_slide_mm", "fov")]
all_fovs <- sort(unique(coords_all$fov))

# Cell groups
stellate_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
hgf_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["HGF", ])
hgf_pos <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]
hgf_neg <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] == 0]
hep4_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.4")
hep5_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.5")

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Distance to nearest target per cell (full distribution)
# ------------------------------------------------------------

compute_nn_distances <- function(hsc_idx, target_idx) {
  nn <- numeric(length(hsc_idx))
  for (i in seq_along(hsc_idx)) {
    cell_i <- hsc_idx[i]
    f <- coords_all$fov[cell_i]
    tgt_in_fov <- target_idx[coords_all$fov[target_idx] == f]
    if (length(tgt_in_fov) == 0) { nn[i] <- NA; next }
    tgt <- coords_all[tgt_in_fov, ]
    d <- sqrt((tgt$x_slide_mm - coords_all$x_slide_mm[cell_i])^2 +
                (tgt$y_slide_mm - coords_all$y_slide_mm[cell_i])^2)
    nn[i] <- min(d) * 1000  # mm -> µm
  }
  nn
}

# Build full distribution data frame
dist_df <- rbind(
  data.frame(HSC_group = "HGF+", Target = "IL6R-high (Hep.4)",
             Distance_um = compute_nn_distances(hgf_pos, hep4_idx)),
  data.frame(HSC_group = "HGF-", Target = "IL6R-high (Hep.4)",
             Distance_um = compute_nn_distances(hgf_neg, hep4_idx)),
  data.frame(HSC_group = "HGF+", Target = "IL6R-low (Hep.5)",
             Distance_um = compute_nn_distances(hgf_pos, hep5_idx)),
  data.frame(HSC_group = "HGF-", Target = "IL6R-low (Hep.5)",
             Distance_um = compute_nn_distances(hgf_neg, hep5_idx))
)
dist_df <- dist_df[!is.na(dist_df$Distance_um), ]

# Summary stats
cat("=== Distance summary (µm) ===\n")
dist_summary <- dist_df %>%
  group_by(HSC_group, Target) %>%
  summarise(
    N = n(),
    Median = round(median(Distance_um), 1),
    Mean = round(mean(Distance_um), 1),
    Q25 = round(quantile(Distance_um, 0.25), 1),
    Q75 = round(quantile(Distance_um, 0.75), 1),
    Pct_within_30 = round(mean(Distance_um <= 30) * 100, 1),
    Pct_within_50 = round(mean(Distance_um <= 50) * 100, 1),
    .groups = "drop"
  )
print(as.data.frame(dist_summary))
write.csv(dist_summary, "HGF_HSC_distance_to_hep4_hep5_summary.csv", row.names = FALSE)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Visualization: distance distribution (violin + box)
# ------------------------------------------------------------

p_dist <- ggplot(dist_df, aes(x = HSC_group, y = Distance_um,
                              fill = Target)) +
  geom_violin(alpha = 0.6, position = position_dodge(0.8),
              scale = "width", trim = TRUE) +
  geom_boxplot(width = 0.15, position = position_dodge(0.8),
               outlier.shape = NA, alpha = 0.9) +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c(
    "IL6R-high (Hep.4)" = "#C73E1D",
    "IL6R-low (Hep.5)" = "#2E86AB"
  )) +
  coord_cartesian(ylim = c(0, 200)) +
  labs(title = "Distance from HGF+/- HSC to hepatocyte subtypes",
       subtitle = "Nearest-neighbor distance (µm)",
       x = NULL, y = "Distance to nearest target cell (µm)") +
  theme_classic(base_size = 12) +
  theme(legend.position = "top",
        legend.title = element_blank())
print(p_dist)
ggsave("Fig4a_HSC_distance_distribution.pdf", p_dist, width = 7, height = 5)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Density correction: cells per FOV area
# ------------------------------------------------------------

# Compute target cell density per FOV (cells per mm²)
fov_area_mm2 <- sapply(all_fovs, function(f) {
  idx <- which(coords_all$fov == f)
  if (length(idx) < 2) return(NA)
  x_range <- diff(range(coords_all$x_slide_mm[idx]))
  y_range <- diff(range(coords_all$y_slide_mm[idx]))
  x_range * y_range
})

density_df <- data.frame(
  FOV = all_fovs,
  Area_mm2 = fov_area_mm2,
  N_Hep4 = sapply(all_fovs, function(f) sum(coords_all$fov[hep4_idx] == f)),
  N_Hep5 = sapply(all_fovs, function(f) sum(coords_all$fov[hep5_idx] == f))
)
density_df$Density_Hep4 <- density_df$N_Hep4 / density_df$Area_mm2
density_df$Density_Hep5 <- density_df$N_Hep5 / density_df$Area_mm2

cat("\n=== Target cell density per FOV ===\n")
cat(sprintf("Hep.4: %.1f ± %.1f cells/mm²\n",
            mean(density_df$Density_Hep4, na.rm = TRUE),
            sd(density_df$Density_Hep4, na.rm = TRUE)))
cat(sprintf("Hep.5: %.1f ± %.1f cells/mm²\n",
            mean(density_df$Density_Hep5, na.rm = TRUE),
            sd(density_df$Density_Hep5, na.rm = TRUE)))
cat(sprintf("Density ratio (Hep.5/Hep.4): %.2f\n",
            mean(density_df$Density_Hep5, na.rm = TRUE) /
              mean(density_df$Density_Hep4, na.rm = TRUE)))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Density-corrected selectivity metric
# ------------------------------------------------------------

# For each HSC, compute the proportion of nearest-neighbor among each type
# Standardized by density: distance × sqrt(density)
dist_df$density <- ifelse(dist_df$Target == "IL6R-high (Hep.4)",
                          mean(density_df$Density_Hep4, na.rm = TRUE),
                          mean(density_df$Density_Hep5, na.rm = TRUE))
dist_df$dist_normalized <- dist_df$Distance_um * sqrt(dist_df$density)

cat("\n=== Density-normalized distance ===\n")
norm_summary <- dist_df %>%
  group_by(HSC_group, Target) %>%
  summarise(
    Median_norm = round(median(dist_normalized), 2),
    Mean_norm = round(mean(dist_normalized), 2),
    .groups = "drop"
  )
print(as.data.frame(norm_summary))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Selective proximity index (SPI)
# ------------------------------------------------------------

# For each HSC, define SPI = log2(dist_to_Hep5 / dist_to_Hep4)
# Positive SPI = HSC closer to Hep.4 (selective)
# Negative SPI = HSC closer to Hep.5
# Calculate per HSC

dist_pos <- compute_nn_distances(hgf_pos, hep4_idx)
dist_pos_h5 <- compute_nn_distances(hgf_pos, hep5_idx)
dist_neg <- compute_nn_distances(hgf_neg, hep4_idx)
dist_neg_h5 <- compute_nn_distances(hgf_neg, hep5_idx)

spi_pos <- log2(dist_pos_h5 / dist_pos)
spi_neg <- log2(dist_neg_h5 / dist_neg)
spi_pos <- spi_pos[!is.na(spi_pos)]
spi_neg <- spi_neg[!is.na(spi_neg)]

cat("\n=== Selective Proximity Index (log2(Hep.5_dist / Hep.4_dist)) ===\n")
cat(sprintf("HGF+ HSC: median SPI = %.3f (mean %.3f)\n",
            median(spi_pos), mean(spi_pos)))
cat(sprintf("HGF- HSC: median SPI = %.3f (mean %.3f)\n",
            median(spi_neg), mean(spi_neg)))
cat(sprintf("Wilcoxon (HGF+ vs HGF-): p = %.4e\n",
            wilcox.test(spi_pos, spi_neg)$p.value))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Spatial distribution of Hep.4 vs Hep.5
# ------------------------------------------------------------

plot_df <- coords_all
plot_df$celltype <- celltype_vec
plot_df$is_hep4 <- seq_len(nrow(plot_df)) %in% hep4_idx
plot_df$is_hep5 <- seq_len(nrow(plot_df)) %in% hep5_idx

p_spatial <- ggplot() +
  geom_point(data = plot_df[!plot_df$is_hep4 & !plot_df$is_hep5, ],
             aes(x = x_slide_mm, y = y_slide_mm),
             size = 0.05, alpha = 0.1, color = "grey85") +
  geom_point(data = plot_df[plot_df$is_hep5, ],
             aes(x = x_slide_mm, y = y_slide_mm),
             size = 0.15, alpha = 0.4, color = "#2E86AB") +
  geom_point(data = plot_df[plot_df$is_hep4, ],
             aes(x = x_slide_mm, y = y_slide_mm),
             size = 0.15, alpha = 0.5, color = "#C73E1D") +
  coord_fixed() +
  labs(title = "Spatial distribution of hepatocyte subtypes",
       subtitle = sprintf("IL6R-high (Hep.4): %d | IL6R-low (Hep.5): %d",
                          length(hep4_idx), length(hep5_idx)),
       x = "x_slide_mm", y = "y_slide_mm") +
  theme_classic()
print(p_spatial)
ggsave("Fig4b_Hep4_Hep5_spatial_distribution.pdf", p_spatial,
       width = 12, height = 10)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. Save
# ------------------------------------------------------------

saveRDS(list(
  dist_df = dist_df,
  dist_summary = dist_summary,
  density_df = density_df,
  spi_pos = spi_pos,
  spi_neg = spi_neg
), "Distance_distribution_correction.rds")

cat("\n=== Analysis completed ===\n")

# ============================================================
# SPI null hypothesis + PDGFRB+ HSC control
# ============================================================

library(Seurat)
library(tidyverse)

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
if (!exists("cosmx_obj")) {
  cosmx_obj <- readRDS("GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
}

celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]
expr_data <- cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data
coords_all <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[, c("x_slide_mm", "y_slide_mm", "fov")]
all_fovs <- sort(unique(coords_all$fov))

stellate_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
hgf_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["HGF", ])
pdgfrb_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["PDGFRB", ])

hgf_pos <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]
hgf_neg <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] == 0]
pdgfrb_pos <- stellate_idx[pdgfrb_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]
pdgfrb_neg <- stellate_idx[pdgfrb_expr[stellate_idx] == 0]

hep4_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.4")
hep5_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.5")

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. SPI_null (density-based null hypothesis)
# ------------------------------------------------------------

compute_nn_distances <- function(hsc_idx, target_idx) {
  nn <- numeric(length(hsc_idx))
  for (i in seq_along(hsc_idx)) {
    cell_i <- hsc_idx[i]
    f <- coords_all$fov[cell_i]
    tgt_in_fov <- target_idx[coords_all$fov[target_idx] == f]
    if (length(tgt_in_fov) == 0) { nn[i] <- NA; next }
    tgt <- coords_all[tgt_in_fov, ]
    d <- sqrt((tgt$x_slide_mm - coords_all$x_slide_mm[cell_i])^2 +
                (tgt$y_slide_mm - coords_all$y_slide_mm[cell_i])^2)
    nn[i] <- min(d) * 1000
  }
  nn
}

# Density per FOV
fov_area_mm2 <- sapply(all_fovs, function(f) {
  idx <- which(coords_all$fov == f)
  if (length(idx) < 2) return(NA)
  diff(range(coords_all$x_slide_mm[idx])) *
    diff(range(coords_all$y_slide_mm[idx]))
})

density_Hep4 <- mean(sapply(all_fovs, function(f) {
  sum(coords_all$fov[hep4_idx] == f)
}) / fov_area_mm2, na.rm = TRUE)

density_Hep5 <- mean(sapply(all_fovs, function(f) {
  sum(coords_all$fov[hep5_idx] == f)
}) / fov_area_mm2, na.rm = TRUE)

# SPI_null: under random distribution, E[d] ∝ 1/sqrt(density)
# SPI_null = log2(sqrt(density_Hep4 / density_Hep5))
SPI_null <- log2(sqrt(density_Hep4 / density_Hep5))

cat("=== SPI null hypothesis ===\n")
cat(sprintf("Hep.4 density: %.1f cells/mm2\n", density_Hep4))
cat(sprintf("Hep.5 density: %.1f cells/mm2\n", density_Hep5))
cat(sprintf("Density ratio (Hep.5/Hep.4): %.3f\n", density_Hep5 / density_Hep4))
cat(sprintf("SPI_null = log2(sqrt(%.1f / %.1f)) = %.4f\n",
            density_Hep4, density_Hep5, SPI_null))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Compute SPI for multiple HSC subsets
# ------------------------------------------------------------

compute_SPI <- function(hsc_idx, label) {
  d_h4 <- compute_nn_distances(hsc_idx, hep4_idx)
  d_h5 <- compute_nn_distances(hsc_idx, hep5_idx)
  spi <- log2(d_h5 / d_h4)
  spi <- spi[!is.na(spi)]
  cat(sprintf("%-25s N = %d, median SPI = %.3f (mean %.3f)\n",
              label, length(spi), median(spi), mean(spi)))
  spi
}

cat("\n=== SPI across HSC subsets ===\n")
spi_hgf_pos <- compute_SPI(hgf_pos, "HGF+ HSC")
spi_hgf_neg <- compute_SPI(hgf_neg, "HGF- HSC")
spi_pdgfrb_pos <- compute_SPI(pdgfrb_pos, "PDGFRB+ HSC")
spi_pdgfrb_neg <- compute_SPI(pdgfrb_neg, "PDGFRB- HSC")

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Statistical tests
# ------------------------------------------------------------

cat("\n=== Statistical tests ===\n")

# HGF+ vs HGF-
p_hgf <- wilcox.test(spi_hgf_pos, spi_hgf_neg)$p.value
cat(sprintf("HGF+ vs HGF-:          p = %.4e\n", p_hgf))

# PDGFRB+ vs PDGFRB-
p_pdgfrb <- wilcox.test(spi_pdgfrb_pos, spi_pdgfrb_neg)$p.value
cat(sprintf("PDGFRB+ vs PDGFRB-:    p = %.4e\n", p_pdgfrb))

# HGF+ vs PDGFRB+
p_hgf_vs_pdgfrb <- wilcox.test(spi_hgf_pos, spi_pdgfrb_pos)$p.value
cat(sprintf("HGF+ vs PDGFRB+:       p = %.4e\n", p_hgf_vs_pdgfrb))

# Compare each subset to SPI_null via one-sample Wilcoxon
cat("\n=== Comparison to SPI_null ===\n")
spi_null_test <- function(spi, label) {
  w <- wilcox.test(spi, mu = SPI_null, alternative = "greater")
  cat(sprintf("%-15s vs SPI_null: p = %.4e, median SPI = %.3f\n",
              label, w$p.value, median(spi)))
}
spi_null_test(spi_hgf_pos, "HGF+ HSC")
spi_null_test(spi_hgf_neg, "HGF- HSC")
spi_null_test(spi_pdgfrb_pos, "PDGFRB+ HSC")
spi_null_test(spi_pdgfrb_neg, "PDGFRB- HSC")

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Visualization: SPI distribution across all HSC subsets
# ------------------------------------------------------------

spi_df <- rbind(
  data.frame(Subset = "HGF+", SPI = spi_hgf_pos),
  data.frame(Subset = "HGF-", SPI = spi_hgf_neg),
  data.frame(Subset = "PDGFRB+", SPI = spi_pdgfrb_pos),
  data.frame(Subset = "PDGFRB-", SPI = spi_pdgfrb_neg)
)

spi_df$Subset <- factor(spi_df$Subset,
                        levels = c("HGF+", "HGF-", "PDGFRB+", "PDGFRB-"))

p_spi <- ggplot(spi_df, aes(x = Subset, y = SPI, fill = Subset)) +
  geom_violin(alpha = 0.5, scale = "width", trim = TRUE) +
  geom_boxplot(width = 0.2, outlier.shape = NA, alpha = 0.9) +
  geom_hline(yintercept = SPI_null, linetype = "dashed",
             color = "red", linewidth = 0.8) +
  annotate("text", x = 4.4, y = SPI_null + 0.15,
           label = sprintf("SPI_null = %.3f", SPI_null),
           color = "red", size = 3.5, hjust = 1) +
  geom_hline(yintercept = 0, linetype = "dotted",
             color = "grey50", linewidth = 0.5) +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c(
    "HGF+" = "#C73E1D", "HGF-" = "#2E86AB",
    "PDGFRB+" = "#F18F01", "PDGFRB-" = "#3B8EA5"
  )) +
  coord_cartesian(ylim = c(-3, 2)) +
  labs(title = "Selective Proximity Index across HSC subsets",
       subtitle = expression("SPI = log"[2]*"(d"[Hep.5]*" / d"[Hep.4]*")"),
       x = NULL, y = "SPI") +
  theme_classic(base_size = 12) +
  theme(legend.position = "none")
print(p_spi)
ggsave("Fig4c_SPI_all_HSC_subsets.pdf", p_spi,
       width = 7, height = 5)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Save results
# ------------------------------------------------------------

spi_summary <- data.frame(
  Subset = c("HGF+", "HGF-", "PDGFRB+", "PDGFRB-"),
  N = c(length(spi_hgf_pos), length(spi_hgf_neg),
        length(spi_pdgfrb_pos), length(spi_pdgfrb_neg)),
  Median_SPI = c(median(spi_hgf_pos), median(spi_hgf_neg),
                 median(spi_pdgfrb_pos), median(spi_pdgfrb_neg)),
  Mean_SPI = c(mean(spi_hgf_pos), mean(spi_hgf_neg),
               mean(spi_pdgfrb_pos), mean(spi_pdgfrb_neg))
)
cat("\n=== SPI summary ===\n")
print(spi_summary)
cat(sprintf("\nSPI_null (density-based): %.4f\n", SPI_null))

write.csv(spi_summary, "SPI_summary_all_HSC_subsets.csv", row.names = FALSE)

saveRDS(list(
  SPI_null = SPI_null,
  spi_hgf_pos = spi_hgf_pos,
  spi_hgf_neg = spi_hgf_neg,
  spi_pdgfrb_pos = spi_pdgfrb_pos,
  spi_pdgfrb_neg = spi_pdgfrb_neg,
  spi_summary = spi_summary
), "SPI_all_subsets.rds")

cat("\n=== Completed ===\n")
# ============================================================
# HGF+ vs PDGFRB+ HSC overlap
# ============================================================

overlap_hgf_pdgfrb <- intersect(hgf_pos, pdgfrb_pos)
cat(sprintf("HGF+ HSC: %d\n", length(hgf_pos)))
cat(sprintf("PDGFRB+ HSC: %d\n", length(pdgfrb_pos)))
cat(sprintf("Overlap: %d\n", length(overlap_hgf_pdgfrb)))
cat(sprintf("HGF+ in PDGFRB+: %.1f%%\n",
            100 * length(overlap_hgf_pdgfrb) / length(hgf_pos)))
cat(sprintf("PDGFRB+ in HGF+: %.1f%%\n",
            100 * length(overlap_hgf_pdgfrb) / length(pdgfrb_pos)))

# Jaccard
jaccard <- length(overlap_hgf_pdgfrb) /
  length(union(hgf_pos, pdgfrb_pos))
cat(sprintf("Jaccard: %.3f\n", jaccard))

# FOV-level direction consistency check
fov_dir <- sapply(all_fovs, function(f) {
  pos_f <- hgf_pos[coords_all$fov[hgf_pos] == f]
  neg_f <- hgf_neg[coords_all$fov[hgf_neg] == f]
  if (length(pos_f) < 3 || length(neg_f) < 3) return(NA)
  # Compare SPI within FOV
  d_pos_h4 <- compute_nn_distances(pos_f, hep4_idx)
  d_pos_h5 <- compute_nn_distances(pos_f, hep5_idx)
  d_neg_h4 <- compute_nn_distances(neg_f, hep4_idx)
  d_neg_h5 <- compute_nn_distances(neg_f, hep5_idx)
  spi_pos_f <- median(log2(d_pos_h5 / d_pos_h4), na.rm = TRUE)
  spi_neg_f <- median(log2(d_neg_h5 / d_neg_h4), na.rm = TRUE)
  spi_pos_f > spi_neg_f  # HGF+ closer to Hep.4 than HGF-
})
cat(sprintf("FOVs with HGF+ SPI > HGF- SPI: %d / %d\n",
            sum(fov_dir, na.rm = TRUE), sum(!is.na(fov_dir))))


# ============================================================
# Characterize reverse-direction FOVs
# ============================================================

library(Seurat)
library(tidyverse)

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
if (!exists("cosmx_obj")) {
  cosmx_obj <- readRDS("GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
}

celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]
expr_data <- cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data
coords_all <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[, c("x_slide_mm", "y_slide_mm", "fov")]
all_fovs <- sort(unique(coords_all$fov))

# Cell groups
stellate_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
hgf_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["HGF", ])
hgf_pos <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]
hgf_neg <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] == 0]
hep4_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.4")
hep5_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.5")

compute_nn_distances <- function(hsc_idx, target_idx) {
  nn <- numeric(length(hsc_idx))
  for (i in seq_along(hsc_idx)) {
    cell_i <- hsc_idx[i]
    f <- coords_all$fov[cell_i]
    tgt_in_fov <- target_idx[coords_all$fov[target_idx] == f]
    if (length(tgt_in_fov) == 0) { nn[i] <- NA; next }
    tgt <- coords_all[tgt_in_fov, ]
    d <- sqrt((tgt$x_slide_mm - coords_all$x_slide_mm[cell_i])^2 +
                (tgt$y_slide_mm - coords_all$y_slide_mm[cell_i])^2)
    nn[i] <- min(d) * 1000
  }
  nn
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Per-FOV SPI for HGF+ and HGF-
# ------------------------------------------------------------

# Fibrotic region definition (from Module 0)
fibro_sig_indep <- c("IL6", "IL1B", "CCL2", "CXCL9", "CXCL10", "TNF",
                     "TGFB1", "TGFBR1", "TGFBR2", "MMP2", "MMP14")
fibro_sig_indep <- fibro_sig_indep[fibro_sig_indep %in% rownames(expr_data)]
expr_indep <- as.matrix(expr_data[fibro_sig_indep, , drop = FALSE])
fibro_score_indep <- colMeans(expr_indep)
non_hsc_idx <- which(celltype_vec != "Stellate.cells")
fibro_score_nonhsc <- colMeans(expr_indep[, non_hsc_idx, drop = FALSE])
thr_nonhsc <- quantile(fibro_score_nonhsc, 0.75, na.rm = TRUE)
is_fibrotic <- fibro_score_indep >= thr_nonhsc

# Per-FOV analysis
fov_summary <- data.frame(
  FOV = all_fovs,
  N_HGFpos = NA_integer_,
  N_HGFneg = NA_integer_,
  SPI_HGFpos = NA_real_,
  SPI_HGFneg = NA_real_,
  SPI_diff = NA_real_,
  Direction = NA_character_,
  Fibrotic_fraction = NA_real_,
  stringsAsFactors = FALSE
)

for (i in seq_along(all_fovs)) {
  f <- all_fovs[i]
  idx_f <- which(coords_all$fov == f)
  pos_f <- intersect(hgf_pos, idx_f)
  neg_f <- intersect(hgf_neg, idx_f)
  
  fov_summary$N_HGFpos[i] <- length(pos_f)
  fov_summary$N_HGFneg[i] <- length(neg_f)
  
  if (length(pos_f) < 3 || length(neg_f) < 3) next
  
  d_pos_h4 <- compute_nn_distances(pos_f, hep4_idx)
  d_pos_h5 <- compute_nn_distances(pos_f, hep5_idx)
  d_neg_h4 <- compute_nn_distances(neg_f, hep4_idx)
  d_neg_h5 <- compute_nn_distances(neg_f, hep5_idx)
  
  spi_pos <- median(log2(d_pos_h5 / d_pos_h4), na.rm = TRUE)
  spi_neg <- median(log2(d_neg_h5 / d_neg_h4), na.rm = TRUE)
  
  fov_summary$SPI_HGFpos[i] <- spi_pos
  fov_summary$SPI_HGFneg[i] <- spi_neg
  fov_summary$SPI_diff[i] <- spi_pos - spi_neg
  fov_summary$Direction[i] <- ifelse(spi_pos > spi_neg,
                                     "Consistent", "Reverse")
  
  # Fibrotic fraction
  fov_summary$Fibrotic_fraction[i] <- mean(is_fibrotic[idx_f])
}

# Filter to FOVs with valid SPI
valid_fovs <- fov_summary[!is.na(fov_summary$Direction), ]
cat(sprintf("Total FOVs with valid SPI: %d\n", nrow(valid_fovs)))
cat(sprintf("Consistent: %d (%.1f%%)\n",
            sum(valid_fovs$Direction == "Consistent"),
            100 * mean(valid_fovs$Direction == "Consistent")))
cat(sprintf("Reverse: %d (%.1f%%)\n",
            sum(valid_fovs$Direction == "Reverse"),
            100 * mean(valid_fovs$Direction == "Reverse")))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Characterize reverse FOVs
# ------------------------------------------------------------

reverse_fovs <- valid_fovs[valid_fovs$Direction == "Reverse", ]
consistent_fovs <- valid_fovs[valid_fovs$Direction == "Consistent", ]

cat("\n=== Reverse FOVs characteristics ===\n")
cat(sprintf("N reverse FOVs: %d\n", nrow(reverse_fovs)))

# 2a. Sample distribution (need sample column)
sample_col <- "Run_Tissue_name"
if (sample_col %in% colnames(cosmx_obj@meta.data)) {
  sample_per_fov <- sapply(all_fovs, function(f) {
    unique(cosmx_obj@meta.data[[sample_col]][cosmx_obj@meta.data$fov == f])[1]
  })
  fov_summary$Sample <- sample_per_fov[match(fov_summary$FOV, all_fovs)]
  reverse_fovs$Sample <- sample_per_fov[match(reverse_fovs$FOV, all_fovs)]
  valid_fovs$Sample <- sample_per_fov[match(valid_fovs$FOV, all_fovs)]
  
  cat("\nSample distribution of reverse FOVs:\n")
  print(table(reverse_fovs$Sample))
  cat("\nSample distribution of all FOVs:\n")
  print(table(valid_fovs$Sample))
} else {
  cat("Sample column not available\n")
}

# 2b. Fibrotic region distribution
cat("\n=== Fibrotic fraction comparison ===\n")
cat(sprintf("Reverse FOVs: median fibrotic fraction = %.3f\n",
            median(reverse_fovs$Fibrotic_fraction, na.rm = TRUE)))
cat(sprintf("Consistent FOVs: median fibrotic fraction = %.3f\n",
            median(consistent_fovs$Fibrotic_fraction, na.rm = TRUE)))
w_fibro <- wilcox.test(reverse_fovs$Fibrotic_fraction,
                       consistent_fovs$Fibrotic_fraction)
cat(sprintf("Wilcoxon (Reverse vs Consistent): p = %.4f\n", w_fibro$p.value))

# 2c. Cell number comparison
cat("\n=== Cell number comparison ===\n")
cat(sprintf("Reverse FOVs: median HGF+ = %.1f, median HGF- = %.1f\n",
            median(reverse_fovs$N_HGFpos),
            median(reverse_fovs$N_HGFneg)))
cat(sprintf("Consistent FOVs: median HGF+ = %.1f, median HGF- = %.1f\n",
            median(consistent_fovs$N_HGFpos),
            median(consistent_fovs$N_HGFneg)))
w_npos <- wilcox.test(reverse_fovs$N_HGFpos, consistent_fovs$N_HGFpos)
w_nneg <- wilcox.test(reverse_fovs$N_HGFneg, consistent_fovs$N_HGFneg)
cat(sprintf("Wilcoxon (N_HGF+): p = %.4f\n", w_npos$p.value))
cat(sprintf("Wilcoxon (N_HGF-): p = %.4f\n", w_nneg$p.value))

# 2d. SPI_diff magnitude
cat("\n=== SPI_diff magnitude ===\n")
cat(sprintf("Reverse FOVs: median SPI_diff = %.3f\n",
            median(reverse_fovs$SPI_diff)))
cat(sprintf("Consistent FOVs: median SPI_diff = %.3f\n",
            median(consistent_fovs$SPI_diff)))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Save Supplementary Table
# ------------------------------------------------------------

supp_table <- reverse_fovs[, c("FOV", "Sample", "N_HGFpos", "N_HGFneg",
                               "SPI_HGFpos", "SPI_HGFneg", "SPI_diff",
                               "Fibrotic_fraction")]
supp_table <- supp_table[order(supp_table$SPI_diff), ]

cat("\n=== Supplementary Table: Reverse FOVs ===\n")
print(supp_table)
write.csv(supp_table, "Supplementary_Table_Reverse_FOVs.csv", row.names = FALSE)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Summary
# ------------------------------------------------------------

cat("\n=== Summary ===\n")
cat(sprintf("Total FOVs analyzed: %d\n", nrow(valid_fovs)))
cat(sprintf("Consistent: %d (%.1f%%)\n",
            nrow(consistent_fovs),
            100 * nrow(consistent_fovs) / nrow(valid_fovs)))
cat(sprintf("Reverse: %d (%.1f%%)\n",
            nrow(reverse_fovs),
            100 * nrow(reverse_fovs) / nrow(valid_fovs)))

# Binomial test
binom_res <- binom.test(nrow(consistent_fovs), nrow(valid_fovs),
                        p = 0.5, alternative = "greater")
cat(sprintf("\nBinomial test (vs 50%%): p = %.4e\n", binom_res$p.value))
cat(sprintf("95%% CI: [%.3f, %.3f]\n",
            binom_res$conf.int[1], binom_res$conf.int[2]))

saveRDS(list(
  fov_summary = fov_summary,
  reverse_fovs = reverse_fovs,
  consistent_fovs = consistent_fovs,
  supp_table = supp_table,
  binom_p = binom_res$p.value
), "Reverse_FOV_characterization.rds")

cat("\n=== Completed ===\n")

# Manual grouping based on x/y cutoffs
assign_group <- function(df, slide_label) {
  df$Group <- NA
  df$Group[df$x < 5 & df$y > 5] <- paste0(slide_label, "_A")
  df$Group[df$x < 5 & df$y <= 5] <- paste0(slide_label, "_B")
  df$Group[df$x >= 5 & df$y > 5] <- paste0(slide_label, "_C")
  df$Group[df$x >= 5 & df$y <= 5] <- paste0(slide_label, "_D")
  df
}

fov_centroids1 <- assign_group(fov_centroids1, "Slide1")
fov_centroids2 <- assign_group(fov_centroids2, "Slide2")

cat("=== Slide1 FOV groups ===\n")
print(table(fov_centroids1$Group))

cat("\n=== Slide2 FOV groups ===\n")
print(table(fov_centroids2$Group))

# Save
write.csv(fov_centroids1, "Slide1_FOV_groups.csv", row.names = FALSE)
write.csv(fov_centroids2, "Slide2_FOV_groups.csv", row.names = FALSE)

library(ggplot2)

  geom_point(size = 2) +
  geom_text(size = 2.5, vjust = -0.5) +
  coord_fixed() +
  labs(title = "Slide1 FOV groups (manual assignment)",

# ============================================================
# Per-group SPI analysis
# ============================================================

library(Seurat)
library(tidyverse)

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
if (!exists("cosmx_obj")) {
  cosmx_obj <- readRDS("GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
}

celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]
expr_data <- cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data
coords_all <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[, c("x_slide_mm", "y_slide_mm", "fov")]

stellate_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
hgf_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["HGF", ])
hgf_pos <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]
hgf_neg <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] == 0]
hep4_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.4")
hep5_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.5")

compute_nn_distances <- function(hsc_idx, target_idx) {
  nn <- numeric(length(hsc_idx))
  for (i in seq_along(hsc_idx)) {
    cell_i <- hsc_idx[i]
    f <- coords_all$fov[cell_i]
    tgt_in_fov <- target_idx[coords_all$fov[target_idx] == f]
    if (length(tgt_in_fov) == 0) { nn[i] <- NA; next }
    tgt <- coords_all[tgt_in_fov, ]
    d <- sqrt((tgt$x_slide_mm - coords_all$x_slide_mm[cell_i])^2 +
                (tgt$y_slide_mm - coords_all$y_slide_mm[cell_i])^2)
    nn[i] <- min(d) * 1000
  }
  nn
}

# Assign group per cell based on FOV
fov_centroids1 <- read.csv("Slide1_FOV_groups.csv")
fov_groups <- setNames(fov_centroids1$Group, fov_centroids1$fov)
cell_group <- fov_groups[as.character(coords_all$fov)]

# SPI per group
cat("=== SPI per spatial group ===\n")
group_results <- data.frame(
  Group = character(), N_HGFpos = integer(), N_HGFneg = integer(),
  SPI_HGFpos = numeric(), SPI_HGFneg = numeric(),
  p_value = numeric(), stringsAsFactors = FALSE
)

for (g in unique(na.omit(cell_group))) {
  idx_g <- which(cell_group == g)
  pos_g <- intersect(hgf_pos, idx_g)
  neg_g <- intersect(hgf_neg, idx_g)
  
  if (length(pos_g) < 10 || length(neg_g) < 10) next
  
  d_pos_h4 <- compute_nn_distances(pos_g, hep4_idx)
  d_pos_h5 <- compute_nn_distances(pos_g, hep5_idx)
  d_neg_h4 <- compute_nn_distances(neg_g, hep4_idx)
  d_neg_h5 <- compute_nn_distances(neg_g, hep5_idx)
  
  spi_pos <- log2(d_pos_h5 / d_pos_h4)
  spi_neg <- log2(d_neg_h5 / d_neg_h4)
  spi_pos <- spi_pos[!is.na(spi_pos)]
  spi_neg <- spi_neg[!is.na(spi_neg)]
  
  p_val <- wilcox.test(spi_pos, spi_neg)$p.value
  
  group_results <- rbind(group_results, data.frame(
    Group = g,
    N_HGFpos = length(spi_pos),
    N_HGFneg = length(spi_neg),
    SPI_HGFpos = round(median(spi_pos), 3),
    SPI_HGFneg = round(median(spi_neg), 3),
    p_value = p_val
  ))
}

print(group_results)
write.csv(group_results, "SPI_by_spatial_group.csv", row.names = FALSE)

# Direction consistency
group_results$Direction <- ifelse(
  group_results$SPI_HGFpos > group_results$SPI_HGFneg,
  "Consistent", "Reverse")

cat("\n=== Direction consistency ===\n")
cat(sprintf("Groups with consistent direction: %d / %d\n",
            sum(group_results$Direction == "Consistent"),
            nrow(group_results)))

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")

# Merge count files into one matrix + align with phenotype

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
download_dir <- "data/GSE135251_counts"

count_files <- list.files(download_dir, pattern = "\\.counts\\.txt\\.gz$",
                          full.names = TRUE)
cat(sprintf("Total files: %d\n", length(count_files)))

gsm_ids <- sub("_.*", "", basename(count_files))

# ---- Read with error handling ----
cat("Reading files (5-10 min)...\n")
count_list <- vector("list", length(count_files))
read_ok <- logical(length(count_files))

for (i in seq_along(count_files)) {
  if (i %% 50 == 0) cat(sprintf("  Reading %d/%d...\n", i, length(count_files)))
  
  read_ok[i] <- tryCatch({
    df <- read.table(gzfile(count_files[i]), header = TRUE,
                     stringsAsFactors = FALSE, row.names = 1)
    count_list[[i]] <- setNames(df[, 1], rownames(df))
    TRUE
  }, error = function(e) {
    cat(sprintf("  SKIP corrupted: %s\n", basename(count_files[i])))
    FALSE
  })
}

cat(sprintf("\n=== Read summary: success = %d, fail = %d ===\n",
            sum(read_ok), sum(!read_ok)))

# ---- Keep valid files only ----
count_list <- count_list[read_ok]
gsm_ids <- gsm_ids[read_ok]
cat(sprintf("Valid samples for merging: %d\n", length(count_list)))

# ============================================================
# Merge 216 count files into one matrix + align with phenotype
# ============================================================

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
download_dir <- "data/GSE135251_counts"

count_files <- list.files(download_dir, pattern = "\\.counts\\.txt\\.gz$",
                          full.names = TRUE)
gsm_ids <- sub("_.*", "", basename(count_files))
cat(sprintf("Total files: %d\n", length(count_files)))

# ---- Read all files ----
cat("Reading files (5-10 min)...\n")
count_list <- vector("list", length(count_files))
read_ok <- logical(length(count_files))

for (i in seq_along(count_files)) {
  if (i %% 50 == 0) cat(sprintf("  Reading %d/%d...\n", i, length(count_files)))
  read_ok[i] <- tryCatch({
    df <- read.table(gzfile(count_files[i]), header = TRUE,
                     stringsAsFactors = FALSE, row.names = 1)
    count_list[[i]] <- setNames(df[, 1], rownames(df))
    TRUE
  }, error = function(e) {
    cat(sprintf("  SKIP corrupted: %s\n", basename(count_files[i])))
    FALSE
  })
}

cat(sprintf("Valid samples: %d / %d\n", sum(read_ok), length(read_ok)))

count_list <- count_list[read_ok]
gsm_ids <- gsm_ids[read_ok]

# ---- Merge ----
cat("Merging...\n")
all_genes <- Reduce(union, lapply(count_list, names))
cat(sprintf("Total unique genes: %d\n", length(all_genes)))

count_matrix <- matrix(0, nrow = length(all_genes), ncol = length(count_list),
                       dimnames = list(all_genes, gsm_ids))
for (i in seq_along(count_list)) {
  count_matrix[names(count_list[[i]]), i] <- count_list[[i]]
}

cat(sprintf("Final matrix: %d genes x %d samples\n",
            nrow(count_matrix), ncol(count_matrix)))

# ---- Align with phenotype ----
pheno_135251 <- pData(gse135251[[1]])
common_samples <- intersect(colnames(count_matrix), rownames(pheno_135251))
cat(sprintf("Common samples: %d\n", length(common_samples)))

count_matrix <- count_matrix[, common_samples]
pheno_135251 <- pheno_135251[common_samples, ]

# ---- Build grouping ----
fibro_stage <- as.numeric(as.character(pheno_135251$`fibrosis stage:ch1`))
group <- factor(ifelse(fibro_stage >= 2, "High", "Low"),
                levels = c("Low", "High"))

cat("\n=== Group distribution ===\n")
print(table(group))

# ---- Save ----
saveRDS(list(
  count_matrix = count_matrix,
  pheno = pheno_135251,
  fibro_stage = fibro_stage,
  group = group
), "results/GSE135251_aligned_data.rds")

cat("\n=== Saved to results/GSE135251_aligned_data.rds ===\n")


# ============================================================
# Step 2: Differential expression analysis (limma)
# ============================================================

library(limma)

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")

# Load aligned data
data <- readRDS("results/GSE135251_aligned_data.rds")
count_matrix <- data$count_matrix
group <- data$group
fibro_stage <- data$fibro_stage

cat(sprintf("Input matrix: %d genes x %d samples\n",
            nrow(count_matrix), ncol(count_matrix)))
cat(sprintf("Group: Low = %d, High = %d\n",
            sum(group == "Low"), sum(group == "High")))

# ---- Filter low-expression genes ----
keep <- rowSums(count_matrix >= 10) >= 3
count_matrix <- count_matrix[keep, ]
cat(sprintf("Genes after filtering (counts >= 10 in >= 3 samples): %d\n",
            nrow(count_matrix)))

# ---- log2 transform ----
log_expr <- log2(count_matrix + 1)

# ---- Design matrix ----
design <- model.matrix(~ group)
cat("\n=== Design matrix ===\n")
print(head(design))

# ---- Linear model ----
fit <- lmFit(log_expr, design)
fit <- eBayes(fit)

# ---- Extract DEGs ----
deg <- topTable(fit, coef = 2, number = Inf, sort.by = "P")
deg_sig <- deg[abs(deg$logFC) > 0.5 & deg$adj.P.Val < 0.05, ]

cat(sprintf("\n=== DEG summary ===\n"))
cat(sprintf("Total genes tested: %d\n", nrow(deg)))
cat(sprintf("DEGs (|logFC|>0.5, adj.P<0.05): %d\n", nrow(deg_sig)))
cat(sprintf("  Up in High fibrosis: %d\n", sum(deg_sig$logFC > 0.5)))
cat(sprintf("  Down in High fibrosis: %d\n", sum(deg_sig$logFC < -0.5)))

cat("\n=== Top 20 DEGs ===\n")
print(head(deg_sig[, c("logFC", "AveExpr", "t", "P.Value", "adj.P.Val")], 20))

# ---- Save ----
write.csv(deg, "results/GSE135251_all_genes_DE.csv")
write.csv(deg_sig, "results/GSE135251_DEGs.csv")
saveRDS(list(deg = deg, deg_sig = deg_sig, log_expr = log_expr,
             group = group, fibro_stage = fibro_stage),
        "results/GSE135251_DEG_results.rds")

cat("\n=== DEG analysis completed ===\n")
cat("Saved: results/GSE135251_DEGs.csv\n")

# ============================================================
# Step 3: WGCNA
# ============================================================
options(repos = c(CRAN = "https://cloud.r-project.org"))
BiocManager::install("impute", update = FALSE, ask = FALSE, type = "binary")
BiocManager::install("preprocessCore", update = FALSE, ask = FALSE, type = "binary")

library(WGCNA)
enableWGCNAThreads()

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")

# Load DEG results
data <- readRDS("results/GSE135251_DEG_results.rds")
log_expr <- data$log_expr
group <- data$group
fibro_stage <- data$fibro_stage

cat(sprintf("Input: %d genes x %d samples\n", nrow(log_expr), ncol(log_expr)))

# ---- 3.1 Transpose ----
datExpr <- t(log_expr)
cat(sprintf("datExpr: %d samples x %d genes\n", nrow(datExpr), ncol(datExpr)))

# ---- 3.2 Check for outlier samples ----
gsg <- goodSamplesGenes(datExpr, verbose = 3)
if (!gsg$allOK) {
  datExpr <- datExpr[gsg$goodSamples, gsg$goodGenes]
}
cat(sprintf("After filtering: %d samples x %d genes\n",
            nrow(datExpr), ncol(datExpr)))

# ---- 3.3 Sample clustering ----
sampleTree <- hclust(dist(datExpr), method = "average")
pdf("figures/WGCNA_sample_clustering.pdf", width = 10, height = 5)
par(cex = 0.6)
plot(sampleTree, main = "Sample clustering",
     sub = "", xlab = "", cex.lab = 0.8)
dev.off()

# ---- 3.4 Pick soft-thresholding power ----
powers <- c(1:10, seq(12, 20, by = 2))
sft <- pickSoftThreshold(datExpr, powerVector = powers,
                         networkType = "signed",
                         verbose = 5)

cat("\n=== Soft-thresholding power selection ===\n")
sft_df <- data.frame(
  Power = sft$fitIndices$Power,
  SFT_R2 = -sign(sft$fitIndices$slope) * sft$fitIndices$SFT.R.sq,
  MeanConnectivity = sft$fitIndices$mean.k.
)
print(sft_df)

power <- sft$fitIndices$Power[
  which(-sign(sft$fitIndices$slope) * sft$fitIndices$SFT.R.sq > 0.85)[1]
]
cat(sprintf("\nSelected power: %d\n", power))

pdf("figures/WGCNA_soft_threshold.pdf", width = 10, height = 5)
par(mfrow = c(1, 2))
plot(sft$fitIndices[, 1],
     -sign(sft$fitIndices[, 3]) * sft$fitIndices[, 2],
     xlab = "Soft threshold power", ylab = "Scale free topology R^2",
     main = "Scale independence", type = "n")
text(sft$fitIndices[, 1],
     -sign(sft$fitIndices[, 3]) * sft$fitIndices[, 2],
     labels = powers, cex = 0.9, col = "red")
abline(h = 0.85, col = "blue")
plot(sft$fitIndices[, 1], sft$fitIndices[, 5],
     xlab = "Soft threshold power", ylab = "Mean connectivity",
     main = "Mean connectivity", type = "n")
text(sft$fitIndices[, 1], sft$fitIndices[, 5],
     labels = powers, cex = 0.9, col = "red")
dev.off()

# ---- 3.5 Build network (SLOW: 20-40 min) ----
cat("\n=== Building network (20-40 min) ===\n")
net <- blockwiseModules(
  datExpr,
  power = power,
  networkType = "signed",
  TOMType = "signed",
  minModuleSize = 30,
  mergeCutHeight = 0.25,
  numericLabels = TRUE,
  pamRespectsDendro = FALSE,
  saveTOMs = FALSE,
  verbose = 3
)

# ---- 3.6 Module colors ----
moduleColors <- labels2colors(net$colors)
cat("\n=== Module distribution ===\n")
print(table(moduleColors))

# ---- 3.7 Module-trait correlation ----
MEs <- net$MEs
fibro_numeric <- as.numeric(group == "High")

moduleTraitCor <- cor(MEs, fibro_numeric, use = "p")
moduleTraitPvalue <- corPvalueStudent(moduleTraitCor, nrow(datExpr))

cat("\n=== Module-fibrosis correlation ===\n")
cor_df <- data.frame(
  Module = rownames(moduleTraitCor),
  Correlation = round(moduleTraitCor[, 1], 3),
  Pvalue = moduleTraitPvalue[, 1]
)
cor_df <- cor_df[order(-abs(cor_df$Correlation)), ]
print(cor_df)

# ---- 3.8 Top module ----
top_module <- rownames(moduleTraitCor)[which.max(abs(moduleTraitCor))]
cat(sprintf("\nTop fibrosis-correlated module: %s (r = %.3f, p = %.2e)\n",
            top_module,
            moduleTraitCor[top_module, 1],
            moduleTraitPvalue[top_module, 1]))

# ---- 3.9 Extract genes ----
module_label <- as.numeric(gsub("ME", "", top_module))
module_genes <- colnames(datExpr)[net$colors == module_label]
cat(sprintf("Genes in top module: %d\n", length(module_genes)))

write.csv(data.frame(gene = module_genes),
          "results/WGCNA_top_module_genes.csv", row.names = FALSE)

# ---- 3.10 Save ----
saveRDS(list(
  net = net,
  moduleColors = moduleColors,
  MEs = MEs,
  moduleTraitCor = moduleTraitCor,
  moduleTraitPvalue = moduleTraitPvalue,
  top_module = top_module,
  module_genes = module_genes,
  power = power,
  datExpr = datExpr
), "results/WGCNA_results.rds")

cat("\n=== WGCNA completed ===\n")

  cat("\n=== Too few genes, adding ME14 (negative correlation) ===\n")
  
  net <- wgcna$net
  module_label_14 <- as.numeric(gsub("ME", "", "ME14"))
  module_genes_14 <- wgcna$datExpr %>%
    colnames() %>%
    .[net$colors == module_label_14]
  
  cat(sprintf("ME14 genes: %d\n", length(module_genes_14)))
  
  candidate_genes_14 <- intersect(module_genes_14, deg_genes)
  cat(sprintf("ME14 ∩ DEG: %d\n", length(candidate_genes_14)))
  
  # Combine
  candidate_genes <- unique(c(candidate_genes, candidate_genes_14))
  cat(sprintf("Combined candidate genes: %d\n", length(candidate_genes)))
}

# ---- Save ----
write.csv(data.frame(gene = candidate_genes),
          "results/candidate_genes_WGCNA_DEG.csv", row.names = FALSE)

cat("\n=== Step 4 completed ===\n")
