# ============================================================
# Filter candidate genes: keep protein-coding only
# ============================================================

library(org.Hs.eg.db)

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")

# Load mapped genes
mapping_df <- read.csv("results/candidate_genes_mapped.csv")
cat(sprintf("Before filtering: %d\n", nrow(mapping_df)))

# Remove NA symbols
mapping_df <- mapping_df[!is.na(mapping_df$Symbol), ]

# Remove lncRNA (LINC*, *-AS*, MIR*, LOC*)
is_lncRNA <- grepl("^(LINC|MIR|LOC|.*-AS[0-9]*$)", mapping_df$Symbol)
mapping_df <- mapping_df[!is_lncRNA, ]
cat(sprintf("After removing lncRNA: %d\n", nrow(mapping_df)))

# Keep only protein-coding genes
gene_info <- select(org.Hs.eg.db,
                    keys = mapping_df$Symbol,
                    columns = c("SYMBOL", "GENETYPE"),
                    keytype = "SYMBOL")

# Keep only protein-coding
mapping_df <- mapping_df[mapping_df$Symbol %in% 
                           gene_info$SYMBOL[gene_info$GENETYPE == "protein-coding"], ]
cat(sprintf("After keeping protein-coding only: %d\n", nrow(mapping_df)))

print(mapping_df)

# Save filtered list
write.csv(mapping_df, "results/candidate_genes_filtered.csv", row.names = FALSE)


# ============================================================
# Step 5: Four-algorithm feature selection
# ============================================================

required <- c("glmnet", "randomForest", "e1071", "kernlab", "xgboost", "caret")
missing <- required[!sapply(required, requireNamespace, quietly = TRUE)]
if (length(missing) > 0) {
  stop(sprintf("Missing packages: %s", paste(missing, collapse = ", ")))
}

library(glmnet)
library(randomForest)
library(e1071)
library(kernlab)
library(xgboost)
library(caret)

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")

data <- readRDS("results/GSE135251_DEG_results.rds")
log_expr <- data$log_expr
group <- data$group

mapping_df <- read.csv("results/candidate_genes_filtered.csv")
X <- t(log_expr[mapping_df$Ensembl, ])
colnames(X) <- mapping_df$Symbol
y <- as.numeric(group == "High")

cat(sprintf("X: %d samples x %d genes\n", nrow(X), ncol(X)))

# ---- 5a: LASSO ----
cat("\n=== LASSO ===\n")
set.seed(123)
cv_lasso <- cv.glmnet(X, y, family = "binomial", alpha = 1, nfolds = 10)
lasso_coef <- coef(cv_lasso, s = "lambda.min")
lasso_genes <- rownames(lasso_coef)[which(lasso_coef[, 1] != 0)]
lasso_genes <- setdiff(lasso_genes, "(Intercept)")
cat(sprintf("LASSO: %d genes\n", length(lasso_genes)))
print(lasso_genes)

# ---- 5b: Random Forest ----
cat("\n=== Random Forest ===\n")
set.seed(123)
rf_model <- randomForest(x = X, y = as.factor(y),
                         ntree = 500, importance = TRUE)
rf_imp <- importance(rf_model)
rf_genes <- rownames(rf_imp)[order(rf_imp[, "MeanDecreaseGini"],
                                   decreasing = TRUE)][1:min(30, ncol(X))]
cat(sprintf("RF: %d genes\n", length(rf_genes)))

# ============================================================
# 5c: SVM-RFE (manual implementation, no caret dependency)
# ============================================================

cat("\n=== SVM-RFE ===\n")

# Manual SVM-RFE
svm_rfe_manual <- function(X, y, n_features_target = 20, verbose = TRUE) {
  X_current <- as.data.frame(X)
  y_factor <- as.factor(y)
  features <- colnames(X_current)
  
  while (length(features) > n_features_target) {
    if (verbose) cat(sprintf("  Features remaining: %d\n", length(features)))
    
    # Fit SVM
    svm_fit <- svm(x = X_current[, features, drop = FALSE],
                   y = y_factor,
                   kernel = "radial",
                   scale = TRUE)
    
    # Get feature importance (absolute weight in linear approximation)
    # Use permutation-based importance since radial SVM has no direct weights
    # Simpler: use caret's varImp-like approach
    w <- t(svm_fit$coefs) %*% svm_fit$SV
    w <- abs(as.numeric(w))
    
    # If weights length doesn't match (non-linear kernel), fall back to
    # recursive elimination using model performance
    if (length(w) != length(features)) {
      # Use simple heuristic: eliminate features with lowest variance
      var_features <- apply(X_current[, features, drop = FALSE], 2, var)
      remove_idx <- which.min(var_features)
    } else {
      names(w) <- features
      remove_idx <- which.min(w)
    }
    
    features <- features[-remove_idx]
  }
  
  return(features)
}

set.seed(123)
svm_genes <- svm_rfe_manual(X, y, n_features_target = 20, verbose = TRUE)
cat(sprintf("SVM-RFE: %d genes\n", length(svm_genes)))
print(svm_genes)

# ---- 5d: XGBoost ----
cat("\n=== XGBoost ===\n")
set.seed(123)
dtrain <- xgb.DMatrix(data = X, label = y)
xgb_model <- xgb.train(
  params = list(objective = "binary:logistic",
                eval_metric = "auc",
                max_depth = 3, eta = 0.1),
  data = dtrain, nrounds = 100, verbose = 0
)
xgb_imp <- xgb.importance(model = xgb_model)
xgb_genes <- xgb_imp$Feature[1:min(30, ncol(X))]
cat(sprintf("XGBoost: %d genes\n", length(xgb_genes)))

# ---- 5e: Intersect ----
cat("\n=== Intersection ===\n")
core_genes <- Reduce(intersect,
                     list(lasso_genes, rf_genes, svm_genes, xgb_genes))
cat(sprintf("Core genes: %d\n", length(core_genes)))
print(core_genes)

# ---- Save ----
write.csv(data.frame(gene = core_genes),
          "results/core_genes_4algorithms.csv", row.names = FALSE)
saveRDS(list(lasso_genes = lasso_genes, rf_genes = rf_genes,
             svm_genes = svm_genes, xgb_genes = xgb_genes,
             core_genes = core_genes, X = X, y = y),
        "results/Module1_ML_results.rds")

cat("\n=== Step 5 completed ===\n")

     colnames(pheno_126848),

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
dir.create("data/external_validation", showWarnings = FALSE, recursive = TRUE)

# Download from GEO
url <- "https://ftp.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/geo/series/GSE130nnn/GSE130970/suppl/GSE130970_all_sample_salmon_tximport_counts_entrez_gene_ID.csv.gz"
destfile <- "data/external_validation/GSE130970_counts.csv.gz"

options(timeout = 3600)
download.file(url, destfile, mode = "wb", method = "libcurl")

cat(sprintf("Downloaded: %.1f MB\n", file.size(destfile) / 1024^2))


# Read matrix
expr_130970 <- read.csv(gzfile("data/external_validation/GSE130970_counts.csv.gz"),
                        row.names = 1, check.names = FALSE)

cat(sprintf("Matrix: %d genes x %d samples\n",
            nrow(expr_130970), ncol(expr_130970)))
cat("\nFirst 5 row names:\n"); print(head(rownames(expr_130970), 5))
cat("\nFirst 5 col names:\n"); print(head(colnames(expr_130970), 5))
cat("\nFirst 5 rows, first 3 cols:\n"); print(expr_130970[1:5, 1:3])


# Convert Entrez ID to gene symbol
library(org.Hs.eg.db)

entrez_ids <- rownames(expr_130970)
gene_symbols <- mapIds(org.Hs.eg.db,
                       keys = entrez_ids,
                       column = "SYMBOL",
                       keytype = "ENTREZID",
                       multiVals = "first")

# Build data frame with symbols
expr_df <- as.data.frame(expr_130970)
expr_df$Symbol <- gene_symbols

# Remove NA symbols
expr_df <- expr_df[!is.na(expr_df$Symbol), ]
cat(sprintf("After removing NA: %d rows\n", nrow(expr_df)))

# Handle duplicate symbols: keep row with highest mean expression
expr_df$mean_expr <- rowMeans(expr_df[, -ncol(expr_df)])
expr_df <- expr_df[order(-expr_df$mean_expr), ]
expr_df <- expr_df[!duplicated(expr_df$Symbol), ]
expr_df$mean_expr <- NULL

# Set rownames
rownames(expr_df) <- expr_df$Symbol
expr_df$Symbol <- NULL

cat(sprintf("After deduplication: %d genes\n", nrow(expr_df)))

# Save
saveRDS(expr_df, "results/GSE130970_expr_symbol.rds")

# ---- Check core genes ----
ml <- readRDS("results/Module1_ML_results.rds")
core_genes <- ml$core_genes

present <- core_genes[core_genes %in% rownames(expr_df)]
cat(sprintf("\nCore genes present: %d / %d\n", length(present), length(core_genes)))
print(present)

missing <- setdiff(core_genes, rownames(expr_df))
if (length(missing) > 0) {
  cat(sprintf("\nMissing: %d\n", length(missing)))
  print(missing)
}

# ============================================================
# Step 6.1: Internal cross-validation (generate lr_model)
# ============================================================

library(pROC)
library(caret)

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")

data <- readRDS("results/GSE135251_DEG_results.rds")
ml <- readRDS("results/Module1_ML_results.rds")
core_genes <- ml$core_genes
X <- ml$X
y <- ml$y

cat(sprintf("Core genes: %d\n", length(core_genes)))

# Full-data logistic regression
X_core <- X[, core_genes, drop = FALSE]
df_all <- data.frame(X_core, y = y)
lr_model <- glm(y ~ ., data = df_all, family = binomial)
pred_all <- predict(lr_model, type = "response")
roc_all <- roc(y, pred_all)
cat(sprintf("Full-data AUC: %.3f\n", auc(roc_all)))

# 10-fold CV
set.seed(123)
folds <- createFolds(y, k = 10, list = TRUE)
cv_preds <- numeric(length(y))
for (i in seq_along(folds)) {
  test_idx <- folds[[i]]
  train_idx <- setdiff(1:length(y), test_idx)
  df_train <- data.frame(X[train_idx, core_genes, drop = FALSE], y = y[train_idx])
  m <- glm(y ~ ., data = df_train, family = binomial)
  df_test <- data.frame(X[test_idx, core_genes, drop = FALSE])
  cv_preds[test_idx] <- predict(m, newdata = df_test, type = "response")
}
roc_cv <- roc(y, cv_preds)
cat(sprintf("10-fold CV AUC: %.3f\n", auc(roc_cv)))

# Save (THIS is the missing file)
saveRDS(list(lr_model = lr_model,
             roc_all = roc_all,
             roc_cv = roc_cv,
             auc_full = as.numeric(auc(roc_all)),
             auc_cv = as.numeric(auc(roc_cv))),
        "results/Module1_ROC_results.rds")

cat("\n=== Module1_ROC_results.rds saved ===\n")

# ============================================================
# External validation: GSE126848
# ============================================================

library(pROC)

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")

# Load matrix
expr_df <- readRDS("results/GSE126848_expr_symbol.rds")

# Align by position
expr_aligned <- expr_df
colnames(expr_aligned) <- rownames(pheno_126848)

# Grouping: NASH vs Healthy
disease_status <- pheno_126848$`disease:ch1`
group_val <- ifelse(disease_status == "NASH", 1,
                    ifelse(disease_status == "healthy", 0, NA))
keep_idx <- !is.na(group_val)
cat(sprintf("NASH: %d, Healthy: %d\n",
            sum(group_val == 1, na.rm = TRUE),
            sum(group_val == 0, na.rm = TRUE)))

expr_clean <- expr_aligned[, keep_idx]
group_val <- group_val[keep_idx]

# Sanity check: known fibrosis genes should be higher in NASH
cat("\n=== Sanity check ===\n")
check_genes <- c("COL1A1", "COL3A1", "ACTA2", "TGFB1")
for (g in check_genes) {
  if (g %in% rownames(expr_clean)) {
    expr_g <- as.numeric(expr_clean[g, ])
    fc <- log2((mean(expr_g[group_val == 1]) + 1) /
                 (mean(expr_g[group_val == 0]) + 1))
    cat(sprintf("%-10s log2FC = %.2f\n", g, fc))
  }
}

# External ROC
ml <- readRDS("results/Module1_ML_results.rds")
core_genes <- ml$core_genes
lr_model <- readRDS("results/Module1_ROC_results.rds")$lr_model

X_val <- t(expr_clean[core_genes, ])
X_val <- as.data.frame(X_val)[, core_genes, drop = FALSE]

pred_val <- predict(lr_model, newdata = X_val, type = "response")
roc_val <- roc(group_val, pred_val)
cat(sprintf("\n=== GSE126848 external AUC: %.3f ===\n", auc(roc_val)))

saveRDS(list(roc_val = roc_val, auc_val = as.numeric(auc(roc_val))),
        "results/Module1_external_ROC_GSE126848.rds")

library(Seurat)

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
cosmx_obj <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")

celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]
expr_data <- cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data

# HSC groups
stellate_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
hgf_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["HGF", ])
hgf_pos <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]
hgf_neg <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] == 0]

# SULF2
sulf2 <- as.numeric(expr_data["SULF2", ])

cat(sprintf("HGF+ HSC: %d, HGF- HSC: %d\n", length(hgf_pos), length(hgf_neg)))
cat(sprintf("\nSULF2 in HGF+ HSC: mean = %.3f, pos = %.3f\n",
            mean(sulf2[hgf_pos]), mean(sulf2[hgf_pos] > 0)))
cat(sprintf("SULF2 in HGF- HSC: mean = %.3f, pos = %.3f\n",
            mean(sulf2[hgf_neg]), mean(sulf2[hgf_neg] > 0)))

w <- wilcox.test(sulf2[hgf_pos], sulf2[hgf_neg])
fc <- mean(sulf2[hgf_pos]) / (mean(sulf2[hgf_neg]) + 1e-6)
cat(sprintf("\nFold change: %.2f\n", fc))
cat(sprintf("Wilcoxon p = %.4e\n", w$p.value))

# Also check SULF2 in other cell types
cat("\n=== SULF2 across cell types ===\n")
sulf2_by_type <- tapply(sulf2, celltype_vec, function(x) {
  c(mean = mean(x), pos = mean(x > 0))
})
sulf2_df <- data.frame(
  CellType = names(sulf2_by_type),
  Mean = sapply(sulf2_by_type, `[`, "mean"),
  PosRate = sapply(sulf2_by_type, `[`, "pos")
)
sulf2_df <- sulf2_df[order(-sulf2_df$Mean), ]
print(head(sulf2_df, 10))



library(tidyverse)
library(org.Hs.eg.db)

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")

# ---- Load HGF+ HSC genes (symbols) ----
deg_hsc <- read.csv("results/HGFpos_HSC_DEGs_strict.csv")
hsc_genes <- deg_hsc$gene[deg_hsc$avg_log2FC > 0]
cat(sprintf("HGF+ HSC up genes: %d\n", length(hsc_genes)))

# ---- Load GSE135251 ----
data <- readRDS("results/GSE135251_DEG_results.rds")
log_expr <- data$log_expr
fibro_stage <- data$fibro_stage

# ---- Convert GSE135251 Ensembl to Symbol ----
ensembl_ids <- rownames(log_expr)
cat(sprintf("GSE135251 genes (Ensembl): %d\n", length(ensembl_ids)))

symbols <- mapIds(org.Hs.eg.db,
                  keys = ensembl_ids,
                  column = "SYMBOL",
                  keytype = "ENSEMBL",
                  multiVals = "first")

cat(sprintf("Mapped to symbols: %d / %d\n",
            sum(!is.na(symbols)), length(symbols)))

# Build new matrix with symbols
valid_idx <- !is.na(symbols) & !duplicated(symbols)
log_expr_symbol <- log_expr[valid_idx, ]
rownames(log_expr_symbol) <- symbols[valid_idx]

cat(sprintf("After symbol mapping and dedup: %d genes\n",
            nrow(log_expr_symbol)))

# ---- Intersect ----
overlap <- intersect(hsc_genes, rownames(log_expr_symbol))
cat(sprintf("\nHGF+ HSC genes detectable in GSE135251: %d\n", length(overlap)))
print(overlap)

# ---- Correlate with fibrosis stage ----
if (length(overlap) > 0) {
  cor_results <- data.frame(
    Gene = overlap,
    Spearman_rho = NA_real_,
    P_value = NA_real_
  )
  
  for (i in seq_along(overlap)) {
    g <- overlap[i]
    expr_g <- as.numeric(log_expr_symbol[g, ])
    ct <- cor.test(expr_g, fibro_stage, method = "spearman")
    cor_results$Spearman_rho[i] <- ct$estimate
    cor_results$P_value[i] <- ct$p.value
  }
  
  cor_results <- cor_results[order(-cor_results$Spearman_rho), ]
  print(cor_results)
  
  cat(sprintf("\nPositive correlation: %d / %d\n",
              sum(cor_results$Spearman_rho > 0), nrow(cor_results)))
  cat(sprintf("Significant positive (p<0.05): %d / %d\n",
              sum(cor_results$Spearman_rho > 0 & cor_results$P_value < 0.05),
              nrow(cor_results)))
  
  write.csv(cor_results, "results/HSC_signature_bulk_validation.csv",
            row.names = FALSE)
}

# ============================================================
# Fig 2d: HGF+ HSC signature genes vs fibrosis stage
# ============================================================

library(ggplot2)
library(tidyr)

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")

# Load correlation results
cor_results <- read.csv("results/HSC_signature_bulk_validation.csv")

# Take top 10 genes
top10 <- head(cor_results[order(-cor_results$Spearman_rho), ], 10)

# Load log_expr
data <- readRDS("results/GSE135251_DEG_results.rds")
log_expr_symbol <- readRDS("results/GSE135251_DEG_results.rds")$log_expr
fibro_stage <- data$fibro_stage

# Actually need to rebuild symbol-indexed matrix (from previous code)
library(org.Hs.eg.db)
log_expr <- data$log_expr
symbols <- mapIds(org.Hs.eg.db,
                  keys = rownames(log_expr),
                  column = "SYMBOL",
                  keytype = "ENSEMBL",
                  multiVals = "first")
valid_idx <- !is.na(symbols) & !duplicated(symbols)
log_expr_symbol <- log_expr[valid_idx, ]
rownames(log_expr_symbol) <- symbols[valid_idx]

# Build data frame for plotting
plot_list <- list()
for (i in 1:nrow(top10)) {
  g <- top10$Gene[i]
  plot_list[[i]] <- data.frame(
    Gene = g,
    Expr = as.numeric(log_expr_symbol[g, ]),
    Stage = fibro_stage,
    Rho = round(top10$Spearman_rho[i], 3),
    P = format(top10$P_value[i], scientific = TRUE, digits = 2)
  )
}
plot_df <- do.call(rbind, plot_list)
plot_df$Label <- paste0(plot_df$Gene, "\n(rho=", plot_df$Rho, ", p=", plot_df$P, ")")

p_cor <- ggplot(plot_df, aes(x = factor(Stage), y = Expr)) +
  geom_boxplot(aes(fill = factor(Stage)), alpha = 0.6, outlier.shape = NA) +
  geom_jitter(width = 0.2, size = 0.3, alpha = 0.3) +
  geom_smooth(aes(group = 1), method = "lm", se = FALSE, color = "#C73E1D") +
  facet_wrap(~ Label, scales = "free_y", ncol = 5) +
  scale_fill_brewer(palette = "OrRd") +
  labs(title = "HGF+ HSC signature genes vs fibrosis stage in GSE135251",
       x = "Fibrosis stage", y = "log2 expression") +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) +
  theme(legend.position = "none",
        strip.text = element_text(size = 8),
        axis.text.x = element_text(size = 8))

print(p_cor)
ggsave("figures/Fig2d_HSC_signature_vs_stage.pdf", p_cor,
       width = 12, height = 5)

cat("Saved: Fig2d_HSC_signature_vs_stage.pdf\n")

library(tidyverse)
library(Seurat)

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")

# Load region assignments
fov_groups <- read.csv("results/Slide1_FOV_groups.csv")
spi_group <- read.csv("results/SPI_by_spatial_group.csv")
print(spi_group)

# Load CosMx
cosmx_obj <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]
expr_data <- cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data

fov_to_group <- setNames(fov_groups$Group, fov_groups$fov)
cell_group <- fov_to_group[as.character(cosmx_obj@meta.data$fov)]
hgf_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["HGF", ])

# Per-region stats
cat("=== Region statistics ===\n")
region_stats <- data.frame(
  Region = c("Slide1_A", "Slide1_B", "Slide1_C", "Slide1_D"),
  N_cells = NA, N_HSC = NA, N_HGFpos = NA,
  N_Hep4 = NA, N_Hep5 = NA,
  HSC_density = NA, Hep4_density = NA, Hep5_density = NA
)

for (i in 1:nrow(region_stats)) {
  r <- region_stats$Region[i]
  idx_r <- which(cell_group == r)
  if (length(idx_r) == 0) next
  n_total <- length(idx_r)
  region_stats$N_cells[i] <- n_total
  region_stats$N_HSC[i] <- sum(celltype_vec[idx_r] == "Stellate.cells")
  region_stats$N_HGFpos[i] <- sum(idx_r %in% 
                                    which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells" & hgf_expr > 0))
  region_stats$N_Hep4[i] <- sum(celltype_vec[idx_r] == "Hep.4")
  region_stats$N_Hep5[i] <- sum(celltype_vec[idx_r] == "Hep.5")
  region_stats$HSC_density[i] <- region_stats$N_HSC[i] / n_total
  region_stats$Hep4_density[i] <- region_stats$N_Hep4[i] / n_total
  region_stats$Hep5_density[i] <- region_stats$N_Hep5[i] / n_total
}

print(region_stats)
write.csv(region_stats, "results/Region_statistics.csv", row.names = FALSE)

library(Seurat)

cosmx_obj <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]
expr_data <- cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data

# MET expression in Hep.4
hep4_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.4")
hep5_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.5")
met_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["MET", ])

cat(sprintf("MET in Hep.4: mean = %.3f, pos = %.3f\n",
            mean(met_expr[hep4_idx]), mean(met_expr[hep4_idx] > 0)))
cat(sprintf("MET in Hep.5: mean = %.3f, pos = %.3f\n",
            mean(met_expr[hep5_idx]), mean(met_expr[hep5_idx] > 0)))

w <- wilcox.test(met_expr[hep4_idx], met_expr[hep5_idx])
cat(sprintf("Wilcoxon p = %.4e\n", w$p.value))

# Check IL6R
il6r_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["IL6R", ])
cat(sprintf("\nIL6R in Hep.4: mean = %.3f, pos = %.3f\n",
            mean(il6r_expr[hep4_idx]), mean(il6r_expr[hep4_idx] > 0)))
cat(sprintf("IL6R in Hep.5: mean = %.3f, pos = %.3f\n",
            mean(il6r_expr[hep5_idx]), mean(il6r_expr[hep5_idx] > 0)))

# Double-positive cells
met_pos <- met_expr > 0
il6r_pos <- il6r_expr > 0
cat(sprintf("\nHep.4 MET+IL6R+ : %d / %d (%.1f%%)\n",
            sum(met_pos[hep4_idx] & il6r_pos[hep4_idx]),
            length(hep4_idx),
            mean(met_pos[hep4_idx] & il6r_pos[hep4_idx]) * 100))

library(Seurat)
library(tidyverse)

cosmx_obj <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]
expr_data <- cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data
coords_all <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[, c("x_slide_mm", "y_slide_mm", "fov")]
all_fovs <- sort(unique(coords_all$fov))

# HGF+ HSC
stellate_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
hgf_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["HGF", ])
hgf_pos <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]

# Hep.4 subtypes
hep4_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.4")
met_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["MET", ])
il6r_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["IL6R", ])

# Define Hep.4 subgroups
hep4_double_pos <- hep4_idx[met_expr[hep4_idx] > 0 & il6r_expr[hep4_idx] > 0]
hep4_met_only   <- hep4_idx[met_expr[hep4_idx] > 0 & il6r_expr[hep4_idx] == 0]
hep4_il6r_only  <- hep4_idx[met_expr[hep4_idx] == 0 & il6r_expr[hep4_idx] > 0]
hep4_neither    <- hep4_idx[met_expr[hep4_idx] == 0 & il6r_expr[hep4_idx] == 0]

cat(sprintf("Hep.4 total: %d\n", length(hep4_idx)))
cat(sprintf("  MET+IL6R+ (double): %d (%.1f%%)\n",
            length(hep4_double_pos),
            100 * length(hep4_double_pos) / length(hep4_idx)))
cat(sprintf("  MET+IL6R-: %d\n", length(hep4_met_only)))
cat(sprintf("  MET-IL6R+: %d\n", length(hep4_il6r_only)))
cat(sprintf("  MET-IL6R-: %d\n", length(hep4_neither)))

# Compute NN distance from HGF+ HSC to each subgroup
compute_nn <- function(hsc_idx, tgt_idx) {
  nn <- numeric(length(hsc_idx))
  for (i in seq_along(hsc_idx)) {
    cell_i <- hsc_idx[i]
    f <- coords_all$fov[cell_i]
    tgt_in_fov <- tgt_idx[coords_all$fov[tgt_idx] == f]
    if (length(tgt_in_fov) == 0) { nn[i] <- NA; next }
    tgt <- coords_all[tgt_in_fov, ]
    d <- sqrt((tgt$x_slide_mm - coords_all$x_slide_mm[cell_i])^2 +
                (tgt$y_slide_mm - coords_all$y_slide_mm[cell_i])^2)
    nn[i] <- min(d) * 1000
  }
  nn
}

d_double <- compute_nn(hgf_pos, hep4_double_pos)
d_il6r   <- compute_nn(hgf_pos, hep4_il6r_only)
d_met    <- compute_nn(hgf_pos, hep4_met_only)
d_all    <- compute_nn(hgf_pos, hep4_idx)

cat("\n=== HGF+ HSC -> Hep.4 subgroups (median distance, µm) ===\n")
cat(sprintf("  MET+IL6R+ (double): %.1f\n", median(d_double, na.rm = TRUE)))
cat(sprintf("  MET-IL6R+:          %.1f\n", median(d_il6r,   na.rm = TRUE)))
cat(sprintf("  MET+IL6R-:          %.1f\n", median(d_met,    na.rm = TRUE)))
cat(sprintf("  All Hep.4:          %.1f\n", median(d_all,    na.rm = TRUE)))

# Wilcoxon: double vs il6r_only
w1 <- wilcox.test(d_double, d_il6r)
cat(sprintf("\nDouble vs IL6R-only: p = %.4e\n", w1$p.value))

# Save
saveRDS(list(
  hep4_double_pos = hep4_double_pos,
  hep4_il6r_only  = hep4_il6r_only,
  d_double = d_double,
  d_il6r   = d_il6r,
  d_all    = d_all
), "results/HGF_HSC_to_Hep4_subgroups.rds")

cat("\nSaved: results/HGF_HSC_to_Hep4_subgroups.rds\n")

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")

res <- readRDS("results/HGF_HSC_to_Hep4_subgroups.rds")

cat("=== Hep.4 subgroups ===\n")
cat(sprintf("  MET+IL6R+ (double): %d\n", length(res$hep4_double_pos)))
cat(sprintf("  MET-IL6R+:          %d\n", length(res$hep4_il6r_only)))

cat("\n=== HGF+ HSC -> Hep.4 subgroups (median distance, µm) ===\n")
cat(sprintf("  MET+IL6R+ (double): %.1f\n", median(res$d_double, na.rm = TRUE)))
cat(sprintf("  MET-IL6R+:          %.1f\n", median(res$d_il6r,   na.rm = TRUE)))
cat(sprintf("  All Hep.4:          %.1f\n", median(res$d_all,    na.rm = TRUE)))

# Re-run Wilcoxon
w <- wilcox.test(res$d_double, res$d_il6r)
cat(sprintf("\nDouble vs IL6R-only: p = %.4e\n", w$p.value))

library(Seurat)

cosmx_obj <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]
expr_data <- cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data
coords_all <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[, c("x_slide_mm", "y_slide_mm", "fov")]

# HGF+ vs HGF- HSC
stellate_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
hgf_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["HGF", ])
hgf_pos <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]
hgf_neg <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] == 0]

# Hep.4 双阳性
hep4_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.4")
met_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["MET", ])
il6r_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["IL6R", ])
hep4_double <- hep4_idx[met_expr[hep4_idx] > 0 & il6r_expr[hep4_idx] > 0]

# NN distance function
compute_nn <- function(hsc_idx, tgt_idx) {
  nn <- numeric(length(hsc_idx))
  for (i in seq_along(hsc_idx)) {
    cell_i <- hsc_idx[i]
    f <- coords_all$fov[cell_i]
    tgt_in_fov <- tgt_idx[coords_all$fov[tgt_idx] == f]
    if (length(tgt_in_fov) == 0) { nn[i] <- NA; next }
    tgt <- coords_all[tgt_in_fov, ]
    d <- sqrt((tgt$x_slide_mm - coords_all$x_slide_mm[cell_i])^2 +
                (tgt$y_slide_mm - coords_all$y_slide_mm[cell_i])^2)
    nn[i] <- min(d) * 1000
  }
  nn
}

# HGF+ vs HGF- HSC to double-positive cells
d_pos_double <- compute_nn(hgf_pos, hep4_double)
d_neg_double <- compute_nn(hgf_neg, hep4_double)

cat(sprintf("HGF+ HSC -> MET+IL6R+ (double): median = %.1f µm (n = %d)\n",
            median(d_pos_double, na.rm = TRUE), sum(!is.na(d_pos_double))))
cat(sprintf("HGF- HSC -> MET+IL6R+ (double): median = %.1f µm (n = %d)\n",
            median(d_neg_double, na.rm = TRUE), sum(!is.na(d_neg_double))))

w <- wilcox.test(d_pos_double, d_neg_double)
cat(sprintf("\nWilcoxon (HGF+ vs HGF- to double): p = %.4e\n", w$p.value))

# Also compare to MET-IL6R+ cells
hep4_il6r <- hep4_idx[met_expr[hep4_idx] == 0 & il6r_expr[hep4_idx] > 0]
d_pos_il6r <- compute_nn(hgf_pos, hep4_il6r)
d_neg_il6r <- compute_nn(hgf_neg, hep4_il6r)

cat(sprintf("\nHGF+ HSC -> MET-IL6R+: median = %.1f µm\n", median(d_pos_il6r, na.rm = TRUE)))
cat(sprintf("HGF- HSC -> MET-IL6R+: median = %.1f µm\n", median(d_neg_il6r, na.rm = TRUE)))

w2 <- wilcox.test(d_pos_il6r, d_neg_il6r)
cat(sprintf("Wilcoxon (HGF+ vs HGF- to IL6R-only): p = %.4e\n", w2$p.value))

# KEY METRIC: selectivity index
# HGF+ HSC to double vs HGF- HSC to double
selectivity_double <- median(d_pos_double, na.rm = TRUE) -
  median(d_neg_double, na.rm = TRUE)
cat(sprintf("\n=== Selectivity ===\n"))
cat(sprintf("HGF+ vs HGF- to double: %+.1f µm (negative = HGF+ closer)\n",
            selectivity_double))

# Save
saveRDS(list(
  d_pos_double = d_pos_double,
  d_neg_double = d_neg_double,
  d_pos_il6r   = d_pos_il6r,
  d_neg_il6r   = d_neg_il6r,
  p_double = w$p.value,
  p_il6r   = w2$p.value
), "results/HGF_HSC_selectivity_double.rds")

library(Seurat)

cosmx_obj <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]
expr_data <- cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data
coords_all <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[, c("x_slide_mm", "y_slide_mm", "fov")]

# HGF+ vs HGF- HSC
stellate_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
hgf_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["HGF", ])
hgf_pos <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]
hgf_neg <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] == 0]

cat(sprintf("HGF+ HSC: %d, HGF- HSC: %d\n", length(hgf_pos), length(hgf_neg)))

# Hep.4 four subgroups
hep4_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.4")
met_expr  <- as.numeric(expr_data["MET", ])
il6r_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["IL6R", ])

hep4_double  <- hep4_idx[met_expr[hep4_idx] > 0 & il6r_expr[hep4_idx] > 0]
hep4_met_only <- hep4_idx[met_expr[hep4_idx] > 0 & il6r_expr[hep4_idx] == 0]
hep4_il6r_only <- hep4_idx[met_expr[hep4_idx] == 0 & il6r_expr[hep4_idx] > 0]
hep4_neither <- hep4_idx[met_expr[hep4_idx] == 0 & il6r_expr[hep4_idx] == 0]

cat(sprintf("\n=== Hep.4 subgroups ===\n"))
cat(sprintf("  MET+IL6R+ (double): %d (%.1f%%)\n",
            length(hep4_double), 100 * length(hep4_double) / length(hep4_idx)))
cat(sprintf("  MET+IL6R-:          %d (%.1f%%)\n",
            length(hep4_met_only), 100 * length(hep4_met_only) / length(hep4_idx)))
cat(sprintf("  MET-IL6R+:          %d (%.1f%%)\n",
            length(hep4_il6r_only), 100 * length(hep4_il6r_only) / length(hep4_idx)))
cat(sprintf("  MET-IL6R-:          %d (%.1f%%)\n",
            length(hep4_neither), 100 * length(hep4_neither) / length(hep4_idx)))

# NN distance function
compute_nn <- function(hsc_idx, tgt_idx) {
  nn <- numeric(length(hsc_idx))
  for (i in seq_along(hsc_idx)) {
    cell_i <- hsc_idx[i]
    f <- coords_all$fov[cell_i]
    tgt_in_fov <- tgt_idx[coords_all$fov[tgt_idx] == f]
    if (length(tgt_in_fov) == 0) { nn[i] <- NA; next }
    tgt <- coords_all[tgt_in_fov, ]
    d <- sqrt((tgt$x_slide_mm - coords_all$x_slide_mm[cell_i])^2 +
                (tgt$y_slide_mm - coords_all$y_slide_mm[cell_i])^2)
    nn[i] <- min(d) * 1000
  }
  nn
}

# Four subgroups comparison
subgroups <- list(
  "MET+IL6R+" = hep4_double,
  "MET+IL6R-" = hep4_met_only,
  "MET-IL6R+" = hep4_il6r_only,
  "MET-IL6R-" = hep4_neither
)

cat("\n\n========================================\n")
cat("Four-subgroup comparison (HGF+ vs HGF- HSC)\n")
cat("========================================\n\n")

results <- data.frame(
  Subgroup = character(),
  N_target = integer(),
  HGFpos_median = numeric(),
  HGFneg_median = numeric(),
  Delta = numeric(),
  P_value = numeric(),
  stringsAsFactors = FALSE
)

for (sg in names(subgroups)) {
  tgt <- subgroups[[sg]]
  d_pos <- compute_nn(hgf_pos, tgt)
  d_neg <- compute_nn(hgf_neg, tgt)
  
  med_pos <- median(d_pos, na.rm = TRUE)
  med_neg <- median(d_neg, na.rm = TRUE)
  delta <- med_pos - med_neg
  p_val <- wilcox.test(d_pos, d_neg)$p.value
  
  cat(sprintf("=== %s (n = %d) ===\n", sg, length(tgt)))
  cat(sprintf("  HGF+ HSC median: %.1f µm\n", med_pos))
  cat(sprintf("  HGF- HSC median: %.1f µm\n", med_neg))
  cat(sprintf("  Delta (HGF+ - HGF-): %+.1f µm\n", delta))
  cat(sprintf("  Wilcoxon p = %.4e\n\n", p_val))
  
  results <- rbind(results, data.frame(
    Subgroup = sg,
    N_target = length(tgt),
    HGFpos_median = round(med_pos, 1),
    HGFneg_median = round(med_neg, 1),
    Delta = round(delta, 1),
    P_value = p_val
  ))
}

cat("\n========================================\n")
cat("Summary table\n")
cat("========================================\n")
print(results)

write.csv(results, "results/HGF_HSC_four_subgroups.csv", row.names = FALSE)
saveRDS(results, "results/HGF_HSC_four_subgroups.rds")

cat("\nSaved: results/HGF_HSC_four_subgroups.csv\n")

library(Seurat)

cosmx_obj <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]
expr_data <- cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data
coords_all <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[, c("x_slide_mm", "y_slide_mm", "fov")]

stellate_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
hgf_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["HGF", ])
hgf_pos <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]
hgf_neg <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] == 0]

hep4_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.4")
met_expr  <- as.numeric(expr_data["MET", ])
il6r_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["IL6R", ])

hep4_double    <- hep4_idx[met_expr[hep4_idx] > 0 & il6r_expr[hep4_idx] > 0]
hep4_met_only  <- hep4_idx[met_expr[hep4_idx] > 0 & il6r_expr[hep4_idx] == 0]
hep4_il6r_only <- hep4_idx[met_expr[hep4_idx] == 0 & il6r_expr[hep4_idx] > 0]
hep4_neither   <- hep4_idx[met_expr[hep4_idx] == 0 & il6r_expr[hep4_idx] == 0]

compute_nn <- function(hsc_idx, tgt_idx) {
  nn <- rep(NA_real_, length(hsc_idx))
  for (i in seq_along(hsc_idx)) {
    cell_i <- hsc_idx[i]
    f <- coords_all$fov[cell_i]
    tgt_in_fov <- tgt_idx[coords_all$fov[tgt_idx] == f]
    if (length(tgt_in_fov) == 0) next
    tgt <- coords_all[tgt_in_fov, ]
    d <- sqrt((tgt$x_slide_mm - coords_all$x_slide_mm[cell_i])^2 +
                (tgt$y_slide_mm - coords_all$y_slide_mm[cell_i])^2)
    nn[i] <- min(d) * 1000
  }
  nn
}

d_pos_double    <- compute_nn(hgf_pos, hep4_double)
d_pos_il6r_only <- compute_nn(hgf_pos, hep4_il6r_only)
d_pos_met_only  <- compute_nn(hgf_pos, hep4_met_only)
d_pos_neither   <- compute_nn(hgf_pos, hep4_neither)

# Paired comparison: double vs il6r_only (within same HGF+ HSC)
valid1 <- !is.na(d_pos_double) & !is.na(d_pos_il6r_only)
cat(sprintf("HGF+ HSC with both double and IL6R-only in FOV: %d\n", sum(valid1)))
w1 <- wilcox.test(d_pos_double[valid1], d_pos_il6r_only[valid1], paired = TRUE)
cat(sprintf("Paired Wilcoxon (double vs IL6R-only): p = %.4e\n", w1$p.value))
closer_double <- sum(d_pos_double[valid1] < d_pos_il6r_only[valid1], na.rm = TRUE)
cat(sprintf("HGF+ HSC closer to double: %d (%.1f%%)\n",
            closer_double, 100 * closer_double / sum(valid1)))

# Paired comparison: double vs met_only
valid2 <- !is.na(d_pos_double) & !is.na(d_pos_met_only)
cat(sprintf("\nHGF+ HSC with both double and MET-only in FOV: %d\n", sum(valid2)))
if (sum(valid2) > 50) {
  w2 <- wilcox.test(d_pos_double[valid2], d_pos_met_only[valid2], paired = TRUE)
  cat(sprintf("Paired Wilcoxon (double vs MET-only): p = %.4e\n", w2$p.value))
  closer_double2 <- sum(d_pos_double[valid2] < d_pos_met_only[valid2], na.rm = TRUE)
  cat(sprintf("HGF+ HSC closer to double: %d (%.1f%%)\n",
              closer_double2, 100 * closer_double2 / sum(valid2)))
}

# Paired comparison: double vs neither
valid3 <- !is.na(d_pos_double) & !is.na(d_pos_neither)
cat(sprintf("\nHGF+ HSC with both double and neither in FOV: %d\n", sum(valid3)))
w3 <- wilcox.test(d_pos_double[valid3], d_pos_neither[valid3], paired = TRUE)
cat(sprintf("Paired Wilcoxon (double vs neither): p = %.4e\n", w3$p.value))
closer_double3 <- sum(d_pos_double[valid3] < d_pos_neither[valid3], na.rm = TRUE)
cat(sprintf("HGF+ HSC closer to double: %d (%.1f%%)\n",
            closer_double3, 100 * closer_double3 / sum(valid3)))

library(Seurat)

cosmx_obj <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]
expr_data <- cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data

# HGF+ vs HGF- HSC
stellate_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
hgf_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["HGF", ])
hgf_pos <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]
hgf_neg <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] == 0]

# IL6 family ligands
il6_family <- c("IL6", "IL11", "LIF", "OSM", "CTF1", "CNTF")
il6_family <- il6_family[il6_family %in% rownames(expr_data)]

cat("=== IL6 family ligands in HGF+ vs HGF- HSC ===\n\n")
results <- data.frame(
  Ligand = character(),
  HGFpos_mean = numeric(), HGFpos_pos = numeric(),
  HGFneg_mean = numeric(), HGFneg_pos = numeric(),
  FC = numeric(), P_value = numeric(),
  stringsAsFactors = FALSE
)

for (lig in il6_family) {
  x <- as.numeric(expr_data[lig, ])
  mp <- mean(x[hgf_pos]); pp <- mean(x[hgf_pos] > 0)
  mn <- mean(x[hgf_neg]); pn <- mean(x[hgf_neg] > 0)
  fc <- mp / (mn + 1e-6)
  pv <- wilcox.test(x[hgf_pos], x[hgf_neg])$p.value
  cat(sprintf("%-8s HGF+: mean=%.3f pos=%.3f | HGF-: mean=%.3f pos=%.3f | FC=%.2f | p=%.4e\n",
              lig, mp, pp, mn, pn, fc, pv))
  results <- rbind(results, data.frame(
    Ligand = lig,
    HGFpos_mean = round(mp, 3), HGFpos_pos = round(pp, 3),
    HGFneg_mean = round(mn, 3), HGFneg_pos = round(pn, 3),
    FC = round(fc, 2), P_value = pv
  ))
}

write.csv(results, "results/HGF_HSC_IL6_family.csv", row.names = FALSE)



setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
library(Seurat)

cosmx_obj <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]
expr_data <- cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data
panel <- rownames(expr_data)

# ---- Step 1: Check panel coverage ----
il6_receptors <- c("IL6R", "IL6ST", "OSMR", "IL11RA", "LIFR", "CNTFR")
cat("=== Panel coverage for IL6 family receptors ===\n")
for (g in il6_receptors) {
  cat(sprintf("%-10s in panel: %s\n", g, g %in% panel))
}

# ---- Step 2: Expression in Hep.4 vs Hep.5 ----
hep4_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.4")
hep5_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.5")

cat(sprintf("\nHep.4: %d, Hep.5: %d\n", length(hep4_idx), length(hep5_idx)))

# Filter to receptors in panel
il6_receptors_panel <- il6_receptors[il6_receptors %in% panel]
cat(sprintf("\nTesting %d receptors:\n", length(il6_receptors_panel)))

results <- data.frame(
  Receptor = character(),
  Hep4_mean = numeric(), Hep4_pos = numeric(),
  Hep5_mean = numeric(), Hep5_pos = numeric(),
  FC = numeric(), P_value = numeric(),
  stringsAsFactors = FALSE
)

for (g in il6_receptors_panel) {
  x <- as.numeric(expr_data[g, ])
  m4 <- mean(x[hep4_idx]); p4 <- mean(x[hep4_idx] > 0)
  m5 <- mean(x[hep5_idx]); p5 <- mean(x[hep5_idx] > 0)
  fc <- m4 / (m5 + 1e-6)
  pv <- wilcox.test(x[hep4_idx], x[hep5_idx])$p.value
  
  cat(sprintf("%-10s Hep.4: mean=%.3f pos=%.3f | Hep.5: mean=%.3f pos=%.3f | FC=%.2f | p=%.4e\n",
              g, m4, p4, m5, p5, fc, pv))
  
  results <- rbind(results, data.frame(
    Receptor = g,
    Hep4_mean = round(m4, 3), Hep4_pos = round(p4, 3),
    Hep5_mean = round(m5, 3), Hep5_pos = round(p5, 3),
    FC = round(fc, 2), P_value = pv
  ))
}

# ---- Step 3: Also check in HGF+ vs HGF- HSC (as source cells) ----
stellate_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
hgf_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["HGF", ])
hgf_pos <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]
hgf_neg <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] == 0]

cat("\n=== IL6 family receptors in HGF+ vs HGF- HSC ===\n")
for (g in il6_receptors_panel) {
  x <- as.numeric(expr_data[g, ])
  mp <- mean(x[hgf_pos]); pp <- mean(x[hgf_pos] > 0)
  mn <- mean(x[hgf_neg]); pn <- mean(x[hgf_neg] > 0)
  fc <- mp / (mn + 1e-6)
  cat(sprintf("%-10s HGF+ HSC: mean=%.3f pos=%.3f | HGF- HSC: mean=%.3f pos=%.3f | FC=%.2f\n",
              g, mp, pp, mn, pn, fc))
}

# ---- Save ----
write.csv(results, "results/Hep4_IL6_family_receptors.csv", row.names = FALSE)
saveRDS(results, "results/Hep4_IL6_family_receptors.rds")

cat("\n=== Saved: results/Hep4_IL6_family_receptors.csv ===\n")


setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
res <- readRDS("results/Hep4_IL6_family_receptors.rds") 
print(res)

library(Seurat)

cosmx_obj <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")

celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]
expr_data <- cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data

stellate_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
hgf_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["HGF", ])
hgf_pos <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]
hgf_neg <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] == 0]

receptors <- c("IL6R", "IL6ST", "OSMR", "IL11RA", "LIFR", "CNTFR")

hsc_rec <- data.frame(
  Receptor = receptors,
  HGFpos_mean = NA_real_, HGFpos_pos = NA_real_,
  HGFneg_mean = NA_real_, HGFneg_pos = NA_real_,
  FC = NA_real_, P_value = NA_real_,
  stringsAsFactors = FALSE
)

for (i in seq_along(receptors)) {
  g <- receptors[i]
  x <- as.numeric(expr_data[g, ])
  hsc_rec$HGFpos_mean[i] <- round(mean(x[hgf_pos]), 3)
  hsc_rec$HGFpos_pos[i]  <- round(mean(x[hgf_pos] > 0), 3)
  hsc_rec$HGFneg_mean[i] <- round(mean(x[hgf_neg]), 3)
  hsc_rec$HGFneg_pos[i]  <- round(mean(x[hgf_neg] > 0), 3)
  hsc_rec$FC[i] <- round(mean(x[hgf_pos]) / (mean(x[hgf_neg]) + 1e-6), 2)
  hsc_rec$P_value[i] <- wilcox.test(x[hgf_pos], x[hgf_neg])$p.value
}

write.csv(hsc_rec, "results/HGF_HSC_IL6_family_receptors.csv", row.names = FALSE)

library(Seurat)

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
library(Seurat)

cosmx_obj <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]
expr_data <- cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data

stellate_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
pdgfrb_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["PDGFRB", ])
pdgfrb_pos <- stellate_idx[pdgfrb_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]
pdgfrb_neg <- stellate_idx[pdgfrb_expr[stellate_idx] == 0]

receptors <- c("LIFR", "OSMR", "IL6R", "IL11RA", "IL6ST")
receptors <- receptors[receptors %in% rownames(expr_data)]

cat(sprintf("PDGFRB+ HSC: %d, PDGFRB- HSC: %d\n\n",
            length(pdgfrb_pos), length(pdgfrb_neg)))

res <- data.frame(
  Receptor = character(),
  PDGFRBpos_pos = numeric(), PDGFRBneg_pos = numeric(),
  FC = numeric(), P_value = numeric(),
  stringsAsFactors = FALSE
)

for (g in receptors) {
  x <- as.numeric(expr_data[g, ])
  pp <- mean(x[pdgfrb_pos] > 0)
  pn <- mean(x[pdgfrb_neg] > 0)
  mp <- mean(x[pdgfrb_pos])
  mn <- mean(x[pdgfrb_neg])
  fc <- mp / (mn + 1e-6)
  pv <- wilcox.test(x[pdgfrb_pos], x[pdgfrb_neg])$p.value
  
  cat(sprintf("%-8s PDGFRB+: pos=%.3f mean=%.3f | PDGFRB-: pos=%.3f mean=%.3f | FC=%.2f | p=%.4e\n",
              g, pp, mp, pn, mn, fc, pv))
  
  res <- rbind(res, data.frame(
    Receptor = g,
    PDGFRBpos_pos = round(pp, 3), PDGFRBneg_pos = round(pn, 3),
    FC = round(fc, 2), P_value = pv
  ))
}

write.csv(res, "results/PDGFRB_HSC_IL6_receptors.csv", row.names = FALSE)
saveRDS(res, "results/PDGFRB_HSC_IL6_receptors.rds")

cat("\nSaved: results/PDGFRB_HSC_IL6_receptors.csv\n")


# ============================================================
# 05c_HGF_MET_LR_colocalization.R
# 目的：验证 HGF+ HSC 与 MET+IL6R+ Hep.4 的空间 LR 共定位富集
# 输入：CosMx Slide1
# 输出：results/LR_coloc_HGF_MET.csv
#       results/LR_coloc_IL6_IL6R.csv
#       figures/Fig5c_LR_coloc.pdf
# ============================================================
suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(Seurat); library(tidyverse); library(ggplot2)
})

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
if (!exists("cosmx_obj")) {
  cosmx_obj <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
}

celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]
expr_data    <- cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data
coords_all   <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[, c("x_slide_mm", "y_slide_mm", "fov")]
all_fovs     <- sort(unique(coords_all$fov))
radius_mm    <- 0.03   # 30 µm

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. 定义 sender / receiver
# ------------------------------------------------------------
stellate_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
hgf_expr     <- as.numeric(expr_data["HGF", ])
hgf_pos      <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]

hep4_idx  <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.4")
met_expr  <- as.numeric(expr_data["MET", ])
il6r_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["IL6R", ])
il6_expr  <- as.numeric(expr_data["IL6", ])

hep4_double <- hep4_idx[met_expr[hep4_idx] > 0 & il6r_expr[hep4_idx] > 0]
hep4_other  <- setdiff(hep4_idx, hep4_double)

cat(sprintf("Sender HGF+ HSC: %d\n", length(hgf_pos)))
cat(sprintf("Receiver Hep.4 double+: %d\n", length(hep4_double)))
cat(sprintf("Hep.4 other: %d\n\n", length(hep4_other)))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. 对每个 HGF+ HSC，统计 30 µm 内 receiver / other 的数量
# ------------------------------------------------------------
count_in_radius <- function(src_coords, tgt_coords, radius) {
  n <- nrow(src_coords)
  out <- integer(n)
  for (i in seq_len(n)) {
    dx <- tgt_coords$x_slide_mm - src_coords$x_slide_mm[i]
    dy <- tgt_coords$y_slide_mm - src_coords$y_slide_mm[i]
    out[i] <- sum(sqrt(dx^2 + dy^2) <= radius)
  }
  out
}

coords_sender <- coords_all[hgf_pos, ]
coords_recv   <- coords_all[hep4_double, ]
coords_other  <- coords_all[hep4_other, ]

n_recv  <- integer(length(hgf_pos))
n_other <- integer(length(hgf_pos))

for (f in all_fovs) {
  s_in <- which(coords_sender$fov == f)
  r_in <- which(coords_recv$fov   == f)
  o_in <- which(coords_other$fov  == f)
  if (length(s_in) == 0) next
  if (length(r_in) > 0) {
    n_recv[s_in]  <- count_in_radius(coords_sender[s_in, ], coords_recv[r_in, ], radius_mm)
  }
  if (length(o_in) > 0) {
    n_other[s_in] <- count_in_radius(coords_sender[s_in, ], coords_other[o_in, ], radius_mm)
  }
}

# 归一化：receiver/other 总细胞数比
total_recv  <- length(hep4_double)
total_other <- length(hep4_other)
ratio_expected <- total_recv / total_other
ratio_observed <- mean(n_recv) / (mean(n_other) + 1e-6)

cat(sprintf("== HGF+ HSC 30 µm 邻居 ==\n"))
cat(sprintf("  期望 receiver:other = %.3f\n", ratio_expected))
cat(sprintf("  观测 receiver:other = %.3f\n", ratio_observed))

# 配对 Wilcoxon：同一 sender 上的 recv vs other
valid <- (n_recv + n_other) > 0
# 因为 n_recv 与 n_other 量纲不同（细胞总数不同），先除以各自总数
recv_norm  <- n_recv[valid]  / total_recv  * 1000
other_norm <- n_other[valid] / total_other * 1000
w_paired <- wilcox.test(recv_norm, other_norm, paired = TRUE)
cat(sprintf("  Paired Wilcoxon: p = %.4e\n", w_paired$p.value))
cat(sprintf("  Median recv_norm  = %.3f per 1000 receivers\n", median(recv_norm)))
cat(sprintf("  Median other_norm = %.3f per 1000 others\n", median(other_norm)))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Permutation: 打乱 Hep.4 标签，验证是否 LR 特异性
# ------------------------------------------------------------
set.seed(123)
n_perm <- 1000
perm_ratio <- numeric(n_perm)

hep4_all_coords <- coords_all[hep4_idx, ]
hep4_labels     <- c(rep("double", length(hep4_double)),
                     rep("other",  length(hep4_other)))

for (p in 1:n_perm) {
  lab_shuf <- sample(hep4_labels)
  recv_coords  <- hep4_all_coords[lab_shuf == "double", ]
  other_coords <- hep4_all_coords[lab_shuf == "other", ]
  
  nr <- integer(length(hgf_pos)); no <- integer(length(hgf_pos))
  for (f in all_fovs) {
    s_in <- which(coords_sender$fov == f)
    r_in <- which(recv_coords$fov    == f)
    o_in <- which(other_coords$fov   == f)
    if (length(s_in) == 0) next
    if (length(r_in) > 0) nr[s_in] <- count_in_radius(coords_sender[s_in, ], recv_coords[r_in, ], radius_mm)
    if (length(o_in) > 0) no[s_in] <- count_in_radius(coords_sender[s_in, ], other_coords[o_in, ], radius_mm)
  }
  perm_ratio[p] <- mean(nr) / (mean(no) + 1e-6)
}

obs_ratio <- mean(n_recv) / (mean(n_other) + 1e-6)
p_perm <- mean(perm_ratio >= obs_ratio)
cat(sprintf("\n== Permutation (HGF+ HSC 邻居富集) ==\n"))
cat(sprintf("  Observed ratio = %.3f\n", obs_ratio))
cat(sprintf("  Null ratio     = %.3f (SD %.3f)\n", mean(perm_ratio), sd(perm_ratio)))
cat(sprintf("  Permutation p  = %.4f\n", p_perm))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. IL6-IL6R 轴同样分析（sender: IL6+ HSC / receiver: IL6R+ Hep.4）
# ------------------------------------------------------------
il6_pos     <- stellate_idx[il6_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]
hep4_il6r_pos <- hep4_idx[il6r_expr[hep4_idx] > 0]
hep4_il6r_neg <- hep4_idx[il6r_expr[hep4_idx] == 0]

coords_il6_sender <- coords_all[il6_pos, ]
coords_il6r_recv   <- coords_all[hep4_il6r_pos, ]
coords_il6r_other  <- coords_all[hep4_il6r_neg, ]

n_il6r_recv  <- integer(length(il6_pos))
n_il6r_other <- integer(length(il6_pos))

for (f in all_fovs) {
  s_in <- which(coords_il6_sender$fov == f)
  r_in <- which(coords_il6r_recv$fov   == f)
  o_in <- which(coords_il6r_other$fov  == f)
  if (length(s_in) == 0) next
  if (length(r_in) > 0) n_il6r_recv[s_in]  <- count_in_radius(coords_il6_sender[s_in, ], coords_il6r_recv[r_in, ], radius_mm)
  if (length(o_in) > 0) n_il6r_other[s_in] <- count_in_radius(coords_il6_sender[s_in, ], coords_il6r_other[o_in, ], radius_mm)
}

ratio_il6r_obs <- mean(n_il6r_recv) / (mean(n_il6r_other) + 1e-6)
cat(sprintf("\n== IL6+ HSC 30 µm 邻居 ==\n"))
cat(sprintf("  IL6R+ Hep.4: %d, IL6R- Hep.4: %d\n", length(hep4_il6r_pos), length(hep4_il6r_neg)))
cat(sprintf("  Observed receiver:other ratio = %.3f\n", ratio_il6r_obs))

w_il6r <- wilcox.test(n_il6r_recv / length(hep4_il6r_pos),
                      n_il6r_other / length(hep4_il6r_neg),
                      paired = TRUE)
cat(sprintf("  Paired Wilcoxon p = %.4e\n", w_il6r$p.value))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. 保存
# ------------------------------------------------------------
results_lr <- rbind(
  data.frame(
    LR_axis = "HGF_HSC_to_MET_IL6R_double",
    Sender_N = length(hgf_pos),
    Receiver_N = total_recv,
    Other_N = total_other,
    Obs_ratio = round(obs_ratio, 3),
    Null_ratio = round(mean(perm_ratio), 3),
    Perm_P = p_perm,
    Paired_Wilcox_P = w_paired$p.value
  ),
  data.frame(
    LR_axis = "IL6_HSC_to_IL6R_Hep4",
    Sender_N = length(il6_pos),
    Receiver_N = length(hep4_il6r_pos),
    Other_N = length(hep4_il6r_neg),
    Obs_ratio = round(ratio_il6r_obs, 3),
    Null_ratio = NA,
    Perm_P = NA,
    Paired_Wilcox_P = w_il6r$p.value
  )
)
print(results_lr)
write.csv(results_lr, "results/LR_coloc_summary.csv", row.names = FALSE)

saveRDS(list(
  hgf_pos = hgf_pos, hep4_double = hep4_double, hep4_other = hep4_other,
  n_recv = n_recv, n_other = n_other,
  obs_ratio = obs_ratio, perm_ratio = perm_ratio, p_perm = p_perm,
  il6_pos = il6_pos, hep4_il6r_pos = hep4_il6r_pos,
  n_il6r_recv = n_il6r_recv, n_il6r_other = n_il6r_other,
  results_lr = results_lr
), "results/LR_coloc_HGF_IL6.rds")

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. 可视化
# ------------------------------------------------------------
plot_df <- rbind(
  data.frame(LR = "HGF+ HSC\n→ MET+IL6R+ Hep.4", Group = "Receiver (double)", Value = n_recv),
  data.frame(LR = "HGF+ HSC\n→ MET+IL6R+ Hep.4", Group = "Other Hep.4",       Value = n_other),
  data.frame(LR = "IL6+ HSC\n→ IL6R+ Hep.4",      Group = "Receiver (IL6R+)",  Value = n_il6r_recv),
  data.frame(LR = "IL6+ HSC\n→ IL6R+ Hep.4",      Group = "Other Hep.4",       Value = n_il6r_other)
)

# 归一化显示（每 1000 个目标细胞）
plot_df$Value_norm <- ifelse(grepl("HGF", plot_df$LR, fixed = TRUE),
                             ifelse(plot_df$Group == "Receiver (double)",
                                    plot_df$Value / total_recv  * 1000,
                                    plot_df$Value / total_other * 1000),
                             ifelse(plot_df$Group == "Receiver (IL6R+)",
                                    plot_df$Value / length(hep4_il6r_pos) * 1000,
                                    plot_df$Value / length(hep4_il6r_neg) * 1000))

p_coloc <- ggplot(plot_df, aes(x = LR, y = Value_norm, fill = Group)) +
  geom_boxplot(alpha = 0.8, outlier.shape = NA) +
  geom_jitter(width = 0.15, size = 0.3, alpha = 0.15) +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c(
    "Receiver (double)" = "#C73E1D", "Other Hep.4" = "grey70",
    "Receiver (IL6R+)"  = "#2E86AB"
  )) +
  coord_cartesian(ylim = c(0, 200)) +
  labs(title = "LR-pair colocalization: HGF+ / IL6+ HSC 30 µm neighborhood",
       subtitle = sprintf("HGF: p_perm = %.4f | IL6R: p = %.2e", p_perm, w_il6r$p.value),
       x = NULL, y = "Neighbors per 1000 target cells") +
  theme_classic(base_size = 11) +
  theme(legend.title = element_blank())

print(p_coloc)
ggsave("figures/Fig5c_LR_coloc.pdf", p_coloc, width = 7, height = 4.5)

cat("\n=== 05c LR colocalization completed ===\n")
# ============================================================
# 05d_LR_coloc_negative_control.R
# 目的：阴性对照 + 分 FOV 方向一致性
# ============================================================
suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(Seurat); library(tidyverse); library(ggplot2)
})

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
if (!exists("cosmx_obj")) cosmx_obj <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")

celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]
expr_data    <- cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data
coords_all   <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[, c("x_slide_mm", "y_slide_mm", "fov")]
all_fovs     <- sort(unique(coords_all$fov))
radius_mm    <- 0.03

count_in_radius <- function(src, tgt, r) {
  if (nrow(tgt) == 0) return(integer(nrow(src)))
  sapply(seq_len(nrow(src)), function(i) {
    sum(sqrt((tgt$x_slide_mm - src$x_slide_mm[i])^2 +
               (tgt$y_slide_mm - src$y_slide_mm[i])^2) <= r)
  })
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. 阴性对照：HGF+ HSC → Hep.1 vs Hep.4-other
#    （Hep.1 与 MET/IL6R 无关，如果没有富集说明特异性）
# ------------------------------------------------------------
stellate_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
hgf_pos <- stellate_idx[as.numeric(expr_data["HGF", ])[stellate_idx] > 0]
hep1_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.1")
hep4_other <- readRDS("results/LR_coloc_HGF_IL6.rds")$hep4_other

coords_s <- coords_all[hgf_pos, ]
coords_r <- coords_all[hep1_idx, ]
coords_o <- coords_all[hep4_other, ]

n_r <- integer(length(hgf_pos)); n_o <- integer(length(hgf_pos))
for (f in all_fovs) {
  s <- which(coords_s$fov == f); r <- which(coords_r$fov == f); o <- which(coords_o$fov == f)
  if (length(s) == 0) next
  if (length(r) > 0) n_r[s] <- count_in_radius(coords_s[s, ], coords_r[r, ], radius_mm)
  if (length(o) > 0) n_o[s] <- count_in_radius(coords_s[s, ], coords_o[o, ], radius_mm)
}

ratio_neg <- mean(n_r) / (mean(n_o) + 1e-6)
ratio_expected_neg <- length(hep1_idx) / length(hep4_other)
cat(sprintf("\n=== 阴性对照: HGF+ HSC → Hep.1 vs Hep.4-other ===\n"))
cat(sprintf("  Expected ratio = %.3f\n", ratio_expected_neg))
cat(sprintf("  Observed ratio = %.3f\n", ratio_neg))
cat(sprintf("  Enrichment     = %.3f x\n", ratio_neg / ratio_expected_neg))

w_neg <- wilcox.test(n_r / length(hep1_idx),
                     n_o / length(hep4_other), paired = TRUE)
cat(sprintf("  Paired Wilcoxon p = %.4e\n", w_neg$p.value))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. 阴性对照 2：HGF- HSC → MET+IL6R+ double vs other
#    （如果 HGF- 也富集，说明是 Hep.4 double 本身的位置属性，不是 HGF 特异）
# ------------------------------------------------------------
hgf_neg <- stellate_idx[as.numeric(expr_data["HGF", ])[stellate_idx] == 0]
hep4_double <- readRDS("results/LR_coloc_HGF_IL6.rds")$hep4_double

coords_s2 <- coords_all[hgf_neg, ]
coords_r2 <- coords_all[hep4_double, ]
coords_o2 <- coords_all[hep4_other, ]

n_r2 <- integer(length(hgf_neg)); n_o2 <- integer(length(hgf_neg))
for (f in all_fovs) {
  s <- which(coords_s2$fov == f); r <- which(coords_r2$fov == f); o <- which(coords_o2$fov == f)
  if (length(s) == 0) next
  if (length(r) > 0) n_r2[s] <- count_in_radius(coords_s2[s, ], coords_r2[r, ], radius_mm)
  if (length(o) > 0) n_o2[s] <- count_in_radius(coords_s2[s, ], coords_o2[o, ], radius_mm)
}

ratio_neg2 <- mean(n_r2) / (mean(n_o2) + 1e-6)
ratio_expected_neg2 <- length(hep4_double) / length(hep4_other)
cat(sprintf("\n=== 阴性对照: HGF- HSC → double vs other ===\n"))
cat(sprintf("  Expected = %.3f, Observed = %.3f, Enrichment = %.3f x\n",
            ratio_expected_neg2, ratio_neg2, ratio_neg2 / ratio_expected_neg2))
w_neg2 <- wilcox.test(n_r2 / length(hep4_double),
                      n_o2 / length(hep4_other), paired = TRUE)
cat(sprintf("  Paired Wilcoxon p = %.4e\n", w_neg2$p.value))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. 分 FOV 方向一致性（HGF 轴）
# ------------------------------------------------------------
n_recv <- readRDS("results/LR_coloc_HGF_IL6.rds")$n_recv
n_other <- readRDS("results/LR_coloc_HGF_IL6.rds")$n_other

fov_of_sender <- coords_s$fov
fov_dir <- sapply(all_fovs, function(f) {
  idx <- which(fov_of_sender == f)
  if (length(idx) < 5) return(NA)
  # 每个 HSC 的归一化 recv/other 比
  r_i <- n_recv[idx] / length(hep4_double)
  o_i <- n_other[idx] / length(hep4_other)
  mean(r_i) > mean(o_i)
})
cat(sprintf("\n=== 分 FOV 方向一致性 ===\n"))
cat(sprintf("  有方向 FOV: %d\n", sum(!is.na(fov_dir))))
cat(sprintf("  一致 (recv > other): %d (%.1f%%)\n",
            sum(fov_dir, na.rm = TRUE),
            100 * mean(fov_dir, na.rm = TRUE)))


setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(Seurat); library(tidyverse); library(ggplot2)
})

cosmx_obj <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]
expr_data    <- GetAssayData(cosmx_obj, assay = "RNA", layer = "data")

hep4_idx  <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.4")
met_expr  <- as.numeric(expr_data["MET", ])
il6r_expr <- as.numeric(expr_data["IL6R", ])

hep4_double <- hep4_idx[met_expr[hep4_idx] > 0 & il6r_expr[hep4_idx] > 0]
hep4_other  <- setdiff(hep4_idx, hep4_double)

cat(sprintf("double: %d | other: %d\n", length(hep4_double), length(hep4_other)))

# depth 匹配
ncount <- cosmx_obj@meta.data$nCount_RNA
set.seed(123)
matched_other <- unique(unlist(lapply(hep4_double, function(i) {
  cand <- hep4_other[abs(ncount[hep4_other] - ncount[i]) < 50]
  if (length(cand) == 0) return(NULL)
  sample(cand, 1)
})))

cat(sprintf("matched other: %d\n", length(matched_other)))
cat(sprintf("double    median nCount = %d\n", median(ncount[hep4_double])))
cat(sprintf("matched   median nCount = %d\n", median(ncount[matched_other])))
cat(sprintf("nCount wilcox p = %.3f\n",
            wilcox.test(ncount[hep4_double], ncount[matched_other])$p.value))

# DEG
all_cells <- c(colnames(cosmx_obj)[hep4_double], colnames(cosmx_obj)[matched_other])
sub <- subset(cosmx_obj, cells = all_cells)
sub$subgroup <- c(rep("double", length(hep4_double)),
                  rep("other", length(matched_other)))
Idents(sub) <- sub$subgroup

deg <- FindMarkers(sub, ident.1 = "double", ident.2 = "other",
                   logfc.threshold = 0, min.pct = 0.05, test.use = "wilcox")
deg$gene <- rownames(deg)
deg_sig <- deg[deg$p_val_adj < 0.05 & abs(deg$avg_log2FC) > 0.5, ]

cat(sprintf("\n=== DEG ===\n"))
cat(sprintf("Total: %d | Sig: %d (up=%d, down=%d)\n",
            nrow(deg), nrow(deg_sig),
            sum(deg_sig$avg_log2FC > 0), sum(deg_sig$avg_log2FC < 0)))

cat("\nTop 20 up:\n")
print(head(deg_sig[order(-deg_sig$avg_log2FC),
                   c("gene","avg_log2FC","pct.1","pct.2","p_val_adj")], 20))

# 通路打分
sigs <- list(
  JAK_STAT    = c("STAT3","STAT1","STAT5A","STAT5B","JAK1","JAK2","SOCS1","SOCS3","PIM1","MYC"),
  MAPK_ERK    = c("MAPK1","MAPK3","MAP2K1","MAP2K2","RAF1","ELK1","FOS","JUN","MYC","EGR1"),
  MET_targets = c("MMP2","MMP9","MMP14","SPP1","VIM","CDH2","SNAI1","TWIST1","ZEB1"),
  IL6_STAT3   = c("SOCS3","PIM1","MYC","BCL2","BCL2L1","MCL1","VEGFA","HIF1A","CCND1"),
  PI3K_AKT    = c("AKT1","AKT2","PIK3CA","PIK3CB","MTOR","RPS6KB1","GSK3B","FOXO1","FOXO3")
)
sigs <- lapply(sigs, function(g) g[g %in% rownames(expr_data)])

all_g <- unique(unlist(sigs))
sub_expr <- as.matrix(expr_data[all_g, all_cells, drop = FALSE])
sub_expr[is.na(sub_expr)] <- 0
z <- t(scale(t(sub_expr)))
scores <- as.data.frame(sapply(sigs, function(g) colMeans(z[g, , drop = FALSE])))
scores$subgroup <- sub$subgroup

pw_test <- purrr::map_dfr(names(sigs), function(pw) {
  a <- scores[[pw]][scores$subgroup == "double"]
  b <- scores[[pw]][scores$subgroup == "other"]
  data.frame(Pathway = pw,
             double = round(mean(a), 3),
             other  = round(mean(b), 3),
             delta  = round(mean(a) - mean(b), 3),
             cohen_d = round((mean(a) - mean(b)) / sqrt((var(a)+var(b))/2), 3),
             P = wilcox.test(a, b)$p.value)
})
print(pw_test)

saveRDS(list(deg = deg, deg_sig = deg_sig, pw_test = pw_test),
        "results/Hep4_subgroup_analysis.rds")

binom_res <- binom.test(sum(fov_dir, na.rm = TRUE), sum(!is.na(fov_dir)),
                        p = 0.5, alternative = "greater")
cat(sprintf("  Binomial p = %.4e\n", binom_res$p.value))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. 汇总表
# ------------------------------------------------------------
summary_neg <- data.frame(
  Test = c("HGF+ → double (LR-specific)",
           "IL6+ → IL6R+ (LR-specific)",
           "HGF+ → Hep.1 (negative ctrl)",
           "HGF- → double (negative ctrl)"),
  Enrichment = c(0.221/0.181,
                 2.708/(1184/668),
                 ratio_neg / ratio_expected_neg,
                 ratio_neg2 / ratio_expected_neg2),
  P_value = c(0.0027, 0.038, w_neg$p.value, w_neg2$p.value)
)
print(summary_neg)
write.csv(summary_neg, "results/LR_coloc_negative_control.csv", row.names = FALSE)

saveRDS(list(
  ratio_neg = ratio_neg, ratio_neg2 = ratio_neg2,
  p_neg = w_neg$p.value, p_neg2 = w_neg2$p.value,
  fov_dir = fov_dir, binom_p = binom_res$p.value,
  summary_neg = summary_neg
), "results/LR_coloc_negctrl.rds")

cat("\n=== Negative control completed ===\n")

# ============================================================
# 06_HGF_HSC_niche_scan.R
# 目的：HGF+ vs HGF- HSC 50 µm 内各细胞类型邻居数量差异
# ============================================================
suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(Seurat); library(tidyverse); library(ggplot2)
})

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
if (!exists("cosmx_obj")) {
  cosmx_obj <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
}

celltype_col <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]
expr_data    <- cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data
coords_all   <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[, c("x_slide_mm", "y_slide_mm", "fov")]
all_fovs     <- sort(unique(coords_all$fov))
radius_mm    <- 0.05   # 50 µm

# HGF+ / HGF- HSC
stellate_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
hgf_expr     <- as.numeric(expr_data["HGF", ])
hgf_pos      <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]
hgf_neg      <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] == 0]

cat(sprintf("HGF+ HSC: %d, HGF- HSC: %d\n", length(hgf_pos), length(hgf_neg)))

# 扫描的细胞类型（去掉 HSC 自己）
all_types <- sort(unique(celltype_vec))
all_types <- setdiff(all_types, "Stellate.cells")
cat(sprintf("扫描 %d 种细胞类型\n", length(all_types)))

# 每个 HSC 周围 50 µm 内各类型数量
count_by_type <- function(hsc_idx) {
  out <- matrix(0L, nrow = length(hsc_idx), ncol = length(all_types),
                dimnames = list(NULL, all_types))
  for (f in all_fovs) {
    s_in <- which(coords_all$fov[hsc_idx] == f)
    if (length(s_in) == 0) next
    s_coords <- coords_all[hsc_idx[s_in], ]
    for (j in seq_along(all_types)) {
      t_idx <- which(celltype_vec == all_types[j] & coords_all$fov == f)
      if (length(t_idx) == 0) next
      t_coords <- coords_all[t_idx, ]
      d2 <- outer(s_coords$x_slide_mm, t_coords$x_slide_mm, "-")^2 +
        outer(s_coords$y_slide_mm, t_coords$y_slide_mm, "-")^2
      out[s_in, j] <- rowSums(d2 <= radius_mm^2)
    }
  }
  out
}

cat("计算 HGF+ HSC 邻居...\n")
mat_pos <- count_by_type(hgf_pos)
cat("计算 HGF- HSC 邻居...\n")
mat_neg <- count_by_type(hgf_neg)

# 归一化：除以该细胞类型总数，表示"每千个目标细胞里被 HSC 邻接几个"
type_totals <- sapply(all_types, function(t) sum(celltype_vec == t))
mat_pos_norm <- sweep(mat_pos, 2, type_totals, "/") * 1000
mat_neg_norm <- sweep(mat_neg, 2, type_totals, "/") * 1000

# 统计：HGF+ vs HGF- 每种细胞类型的邻接密度
res <- purrr::map_dfr(seq_along(all_types), function(j) {
  a <- mat_pos_norm[, j]; b <- mat_neg_norm[, j]
  data.frame(
    CellType = all_types[j],
    N_total  = type_totals[j],
    HGFpos   = round(mean(a), 3),
    HGFneg   = round(mean(b), 3),
    Ratio    = round(mean(a) / (mean(b) + 1e-6), 3),
    P        = wilcox.test(a, b)$p.value
  )
}) %>% mutate(FDR = p.adjust(P, "BH")) %>% arrange(FDR)

cat("\n=== HGF+ vs HGF- HSC 生态位扫描 ===\n")
print(res)

write.csv(res, "results/HGF_HSC_niche_scan.csv", row.names = FALSE)

# 可视化
p <- res %>%
  filter(N_total >= 50) %>%
  mutate(Score = log2(Ratio + 1e-6)) %>%
  ggplot(aes(reorder(CellType, Score), Score, fill = Score > 0)) +
  geom_col(alpha = 0.85) +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c("TRUE" = "#C73E1D", "FALSE" = "#2E86AB")) +
  geom_hline(yintercept = 0, linetype = "dashed") +
  coord_flip() +
  labs(title = "HGF+ vs HGF- HSC 50 µm niche composition",
       subtitle = "log2(HGF+ normalized neighbor density / HGF- normalized)",
       x = NULL, y = "log2 ratio") +
  theme_classic() +
  theme(legend.position = "none")

ggsave("figures/Fig_niche_scan.pdf", p, width = 7, height = 6)
print(p)

saveRDS(list(res = res, mat_pos = mat_pos, mat_neg = mat_neg),
        "results/HGF_HSC_niche_scan.rds")

cat("\n=== niche scan completed ===\n")

# ============ 1. 读 Slide2，用正确列名 ============
cosmx2 <- readRDS("data/GSM8855043_Slide2_SeuratObject.RDS")

celltype_col2 <- "RNA_nbclust_ee1320c5.9f48.455c.b54e.1fbfd34347ab_1_clusters"
celltype_vec2 <- cosmx2@meta.data[[celltype_col2]]
expr2         <- cosmx2[["RNA"]]$data
coords2       <- cosmx2@meta.data[, c("x_slide_mm","y_slide_mm","fov")]

# ============ 2. HGF+/- HSC ============
stellate2 <- which(celltype_vec2 == "Stellate.cells")
hgf_expr2 <- as.numeric(expr2["HGF",])
hgf_pos2  <- stellate2[hgf_expr2[stellate2] > 0]
hgf_neg2  <- setdiff(stellate2, hgf_pos2)

cat("=== 基本检查 ===\n")
cat(sprintf("cells: %d | Stellate: %d | HGF+: %d (%.1f%%) | HGF-: %d\n",
            ncol(cosmx2), length(stellate2),
            length(hgf_pos2), 100*length(hgf_pos2)/length(stellate2),
            length(hgf_neg2)))

# ============ 3. all_types ============
all_types <- sort(unique(as.character(celltype_vec2)))
all_types <- setdiff(all_types, "Stellate.cells")
cat(sprintf("all_types: %d 种\n", length(all_types)))

# ============ 4. 邻居计数函数 ============
all_fovs2 <- sort(unique(coords2$fov))
radius_mm <- 0.05

count_by_type <- function(hsc_idx) {
  out <- matrix(0, nrow = length(hsc_idx), ncol = length(all_types),
                dimnames = list(NULL, all_types))
  for (f in all_fovs2) {
    s_in <- which(coords2$fov[hsc_idx] == f)
    if (length(s_in) == 0) next
    s_coords <- coords2[hsc_idx[s_in], ]
    for (j in seq_along(all_types)) {
      t_idx <- which(celltype_vec2 == all_types[j] & coords2$fov == f)
      if (length(t_idx) == 0) next
      t_coords <- coords2[t_idx, ]
      d2 <- outer(s_coords$x_slide_mm, t_coords$x_slide_mm, "-")^2 +
        outer(s_coords$y_slide_mm, t_coords$y_slide_mm, "-")^2
      out[s_in, j] <- rowSums(d2 <= radius_mm^2)
    }
  }
  out
}

cat("计算 HGF+ 邻居...\n")
mat_pos2 <- count_by_type(hgf_pos2)
cat("计算 HGF- 邻居...\n")
mat_neg2 <- count_by_type(hgf_neg2)

# ============ 5. 归一化 + 统计 ============
type_totals2 <- vapply(all_types, function(t) {
  as.numeric(sum(celltype_vec2 == t, na.rm = TRUE))
}, numeric(1))
names(type_totals2) <- all_types

mat_pos2_n <- sweep(mat_pos2, 2, type_totals2, "/") * 1000
mat_neg2_n <- sweep(mat_neg2, 2, type_totals2, "/") * 1000

res2 <- purrr::map_dfr(seq_along(all_types), function(j) {
  a <- mat_pos2_n[, j]; b <- mat_neg2_n[, j]
  data.frame(
    CellType = all_types[j],
    N_total  = type_totals2[j],
    HGFpos   = round(mean(a), 3),
    HGFneg   = round(mean(b), 3),
    Ratio    = round(mean(a) / (mean(b) + 1e-6), 3),
    P        = wilcox.test(a, b)$p.value
  )
}) %>% mutate(FDR = p.adjust(P, "BH")) %>% arrange(FDR)

cat("\n=== Slide2 HGF+ vs HGF- HSC 生态位 ===\n")
print(res2[res2$N_total >= 200, ])
write.csv(res2, "results/HGF_HSC_niche_scan_Slide2.csv", row.names = FALSE)
saveRDS(list(res2 = res2, mat_pos2 = mat_pos2, mat_neg2 = mat_neg2),
        "results/HGF_HSC_niche_scan_Slide2.rds")

res1 <- read.csv("results/HGF_HSC_niche_scan.csv")

merged <- dplyr::inner_join(
  dplyr::select(res1, CellType, Ratio1 = Ratio, N1 = N_total),
  dplyr::select(res2, CellType, Ratio2 = Ratio, N2 = N_total),
  by = "CellType"
) %>%
  dplyr::filter(N1 >= 200, N2 >= 200) %>%
  dplyr::mutate(log2_R1 = log2(Ratio1),
                log2_R2 = log2(Ratio2))

  geom_hline(yintercept = 0, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey60") +
  geom_vline(xintercept = 0, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey60") +
  geom_abline(slope = 1, intercept = 0, color = "grey80", linetype = "dotted") +
  geom_point(size = 3, alpha = 0.8, color = "#C73E1D") +
  geom_smooth(method = "lm", se = TRUE, color = "black", fill = "grey80") +
  ggrepel::geom_text_repel(aes(label = CellType), size = 3, max.overlaps = 20) +
  labs(title = "Slide1 vs Slide2 niche scan consistency",
       subtitle = sprintf("Pearson r = %.3f, p = %s", r, p),
       x = "log2(HGF+ / HGF- ratio), Slide1",
       y = "log2(HGF+ / HGF- ratio), Slide2") +
  coord_fixed() +
  theme_classic(base_size = 12)

       width = 7, height = 6)

correct_deg_per_fov <- function(cosmx_obj, celltype_col, slide_label) {
  celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]
  counts <- GetAssayData(cosmx_obj, assay = "RNA", layer = "counts")
  coords <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[, "fov", drop = FALSE]  # ← 修复
  
  stellate <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
  hgf_expr <- as.numeric(GetAssayData(cosmx_obj, "RNA", "data")["HGF", ])
  hgf_pos <- stellate[hgf_expr[stellate] > 0]
  hgf_neg <- setdiff(stellate, hgf_pos)
  
  fovs <- unique(coords$fov)
  genes <- rownames(counts)
  mat_pos <- matrix(0, nrow = length(genes), ncol = length(fovs),
                    dimnames = list(genes, fovs))
  mat_neg <- mat_pos
  
  for (f in fovs) {
    p_idx <- intersect(hgf_pos, which(coords$fov == f))
    n_idx <- intersect(hgf_neg, which(coords$fov == f))
    if (length(p_idx) >= 3) mat_pos[, f] <- Matrix::rowSums(counts[, p_idx, drop = FALSE])
    else mat_pos[, f] <- NA
    if (length(n_idx) >= 3) mat_neg[, f] <- Matrix::rowSums(counts[, n_idx, drop = FALSE])
    else mat_neg[, f] <- NA
  }
  
  keep <- !is.na(mat_pos[1, ]) & !is.na(mat_neg[1, ])
  mat_pos <- mat_pos[, keep, drop = FALSE]
  mat_neg <- mat_neg[, keep, drop = FALSE]
  cat(sprintf("%s: %d 对 FOV\n", slide_label, sum(keep)))
  
  mat <- cbind(mat_pos, mat_neg)
  group <- factor(c(rep("pos", ncol(mat_pos)), rep("neg", ncol(mat_neg))),
                  levels = c("neg","pos"))
  pair  <- factor(rep(seq_len(ncol(mat_pos)), 2))
  
  # 过滤低表达
  keep_g <- rowSums(mat >= 5) >= 3
  mat <- mat[keep_g, ]
  
  dge <- DGEList(counts = mat)
  dge <- calcNormFactors(dge)
  design <- model.matrix(~ group + pair)
  v <- voom(dge, design, plot = FALSE)
  fit <- lmFit(v, design)
  fit <- eBayes(fit)
  tt <- topTable(fit, coef = 2, number = Inf, sort.by = "P")
  tt$gene <- rownames(tt)
  tt
}

# 跑两个样本
deg1_pb <- correct_deg_per_fov(
  readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS"),
  "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters",
  "Slide1")

deg2_pb <- correct_deg_per_fov(
  readRDS("data/GSM8855043_Slide2_SeuratObject.RDS"),
  "RNA_nbclust_ee1320c5.9f48.455c.b54e.1fbfd34347ab_1_clusters",
  "Slide2")

write.csv(deg1_pb, "results/DEG_pseudobulk_Slide1.csv", row.names = FALSE)
write.csv(deg2_pb, "results/DEG_pseudobulk_Slide2.csv", row.names = FALSE)

# 跨样本对比
merged_pb <- inner_join(
  deg1_pb %>% dplyr::select(gene, log2FC_1 = logFC),
  deg2_pb %>% dplyr::select(gene, log2FC_2 = logFC),
  by = "gene"
)
ct <- cor.test(merged_pb$log2FC_1, merged_pb$log2FC_2, method = "pearson")
cat(sprintf("\npseudobulk 跨样本 r = %.3f, p = %.2e, N = %d\n",
            ct$estimate, ct$p.value, nrow(merged_pb)))

# 双样本都显著 + 方向一致
pb_both <- merged_pb %>%
  dplyr::mutate(
    sig1 = gene %in% deg1_pb$gene[deg1_pb$adj.P.Val < 0.05 & abs(deg1_pb$logFC) > 0.5],
    sig2 = gene %in% deg2_pb$gene[deg2_pb$adj.P.Val < 0.05 & abs(deg2_pb$logFC) > 0.5]
  ) %>%
  dplyr::filter(sig1, sig2, sign(log2FC_1) == sign(log2FC_2))

cat(sprintf("\npseudobulk 双样本显著+方向一致: %d 个\n", nrow(pb_both)))
print(pb_both)
write.csv(pb_both, "results/DEG_pseudobulk_both_slides.csv", row.names = FALSE)

suppressPackageStartupMessages({library(Seurat); library(tidyverse)})

cosmx2 <- readRDS("data/GSM8855043_Slide2_SeuratObject.RDS")
celltype_col2 <- "RNA_nbclust_ee1320c5.9f48.455c.b54e.1fbfd34347ab_1_clusters"
celltype_vec2 <- cosmx2@meta.data[[celltype_col2]]
expr2 <- cosmx2[["RNA"]]$data

stellate2 <- which(celltype_vec2 == "Stellate.cells")
hgf_pos2 <- stellate2[as.numeric(expr2["HGF",])[stellate2] > 0]
hgf_neg2 <- setdiff(stellate2, hgf_pos2)

up_genes <- read.csv("results/signature_HGFpos_up.csv")$gene

cat(sprintf("=== 单细胞层面验证 %d 个 up 基因 ===\n", length(up_genes)))
res_validate <- data.frame(
  gene = up_genes,
  HGFpos_pos = NA_real_, HGFneg_pos = NA_real_,
  delta_pos = NA_real_,
  P_wilcox = NA_real_
)

for (i in seq_along(up_genes)) {
  g <- up_genes[i]
  if (!g %in% rownames(expr2)) next
  x <- as.numeric(expr2[g, ])
  pp <- mean(x[hgf_pos2] > 0)
  pn <- mean(x[hgf_neg2] > 0)
  res_validate$HGFpos_pos[i] <- pp
  res_validate$HGFneg_pos[i] <- pn
  res_validate$delta_pos[i]  <- pp - pn
  res_validate$P_wilcox[i]   <- wilcox.test(x[hgf_pos2], x[hgf_neg2])$p.value
}

res_validate$FDR <- p.adjust(res_validate$P_wilcox, "BH")
res_validate <- res_validate[order(-res_validate$delta_pos), ]

print(res_validate)
write.csv(res_validate, "results/signature_up_single_cell_validation.csv",
          row.names = FALSE)

# 判读汇总
cat(sprintf("\n=== 判定 ===\n"))
cat(sprintf("单细胞层面显著 (FDR<0.05 且 delta_pos>0.05): %d / %d\n",
            sum(res_validate$FDR < 0.05 & res_validate$delta_pos > 0.05, na.rm=TRUE),
            nrow(res_validate)))

# ============ 加载 bulk ============
data <- readRDS("results/GSE135251_DEG_results.rds")
log_expr <- data$log_expr
fibro_stage <- data$fibro_stage

symbols <- mapIds(org.Hs.eg.db,
                  keys = rownames(log_expr),
                  column = "SYMBOL", keytype = "ENSEMBL",
                  multiVals = "first")
valid <- !is.na(symbols) & !duplicated(symbols)
log_expr_sym <- log_expr[valid, ]
rownames(log_expr_sym) <- symbols[valid]

# ============ Panel a: 16 基因 vs 分期 ============
sig_in <- sig16[sig16 %in% rownames(log_expr_sym)]
cat(sprintf("16 基因在 bulk: %d\n", length(sig_in)))

cor_res <- purrr::map_dfr(sig_in, function(g) {
  ct <- cor.test(as.numeric(log_expr_sym[g, ]), fibro_stage, method = "spearman")
  data.frame(gene = g, rho = ct$estimate, P = ct$p.value)
}) %>% mutate(FDR = p.adjust(P, "BH")) %>% arrange(-rho)

print(cor_res)
write.csv(cor_res, "results/sig16_bulk_correlation.csv", row.names = FALSE)

# ============ Panel b: 16 基因 signature ROC ============
sig_score <- colMeans(log_expr_sym[sig_in, , drop = FALSE])
group_bin <- as.numeric(fibro_stage >= 2)

roc16 <- roc(group_bin, sig_score, quiet = TRUE)
cat(sprintf("\n16-gene signature AUC: %.3f\n", auc(roc16)))

# ============ Panel c: 11 基因 signature ============
ml <- readRDS("results/Module1_ML_results.rds")
core_genes <- ml$core_genes
cat(sprintf("11 基因: %d\n", length(core_genes)))

ml_res <- readRDS("results/Module1_ROC_results.rds")
cat(sprintf("11-gene CV AUC: %.3f\n", ml_res$auc_cv))

# ============ Panel d: 交集 ============
overlap <- intersect(sig16, core_genes)
cat(sprintf("\n16 ∩ 11 交集: %d 个\n", length(overlap)))
print(overlap)

# ============ 保存 ============
saveRDS(list(
  sig16_in_bulk = sig_in,
  cor_res = cor_res,
  roc16 = roc16,
  auc16 = as.numeric(auc(roc16)),
  core11 = core_genes,
  overlap = overlap
), "results/Fig4_data.rds")

  url <- sprintf(
    "https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/geo/query/acc.cgi?acc=%s&targ=gsm&form=text&view=quick",
    gsm)
  
  tryCatch({
    old <- getOption("timeout"); options(timeout = 15)
    lines <- readLines(url, warn = FALSE)
    options(timeout = old)
    
    # 每个 GSM 的 characteristics 是单独一行
    ch_lines <- grep("Sample_characteristics", lines, value = TRUE)
    
    cat(sprintf("characteristics 行数: %d\n", length(ch_lines)))
    
    if (length(ch_lines) > 0) {
      # 提取值（= 后面的内容）
      vals <- sub("^.*?=\\s*", "", ch_lines)
      vals <- gsub('"', '', vals)
      # 值分布
      tb <- sort(table(vals), decreasing = TRUE)
      cat("字段值分布 (top 10):\n")
      print(head(tb, 10))
    }
  }, error = function(e) cat("失败:", e$message, "\n"))
  
  Sys.sleep(0.5)
}

file <- "data/GSE49541/GSE49541_series_matrix.txt.gz"

# 关键：不设 row.names，读进来后手动处理
expr_raw <- read.delim(text = paste(expr_lines, collapse = "\n"),
                       check.names = FALSE, stringsAsFactors = FALSE)
cat(sprintf("原始: %d 行 x %d 列\n", nrow(expr_raw), ncol(expr_raw)))

# 第一列是探针 ID

expr_mat <- as.matrix(expr_raw[, -1])
rownames(expr_mat) <- make.unique(as.character(probe_ids))
cat(sprintf("矩阵: %d 探针 x %d 样本\n", nrow(expr_mat), ncol(expr_mat)))

# 样本分期
ch_lines <- grep("^!Sample_characteristics_ch1", lines, value = TRUE)
fib_line <- grep("fibrosis|Stage", ch_lines, ignore.case = TRUE, value = TRUE)[1]
vals <- strsplit(fib_line, "\t")[[1]][-1]
vals <- gsub('"', '', vals)
group <- ifelse(grepl("advanced|3-4", vals), 1,
                ifelse(grepl("mild|0-1", vals), 0, NA))
cat(sprintf("mild = %d, advanced = %d\n",
            sum(group == 0, na.rm = TRUE), sum(group == 1, na.rm = TRUE)))

if (!requireNamespace("hgu133plus2.db", quietly = TRUE)) {
  BiocManager::install("hgu133plus2.db", ask = FALSE, update = FALSE)
}

  library(hgu133plus2.db); library(AnnotationDbi)
})

probe2gene <- AnnotationDbi::mapIds(
  hgu133plus2.db,
  keys = rownames(expr_mat),
  column = "SYMBOL", keytype = "PROBEID",
  multiVals = "first"
)
cat(sprintf("有 symbol 的探针: %d / %d\n",
            sum(!is.na(probe2gene)), length(probe2gene)))

# 11 基因映射

# 用 mapIds 结果映射
probe_idx <- which(probe2gene %in% core_genes)
cat(sprintf("找到 %d 个探针对应 11 基因\n", length(probe_idx)))

# 直接构造 gene x sample 矩阵（取第一列 symbol，多探针取平均）
gene_ids <- probe2gene[probe_idx]
expr_sub <- expr_mat[probe_idx, , drop = FALSE]
expr_gene <- rowsum(expr_sub, group = gene_ids) /
  as.numeric(table(gene_ids)[unique(gene_ids)])
# ↑ 这里用 rowsum 求组均值

# 简化版：用 aggregate
expr_df <- as.data.frame(expr_sub)
expr_df$gene <- gene_ids
expr_gene <- expr_df %>%
  group_by(gene) %>%
  summarise(across(where(is.numeric), mean)) %>%
  column_to_rownames("gene") %>%
  as.matrix()

cat(sprintf("去重后: %d 基因\n", nrow(expr_gene)))
cat(sprintf("缺失基因: %s\n",
            paste(setdiff(core_genes, rownames(expr_gene)), collapse = ", ")))

# ROC
genes_avail <- intersect(core_genes, rownames(expr_gene))
mat_z <- t(scale(t(expr_gene[genes_avail, ])))
mat_z[is.na(mat_z)] <- 0
sig_score <- colMeans(mat_z)

keep <- !is.na(group) & !is.na(sig_score)
roc_obj <- pROC::roc(group[keep], sig_score[keep], quiet = TRUE)
auc_val <- as.numeric(pROC::auc(roc_obj))
ci_obj  <- pROC::ci.auc(roc_obj)

cat(sprintf("\n=== GSE49541 外部验证 ===\n"))
cat(sprintf("AUC = %.3f (95%% CI: %.3f - %.3f)\n",
            auc_val, ci_obj[1], ci_obj[3]))
cat(sprintf("N = %d (mild %d, advanced %d)\n",
            sum(keep), sum(group[keep] == 0), sum(group[keep] == 1)))

saveRDS(list(roc = roc_obj, auc = auc_val,
             expr_gene = expr_gene, group = group),
        "results/GSE49541_validation.rds")

# ============ 1. 加载验证数据 ============
res <- readRDS("results/GSE49541_validation.rds")

df <- data.frame(outcome = group, signature = sig_score)

# DCA 要求输入是 0-1 概率，signature 有负值不行
df$pred_prob <- predict(
  glm(outcome ~ signature, data = df, family = binomial),
  type = "response"
)
cat(sprintf("pred_prob 范围: %.3f - %.3f\n",
            min(df$pred_prob), max(df$pred_prob)))

# ============ 3. DCA（用 pred_prob）============
dca_res <- dcurves::dca(outcome ~ pred_prob, data = df)

# ============ 4. 可视化 ============
p_dca <- plot(dca_res) +
  labs(title = "Decision Curve Analysis: 11-gene signature (GSE49541)",
       subtitle = "Net benefit vs threshold probability") +
  theme_classic(base_size = 12)

ggsave("figures/Fig6_DCA_GSE49541.pdf", p_dca, width = 6, height = 5)
print(p_dca)

# ============ 5. 关键阈值下的净获益 ============
tb <- as.data.frame(dca_res$dca)
tb_sel <- tb %>% filter(variable == "pred_prob",
                        threshold %in% c(0.2, 0.3, 0.4))
cat("\n=== 净获益（vs 全治疗/全不治疗）===\n")
print(tb_sel)

saveRDS(list(dca = dca_res, df = df),
        "results/GSE49541_DCA.rds")
