# ============ 1. Slide1 + Slide2 各自跑一遍 ============
run_tf_analysis <- function(cosmx_obj, celltype_col, slide_label) {
  celltype_vec <- cosmx_obj@meta.data[[celltype_col]]
  expr <- cosmx_obj[["RNA"]]$data
  
  stellate <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
  hgf_expr <- as.numeric(expr["HGF", ])
  hgf_pos <- stellate[hgf_expr[stellate] > 0]
  hgf_neg <- setdiff(stellate, hgf_pos)
  
  cat(sprintf("%s: HGF+ %d, HGF- %d\n", slide_label, length(hgf_pos), length(hgf_neg)))
  
  # 用 HSC 子集跑
  sub <- subset(cosmx_obj, cells = colnames(cosmx_obj)[stellate])
  
  # DoRothEA regulon（A+B+C 高置信度）
  data("dorothea_hs", package = "dorothea")
  regulon <- dorothea_hs %>% dplyr::filter(confidence %in% c("A","B","C"))
  
  # ULM
  mat <- as.matrix(expr[, stellate])
  mat <- mat[rowSums(mat > 0) > ncol(mat) * 0.05, ]
  
  tf_act <- decoupleR::run_ulm(mat = mat, net = regulon,
                               .source = "tf", .target = "target",
                               .mor = "mor", minsize = 5)
  
  # 转矩阵
  tf_mat <- tf_act %>%
    dplyr::select(condition, source, score) %>%
    pivot_wider(names_from = source, values_from = score) %>%
    column_to_rownames("condition")
  tf_mat <- tf_mat[colnames(mat), ]
  
  # 分组
  grp <- ifelse(seq_along(stellate) %in% match(hgf_pos, stellate), "pos", "neg")
  # 更稳的写法
  grp <- ifelse(colnames(mat) %in% colnames(cosmx_obj)[hgf_pos], "pos", "neg")
  
  # HGF+ vs HGF- 差异
  tf_diff <- purrr::map_dfr(colnames(tf_mat), function(tf) {
    a <- tf_mat[[tf]][grp == "pos"]
    b <- tf_mat[[tf]][grp == "neg"]
    data.frame(TF = tf,
               delta = mean(a) - mean(b),
               P = wilcox.test(a, b)$p.value)
  }) %>% mutate(FDR = p.adjust(P, "BH"),
                Slide = slide_label) %>% arrange(FDR)
  
  return(tf_diff)
}

# Slide1
cosmx1 <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
res1 <- run_tf_analysis(cosmx1,
                        "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters", "Slide1")

# Slide2
cosmx2 <- readRDS("data/GSM8855043_Slide2_SeuratObject.RDS")
res2 <- run_tf_analysis(cosmx2,
                        "RNA_nbclust_ee1320c5.9f48.455c.b54e.1fbfd34347ab_1_clusters", "Slide2")

# ============ 2. 双切片都显著的 TF ============
cat("\n=== Slide1 top 10 ===\n")
print(head(res1[, c("TF","delta","FDR")], 10))
cat("\n=== Slide2 top 10 ===\n")
print(head(res2[, c("TF","delta","FDR")], 10))

# 交集：双切片 FDR<0.05 且方向一致
sig1 <- res1 %>% dplyr::filter(FDR < 0.05) %>% dplyr::select(TF, delta1 = delta)
sig2 <- res2 %>% dplyr::filter(FDR < 0.05) %>% dplyr::select(TF, delta2 = delta)

common <- inner_join(sig1, sig2, by = "TF") %>%
  dplyr::filter(sign(delta1) == sign(delta2)) %>%
  arrange(desc(abs(delta1) + abs(delta2)))

cat(sprintf("\n=== 双切片都显著 + 方向一致: %d 个 TF ===\n", nrow(common)))
print(common)

write.csv(res1, "results/TF_diff_Slide1.csv", row.names = FALSE)
write.csv(res2, "results/TF_diff_Slide2.csv", row.names = FALSE)
write.csv(common, "results/TF_diff_common.csv", row.names = FALSE)

saveRDS(list(slide1 = res1, slide2 = res2, common = common),
        "results/TF_analysis.rds")


setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({library(tidyverse); library(dorothea)})
data("dorothea_hs", package = "dorothea")

# VDR 的靶基因（DoRothEA A+B+C）
vdr_targets <- dorothea_hs %>%
  dplyr::filter(tf == "VDR", confidence %in% c("A","B","C")) %>%
  pull(target)

suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(httr); library(jsonlite); library(tidyverse)
})

if (!requireNamespace("httr", quietly = TRUE)) install.packages("httr")
if (!requireNamespace("jsonlite", quietly = TRUE)) install.packages("jsonlite")

sig16 <- read.csv("results/signature_HGFpos_up.csv")$gene
cat(sprintf("Query genes: %d\n", length(sig16)))

# DGIdb API
url <- "https://dgidb.org/api/graphql"
gene_list <- paste(sprintf('"%s"', sig16), collapse = ", ")

query <- sprintf('{
  genes(names: [%s]) {
    nodes {
      name
      interactions {
        drug { name approved }
        interactionScore
        interactionTypes { type }
      }
    }
  }
}', gene_list)

res <- POST(url, body = list(query = query), encode = "json")
dat <- content(res, "parsed")

# 提取药物
drugs <- map_dfr(dat$data$genes$nodes, function(g) {
  if (length(g$interactions) == 0) return(NULL)
  map_dfr(g$interactions, function(i) {
    data.frame(
      gene = g$name,
      drug = i$drug$name,
      approved = i$drug$approved,
      score = i$interactionScore,
      type = if (length(i$interactionTypes) > 0) i$interactionTypes[[1]]$type else NA
    )
  })
})

if (nrow(drugs) > 0) {
  cat(sprintf("\n=== 找到 %d 条 gene-drug 关联 ===\n", nrow(drugs)))
  cat(sprintf("涉及 %d 个药物\n", length(unique(drugs$drug))))
  
  # 只看已获批的药
  approved_drugs <- drugs %>%
    dplyr::filter(approved) %>%
    dplyr::arrange(desc(score))
  
  cat(sprintf("\n=== 已获批药物 ===\n"))
  print(head(approved_drugs, 20))
  
  # 按药物聚合
  drug_summary <- approved_drugs %>%
    group_by(drug) %>%
    summarise(n_genes = n(),
              genes = paste(gene, collapse = ", "),
              max_score = max(score),
              .groups = "drop") %>%
    arrange(desc(n_genes), desc(max_score))
  
  cat("\n=== 药物汇总 ===\n")
  print(head(drug_summary, 15))
  
  write.csv(drugs, "results/DGIdb_signature_drugs.csv", row.names = FALSE)
  write.csv(drug_summary, "results/DGIdb_drug_summary.csv", row.names = FALSE)
} else {
  cat("DGIdb 没找到关联药物\n")
}

suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(Seurat); library(tidyverse); library(org.Hs.eg.db)
})

# ============ 1. 加载 GSE135251 bulk ============
data <- readRDS("results/GSE135251_DEG_results.rds")
log_expr <- data$log_expr
fibro_stage <- data$fibro_stage

symbols <- mapIds(org.Hs.eg.db, keys = rownames(log_expr),
                  column = "SYMBOL", keytype = "ENSEMBL", multiVals = "first")
valid <- !is.na(symbols) & !duplicated(symbols)
log_expr_sym <- log_expr[valid, ]
rownames(log_expr_sym) <- symbols[valid]

# ============ 2. 16 基因 signature 打分 ============
sig16 <- read.csv("results/signature_HGFpos_up.csv")$gene
sig_in <- intersect(sig16, rownames(log_expr_sym))
cat(sprintf("Signature: %d / %d 基因\n", length(sig_in), length(sig16)))

# z-score 后平均
mat_z <- t(scale(t(log_expr_sym[sig_in, , drop = FALSE])))
mat_z[is.na(mat_z)] <- 0
sig_score <- colMeans(mat_z)

df <- data.frame(stage = fibro_stage, sig_score = sig_score)

# ============ 3. 与分期相关 ============
ct <- cor.test(df$stage, df$sig_score, method = "spearman")
cat(sprintf("Signature vs stage: rho = %.3f, p = %.2e\n",
            ct$estimate, ct$p.value))

df$group <- ifelse(df$stage >= 2, "F2-F4", "F0-F1")
wt <- wilcox.test(sig_score ~ group, data = df)
cat(sprintf("F0-F1 vs F2-F4: p = %.2e\n", wt$p.value))

# ============ 4. 可视化 ============
p <- ggplot(df, aes(factor(stage), sig_score, fill = factor(stage))) +
  geom_boxplot(alpha = 0.85, outlier.shape = NA) +
  geom_jitter(width = 0.2, size = 0.5, alpha = 0.3) +
  geom_smooth(aes(group = 1), method = "lm", se = TRUE,
              color = "#C73E1D", fill = "#C73E1D", alpha = 0.15) +
  scale_fill_brewer(palette = "OrRd") +
  labs(title = "HGF+ HSC signature vs fibrosis stage (GSE135251)",
       subtitle = sprintf("Spearman rho = %.3f, p = %.2e",
                          ct$estimate, ct$p.value),
       x = "Fibrosis stage", y = "Signature score (z-mean)") +
  theme_classic(base_size = 12) +
  theme(legend.position = "none")

ggsave("figures/Fig5_sig_vs_stage.pdf", p, width = 6, height = 4.5)
ggsave("figures/Fig5_sig_vs_stage.png", p, width = 6, height = 4.5, dpi = 300)
print(p)

saveRDS(list(df = df, rho = ct$estimate, p = ct$p.value),
        "results/sig16_vs_stage.rds")

# Slide1 和 Slide2 都跑
results <- data.frame()

for (slide in c("Slide1", "Slide2")) {
  file <- sprintf("data/GSM%s_%s_SeuratObject.RDS",
                  ifelse(slide == "Slide1", "8855042", "8855043"), slide)
  col <- ifelse(slide == "Slide1",
                "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters",
                "RNA_nbclust_ee1320c5.9f48.455c.b54e.1fbfd34347ab_1_clusters")
  
  obj <- readRDS(file)
  celltype_vec <- obj@meta.data[[col]]
  expr <- obj[["RNA"]]$data
  
  stellate <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
  hgf_pos <- stellate[as.numeric(expr["HGF", ])[stellate] > 0]
  hgf_neg <- setdiff(stellate, hgf_pos)
  
  for (g in c("LIFR", "OSMR", "IL6R", "IL6ST", "IL11RA")) {
    if (!g %in% rownames(expr)) next
    x <- as.numeric(expr[g, ])
    
    pos_pos <- mean(x[hgf_pos] > 0)
    pos_neg <- mean(x[hgf_neg] > 0)
    
    results <- rbind(results, data.frame(
      Slide = slide, Gene = g,
      HGFpos_posrate = round(pos_pos, 3),
      HGFneg_posrate = round(pos_neg, 3),
      delta_posrate = round(pos_pos - pos_neg, 3),
      HGFpos_mean = round(mean(x[hgf_pos]), 3),
      HGFneg_mean = round(mean(x[hgf_neg]), 3),
      P_wilcox = wilcox.test(x[hgf_pos], x[hgf_neg])$p.value
    ))
  }
}

print(results)
write.csv(results, "results/LIFR_OSMR_posrate_check.csv", row.names = FALSE)

suppressPackageStartupMessages({library(Seurat); library(tidyverse)})

# Slide1
cosmx1 <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
col1 <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec1 <- cosmx1@meta.data[[col1]]
expr1 <- cosmx1[["RNA"]]$data

stellate1 <- which(celltype_vec1 == "Stellate.cells")
hgf_expr1 <- as.numeric(expr1["HGF", ])[stellate1]

# 不同阈值
cat("=== Slide1 HGF 表达分布 ===\n")
print(summary(hgf_expr1))
cat(sprintf("HGF > 0: %d (%.1f%%)\n", sum(hgf_expr1 > 0), 100*mean(hgf_expr1 > 0)))
cat(sprintf("HGF > 1: %d (%.1f%%)\n", sum(hgf_expr1 > 1), 100*mean(hgf_expr1 > 1)))
cat(sprintf("HGF > 2: %d (%.1f%%)\n", sum(hgf_expr1 > 2), 100*mean(hgf_expr1 > 2)))

# ============ 关键：不同阈值下的空间生态位 ============
coords1 <- cosmx1@meta.data[, c("x_slide_mm","y_slide_mm","fov")]
hep4_idx <- which(celltype_vec1 == "Hep.4")
all_types_scan <- c("Hep.4", "Periportal.LSECs", "Cholangiocytes", "Portal.endothelial.cells")

run_niche <- function(hgf_pos_idx, hgf_neg_idx, threshold_label) {
  res <- data.frame()
  for (ct in all_types_scan) {
    ct_idx <- which(celltype_vec1 == ct)
    ratio <- tryCatch({
      # 每个 HGF+ 周围 50µm 内该细胞类型数量
      pos_counts <- sapply(hgf_pos_idx, function(i) {
        f <- coords1$fov[i]
        tgt <- ct_idx[coords1$fov[ct_idx] == f]
        if (length(tgt) == 0) return(0)
        d <- sqrt((coords1$x_slide_mm[tgt] - coords1$x_slide_mm[i])^2 +
                    (coords1$y_slide_mm[tgt] - coords1$y_slide_mm[i])^2)
        sum(d <= 0.05)
      })
      neg_counts <- sapply(hgf_neg_idx, function(i) {
        f <- coords1$fov[i]
        tgt <- ct_idx[coords1$fov[ct_idx] == f]
        if (length(tgt) == 0) return(0)
        d <- sqrt((coords1$x_slide_mm[tgt] - coords1$x_slide_mm[i])^2 +
                    (coords1$y_slide_mm[tgt] - coords1$y_slide_mm[i])^2)
        sum(d <= 0.05)
      })
      n_ct <- length(ct_idx)
      mean(pos_counts)/n_ct / (mean(neg_counts)/n_ct + 1e-6)
    }, error = function(e) NA)
    
    res <- rbind(res, data.frame(Threshold = threshold_label,
                                 CellType = ct,
                                 Ratio = round(ratio, 3)))
  }
  res
}

# 三个阈值
hgf0_pos <- stellate1[hgf_expr1 > 0]; hgf0_neg <- stellate1[hgf_expr1 == 0]
hgf1_pos <- stellate1[hgf_expr1 > 1]; hgf1_neg <- stellate1[hgf_expr1 <= 1]
hgf2_pos <- stellate1[hgf_expr1 > 2]; hgf2_neg <- stellate1[hgf_expr1 <= 2]

cat("\n=== 不同阈值下 HGF+ HSC 数 ===\n")
cat(sprintf(">0: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\n", length(hgf0_pos), length(stellate1),
            100*length(hgf0_pos)/length(stellate1)))
cat(sprintf(">1: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\n", length(hgf1_pos), length(stellate1),
            100*length(hgf1_pos)/length(stellate1)))
cat(sprintf(">2: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\n", length(hgf2_pos), length(stellate1),
            100*length(hgf2_pos)/length(stellate1)))

# 计算不同阈值下的 niche scan
cat("\n=== 空间生态位（不同阈值）===\n")
res_all <- rbind(
  run_niche(hgf0_pos, hgf0_neg, "HGF>0"),
  run_niche(hgf1_pos, hgf1_neg, "HGF>1"),
  run_niche(hgf2_pos, hgf2_neg, "HGF>2")
)
print(res_all)
write.csv(res_all, "results/HGF_threshold_sensitivity.csv", row.names = FALSE)

# ============ 2. 数值判断双峰性 ============
# 简单启发式：HGF>0 的细胞里，看中位数附近有没有低谷
hgf_pos_vals <- hgf_expr[hgf_expr > 0]
cat(sprintf("\nHGF+ 细胞: %d\n", length(hgf_pos_vals)))

print(quantile(hgf_pos_vals, probs = seq(0, 1, 0.1)))

# 分箱计数（看是否有谷）
cat("\n=== HGF 表达分箱计数 ===\n")
bins <- cut(hgf_expr, breaks = c(0, 0.001, 0.5, 1, 1.5, 2, 3, 5, 20),
            labels = c("=0", "(0,0.5]", "(0.5,1]", "(1,1.5]",
                       "(1.5,2]", "(2,3]", "(3,5]", ">5"))
print(table(bins))

# dip test（双峰性检验）
if (!requireNamespace("diptest", quietly = TRUE)) {
  install.packages("diptest")
}
library(diptest)
dip_res <- dip.test(hgf_expr)
cat(sprintf("\n=== Dip test ===\n"))
cat(sprintf("D = %.4f, p = %.4f\n", dip_res$statistic, dip_res$p.value))
cat(sprintf("结论: %s\n",
            ifelse(dip_res$p.value < 0.05,
                   "显著偏离单峰 → 可能存在双峰",
                   "不能拒绝单峰 → 支持连续谱")))

raw_counts <- GetAssayData(cosmx1, "RNA", "counts")["HGF", stellate1]
tb <- table(raw_counts)
df_counts <- data.frame(
  count = as.numeric(names(tb)),
  n = as.numeric(tb),
  pct = round(100 * as.numeric(tb) / length(raw_counts), 1)
)
print(df_counts)

write.csv(df_counts, "results/HGF_raw_counts_distribution.csv", row.names = FALSE)

#####Fig1：CosMx 概览 + Stellate 纤维化区富集###
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({

dir.create("figures", showWarnings = FALSE)

cosmx1 <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
col1 <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec1 <- cosmx1@meta.data[[col1]]
coords1 <- cosmx1@meta.data[, c("x_slide_mm","y_slide_mm","fov")]

# --- 1a. 细胞类型组成 ---
ct_df <- data.frame(
  CellType = names(table(celltype_vec1)),
  N        = as.numeric(table(celltype_vec1))
) %>%
  mutate(Pct = N / sum(N) * 100) %>%
  arrange(desc(N)) %>% head(15)

p1a <- ggplot(ct_df, aes(reorder(CellType, N), N, fill = CellType)) +
  geom_col(alpha = 0.85) +
  coord_flip() +
  labs(title = "Cell type composition", x = NULL, y = "Cell count") +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) + theme(legend.position = "none")

# --- 1b. Stellate 空间分布 ---

plot_df$is_stellate <- celltype_vec1 == "Stellate.cells"

p1b <- ggplot() +
  geom_point(data = plot_df[!plot_df$is_stellate, ],
             aes(x_slide_mm, y_slide_mm),
             size = 0.05, alpha = 0.1, color = "grey80") +
  geom_point(data = plot_df[plot_df$is_stellate, ],
             aes(x_slide_mm, y_slide_mm),
             size = 0.3, alpha = 0.6, color = "#C73E1D") +
  coord_fixed() +
  labs(title = "Stellate cells spatial distribution",
       x = "x_slide_mm", y = "y_slide_mm") +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10)

# --- 1c. Stellate 纤维化区富集 ---
enrich <- readRDS("results/GSE292268_Stellate_enrichment_independent.rds")
or_val <- enrich$OR_indep
ci_val <- enrich$CI

p1c <- ggplot() +
  geom_point(aes(x = or_val, y = 1), size = 5, color = "#C73E1D") +
  geom_errorbarh(aes(xmin = ci_val[1], xmax = ci_val[2], y = 1),
                 height = 0.15, color = "#C73E1D", linewidth = 1) +
  geom_vline(xintercept = 1, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey40") +
  scale_x_log10() +
  labs(title = "Stellate enrichment in fibrotic region",
       subtitle = sprintf("OR = %.2f (95%% CI: %.2f-%.2f)",
                          or_val, ci_val[1], ci_val[2]),
       x = "Odds ratio (log scale)", y = NULL) +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) +
  theme(axis.text.y = element_blank(),
        axis.ticks.y = element_blank())

# --- 2a. niche scan Slide1 vs Slide2 ---
res1 <- read.csv("results/HGF_HSC_niche_scan.csv")

res1$Slide <- "Slide1"; res2$Slide <- "Slide2"

  filter(N_total >= 200) %>%
  mutate(Score = log2(Ratio + 1e-6))

p2a <- ggplot(niche_df, aes(reorder(CellType, Score), Score, fill = Slide)) +
  geom_col(position = position_dodge(0.8), alpha = 0.9) +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c("Slide1" = "#C73E1D", "Slide2" = "#2E86AB")) +

  coord_flip() +
  labs(title = "HGF+ HSC niche scan (50 µm)",
       subtitle = "log2(enrichment ratio) across two slides",
       x = NULL, y = "log2 ratio") +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) +
  theme(legend.position = "top",
        axis.text.y = element_text(size = 7))

                               "Other" = "grey70")) +

  theme(legend.position = "none") +

  geom_hline(yintercept = 0, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey60") +

# --- 3a. pseudobulk 跨切片散点 ---
merged_pb <- inner_join(

#####Fig5：11 基因诊断 + 16 基因分期对比####

# --- 5a. 训练 ROC ---
roc_train <- readRDS("results/Module1_ROC_results.rds")

roc_df_train <- data.frame(
  FPR = 1 - roc_train$roc_cv$specificities,
  TPR = roc_train$roc_cv$sensitivities
)
p5a <- ggplot(roc_df_train, aes(FPR, TPR)) +
  geom_line(color = "#C73E1D", linewidth = 1.2) +
  geom_abline(intercept = 1, slope = 1, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey60") +
  annotate("text", x = 0.65, y = 0.15,
           label = sprintf("CV AUC = %.3f", roc_train$auc_cv),
           size = 4, color = "#C73E1D") +
  labs(title = "Training ROC (GSE135251)",
       subtitle = "11-gene signature, 10-fold CV",
       x = "1 - Specificity", y = "Sensitivity") +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) + coord_fixed()

# --- 5b. 验证 ROC ---
val <- readRDS("results/GSE49541_validation.rds")
roc_df_val <- data.frame(
  FPR = 1 - val$roc$specificities,
  TPR = val$roc$sensitivities
)
ci <- pROC::ci.auc(val$roc)
p5b <- ggplot(roc_df_val, aes(FPR, TPR)) +
  geom_line(color = "#C73E1D", linewidth = 1.2) +
  geom_abline(intercept = 1, slope = 1, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey60") +
  annotate("text", x = 0.65, y = 0.15,
           label = sprintf("AUC = %.3f\n95%% CI: %.3f-%.3f",
                           val$auc, ci[1], ci[3]),
           size = 3.5, color = "#C73E1D") +
  labs(title = "External validation ROC (GSE49541)",
       subtitle = "11-gene signature, n=72",
       x = "1 - Specificity", y = "Sensitivity") +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) + coord_fixed()

# --- 5c. DCA（重算版）---
suppressPackageStartupMessages({library(dcurves); library(tidyverse)})

# 从验证数据重建
val <- readRDS("results/GSE49541_validation.rds")
sig_score <- colMeans(t(scale(t(val$expr_gene))))
group <- val$group

df_dca <- data.frame(outcome = group, signature = sig_score)
df_dca <- df_dca[!is.na(df_dca$outcome) & !is.na(df_dca$signature), ]

df_dca$pred_prob <- predict(
  glm(outcome ~ signature, data = df_dca, family = binomial),
  type = "response"
)

dca_obj <- dcurves::dca(outcome ~ pred_prob, data = df_dca)

# 提取内部 data.frame：dca_obj$dca
dca_df <- dca_obj$dca

# 检查列名
cat("DCA 数据列名:", paste(colnames(dca_df), collapse = ", "), "\n")

dca_long <- dca_df %>%
  dplyr::select(threshold, variable, net_benefit) %>%
  dplyr::filter(variable %in% c("pred_prob", "all", "none")) %>%
  dplyr::mutate(variable = recode(variable,
                                  "pred_prob" = "11-gene signature",
                                  "all"       = "Treat all",
                                  "none"      = "Treat none"))

p5c <- ggplot(dca_long, aes(threshold, net_benefit, color = variable)) +
  geom_line(linewidth = 1.2) +
  scale_color_manual(values = c(
    "11-gene signature" = "#C73E1D",
    "Treat all"         = "grey40",
    "Treat none"        = "black"
  )) +
  geom_hline(yintercept = 0, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey70") +
  labs(title = "Decision curve analysis",
       subtitle = "Net benefit across thresholds",
       x = "Threshold probability", y = "Net benefit",
       color = NULL) +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) +
  theme(legend.position = "top")

print(p5c)
# --- 5d. 16 基因 vs 分期 ---
ss <- readRDS("results/sig16_vs_stage.rds")
df_ss <- ss$df

p5d <- ggplot(df_ss, aes(factor(stage), sig_score, fill = factor(stage))) +
  geom_boxplot(alpha = 0.85, outlier.shape = NA) +
  geom_jitter(width = 0.2, size = 0.4, alpha = 0.3) +
  scale_fill_brewer(palette = "OrRd") +
  labs(title = "16-gene signature vs fibrosis stage",
       subtitle = sprintf("Spearman rho = %.3f, p = %.2f (n.s.)",
                          ss$rho, ss$p),
       x = "Fibrosis stage", y = "Signature score") +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) +
  theme(legend.position = "none")

fig5 <- (p5a | p5b) / (p5c | p5d) + plot_annotation(tag_levels = "a")
ggsave("figures/Figure5.pdf", fig5, width = 12, height = 10)
ggsave("figures/Figure5.png", fig5, width = 12, height = 10, dpi = 300)
cat("Figure5 saved\n")

#####Fig6：药物预测####
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(tidyverse); library(ggplot2); library(patchwork)
})

drugs <- read.csv("results/DGIdb_signature_drugs.csv")

target_drugs <- c("TRETINOIN", "BEXAROTENE", "NINTEDANIB ESYLATE",
                  "PEMIGATINIB", "ERDAFITINIB", "ALITRETINOIN")

plot_df <- drugs %>%
  dplyr::filter(approved, drug %in% target_drugs) %>%
  dplyr::mutate(Class = case_when(
    grepl("RETIN|BEXAR|ALITRET", drug) ~ "RXR/retinoid agonist",
    grepl("NINTED|PEMIG|ERDAFIT", drug) ~ "FGFR inhibitor",
    TRUE ~ "Other"
  ))

p6a <- ggplot(plot_df, aes(reorder(drug, score), score, fill = Class)) +
  geom_col(alpha = 0.9) +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c(
    "RXR/retinoid agonist" = "#C73E1D",
    "FGFR inhibitor" = "#2E86AB"
  )) +
  coord_flip() +
  labs(title = "Candidate drugs targeting HGF+ HSC signature (DGIdb)",
       subtitle = "Approved compounds with pathway relevance",
       x = NULL, y = "DGIdb interaction score",
       fill = NULL) +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) +
  theme(legend.position = "top")

fig6 <- p6a + plot_annotation(tag_levels = "a")

# ============ Fig4a: 自分泌阳性率（重跑一次，确保数据一致）============
results <- data.frame()
for (slide in c("Slide1", "Slide2")) {
  file <- sprintf("data/GSM%s_%s_SeuratObject.RDS",
                  ifelse(slide == "Slide1", "8855042", "8855043"), slide)
  col <- ifelse(slide == "Slide1",
                "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters",
                "RNA_nbclust_ee1320c5.9f48.455c.b54e.1fbfd34347ab_1_clusters")
  obj <- readRDS(file)
  celltype_vec <- obj@meta.data[[col]]
  expr <- obj[["RNA"]]$data
  stellate <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
  hgf_pos <- stellate[as.numeric(expr["HGF", ])[stellate] > 0]
  hgf_neg <- setdiff(stellate, hgf_pos)
  
  for (g in c("LIFR", "IL6ST", "OSMR")) {
    if (!g %in% rownames(expr)) next
    x <- as.numeric(expr[g, ])
    results <- rbind(results, data.frame(
      Slide = slide, Gene = g,
      HGFpos = mean(x[hgf_pos] > 0),
      HGFneg = mean(x[hgf_neg] > 0)
    ))
  }
}

pos_long <- results %>%
  pivot_longer(c(HGFpos, HGFneg), names_to = "Group", values_to = "PosRate") %>%
  mutate(Group = ifelse(Group == "HGFpos", "HGF-expressing", "HGF-negative"))

p4a <- ggplot(pos_long, aes(Gene, PosRate, fill = Group)) +
  geom_col(position = position_dodge(0.8), alpha = 0.9) +
  facet_wrap(~ Slide) +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c("HGF-expressing" = "#C73E1D",
                               "HGF-negative" = "#2E86AB")) +
  labs(title = "IL6-family receptor positive rates",
       x = NULL, y = "Positive rate") +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) +
  theme(legend.position = "top")

# ============ Fig4b: TF 活性（TFAP2A 特殊标记）============
tf <- read.csv("results/TF_significant_final.csv")

tf_long <- tf %>%
  dplyr::select(TF, Slide1 = delta1, Slide2 = delta2) %>%
  pivot_longer(-TF, names_to = "Slide", values_to = "delta")

tf_long$TF <- factor(tf_long$TF, levels = tf$TF[order(tf$delta1)])
tf_long$is_key <- tf_long$TF == "TFAP2A"

p4b <- ggplot(tf_long, aes(delta, TF, fill = delta)) +
  geom_col(position = position_dodge(0.8), alpha = 0.9, width = 0.7) +
  scale_fill_gradient2(low = "#2E86AB", mid = "white", high = "#C73E1D",
                       midpoint = 0) +
  geom_vline(xintercept = 0, color = "grey40") +
  geom_text(data = subset(tf_long, is_key),
            aes(x = delta - 0.05 * sign(delta), y = TF, label = "★"),
            color = "gold4", size = 5, fontface = "bold") +
  labs(title = "TF activity in HGF-expressing HSCs",
       subtitle = "★ TFAP2A: only TF targeting HGF",
       x = "TF activity delta score", y = NULL) +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) +
  theme(legend.position = "none")

p4c <- ggplot(go_df, aes(Description, -log10(p.adjust), fill = -log10(p.adjust))) +
  geom_col(alpha = 0.9) +
  scale_fill_gradient(low = "#F4A582", high = "#C73E1D") +
  coord_flip() +
  labs(title = "SP3 target genes: GO BP enrichment",
       x = NULL, y = "-log10(adj.P)") +

suppressPackageStartupMessages({

data("dorothea_hs", package = "dorothea")

# ============ 1. TFAP2A 靶基因 + mor ============
tfap2a_reg <- dorothea_hs %>%
  dplyr::filter(tf == "TFAP2A", confidence %in% c("A","B","C"))
tfap2a_targets <- tfap2a_reg$target

cat(sprintf("TFAP2A targets: %d\n", length(tfap2a_targets)))
cat(sprintf("TFAP2A mor (mode of regulation): %s\n",
            paste(unique(tfap2a_reg$mor), collapse = ", ")))

# ============ 2. HGF-low 特征基因 ============
# down_genes 是 HGF+ 里 DOWN 的 = HGF-low 里相对高的
down_genes <- read.csv("results/signature_HGFpos_down.csv")$gene
cat(sprintf("\nHGF-low 特征基因 (HGF+ 里下调): %d\n", length(down_genes)))

# ============ 3. 交集 ============
overlap <- intersect(tfap2a_targets, down_genes)
cat(sprintf("\nTFAP2A targets ∩ HGF-low 特征: %d\n", length(overlap)))
if (length(overlap) > 0) print(overlap)

# ============ 4. Fisher 富集检验 ============
# 用 HSC 表达的基因作为背景
cosmx1 <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
col1 <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx1@meta.data[[col1]]
expr <- cosmx1[["RNA"]]$data
stellate <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
hsc_expr <- as.matrix(expr[, stellate])
hsc_expr <- hsc_expr[rowSums(hsc_expr > 0) > ncol(hsc_expr) * 0.1, ]
hsc_genes <- rownames(hsc_expr)
cat(sprintf("\nHSC panel 基因: %d\n", length(hsc_genes)))

# 限制在 HSC 背景里
a <- length(intersect(overlap, hsc_genes))
b <- length(intersect(tfap2a_targets, hsc_genes)) - a
c <- length(intersect(down_genes, hsc_genes)) - a
d <- length(hsc_genes) - a - b - c

ft <- fisher.test(matrix(c(a, b, c, d), nrow = 2))
cat(sprintf("\n=== Fisher 富集检验 ===\n"))
cat(sprintf("2x2 表: a=%d, b=%d, c=%d, d=%d\n", a, b, c, d))
cat(sprintf("OR = %.3f, p = %.4e\n", ft$estimate, ft$p.value))

# ============ 5. 富集分析（TFAP2A targets 交集基因的 GO）============
if (length(overlap) >= 5) {
  gene_ids <- bitr(overlap, fromType = "SYMBOL", toType = "ENTREZID",
                   OrgDb = org.Hs.eg.db)
  if (nrow(gene_ids) >= 5) {
    ego <- enrichGO(gene = gene_ids$ENTREZID, OrgDb = org.Hs.eg.db,
                    ont = "BP", pAdjustMethod = "BH",
                    pvalueCutoff = 0.1, readable = TRUE)
    cat("\n=== 交集基因 GO BP ===\n")
    if (!is.null(ego)) print(head(as.data.frame(ego)[, c("Description","GeneRatio","p.adjust")], 10))
  }
}

# ============ 6. 保存 ============
saveRDS(list(
  tfap2a_targets = tfap2a_targets,
  down_genes = down_genes,
  overlap = overlap,
  fisher_p = ft$p.value,
  fisher_or = ft$estimate
), "results/TFAP2A_HGF_low_overlap.rds")

# ============ 修复 2: Fig 2b 去硬编码（从数据重算） ============
# 从之前的 Reverse_FOV_characterization 重算
fov <- readRDS("results/Reverse_FOV_characterization.rds")
n_cons <- nrow(fov$consistent_fovs)
n_rev  <- nrow(fov$reverse_fovs)

fov_df <- data.frame(
  Direction = c("HGF-expressing closer to Hep.4", "Other"),
  N = c(n_cons, n_rev)
)
fov_df$Pct <- fov_df$N / sum(fov_df$N) * 100

p2b <- ggplot(fov_df, aes(Direction, N, fill = Direction)) +
  geom_col(alpha = 0.9) +
  geom_text(aes(label = sprintf("%d (%.0f%%)", N, Pct)),
            vjust = -0.5, size = 4) +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c("HGF-expressing closer to Hep.4" = "#C73E1D",
                               "Other" = "grey70")) +
  labs(title = "FOV-level direction consistency",
       subtitle = sprintf("Binomial p = %.2e, n = %d FOVs",
                          fov$binom_p, n_cons + n_rev),
       x = NULL, y = "Number of FOVs") +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) +
  theme(legend.position = "none") +
  coord_cartesian(ylim = c(0, n_cons + n_rev + 5))

ggsave("figures/Fig2b_FOV_consistency_fixed.pdf", p2b, width = 5, height = 4)

# ============================================================
# Figure 1: CosMx 概览 + Stellate 纤维化区富集
# ============================================================
cosmx1 <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
col1 <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec1 <- cosmx1@meta.data[[col1]]
coords1 <- cosmx1@meta.data[, c("x_slide_mm","y_slide_mm","fov")]

# 1a. 细胞类型组成
ct_df <- data.frame(
  CellType = names(table(celltype_vec1)),
  N = as.numeric(table(celltype_vec1))
) %>% mutate(Pct = N/sum(N)*100) %>% arrange(desc(N)) %>% head(15)

p1a <- ggplot(ct_df, aes(reorder(CellType, N), N, fill = CellType)) +
  geom_col(alpha = 0.85) + coord_flip() +
  labs(title = "Cell type composition", x = NULL, y = "Cell count") +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) + theme(legend.position = "none")

# 1b. Stellate 空间分布
plot_df <- coords1
plot_df$is_stellate <- celltype_vec1 == "Stellate.cells"

p1b <- ggplot() +

)

       subtitle = sprintf("Binomial p = %.2e, n = %d FOVs",

  theme(legend.position = "none") +

roc_train <- readRDS("results/Module1_ROC_results.rds")
roc_df_train <- data.frame(
  FPR = 1 - roc_train$roc_cv$specificities,
  TPR = roc_train$roc_cv$sensitivities
)
p5a <- ggplot(roc_df_train, aes(FPR, TPR)) +
  geom_line(color = "#C73E1D", linewidth = 1.2) +
  geom_abline(intercept = 1, slope = 1, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey60") +
  annotate("text", x = 0.65, y = 0.15,
           label = sprintf("CV AUC = %.3f", roc_train$auc_cv),
           size = 4, color = "#C73E1D") +
  labs(title = "Training ROC (GSE135251)",
       subtitle = "11-gene signature, 10-fold CV",
       x = "1 - Specificity", y = "Sensitivity") +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) + coord_fixed()

val <- readRDS("results/GSE49541_validation.rds")
roc_df_val <- data.frame(
  FPR = 1 - val$roc$specificities,
  TPR = val$roc$sensitivities
)
ci_val <- pROC::ci.auc(val$roc)
p5b <- ggplot(roc_df_val, aes(FPR, TPR)) +
  geom_line(color = "#C73E1D", linewidth = 1.2) +
  geom_abline(intercept = 1, slope = 1, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey60") +
  annotate("text", x = 0.65, y = 0.15,
           label = sprintf("AUC = %.3f\n95%% CI: %.3f-%.3f",
                           val$auc, ci_val[1], ci_val[3]),
           size = 3.5, color = "#C73E1D") +
  labs(title = "External validation ROC (GSE49541)",
       subtitle = "11-gene signature, n=72",
       x = "1 - Specificity", y = "Sensitivity") +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) + coord_fixed()

# DCA 重算
sig_score <- colMeans(t(scale(t(val$expr_gene))))
group <- val$group
df_dca <- data.frame(outcome = group, signature = sig_score)
df_dca <- df_dca[!is.na(df_dca$outcome) & !is.na(df_dca$signature), ]
df_dca$pred_prob <- predict(glm(outcome ~ signature, data = df_dca,
                                family = binomial), type = "response")
dca_obj <- dcurves::dca(outcome ~ pred_prob, data = df_dca)
dca_df <- dca_obj$dca

dca_long <- dca_df %>%
  dplyr::select(threshold, variable, net_benefit) %>%
  dplyr::filter(variable %in% c("pred_prob", "all", "none")) %>%
  dplyr::mutate(variable = recode(variable,
                                  "pred_prob" = "11-gene signature",
                                  "all" = "Treat all",
                                  "none" = "Treat none"))

p5c <- ggplot(dca_long, aes(threshold, net_benefit, color = variable)) +
  geom_line(linewidth = 1.2) +
  scale_color_manual(values = c("11-gene signature" = "#C73E1D",
                                "Treat all" = "grey40",
                                "Treat none" = "black")) +
  geom_hline(yintercept = 0, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey70") +
  labs(title = "Decision curve analysis",
       x = "Threshold probability", y = "Net benefit", color = NULL) +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) + theme(legend.position = "top")

# 三个 panel（5d 移除）
fig5 <- (p5a | p5b | p5c) + plot_annotation(tag_levels = "a")
ggsave("figures/final/Figure5.pdf", fig5, width = 15, height = 5)
ggsave("figures/final/Figure5.png", fig5, width = 15, height = 5, dpi = 300)
cat("✅ Figure5 saved\n")

###补充分析 2026.10.6####
#=====距离分布直方图=======
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({library(Seurat); library(tidyverse); library(ggplot2)})

cosmx1 <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
col1 <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx1@meta.data[[col1]]
expr <- cosmx1[["RNA"]]$data
coords <- cosmx1@meta.data[, c("x_slide_mm","y_slide_mm","fov")]

stellate <- which(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells")
hgf_expr <- as.numeric(expr["HGF", ])
hgf_pos <- stellate[hgf_expr[stellate] > 0]
hgf_neg <- setdiff(stellate, hgf_pos)
hep4_idx <- which(celltype_vec == "Hep.4")

compute_nn <- function(src, tgt) {
  out <- rep(NA_real_, length(src))
  for (f in unique(coords$fov)) {
    s <- which(coords$fov[src] == f)
    t <- which(coords$fov[tgt] == f)
    if (length(s) == 0 || length(t) == 0) next
    sc <- coords[src[s], ]; tc <- coords[tgt[t], ]
    for (i in seq_len(nrow(sc))) {
      d <- sqrt((tc$x_slide_mm - sc$x_slide_mm[i])^2 +
                  (tc$y_slide_mm - sc$y_slide_mm[i])^2)
      out[s[i]] <- min(d) * 1000
    }
  }
  out
}

d_pos <- compute_nn(hgf_pos, hep4_idx)
d_neg <- compute_nn(hgf_neg, hep4_idx)

df <- rbind(
  data.frame(Group = "HGF-expressing", Dist = d_pos),
  data.frame(Group = "HGF-low",        Dist = d_neg)
)
df <- df[!is.na(df$Dist), ]

# KS 检验
ks <- ks.test(d_pos[!is.na(d_pos)], d_neg[!is.na(d_neg)])
cat(sprintf("KS test: D = %.3f, p = %.2e\n", ks$statistic, ks$p.value))
cat(sprintf("Median HGF+: %.1f µm | HGF-: %.1f µm\n",
            median(d_pos, na.rm=TRUE), median(d_neg, na.rm=TRUE)))

p_dist <- ggplot(df, aes(Dist, fill = Group)) +
  geom_histogram(position = "identity", alpha = 0.6, bins = 40,
                 color = "white") +
  geom_vline(xintercept = median(d_pos, na.rm=TRUE),
             color = "#C73E1D", linetype = "dashed", linewidth = 1) +
  geom_vline(xintercept = median(d_neg, na.rm=TRUE),
             color = "#2E86AB", linetype = "dashed", linewidth = 1) +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c("HGF-expressing" = "#C73E1D",
                               "HGF-low" = "#2E86AB")) +
  coord_cartesian(xlim = c(0, 200)) +
  labs(title = "Distance from HSC to nearest Hep.4",
       subtitle = sprintf("KS p = %.2e | Median: %.1f vs %.1f µm",
                          ks$p.value,
                          median(d_pos, na.rm=TRUE),
                          median(d_neg, na.rm=TRUE)),
       x = "Distance (µm)", y = "Cell count") +
  theme_classic(base_size = 11)

ggsave("figures/final/Fig2f_distance_distribution.pdf", p_dist, width = 7, height = 4)
print(p_dist)

sc_obj <- readRDS("results/GSE202379_SeuratObject_AllCells.rds")
cat(sprintf("Cells: %d | Genes: %d\n", ncol(sc_obj), nrow(sc_obj)))

# 只保留需要的信息，删掉原始 counts
DefaultAssay(sc_obj) <- "RNA"
sc_obj[["RNA"]]@counts <- new("dgCMatrix")
gc()

# Signature score
sig16 <- read.csv("results/signature_HGFpos_up.csv")$gene
sig_in <- sig16[sig16 %in% rownames(sc_obj)]
cat(sprintf("Signature: %d / %d\n", length(sig_in), length(sig16)))

sc_obj <- AddModuleScore(sc_obj, features = list(sig_in),
                         name = "HGF_HSC_sig", nbin = 24)

# Stellate marker score
stellate_markers <- c("PDGFRB","COL1A1","COL1A2","ACTA2","DCN","LRAT")
stellate_markers <- stellate_markers[stellate_markers %in% rownames(sc_obj)]

                         name = "Stellate_sig", nbin = 24)

# ====Endothelial vs Stellate 2026.10.6=====
endo <- which(sc_obj$cell.annotation == "Endothelial")
stellate <- which(sc_obj$cell.annotation == "Stellate")

cat(sprintf("\nEndothelial: %d | Stellate: %d\n", length(endo), length(stellate)))
cat(sprintf("\nHGF-HSC sig in Endothelial: %.3f\n", mean(sc_obj$HGF_HSC_sig1[endo])))
cat(sprintf("HGF-HSC sig in Stellate:    %.3f\n", mean(sc_obj$HGF_HSC_sig1[stellate])))
cat(sprintf("\nStellate marker in Endothelial: %.3f\n", mean(sc_obj$Stellate_sig1[endo])))
cat(sprintf("Stellate marker in Stellate:    %.3f\n", mean(sc_obj$Stellate_sig1[stellate])))

cohens_d <- function(x, y) {
  (mean(x) - mean(y)) / sqrt((var(x) + var(y)) / 2)
}
cat(sprintf("\nCohen's d (HGF-HSC sig, Stellate vs Endo): %.2f\n",
            cohens_d(sc_obj$HGF_HSC_sig1[stellate], sc_obj$HGF_HSC_sig1[endo])))
cat(sprintf("Cohen's d (Stellate marker, Stellate vs Endo): %.2f\n",
            cohens_d(sc_obj$Stellate_sig1[stellate], sc_obj$Stellate_sig1[endo])))

#====校准曲线 2026.10.6====
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({library(tidyverse); library(pROC)})

val <- readRDS("results/GSE49541_validation.rds")
sig_score <- colMeans(t(scale(t(val$expr_gene))))
group <- val$group

df <- data.frame(outcome = group, signature = sig_score)
df <- df[!is.na(df$outcome) & !is.na(df$signature), ]

df$pred_prob <- predict(glm(outcome ~ signature, data = df,
                            family = binomial), type = "response")

# 分箱
df$bin <- cut(df$pred_prob, breaks = seq(0, 1, 0.1), include.lowest = TRUE)

cal_df <- df %>%
  group_by(bin) %>%
  summarise(mean_pred = mean(pred_prob),
            obs_rate = mean(outcome),
            n = n(),
            .groups = "drop") %>%
  filter(n >= 2)

p_cal <- ggplot(cal_df, aes(mean_pred, obs_rate)) +
  geom_abline(intercept = 0, slope = 1, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey60") +
  geom_point(aes(size = n), color = "#C73E1D", alpha = 0.8) +
  geom_line(color = "#C73E1D") +
  labs(title = "Calibration plot: 11-gene signature (GSE49541)",
       subtitle = sprintf("Brier score = %.3f",
                          mean((df$pred_prob - df$outcome)^2)),
       x = "Predicted probability", y = "Observed rate", size = "N") +
  coord_fixed(xlim = c(0, 1), ylim = c(0, 1)) +
  theme_classic(base_size = 11)

ggsave("figures/final/Fig5d_calibration.pdf", p_cal, width = 5, height = 5)
print(p_cal)

brier <- mean((df$pred_prob - df$outcome)^2)
cat(sprintf("Brier score: %.3f (lower is better; 0.25 = baseline)\n", brier))
cat(sprintf("N = %d, events = %d\n", nrow(df), sum(df$outcome)))

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")

  library(tidyverse); library(clusterProfiler); library(org.Hs.eg.db)

sig16 <- read.csv("results/signature_HGFpos_up.csv")$gene

                 OrgDb = org.Hs.eg.db)

                    ont = "BP", pAdjustMethod = "BH",
                    pvalueCutoff = 0.5, readable = TRUE)

ego_df <- as.data.frame(ego_all)

# 筛选 ECM / collagen / adhesion 相关
ecm_keywords <- c("extracellular matrix", "collagen", "cell adhesion",
                  "cell-substrate", "integrin", "basement membrane",
                  "connective tissue", "wound healing")

ego_ecm <- ego_df %>%
  dplyr::filter(grepl(paste(ecm_keywords, collapse = "|"),
                      Description, ignore.case = TRUE)) %>%
  arrange(p.adjust) %>%
  head(10)

cat("\n=== ECM-related GO terms ===\n")
print(ego_ecm[, c("Description","GeneRatio","p.adjust")])

if (nrow(ego_ecm) > 0) {
  plot_df <- ego_ecm
  plot_df$Description <- reorder(plot_df$Description, -log10(plot_df$p.adjust))
  
  p3c <- ggplot(plot_df, aes(Description, -log10(p.adjust),
                             fill = -log10(p.adjust))) +
    geom_col(alpha = 0.9) +
    scale_fill_gradient(low = "#F4A582", high = "#C73E1D") +
    coord_flip() +
    labs(title = "GO BP enrichment: ECM-related terms (16-gene signature)",
         subtitle = "Filtered for ECM/collagen/adhesion terms",
         x = NULL, y = "-log10(adj.P)") +
    theme_classic(base_size = 10) +
    theme(legend.position = "none",
          axis.text.y = element_text(size = 8))
  
  ggsave("figures/final/Fig3c_ECM_GO.pdf", p3c, width = 7, height = 4)
  print(p3c)
  write.csv(ego_ecm, "results/Fig3c_ECM_GO.csv", row.names = FALSE)
}

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({library(tidyverse)})

file <- "data/GSE84044/GSE84044_series_matrix.txt.gz"
con <- gzfile(file, "r")
lines <- readLines(con)
close(con)

cat(sprintf("总行数: %d\n", length(lines)))

# 找表格边界
start <- grep("^!series_matrix_table_begin", lines)
end   <- grep("^!series_matrix_table_end", lines)
cat(sprintf("表格起止行: %d, %d\n", start, end))

if (length(start) == 0 || length(end) == 0) {
  cat("!! 找不到表格边界，可能格式不标准\n")
  cat("前 50 行预览:\n")
  cat(lines[1:min(50, length(lines))], sep = "\n")
} else {
  # 提取表达矩阵
  expr_lines <- lines[(start + 1):(end - 1)]
  expr_raw <- read.delim(text = paste(expr_lines, collapse = "\n"),
                         check.names = FALSE, stringsAsFactors = FALSE)
  cat(sprintf("矩阵: %d 行 x %d 列\n", nrow(expr_raw), ncol(expr_raw)))
  cat("第一列前 5 个:", paste(head(expr_raw[[1]], 5), collapse = ", "), "\n")
  
  # 提取临床信息
  ch_lines <- grep("^!Sample_characteristics_ch1", lines, value = TRUE)
  cat(sprintf("\ncharacteristics 行数: %d\n", length(ch_lines)))
  
  for (l in ch_lines) {
    field <- sub("\t.*", "", l)
    vals <- strsplit(l, "\t")[[1]][-1]
    cat(sprintf("\n[%s]: %d 个值\n", field, length(vals)))
    cat(sprintf("  示例: %s\n", paste(head(vals, 5), collapse = " | ")))
    print(table(vals, useNA = "ifany"))
  }
  
  # 提取平台
  plat_line <- grep("^!Series_platform_id", lines, value = TRUE)
  cat(sprintf("\n平台: %s\n", plat_line))
}

dir.create("data/GSE84044/raw", recursive = TRUE, showWarnings = FALSE)

url <- "https://ftp.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/geo/series/GSE84nnn/GSE84044/suppl/GSE84044_RAW.tar"
dest <- "data/GSE84044/raw/GSE84044_RAW.tar"

# 检查是否已下过
if (file.exists(dest) && file.size(dest) > 10 * 1024^2) {
  cat(sprintf("已存在: %.1f MB\n", file.size(dest) / 1024^2))
} else {
  cat("下载中...（40 MB，5-15 分钟）\n")
  options(timeout = 1800)
  download.file(url, dest, mode = "wb", method = "libcurl",
                quiet = FALSE)
  cat(sprintf("下载完成: %.1f MB\n", file.size(dest) / 1024^2))
}


##====统一11基因在两个队列的打分方式====####
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(tidyverse); library(pROC)
})

# 11 基因 signature
ml <- readRDS("results/Module1_ML_results.rds")
core_genes <- ml$core_genes
cat(sprintf("11 基因: %s\n", paste(core_genes, collapse = ", ")))

dir.create("results/multi_etiology", showWarnings = FALSE)

# ============ 队列 1：GSE135251（MASLD 训练）============
data1 <- readRDS("results/GSE135251_DEG_results.rds")
log_expr1 <- data1$log_expr
stage1 <- data1$fibro_stage

# Ensembl -> Symbol
suppressPackageStartupMessages(library(org.Hs.eg.db))
sym1 <- mapIds(org.Hs.eg.db, keys = rownames(log_expr1),
               column = "SYMBOL", keytype = "ENSEMBL", multiVals = "first")
valid1 <- !is.na(sym1) & !duplicated(sym1)
log_expr1_sym <- log_expr1[valid1, ]
rownames(log_expr1_sym) <- sym1[valid1]

genes_in_1 <- intersect(core_genes, rownames(log_expr1_sym))
cat(sprintf("GSE135251: %d/11 基因\n", length(genes_in_1)))

# z-score 打分
mat_z1 <- t(scale(t(log_expr1_sym[genes_in_1, , drop = FALSE])))
mat_z1[is.na(mat_z1)] <- 0
score1 <- colMeans(mat_z1)

# 二分类
group1 <- as.numeric(stage1 >= 2)
roc1 <- roc(group1, score1, quiet = TRUE)
cat(sprintf("GSE135251 AUC: %.3f\n", auc(roc1)))

# ============ 队列 2：GSE49541（NASH 验证）============
val2 <- readRDS("results/GSE49541_validation.rds")
expr2 <- val2$expr_gene
group2 <- val2$group

genes_in_2 <- intersect(core_genes, rownames(expr2))
cat(sprintf("\nGSE49541: %d/11 基因\n", length(genes_in_2)))

mat_z2 <- t(scale(t(expr2[genes_in_2, , drop = FALSE])))
mat_z2[is.na(mat_z2)] <- 0
score2 <- colMeans(mat_z2)

roc2 <- roc(group2[!is.na(group2)], score2[!is.na(group2)], quiet = TRUE)
cat(sprintf("GSE49541 AUC: %.3f\n", auc(roc2)))

# ============ 保存 ============
saveRDS(list(
  GSE135251 = list(score = score1, group = group1, roc = roc1,
                   auc = as.numeric(auc(roc1)), n = length(group1),
                   etiology = "MASLD"),
  GSE49541  = list(score = score2, group = group2, roc = roc2,
                   auc = as.numeric(auc(roc2)), n = sum(!is.na(group2)),
                   etiology = "NASH")
), "results/multi_etiology/two_cohorts.rds")

#####核实：两个数字的来源必须查清
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")

# ============ 1. 原始 Module1 结果 ============
roc1 <- readRDS("results/Module1_ROC_results.rds")
cat("=== Module1_ROC_results.rds ===\n")
cat(sprintf("auc_full: %.4f\n", roc1$auc_full))
cat(sprintf("auc_cv:   %.4f\n", roc1$auc_cv))
cat(sprintf("lr_model 系数数: %d\n", length(coef(roc1$lr_model))))

# ============ 2. 多队列框架重跑结果 ============
d1 <- readRDS("results/GSE135251_DEG_results.rds")
log_expr1 <- d1$log_expr
stage1 <- d1$fibro_stage

suppressPackageStartupMessages(library(org.Hs.eg.db))
sym1 <- mapIds(org.Hs.eg.db, keys = rownames(log_expr1),
               column = "SYMBOL", keytype = "ENSEMBL", multiVals = "first")
valid1 <- !is.na(sym1) & !duplicated(sym1)
log_expr1_sym <- log_expr1[valid1, ]
rownames(log_expr1_sym) <- sym1[valid1]

ml <- readRDS("results/Module1_ML_results.rds")
core_genes <- ml$core_genes

# 方法 A: z-score 平均
mat_z <- t(scale(t(log_expr1_sym[core_genes, , drop = FALSE])))
mat_z[is.na(mat_z)] <- 0
score_z <- colMeans(mat_z)
roc_z <- pROC::roc(as.numeric(stage1 >= 2), score_z, quiet = TRUE)
cat("\n=== z-score 平均（无 CV）===\n")
cat(sprintf("AUC: %.4f\n", as.numeric(pROC::auc(roc_z))))

# 方法 B: logistic 回归（无 CV）
df <- data.frame(t(log_expr1_sym[core_genes, ]), y = as.numeric(stage1 >= 2))
lr <- glm(y ~ ., data = df, family = binomial)
pred_lr <- predict(lr, type = "response")
roc_lr <- pROC::roc(df$y, pred_lr, quiet = TRUE)
cat("\n=== logistic 回归（无 CV）===\n")
cat(sprintf("AUC: %.4f\n", as.numeric(pROC::auc(roc_lr))))




###数字朔源表###
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({library(tidyverse); library(pROC); library(dcurves)})

# ============ 1. 加载所有源数据 ============
roc1 <- readRDS("results/Module1_ROC_results.rds")
val  <- readRDS("results/GSE49541_validation.rds")
ci   <- pROC::ci.auc(val$roc)

# DCA
sig_score <- colMeans(t(scale(t(val$expr_gene))))
df_dca <- data.frame(outcome = val$group, signature = sig_score)
df_dca <- df_dca[!is.na(df_dca$outcome) & !is.na(df_dca$signature), ]
df_dca$pred_prob <- predict(glm(outcome ~ signature, data = df_dca,
                                family = binomial), type = "response")
dca_obj <- dcurves::dca(outcome ~ pred_prob, data = df_dca)
dca_df  <- dca_obj$dca
sel     <- dca_df[dca_df$variable == "pred_prob" &
                    dca_df$threshold %in% c(0.2, 0.3, 0.4), ]

# Brier
brier <- mean((df_dca$pred_prob - df_dca$outcome)^2)

# ============ 2. 建溯源表 ============
digits <- data.frame(
  Item = c(
    "Training CV AUC",
    "Training full AUC (overfit, NOT reported)",
    "Validation AUC",
    "Validation 95% CI low",
    "Validation 95% CI high",
    "Brier score",
    "DCA net benefit @ 20%",
    "DCA net benefit @ 30%",
    "DCA net benefit @ 40%",
    "Validation N (events)"
  ),
  Value = c(
    sprintf("%.3f", roc1$auc_cv),
    sprintf("%.3f", roc1$auc_full),
    sprintf("%.3f", val$auc),
    sprintf("%.3f", ci[1]),
    sprintf("%.3f", ci[3]),
    sprintf("%.3f", brier),
    sprintf("%.3f", sel$net_benefit[sel$threshold == 0.2]),
    sprintf("%.3f", sel$net_benefit[sel$threshold == 0.3]),
    sprintf("%.3f", sel$net_benefit[sel$threshold == 0.4]),
    sprintf("%d (%d)", nrow(df_dca), sum(df_dca$outcome))
  ),
  Source = c(
    "results/Module1_ROC_results.rds",
    "results/Module1_ROC_results.rds",
    "results/GSE49541_validation.rds",
    "results/GSE49541_validation.rds",
    "results/GSE49541_validation.rds",
    "computed from GSE49541_validation.rds",
    "recomputed via dcurves",
    "recomputed via dcurves",
    "recomputed via dcurves",
    "results/GSE49541_validation.rds"
  ),
  Method = c(
    "logistic regression, 10-fold CV",
    "logistic regression, full-data",
    "external validation",
    "95% CI, DeLong",
    "95% CI, DeLong",
    "calibration",
    "dcurves::dca",
    "dcurves::dca",
    "dcurves::dca",
    "external validation"
  ),
  stringsAsFactors = FALSE
)

print(digits)
write.csv(digits, "results/digits_source_table.csv", row.names = FALSE)
cat("\n✅ Saved: results/digits_source_table.csv\n")


####补充验证：训练/验证模型一致性问题####
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({library(tidyverse); library(pROC)})

# ============ 1. 加载训练好的模型 ============
roc_res <- readRDS("results/Module1_ROC_results.rds")
lr_model <- roc_res$lr_model
coef_vec <- coef(lr_model)

cat("模型系数:\n")
print(coef_vec)

core_genes <- names(coef_vec)[-1]
cat(sprintf("\n模型基因 (%d): %s\n", length(core_genes),
            paste(core_genes, collapse = ", ")))

# ============ 2. 加载验证数据 ============
val <- readRDS("results/GSE49541_validation.rds")
expr_val <- val$expr_gene
group_val <- val$group

# 对齐基因顺序
expr_val <- expr_val[core_genes, ]
cat(sprintf("\n验证矩阵: %d x %d\n", nrow(expr_val), ncol(expr_val)))

# ============ 3. 对验证队列做 z-score（跨平台必需）============
expr_z <- t(scale(t(expr_val)))
expr_z[is.na(expr_z)] <- 0

# ============ 4. 用 lr_model 预测 ============
df_val <- as.data.frame(t(expr_z))
colnames(df_val) <- core_genes

pred_prob <- predict(lr_model, newdata = df_val, type = "response")

# ============ 5. AUC ============
keep <- !is.na(group_val)
roc_new <- roc(group_val[keep], pred_prob[keep], quiet = TRUE)
auc_new <- as.numeric(auc(roc_new))
ci_new  <- ci.auc(roc_new)

cat(sprintf("\n=== 统一模型的外部验证 ===\n"))
cat(sprintf("AUC = %.3f (95%% CI: %.3f-%.3f)\n",
            auc_new, ci_new[1], ci_new[3]))
cat(sprintf("N = %d (events = %d)\n",
            sum(keep), sum(group_val[keep] == 1)))

# ============ 6. 与旧版（z-score 平均）对比 ============
sig_score_avg <- colMeans(expr_z)
roc_old <- roc(group_val[keep], sig_score_avg[keep], quiet = TRUE)
cat(sprintf("\n旧版（z-score 平均）AUC: %.3f\n",
            as.numeric(auc(roc_old))))

# ============ 7. 保存 ============
saveRDS(list(
  roc = roc_new,
  auc = auc_new,
  ci = ci_new,
  pred_prob = pred_prob,
  expr_z = expr_z,
  group = group_val,
  method = "logistic model on z-scored features"
), "results/GSE49541_validation_unified.rds")

cat("\n✅ Saved: results/GSE49541_validation_unified.rds\n")

# 加载统一模型的预测结果

df <- data.frame(outcome = group[keep], pred_prob = pred_prob[keep])

# ============ Brier ============

cat(sprintf("Brier score: %.3f\n", brier))

dca_df  <- dca_obj$dca
sel     <- dca_df[dca_df$variable == "pred_prob" &
                    dca_df$threshold %in% c(0.2, 0.3, 0.4), ]
cat("\n=== DCA net benefit ===\n")

    sprintf("%.3f", unif$ci[1]),

    sprintf("%.3f", sel$net_benefit[sel$threshold == 0.3]),

    sprintf("%d (%d)", sum(keep), sum(df$outcome))
  ),
  Source = c(
    "results/Module1_ROC_results.rds",

    "results/GSE49541_validation_unified.rds",

    "recomputed via dcurves",
    "recomputed via dcurves",
    "recomputed via dcurves",

####====重新用GSE135251生产CV预测概率并保存====
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({library(tidyverse); library(caret); library(pROC)})

data <- readRDS("results/GSE135251_DEG_results.rds")
ml   <- readRDS("results/Module1_ML_results.rds")
core_genes <- ml$core_genes
X <- ml$X
y <- ml$y

# ---- 10-fold CV 逐样本预测 ----
set.seed(123)
folds <- createFolds(y, k = 10, list = TRUE)
cv_pred <- numeric(length(y))
for (i in seq_along(folds)) {
  test_idx  <- folds[[i]]
  train_idx <- setdiff(seq_along(y), test_idx)
  df_tr <- data.frame(X[train_idx, core_genes, drop = FALSE], y = y[train_idx])
  m <- glm(y ~ ., data = df_tr, family = binomial)
  df_te <- data.frame(X[test_idx, core_genes, drop = FALSE])
  cv_pred[test_idx] <- predict(m, newdata = df_te, type = "response")
}

saveRDS(list(cv_pred = cv_pred, y = y, core_genes = core_genes),
        "results/Module1_train_cv_pred.rds")
cat("Saved: results/Module1_train_cv_pred.rds\n")

###=====Platt校准+重新计算Brier/DCA======
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({library(tidyverse); library(dcurves); library(pROC)})

# ---- 训练集 CV 概率 ----
tr <- readRDS("results/Module1_train_cv_pred.rds")
train_df <- data.frame(
  outcome   = as.integer(tr$y),
  pred_prob = as.numeric(tr$cv_pred)
)
train_df$logit_p <- qlogis(pmin(pmax(train_df$pred_prob, 1e-10), 1 - 1e-10))
cal_model <- glm(outcome ~ logit_p, family = binomial, data = train_df)
cat("Platt 校准模型系数:\n"); print(coef(cal_model))

# ---- 验证集原始概率（未校准） ----
unif <- readRDS("results/GSE49541_validation_unified.rds")
df <- data.frame(
  outcome   = as.integer(unif$group),
  pred_prob = as.numeric(unif$pred_prob)
)
df$logit_p <- qlogis(pmin(pmax(df$pred_prob, 1e-10), 1 - 1e-10))
df$pred_prob_cal <- predict(cal_model, newdata = df, type = "response")

cat("\n验证集校准后概率范围:",
    sprintf("%.3f - %.3f", min(df$pred_prob_cal), max(df$pred_prob_cal)), "\n")
print(table(df$pred_prob_cal >= 0.2, df$outcome))
print(table(df$pred_prob_cal >= 0.3, df$outcome))

# ---- Brier（校准后） ----
brier_cal <- mean((df$pred_prob_cal - df$outcome)^2)
cat(sprintf("\nBrier (calibrated): %.3f\n", brier_cal))

# ---- DCA（校准后） ----
dca_obj <- dcurves::dca(outcome ~ pred_prob_cal, data = df)
dca_df  <- if (!is.null(dca_obj$dca)) dca_obj$dca else dca_obj
sel <- dca_df %>%
  filter(variable == "pred_prob_cal",
         round(threshold, 3) %in% c(0.2, 0.3, 0.4))
cat("\n=== DCA (calibrated) ===\n"); print(sel[, c("threshold","net_benefit")])

# ---- 溯源表 ----
ci_vec <- as.numeric(unif$ci)
digits <- data.frame(
  Item = c("Training CV AUC",
           "Validation AUC (unified logistic)",
           "Validation 95% CI low",
           "Validation 95% CI high",
           "Brier (calibrated)",
           "DCA NB @ 20% (calibrated)",
           "DCA NB @ 30% (calibrated)",
           "DCA NB @ 40% (calibrated)",
           "Validation N (events)"),
  Value = c("0.841",
            sprintf("%.3f", as.numeric(unif$auc)),
            sprintf("%.3f", ci_vec[1]),
            sprintf("%.3f", ci_vec[3]),
            sprintf("%.3f", brier_cal),
            sprintf("%.3f", sel$net_benefit[sel$threshold == 0.2]),
            sprintf("%.3f", sel$net_benefit[sel$threshold == 0.3]),
            sprintf("%.3f", sel$net_benefit[sel$threshold == 0.4]),
            sprintf("%d (%d)", nrow(df), sum(df$outcome))),
  Source = c("results/Module1_ROC_results.rds",
             "results/GSE49541_validation_unified.rds",
             "results/GSE49541_validation_unified.rds",
             "results/GSE49541_validation_unified.rds",
             "Platt-calibrated from training CV",
             "dcurves (calibrated)",
             "dcurves (calibrated)",
             "dcurves (calibrated)",
             "results/GSE49541_validation_unified.rds")
)
print(digits)
write.csv(digits, "results/digits_source_table_calibrated.csv", row.names = FALSE)

# ---- 1. 训练集 CV 预测概率的分布 ----

train_df <- data.frame(
  outcome   = as.integer(tr$y),
  pred_prob = as.numeric(tr$cv_pred)
)
train_df$logit_p <- qlogis(pmin(pmax(train_df$pred_prob, 1e-10), 1 - 1e-10))

  outcome   = as.integer(unif$group),

####====修复：重新训练模型，保持训练和验证特征在同一尺度====
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(tidyverse); library(caret); library(pROC)
})

data <- readRDS("results/GSE135251_DEG_results.rds")
ml   <- readRDS("results/Module1_ML_results.rds")
core_genes <- ml$core_genes
X <- ml$X                 # 样本 x 基因，log2 表达
y <- ml$y

# ---- 1. 训练集 z-score（按基因，即按列）----
X_z <- scale(X)                                  # 每列 mean=0, sd=1
cat(sprintf("X_z 范围: %.2f - %.2f\n", min(X_z), max(X_z)))

# ---- 2. 用 z-score 后的特征重训 logistic ----
df_all <- data.frame(X_z[, core_genes, drop = FALSE], y = y)
lr_model_z <- glm(y ~ ., data = df_all, family = binomial)

cat("\n=== 新模型系数 ===\n")
print(coef(lr_model_z))

# ---- 3. 训练集内部 10-fold CV（也用 z-score）----
set.seed(123)
folds <- createFolds(y, k = 10, list = TRUE)
cv_pred <- numeric(length(y))
for (i in seq_along(folds)) {
  test_idx  <- folds[[i]]
  train_idx <- setdiff(seq_along(y), test_idx)
  df_tr <- data.frame(X_z[train_idx, core_genes, drop = FALSE], y = y[train_idx])
  m <- glm(y ~ ., data = df_tr, family = binomial)
  df_te <- data.frame(X_z[test_idx, core_genes, drop = FALSE])
  cv_pred[test_idx] <- predict(m, newdata = df_te, type = "response")
}

cat(sprintf("\nTraining CV AUC (z-scored): %.3f\n",
            as.numeric(auc(roc(y, cv_pred, quiet = TRUE)))))
cat(sprintf("Training full AUC (z-scored): %.3f\n",
            as.numeric(auc(roc(y, predict(lr_model_z, type = "response"), quiet = TRUE)))))

# ---- 4. 保存新模型 + 新 CV 预测 ----
saveRDS(list(
  lr_model = lr_model_z,
  cv_pred  = cv_pred,
  y        = y,
  core_genes = core_genes,
  X_z      = X_z
), "results/Module1_ROC_results_zscored.rds")

cat("\n✅ Saved: results/Module1_ROC_results_zscored.rds\n")
###===跑验证集===###
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(tidyverse); library(pROC); library(dcurves)
})

# ---- 1. 加载新模型 ----
m  <- readRDS("results/Module1_ROC_results_zscored.rds")
lr_model_z <- m$lr_model
core_genes <- m$core_genes

# ---- 2. 加载验证数据 ----
val <- readRDS("results/GSE49541_validation.rds")
expr_val <- val$expr_gene[core_genes, ]
group_val <- val$group

# 验证集 z-score（和训练同样的处理）
expr_z <- t(scale(t(expr_val)))
expr_z[is.na(expr_z)] <- 0

# ---- 3. 预测 ----
df_val <- as.data.frame(t(expr_z))
colnames(df_val) <- core_genes
pred_prob <- predict(lr_model_z, newdata = df_val, type = "response")

cat("=== 验证集新概率分布 ===\n")
print(summary(pred_prob))
cat(">=0.2 比例:", mean(pred_prob >= 0.2), "\n")
cat(">=0.3 比例:", mean(pred_prob >= 0.3), "\n")
cat(">=0.4 比例:", mean(pred_prob >= 0.4), "\n")

# ---- 4. AUC ----
keep <- !is.na(group_val)
roc_new <- roc(group_val[keep], pred_prob[keep], quiet = TRUE)
ci_new  <- ci.auc(roc_new)
auc_new <- as.numeric(auc(roc_new))

cat(sprintf("\n=== 验证集 AUC = %.3f (95%% CI: %.3f - %.3f) ===\n",
            auc_new, ci_new[1], ci_new[3]))
cat(sprintf("N = %d (events = %d)\n",
            sum(keep), sum(group_val[keep] == 1)))

# ---- 5. Brier ----
df <- data.frame(outcome = group_val[keep], pred_prob = pred_prob[keep])
brier <- mean((df$pred_prob - df$outcome)^2)
cat(sprintf("Brier: %.3f\n", brier))

# ---- 6. DCA ----
dca_obj <- dcurves::dca(outcome ~ pred_prob, data = df)
dca_df  <- dca_obj$dca
sel <- dca_df[dca_df$variable == "pred_prob" &
                round(dca_df$threshold, 3) %in% c(0.2, 0.3, 0.4), ]
cat("\n=== DCA net benefit ===\n")
print(sel[, c("threshold", "net_benefit")])

# ---- 7. 保存 ----
saveRDS(list(
  roc = roc_new, auc = auc_new, ci = ci_new,
  pred_prob = pred_prob, expr_z = expr_z,
  group = group_val,
  method = "logistic on z-scored features (matched scale)"
), "results/GSE49541_validation_unified_zscored.rds")

cat("\n✅ Saved: results/GSE49541_validation_unified_zscored.rds\n")

####====Figure5重新制作====####
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(tidyverse); library(pROC); library(dcurves); library(patchwork)
})

unif <- readRDS("results/GSE49541_validation_unified_zscored.rds")
roc_obj <- unif$roc
ci <- as.numeric(unif$ci)

roc_df <- data.frame(
  FPR = 1 - roc_obj$specificities,
  TPR = roc_obj$sensitivities
)

p5b <- ggplot(roc_df, aes(FPR, TPR)) +
  geom_line(color = "#C73E1D", linewidth = 1.2) +
  geom_abline(intercept = 1, slope = 1, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey60") +
  annotate("text", x = 0.65, y = 0.15,
           label = sprintf("AUC = %.3f\n95%% CI: %.3f-%.3f",
                           unif$auc, ci[1], ci[3]),
           size = 3.5, color = "#C73E1D") +
  labs(title = "External validation ROC (GSE49541)",
       subtitle = "11-gene signature, N = 72",
       x = "1 - Specificity", y = "Sensitivity") +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) + coord_fixed()

# DCA
df <- data.frame(outcome = unif$group, pred_prob = unif$pred_prob)
df <- df[!is.na(df$outcome), ]
dca_obj <- dcurves::dca(outcome ~ pred_prob, data = df)
dca_df <- dca_obj$dca

dca_long <- dca_df %>%
  dplyr::select(threshold, variable, net_benefit) %>%
  dplyr::filter(variable %in% c("pred_prob", "all", "none")) %>%
  dplyr::mutate(variable = recode(variable,
                                  "pred_prob" = "11-gene signature",
                                  "all"       = "Treat all",
                                  "none"      = "Treat none"))

p5c <- ggplot(dca_long, aes(threshold, net_benefit, color = variable)) +
  geom_line(linewidth = 1.2) +
  scale_color_manual(values = c("11-gene signature" = "#C73E1D",
                                "Treat all" = "grey40",
                                "Treat none" = "black")) +
  geom_hline(yintercept = 0, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey70") +
  labs(title = "Decision curve analysis",
       subtitle = "Net benefit across thresholds",
       x = "Threshold probability", y = "Net benefit", color = NULL) +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) + theme(legend.position = "top")

ggsave("figures/final/Fig5b_ROC_zscored.pdf", p5b, width = 5, height = 5)
ggsave("figures/final/Fig5c_DCA_zscored.pdf", p5c, width = 6, height = 5)
# ============================================================
# Figure 5: 11-gene signature diagnostic performance (z-scored model)
# Panel a: Training ROC (10-fold CV)
# Panel b: External validation ROC (GSE49541)
# Panel c: Decision curve analysis
# ============================================================
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(tidyverse)
  library(ggplot2)
  library(patchwork)
  library(pROC)
  library(dcurves)
})

dir.create("figures/final", showWarnings = FALSE, recursive = TRUE)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 数据准备
# ------------------------------------------------------------

# --- Panel a: 训练 CV ---
m <- readRDS("results/Module1_ROC_results_zscored.rds")
roc_cv <- pROC::roc(m$y, m$cv_pred, quiet = TRUE)
auc_cv <- as.numeric(pROC::auc(roc_cv))
ci_cv  <- as.numeric(pROC::ci.auc(roc_cv))

roc_cv_df <- data.frame(
  FPR = 1 - roc_cv$specificities,
  TPR = roc_cv$sensitivities
)

# --- Panel b: 外部验证 ---
val <- readRDS("results/GSE49541_validation_unified_zscored.rds")
roc_val <- val$roc
auc_val <- as.numeric(val$auc)
ci_val  <- as.numeric(val$ci)

roc_val_df <- data.frame(
  FPR = 1 - roc_val$specificities,
  TPR = roc_val$sensitivities
)

# --- Panel c: DCA ---
df_dca <- data.frame(
  outcome   = as.integer(val$group),
  pred_prob = as.numeric(val$pred_prob)
)
df_dca <- df_dca[!is.na(df_dca$outcome) & !is.na(df_dca$pred_prob), ]

dca_obj <- dcurves::dca(outcome ~ pred_prob, data = df_dca)
dca_df  <- dca_obj$dca

dca_long <- dca_df %>%
  dplyr::select(threshold, variable, net_benefit) %>%
  dplyr::filter(variable %in% c("pred_prob", "all", "none")) %>%
  dplyr::mutate(variable = dplyr::recode(variable,
                                         "pred_prob" = "11-gene signature",
                                         "all"       = "Treat all",
                                         "none"      = "Treat none"))

# ============================================================
# Panel a: Training ROC
# ============================================================
p5a <- ggplot(roc_cv_df, aes(FPR, TPR)) +
  geom_line(color = "#C73E1D", linewidth = 1.2) +
  geom_abline(intercept = 1, slope = 1,
              linetype = "dashed", color = "grey60") +
  annotate("text", x = 0.62, y = 0.12,
           label = sprintf("CV AUC = %.3f\n95%% CI: %.3f-%.3f",
                           auc_cv, ci_cv[1], ci_cv[3]),
           size = 3.5, color = "#C73E1D", hjust = 0) +
  labs(title = "Training performance",
       subtitle = "GSE135251, 10-fold CV",
       x = "1 - Specificity", y = "Sensitivity") +
  coord_fixed(xlim = c(0, 1), ylim = c(0, 1)) +
  theme_classic(base_size = 11) +
  theme(plot.title = element_text(size = 11, face = "bold"),
        plot.subtitle = element_text(size = 9, color = "grey30"))

# ============================================================
# Panel b: External validation ROC
# ============================================================
p5b <- ggplot(roc_val_df, aes(FPR, TPR)) +
  geom_line(color = "#C73E1D", linewidth = 1.2) +
  geom_abline(intercept = 1, slope = 1,
              linetype = "dashed", color = "grey60") +
  annotate("text", x = 0.62, y = 0.12,
           label = sprintf("AUC = %.3f\n95%% CI: %.3f-%.3f",
                           auc_val, ci_val[1], ci_val[3]),
           size = 3.5, color = "#C73E1D", hjust = 0) +
  labs(title = "External validation",
       subtitle = sprintf("GSE49541, N = %d (%d events)",
                          nrow(df_dca), sum(df_dca$outcome)),
       x = "1 - Specificity", y = "Sensitivity") +
  coord_fixed(xlim = c(0, 1), ylim = c(0, 1)) +
  theme_classic(base_size = 11) +
  theme(plot.title = element_text(size = 11, face = "bold"),
        plot.subtitle = element_text(size = 9, color = "grey30"))

# ============================================================
# Panel c: DCA
# ============================================================
p5c <- ggplot(dca_long, aes(threshold, net_benefit, color = variable)) +
  geom_line(linewidth = 1.1) +
  scale_color_manual(values = c(
    "11-gene signature" = "#C73E1D",
    "Treat all"         = "grey40",
    "Treat none"        = "black"
  )) +
  geom_hline(yintercept = 0, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey70") +
  coord_cartesian(xlim = c(0, 0.6), ylim = c(-0.05, 0.45)) +
  labs(title = "Decision curve analysis",
       subtitle = "External validation cohort",
       x = "Threshold probability",
       y = "Net benefit",
       color = NULL) +
  theme_classic(base_size = 11) +
  theme(plot.title = element_text(size = 11, face = "bold"),
        plot.subtitle = element_text(size = 9, color = "grey30"),
        legend.position = c(0.75, 0.85),
        legend.background = element_rect(fill = "white", color = NA),
        legend.key.size = unit(0.4, "cm"),
        legend.text = element_text(size = 9))

# ============================================================
# 拼接与导出
# ============================================================
fig5 <- (p5a | p5b | p5c) +
  plot_annotation(tag_levels = "a") &
  theme(plot.tag = element_text(size = 12, face = "bold"))

ggsave("figures/final/Figure5.pdf", fig5,
       width = 13, height = 4.5)
ggsave("figures/final/Figure5.png", fig5,
       width = 13, height = 4.5, dpi = 300)

cat("✅ Figure5.pdf / Figure5.png saved\n")
cat(sprintf("  Panel a: Training CV AUC = %.3f (%.3f-%.3f)\n",
            auc_cv, ci_cv[1], ci_cv[3]))
cat(sprintf("  Panel b: Validation AUC = %.3f (%.3f-%.3f), Brier = %.3f\n",
            auc_val, ci_val[1], ci_val[3],
            mean((df_dca$pred_prob - df_dca$outcome)^2)))
cat(sprintf("  Panel c: DCA @ 20/30/40%% = %.3f / %.3f / %.3f\n",
            dca_long$net_benefit[dca_long$variable == "11-gene signature" &
                                   dca_long$threshold == 0.2],
            dca_long$net_benefit[dca_long$variable == "11-gene signature" &
                                   dca_long$threshold == 0.3],
            dca_long$net_benefit[dca_long$variable == "11-gene signature" &
                                   dca_long$threshold == 0.4]))

###====重新计算Nested CV=====###
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(tidyverse); library(caret); library(pROC)
  library(glmnet); library(randomForest); library(e1071); library(xgboost)
})

ml <- readRDS("results/Module1_ML_results.rds")
X <- ml$X          # 样本 x 候选基因（全候选集，不是 11）
y <- ml$y
cat(sprintf("X: %d x %d, y: %d\n", nrow(X), ncol(X), length(y)))

# ---- 折内 SVM-RFE（简化版，节省时间：用线性核权重） ----
svm_rfe_fast <- function(X, y, n_features_target = 20) {
  X <- as.data.frame(X)
  features <- colnames(X)
  y_factor <- as.factor(y)
  while (length(features) > n_features_target) {
    fit <- svm(x = X[, features, drop = FALSE], y = y_factor, kernel = "linear")
    w <- t(fit$coefs) %*% fit$SV
    w <- abs(as.numeric(w))
    if (length(w) != length(features)) break
    names(w) <- features
    features <- features[-which.min(w)]
  }
  features
}

set.seed(123)
folds <- createFolds(y, k = 10, list = TRUE)
cv_pred_nested <- numeric(length(y))
gene_count_per_fold <- integer(length(folds))

for (i in seq_along(folds)) {
  cat(sprintf("Fold %d / %d\n", i, length(folds)))
  test_idx  <- folds[[i]]
  train_idx <- setdiff(seq_along(y), test_idx)
  
  X_tr <- X[train_idx, , drop = FALSE]
  y_tr <- y[train_idx]
  
  # 折内 z-score
  X_tr_z <- scale(X_tr)
  mu <- attr(X_tr_z, "scaled:center")
  sdv <- attr(X_tr_z, "scaled:scale")
  X_tr_z[is.na(X_tr_z)] <- 0
  
  # --- LASSO ---
  set.seed(100 + i)
  cv_l <- cv.glmnet(X_tr_z, y_tr, family = "binomial",
                    alpha = 1, nfolds = 5)
  coef_l <- coef(cv_l, s = "lambda.min")
  g_lasso <- setdiff(rownames(coef_l)[which(coef_l[, 1] != 0)], "(Intercept)")
  
  # --- RF ---
  set.seed(200 + i)
  rf <- randomForest(x = X_tr_z, y = as.factor(y_tr),
                     ntree = 500, importance = TRUE)
  imp <- importance(rf)
  g_rf <- rownames(imp)[order(imp[, "MeanDecreaseGini"],
                              decreasing = TRUE)][1:min(30, ncol(X_tr_z))]
  
  # --- SVM-RFE ---
  set.seed(300 + i)
  g_svm <- tryCatch(
    svm_rfe_fast(X_tr_z, y_tr, 20),
    error = function(e) g_rf  # 失败就用 RF 的
  )
  
  # --- XGBoost ---
  set.seed(400 + i)
  dtrain <- xgb.DMatrix(data = X_tr_z, label = y_tr)
  xgbm <- xgb.train(
    params = list(objective = "binary:logistic", eval_metric = "auc",
                  max_depth = 3, eta = 0.1),
    data = dtrain, nrounds = 100, verbose = 0)
  imp_x <- xgb.importance(model = xgbm)
  g_xgb <- imp_x$Feature[1:min(30, ncol(X_tr_z))]
  
  # --- 交集 ---
  core_i <- Reduce(intersect, list(g_lasso, g_rf, g_svm, g_xgb))
  
  # 交集太小就 fallback
  if (length(core_i) < 3) {
    all_g <- unique(c(g_lasso, g_rf, g_svm, g_xgb))
    core_i <- all_g[1:min(15, length(all_g))]
    cat(sprintf("  Fold %d: intersection < 3, fallback to %d genes\n",
                i, length(core_i)))
  }
  gene_count_per_fold[i] <- length(core_i)
  
  # --- 训练 logistic ---
  df_tr <- data.frame(X_tr_z[, core_i, drop = FALSE], y = y_tr)
  m <- glm(y ~ ., data = df_tr, family = binomial)
  
  # --- 测试集 z-score（用训练参数） ---
  X_te <- X[test_idx, , drop = FALSE]
  X_te_z <- scale(X_te, center = mu, scale = sdv)
  X_te_z[is.na(X_te_z)] <- 0
  
  # 补齐基因（若某折选出的基因在测试集列名缺失）
  for (g in core_i) {
    if (!(g %in% colnames(X_te_z))) {
      X_te_z <- cbind(X_te_z, 0); colnames(X_te_z)[ncol(X_te_z)] <- g
    }
  }
  
  df_te <- data.frame(X_te_z[, core_i, drop = FALSE])
  cv_pred_nested[test_idx] <- predict(m, newdata = df_te, type = "response")
}

roc_nested <- roc(y, cv_pred_nested, quiet = TRUE)
ci_nested  <- ci.auc(roc_nested)
auc_nested <- as.numeric(auc(roc_nested))

cat(sprintf("\n=== Nested CV AUC = %.3f (95%% CI: %.3f-%.3f) ===\n",
            auc_nested, ci_nested[1], ci_nested[3]))
cat(sprintf("每折基因数: %s\n", paste(gene_count_per_fold, collapse = ", ")))
cat(sprintf("中位基因数: %d\n", median(gene_count_per_fold)))

saveRDS(list(
  cv_pred_nested = cv_pred_nested,
  roc_nested = roc_nested,
  auc_nested = auc_nested,
  ci_nested = ci_nested,
  gene_count_per_fold = gene_count_per_fold
), "results/Module1_nested_cv.rds")

# ---- 加载 ----
data <- readRDS("results/GSE135251_DEG_results.rds")
log_expr <- data$log_expr
stage <- data$fibro_stage

ml <- readRDS("results/Module1_ML_results.rds")
core_genes <- ml$core_genes

suppressPackageStartupMessages(library(org.Hs.eg.db))
sym <- mapIds(org.Hs.eg.db, keys = rownames(log_expr),
              column = "SYMBOL", keytype = "ENSEMBL", multiVals = "first")
valid <- !is.na(sym) & !duplicated(sym)
log_expr_sym <- log_expr[valid, ]
rownames(log_expr_sym) <- sym[valid]

# ---- 找出 control 样本 ----
pheno <- data$pheno
ctrl_ids <- rownames(pheno)[pheno$`disease:ch1` == "Control"]
cat(sprintf("Control samples: %d\n", length(ctrl_ids)))
print(ctrl_ids)

# ---- 全样本 CV ----
X_all <- t(log_expr_sym[core_genes, ])
y_all <- as.numeric(stage >= 2)

run_cv <- function(X, y, k = 10, seed = 123) {
  set.seed(seed)
  X_z <- scale(X)
  X_z[is.na(X_z)] <- 0
  folds <- createFolds(y, k = k, list = TRUE)
  pred <- numeric(length(y))
  for (i in seq_along(folds)) {
    te <- folds[[i]]; tr <- setdiff(seq_along(y), te)
    m <- glm(y[tr] ~ ., data = data.frame(X_z[tr, , drop = FALSE]),
             family = binomial)
    pred[te] <- predict(m, newdata = data.frame(X_z[te, , drop = FALSE]),
                        type = "response")
  }
  roc_obj <- roc(y, pred, quiet = TRUE)
  list(roc = roc_obj, auc = as.numeric(auc(roc_obj)))
}

res_all <- run_cv(X_all, y_all)
cat(sprintf("\n全样本 (N=%d) CV AUC: %.3f\n", nrow(X_all), res_all$auc))

# ---- NAFLD-only CV ----
keep <- !(colnames(X_all) %in% ctrl_ids)
X_n <- X_all[keep, ]
y_n <- y_all[keep]

cat(sprintf("\nNAFLD-only: N = %d (Low = %d, High = %d)\n",
            nrow(X_n), sum(y_n == 0), sum(y_n == 1)))

res_n <- run_cv(X_n, y_n)
cat(sprintf("NAFLD-only CV AUC: %.3f\n", res_n$auc))

cat(sprintf("\n=== 差异: %.3f ===\n", res_all$auc - res_n$auc))

# ---- 4. CV 函数 ----
run_cv <- function(X, y, k = 10, seed = 123) {
  set.seed(seed)
  X_z <- scale(X)
  X_z[is.na(X_z)] <- 0
  folds <- createFolds(y, k = k, list = TRUE)
  pred <- numeric(length(y))
  for (i in seq_along(folds)) {
    te <- folds[[i]]; tr <- setdiff(seq_along(y), te)
    m <- glm(y[tr] ~ ., data = data.frame(X_z[tr, , drop = FALSE]),
             family = binomial)
    pred[te] <- predict(m, newdata = data.frame(X_z[te, , drop = FALSE]),
                        type = "response")
  }
  roc_obj <- roc(y, pred, quiet = TRUE)
  list(roc = roc_obj, auc = as.numeric(auc(roc_obj)))
}

# ---- 5. 全样本 CV ----
res_all <- run_cv(X_all, y_all)
cat(sprintf("\n全样本 (N=%d) CV AUC: %.3f\n", nrow(X_all), res_all$auc))

# ---- 6. NAFLD-only CV ----
keep <- !(rownames(X_all) %in% ctrl_ids)   # ← 用 rownames 不是 colnames

            nrow(X_n), sum(y_n == 0), sum(y_n == 1)))

cat(sprintf("NAFLD-only CV AUC: %.3f\n", res_n$auc))

cat(sprintf("\n=== 差异: %.3f ===\n", res_all$auc - res_n$auc))

# ---- 7. CV 函数 ----
run_cv <- function(X, y, k = 10, seed = 123) {
  set.seed(seed)
  X_z <- scale(X)
  X_z[is.na(X_z)] <- 0
  folds <- createFolds(y, k = k, list = TRUE)
  pred <- numeric(length(y))
  for (i in seq_along(folds)) {
    te <- folds[[i]]; tr <- setdiff(seq_along(y), te)
    m <- glm(y[tr] ~ ., data = data.frame(X_z[tr, , drop = FALSE]),
             family = binomial)
    pred[te] <- predict(m, newdata = data.frame(X_z[te, , drop = FALSE]),
                        type = "response")
  }
  roc_obj <- roc(y, pred, quiet = TRUE)
  list(roc = roc_obj, auc = as.numeric(auc(roc_obj)))
}

# ---- 8. 全样本 CV ----
res_all <- run_cv(X_all, y_all)

# ---- 5. CV 函数 ----
run_cv <- function(X, y, k = 10, seed = 123) {
  set.seed(seed)
  X_z <- scale(X)
  X_z[is.na(X_z)] <- 0
  folds <- createFolds(y, k = k, list = TRUE)
  pred <- numeric(length(y))
  for (i in seq_along(folds)) {
    te <- folds[[i]]; tr <- setdiff(seq_along(y), te)
    m <- glm(y[tr] ~ ., data = data.frame(X_z[tr, , drop = FALSE]),
             family = binomial)
    pred[te] <- predict(m, newdata = data.frame(X_z[te, , drop = FALSE]),
                        type = "response")
  }
  roc_obj <- roc(y, pred, quiet = TRUE)
  list(roc = roc_obj, auc = as.numeric(auc(roc_obj)))
}

# ---- 6. 全样本 CV ----
res_all <- run_cv(X_all, y_all)
cat(sprintf("\n全样本 (N=%d) CV AUC: %.3f\n", nrow(X_all), res_all$auc))

# ---- 7. NAFLD-only CV（按行号排除 control） ----
keep <- !(seq_len(nrow(X_all)) %in% ctrl_rows)
X_n <- X_all[keep, , drop = FALSE]
y_n <- y_all[keep]

cat(sprintf("\nNAFLD-only: N = %d (Low = %d, High = %d)\n",
            nrow(X_n), sum(y_n == 0), sum(y_n == 1)))

res_n <- run_cv(X_n, y_n)
cat(sprintf("NAFLD-only CV AUC: %.3f\n", res_n$auc))

cat(sprintf("\n=== 差异: %.3f ===\n", res_all$auc - res_n$auc))


####重新加载信息列####
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(tidyverse); library(GEOquery)
})

# ---- 1. 重新拉 GEO pheno ----
gse <- getGEO("GSE135251", GSEMatrix = TRUE, getGPL = FALSE)
pheno_geo <- pData(gse[[1]])

cat(sprintf("GEO pheno: %d 行\n", nrow(pheno_geo)))
cat("\n=== GEO disease 分布 ===\n")
print(table(pheno_geo[["disease:ch1"]]))

cat("\n=== GEO fibrosis stage 分布 ===\n")
print(table(pheno_geo[["fibrosis stage:ch1"]]))

# ---- 2. 对比 stage 顺序 ----
data <- readRDS("results/GSE135251_DEG_results.rds")
stage_current <- data$fibro_stage

stage_geo <- as.numeric(as.character(pheno_geo[["fibrosis stage:ch1"]]))

cat(sprintf("\nstage_geo 长度: %d\n", length(stage_geo)))
cat(sprintf("stage_current 长度: %d\n", length(stage_current)))

# 检查是否完全一致（顺序也一致）
n_match <- sum(stage_geo == stage_current, na.rm = TRUE)
cat(sprintf("\n顺序一致的位置: %d / %d\n", n_match, length(stage_current)))

# 如果完全一致，说明顺序对得上
if (n_match == length(stage_current)) {
  cat("\n✓ 顺序一致，可以直接用 ctrl_rows 排除\n")
} else {
  cat("\n⚠️ 顺序不一致，需要重新对齐\n")
}

# ---- 3. 找 control 行号 ----
ctrl_rows <- which(pheno_geo[["disease:ch1"]] == "Control")
cat(sprintf("\nControl 行号: %s\n", paste(ctrl_rows, collapse = ", ")))
cat(sprintf("Control 对应的 GSM: %s\n",
            paste(rownames(pheno_geo)[ctrl_rows], collapse = ", ")))
cat(sprintf("Control 对应的 fibrosis stage: %s\n",
            paste(pheno_geo[["fibrosis stage:ch1"]][ctrl_rows],
                  collapse = ", ")))

# ---- 4. 如果顺序一致，排除 control 重跑 CV ----
if (n_match == length(stage_current)) {
  suppressPackageStartupMessages({
    library(caret); library(pROC); library(org.Hs.eg.db)
  })
  
  log_expr <- data$log_expr
  ml <- readRDS("results/Module1_ML_results.rds")
  core_genes <- ml$core_genes
  
  sym <- mapIds(org.Hs.eg.db, keys = rownames(log_expr),
                column = "SYMBOL", keytype = "ENSEMBL", multiVals = "first")
  valid <- !is.na(sym) & !duplicated(sym)
  log_expr_sym <- log_expr[valid, ]
  rownames(log_expr_sym) <- sym[valid]
  
  X_all <- t(log_expr_sym[core_genes, ])
  y_all <- as.numeric(stage_current >= 2)
  
  run_cv <- function(X, y, k = 10, seed = 123) {
    set.seed(seed)
    X_z <- scale(X); X_z[is.na(X_z)] <- 0
    folds <- createFolds(y, k = k, list = TRUE)
    pred <- numeric(length(y))
    for (i in seq_along(folds)) {
      te <- folds[[i]]; tr <- setdiff(seq_along(y), te)
      m <- glm(y[tr] ~ ., data = data.frame(X_z[tr, , drop = FALSE]),
               family = binomial)
      pred[te] <- predict(m, newdata = data.frame(X_z[te, , drop = FALSE]),
                          type = "response")
    }
    as.numeric(auc(roc(y, pred, quiet = TRUE)))
  }
  
  # 全样本
  auc_all <- run_cv(X_all, y_all)
  cat(sprintf("\n全样本 (N=%d) CV AUC: %.3f\n", nrow(X_all), auc_all))
  
  # NAFLD-only
  keep <- !(seq_len(nrow(X_all)) %in% ctrl_rows)
  X_n <- X_all[keep, , drop = FALSE]
  y_n <- y_all[keep]
  
  cat(sprintf("\nNAFLD-only: N = %d (Low = %d, High = %d)\n",
              nrow(X_n), sum(y_n == 0), sum(y_n == 1)))
  auc_n <- run_cv(X_n, y_n)
  cat(sprintf("NAFLD-only CV AUC: %.3f\n", auc_n))
  
  cat(sprintf("\n=== 差异: %.3f ===\n", auc_all - auc_n))
}

fov_df <- data.frame(
  Direction = factor(c("HGF-expressing closer to Hep.4", "Other"),
                     levels = c("HGF-expressing closer to Hep.4", "Other")),
  N = c(n_cons, n_rev)
)
fov_df$Pct <- fov_df$N / total * 100

p2b <- ggplot(fov_df, aes(Direction, N, fill = Direction)) +
  geom_col(alpha = 0.9, width = 0.65) +
  geom_text(aes(label = sprintf("%d (%.1f%%)", N, Pct)),
            vjust = -0.5, size = 4) +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c(
    "HGF-expressing closer to Hep.4" = "#C73E1D",
    "Other" = "grey70"
  )) +
  labs(title = "FOV-level direction consistency",
       subtitle = sprintf("Binomial P = %.2e, n = %d FOVs", fov_res$binom_p, total),
       x = NULL, y = "Number of FOVs") +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) +
  theme(legend.position = "none") +
  coord_cartesian(ylim = c(0, max(fov_df$N) * 1.15))

ggsave("figures/final/Fig2b_FOV_consistency.pdf", p2b,
       width = 5, height = 4)
print(p2b)
