# 通用主题
theme_pub <- function(base = 10) {
  theme_classic(base_size = base) +
    theme(plot.title = element_text(size = base + 1, face = "bold"),
          plot.subtitle = element_text(size = base - 1, color = "grey30"),
          plot.tag = element_text(size = base + 2, face = "bold"))
}

cat("=== 开始重画所有 Figure ===\n\n")

# ============================================================
# Figure 1: CosMx 概览 + Stellate 纤维化区富集（OR=1.49）
# ============================================================
cat("[1/6] Figure 1 ...\n")

cosmx1 <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
col1 <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec1 <- cosmx1@meta.data[[col1]]
coords1 <- cosmx1@meta.data[, c("x_slide_mm","y_slide_mm","fov")]

# 1a: 细胞类型组成
ct_df <- data.frame(
  CellType = names(table(celltype_vec1)),
  N        = as.numeric(table(celltype_vec1))
) %>%
  mutate(Pct = N / sum(N) * 100) %>%
  arrange(desc(N)) %>% head(15)

p1a <- ggplot(ct_df, aes(reorder(CellType, N), N, fill = CellType)) +
  geom_col(alpha = 0.85) +
  coord_flip() +
  labs(title = "Cell type composition",
       subtitle = sprintf("CosMx Slide1 (n = %s cells)", format(ncol(cosmx1), big.mark = ",")),
       x = NULL, y = "Cell count") +
  theme_pub(9) +
  theme(legend.position = "none")

# 1b: Stellate 空间分布
plot_df <- coords1
plot_df$is_stellate <- celltype_vec1 == "Stellate.cells"

p1b <- ggplot() +
  geom_point(data = plot_df[!plot_df$is_stellate, ],
             aes(x_slide_mm, y_slide_mm),
             size = 0.05, alpha = 0.1, color = "grey80") +
  geom_point(data = plot_df[plot_df$is_stellate, ],
             aes(x_slide_mm, y_slide_mm),
             size = 0.3, alpha = 0.6, color = "#C73E1D") +
  coord_fixed() +
  labs(title = "Stellate cells spatial distribution",
       subtitle = sprintf("n = %s (%.1f%%)",
                          format(sum(plot_df$is_stellate), big.mark = ","),
                          100 * mean(plot_df$is_stellate)),
       x = "x_slide_mm", y = "y_slide_mm") +
  theme_pub(9)

# 1c: 富集 OR = 1.49 (HSC-independent)
enrich <- readRDS("results/GSE292268_Stellate_enrichment_independent.rds")
or_val <- enrich$OR_indep
ci_val <- enrich$CI
cat(sprintf("      OR = %.3f (95%% CI: %.3f-%.3f)\n", or_val, ci_val[1], ci_val[2]))

p1c <- ggplot() +
  geom_vline(xintercept = 1, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey40") +
  geom_point(aes(x = or_val, y = 1), size = 5, color = "#C73E1D") +
  geom_errorbarh(aes(xmin = ci_val[1], xmax = ci_val[2], y = 1),
                 height = 0.15, color = "#C73E1D", linewidth = 1) +
  scale_x_log10(limits = c(1.2, 1.8),
                breaks = c(1.2, 1.4, 1.6, 1.8)) +
  labs(title = "Stellate enrichment in fibrotic region",
       subtitle = sprintf("OR = %.2f (95%% CI: %.2f-%.2f)\nHSC-independent signature",
                          or_val, ci_val[1], ci_val[2]),
       x = "Odds ratio (log scale)", y = NULL) +
  theme_pub(9) +
  theme(axis.text.y = element_blank(),
        axis.ticks.y = element_blank())

fig1 <- (p1a | p1b | p1c) + plot_annotation(tag_levels = "a")
ggsave(file.path(OUT, "Figure1.pdf"), fig1, width = 15, height = 5)
ggsave(file.path(OUT, "Figure1.png"), fig1, width = 15, height = 5, dpi = 300)

cat("[2/6] Figure 2 ...\n")

res1 <- read.csv("results/HGF_HSC_niche_scan.csv")
res2 <- read.csv("results/HGF_HSC_niche_scan_Slide2.csv")
res1$Slide <- "Slide1"; res2$Slide <- "Slide2"

niche_df <- rbind(res1, res2) %>%
  filter(N_total >= 200) %>%
  mutate(Score = log2(Ratio + 1e-6))

p2a <- ggplot(niche_df, aes(reorder(CellType, Score), Score, fill = Slide)) +
  geom_col(position = position_dodge(0.8), alpha = 0.9) +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c("Slide1" = "#C73E1D", "Slide2" = "#2E86AB")) +
  geom_hline(yintercept = 0, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey40") +
  coord_flip() +
  labs(title = "HGF+ HSC niche scan (50 µm)",
       subtitle = "log2(enrichment ratio) across two slides",
       x = NULL, y = "log2 ratio") +
  theme_pub(9) +
  theme(legend.position = "top",
        axis.text.y = element_text(size = 7))

# 2b: FOV 一致性（48/62）
fov_res <- readRDS("results/Reverse_FOV_characterization.rds")
n_cons <- nrow(fov_res$consistent_fovs)
n_rev  <- nrow(fov_res$reverse_fovs)
total  <- n_cons + n_rev
cat(sprintf("      FOV: %d/%d (%.1f%%), P = %.2e\n",
            n_cons, total, 100 * n_cons / total, fov_res$binom_p))

fov_df <- data.frame(
  Direction = factor(c("HGF+ closer to Hep.4", "Other"),
                     levels = c("HGF+ closer to Hep.4", "Other")),
  N = c(n_cons, n_rev)
)
fov_df$Pct <- fov_df$N / total * 100

p2b <- ggplot(fov_df, aes(Direction, N, fill = Direction)) +
  geom_col(alpha = 0.9, width = 0.65) +
  geom_text(aes(label = sprintf("%d (%.1f%%)", N, Pct)),
            vjust = -0.5, size = 3.5) +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c("HGF+ closer to Hep.4" = "#C73E1D",
                               "Other" = "grey70")) +
  labs(title = "FOV-level direction consistency",
       subtitle = sprintf("Binomial P = %.2e, n = %d FOVs",
                          fov_res$binom_p, total),
       x = NULL, y = "Number of FOVs") +
  theme_pub(9) +
  theme(legend.position = "none") +
  coord_cartesian(ylim = c(0, max(fov_df$N) * 1.15))

# 2c: 跨切片一致性
merged <- read.csv("results/niche_scan_cross_slide.csv")
ct_2c <- cor.test(merged$log2_R1, merged$log2_R2)

p2c <- ggplot(merged, aes(log2_R1, log2_R2)) +
  geom_hline(yintercept = 0, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey60") +
  geom_vline(xintercept = 0, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey60") +
  geom_abline(slope = 1, intercept = 0, color = "grey80", linetype = "dotted") +
  geom_point(size = 2.5, alpha = 0.85, color = "#C73E1D") +
  geom_smooth(method = "lm", se = TRUE, color = "black", fill = "grey80") +
  ggrepel::geom_text_repel(aes(label = CellType), size = 2.3, max.overlaps = 20) +
  labs(title = "Cross-slide niche consistency",
       subtitle = sprintf("Pearson r = %.3f, P = %.2e",
                          ct_2c$estimate, ct_2c$p.value),
       x = "log2 ratio Slide1", y = "log2 ratio Slide2") +
  coord_fixed() + theme_pub(9)

# 2d: 阈值敏感性

                               "Periportal.LSECs" = "#2E86AB")) +
  labs(title = "Threshold sensitivity (dose-response)",
       x = "HGF threshold", y = "Enrichment ratio", fill = NULL) +
  theme_pub(9) + theme(legend.position = "top")

  dplyr::select(TF, Slide1 = delta1, Slide2 = delta2) %>%

# SP3 GO
data("dorothea_hs", package = "dorothea")
sp3_targets <- dorothea_hs %>%
  dplyr::filter(tf == "SP3", confidence %in% c("A","B","C")) %>% pull(target)
gene_ids <- bitr(sp3_targets, fromType = "SYMBOL", toType = "ENTREZID",
                 OrgDb = org.Hs.eg.db)
ego <- enrichGO(gene = gene_ids$ENTREZID, OrgDb = org.Hs.eg.db,
                ont = "BP", pAdjustMethod = "BH",
                pvalueCutoff = 0.1, readable = TRUE)
sp3_go <- as.data.frame(ego) %>% head(8) %>%
  mutate(Description = reorder(Description, -log10(p.adjust)))
write.csv(as.data.frame(ego), "results/Fig4c_SP3_GO.csv", row.names = FALSE)

p4c <- ggplot(sp3_go, aes(Description, -log10(p.adjust),
                          fill = -log10(p.adjust))) +
  geom_col(alpha = 0.9) +
  scale_fill_gradient(low = "#F4A582", high = "#C73E1D") +
  coord_flip() +
  labs(title = "SP3 target gene enrichment (GO BP)",
       x = NULL, y = "-log10(adj.P)") +
  theme_pub(9) +
  theme(legend.position = "none", axis.text.y = element_text(size = 7))

# ============================================================
# Figure 5: ROC + DCA（用 z-scored 版本，AUC = 0.784）
# ============================================================
cat("[5/6] Figure 5 ...\n")

# 5a: Training ROC
m <- readRDS("results/Module1_ROC_results_zscored.rds")
roc_cv <- pROC::roc(m$y, m$cv_pred, quiet = TRUE)
auc_cv <- as.numeric(pROC::auc(roc_cv))
ci_cv  <- as.numeric(pROC::ci.auc(roc_cv))

roc_cv_df <- data.frame(FPR = 1 - roc_cv$specificities,
                        TPR = roc_cv$sensitivities)

p5a <- ggplot(roc_cv_df, aes(FPR, TPR)) +
  geom_line(color = "#C73E1D", linewidth = 1.2) +
  geom_abline(intercept = 1, slope = 1, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey60") +
  annotate("text", x = 0.62, y = 0.12,
           label = sprintf("CV AUC = %.3f\n95%% CI: %.3f-%.3f",
                           auc_cv, ci_cv[1], ci_cv[3]),
           size = 3.2, color = "#C73E1D", hjust = 0) +
  labs(title = "Training performance",
       subtitle = "GSE135251, 10-fold CV",
       x = "1 - Specificity", y = "Sensitivity") +
  coord_fixed(xlim = c(0, 1), ylim = c(0, 1)) +
  theme_pub(10)

# 5b: Validation ROC
val <- readRDS("results/GSE49541_validation_unified_zscored.rds")
roc_val <- val$roc
auc_val <- as.numeric(val$auc)
ci_val  <- as.numeric(val$ci)

roc_val_df <- data.frame(FPR = 1 - roc_val$specificities,
                         TPR = roc_val$sensitivities)

df_dca <- data.frame(outcome = as.integer(val$group),
                     pred_prob = as.numeric(val$pred_prob))
df_dca <- df_dca[!is.na(df_dca$outcome) & !is.na(df_dca$pred_prob), ]

p5b <- ggplot(roc_val_df, aes(FPR, TPR)) +
  geom_line(color = "#C73E1D", linewidth = 1.2) +
  geom_abline(intercept = 1, slope = 1, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey60") +
  annotate("text", x = 0.62, y = 0.12,
           label = sprintf("AUC = %.3f\n95%% CI: %.3f-%.3f",
                           auc_val, ci_val[1], ci_val[3]),
           size = 3.2, color = "#C73E1D", hjust = 0) +
  labs(title = "External validation",
       subtitle = sprintf("GSE49541, N = %d (%d events)",
                          nrow(df_dca), sum(df_dca$outcome)),
       x = "1 - Specificity", y = "Sensitivity") +
  coord_fixed(xlim = c(0, 1), ylim = c(0, 1)) +
  theme_pub(10)

# 5c: DCA
dca_obj <- dcurves::dca(outcome ~ pred_prob, data = df_dca)
dca_df  <- dca_obj$dca
dca_long <- dca_df %>%
  dplyr::select(threshold, variable, net_benefit) %>%
  dplyr::filter(variable %in% c("pred_prob", "all", "none")) %>%
  dplyr::mutate(variable = dplyr::recode(variable,
                                         "pred_prob" = "11-gene signature",
                                         "all" = "Treat all",
                                         "none" = "Treat none"))

p5c <- ggplot(dca_long, aes(threshold, net_benefit, color = variable)) +
  geom_line(linewidth = 1.1) +
  scale_color_manual(values = c("11-gene signature" = "#C73E1D",
                                "Treat all" = "grey40",
                                "Treat none" = "black")) +
  geom_hline(yintercept = 0, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey70") +
  coord_cartesian(xlim = c(0, 0.6), ylim = c(-0.05, 0.45)) +
  labs(title = "Decision curve analysis",
       subtitle = "External validation cohort",
       x = "Threshold probability", y = "Net benefit", color = NULL) +
  theme_pub(10) +
  theme(legend.position = c(0.75, 0.85),
        legend.background = element_rect(fill = "white", color = NA),
        legend.key.size = unit(0.4, "cm"),
        legend.text = element_text(size = 8))

fig5 <- (p5a | p5b | p5c) + plot_annotation(tag_levels = "a")
ggsave(file.path(OUT, "Figure5.pdf"), fig5, width = 13, height = 4.5)
ggsave(file.path(OUT, "Figure5.png"), fig5, width = 13, height = 4.5, dpi = 300)

cat(sprintf("      Training CV AUC = %.3f\n", auc_cv))
cat(sprintf("      Validation AUC = %.3f (%.3f-%.3f)\n",
            auc_val, ci_val[1], ci_val[3]))

####诊断 1：TGFB1/ TGFBR1/2 从纤维化区定义中删除后，OR 变成多少
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({library(Seurat); library(tidyverse)})

cosmx1 <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
col1 <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec <- cosmx1@meta.data[[col1]]
expr_data <- cosmx1[["RNA"]]$data

# 新的纤维化区定义：删除 TGFB1, TGFBR1, TGFBR2
fibro_sig_v2 <- c("IL6", "IL1B", "CCL2", "CXCL9", "CXCL10",
                  "TNF", "MMP2", "MMP14")
fibro_sig_v2 <- fibro_sig_v2[fibro_sig_v2 %in% rownames(expr_data)]
cat(sprintf("新 signature 基因数: %d\n", length(fibro_sig_v2)))

expr_indep <- as.matrix(expr_data[fibro_sig_v2, , drop = FALSE])
fibro_score_indep <- colMeans(expr_indep)

non_hsc_idx <- which(celltype_vec != "Stellate.cells")
fibro_score_nonhsc <- colMeans(expr_indep[, non_hsc_idx, drop = FALSE])
thr_nonhsc <- quantile(fibro_score_nonhsc, 0.75, na.rm = TRUE)
is_fibrotic <- fibro_score_indep >= thr_nonhsc

ct <- table(
  celltype = ifelse(celltype_vec == "Stellate.cells", "Stellate", "Other"),
  region   = ifelse(is_fibrotic, "Fibrotic", "NonFibrotic")
)
print(ct)

a <- ct["Stellate", "Fibrotic"]; b <- ct["Stellate", "NonFibrotic"]
c <- ct["Other", "Fibrotic"];    d <- ct["Other", "NonFibrotic"]
OR <- (a/b)/(c/d)
se <- sqrt(1/a + 1/b + 1/c + 1/d)
cat(sprintf("\nOR_v2 = %.3f (95%% CI: %.3f-%.3f)\n",
            OR, exp(log(OR) - 1.96*se), exp(log(OR) + 1.96*se)))


####诊断 2：训练队列 F0 构成
data <- readRDS("results/GSE135251_DEG_results.rds")
stage <- data$fibro_stage
cat("=== Fibrosis stage 分布（按 log_expr 列顺序）===\n")
print(table(stage))
cat(sprintf("\nF0: %d, F1: %d, F2: %d, F3: %d, F4: %d\n",
            sum(stage == 0), sum(stage == 1), sum(stage == 2),
            sum(stage == 3), sum(stage == 4)))
cat(sprintf("Low (F0-F1): %d, High (F2-F4): %d\n",
            sum(stage <= 1), sum(stage >= 2)))

#####诊断 3：TMM 标准化是否已做
data <- readRDS("results/GSE135251_aligned_data.rds")
count_matrix <- data$count_matrix
cat(sprintf("count_matrix 列和（前 5 个样本）:\n"))
print(colSums(count_matrix)[1:5])
cat(sprintf("\n最大值: %d\n", max(count_matrix)))
cat(sprintf("是否 log 转换过（max<50 = 已转）: %s\n",
            max(count_matrix) < 50))


setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(Seurat); library(tidyverse); library(ggplot2)
})

dir.create("figures/Figure2", showWarnings = FALSE, recursive = TRUE)

# ---- Fig 1c 用新 OR = 1.537 ----
or_val <- 1.537
ci_val <- c(1.435, 1.647)

p1c <- ggplot() +
  geom_vline(xintercept = 1, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey40") +
  geom_point(aes(x = or_val, y = 1), size = 5, color = "#C73E1D") +
  geom_errorbarh(aes(xmin = ci_val[1], xmax = ci_val[2], y = 1),
                 height = 0.15, color = "#C73E1D", linewidth = 1) +
  scale_x_log10(limits = c(1.3, 1.8),
                breaks = c(1.3, 1.4, 1.5, 1.6, 1.7, 1.8)) +
  labs(title = "Stellate enrichment in fibrotic region",
       subtitle = sprintf("OR = %.2f (95%% CI: %.2f-%.2f)\nHSC-independent signature",
                          or_val, ci_val[1], ci_val[2]),
       x = "Odds ratio (log scale)", y = NULL) +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) +
  theme(axis.text.y = element_blank(), axis.ticks.y = element_blank())

ggsave("figures/Figure2/Fig1c_OR.pdf", p1c, width = 5, height = 3)
print(p1c)

# ---- Fig 2b 用 48/62 ----
fov_res <- readRDS("results/Reverse_FOV_characterization.rds")
n_cons <- nrow(fov_res$consistent_fovs)
n_rev  <- nrow(fov_res$reverse_fovs)
total  <- n_cons + n_rev

fov_df <- data.frame(
  Direction = factor(c("HGF+ closer to Hep.4", "Other"),
                     levels = c("HGF+ closer to Hep.4", "Other")),
  N = c(n_cons, n_rev)
)
fov_df$Pct <- fov_df$N / total * 100

p2b <- ggplot(fov_df, aes(Direction, N, fill = Direction)) +
  geom_col(alpha = 0.9, width = 0.65) +
  geom_text(aes(label = sprintf("%d (%.1f%%)", N, Pct)),
            vjust = -0.5, size = 4) +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c("HGF+ closer to Hep.4" = "#C73E1D",
                               "Other" = "grey70")) +
  labs(title = "FOV-level direction consistency",
       subtitle = sprintf("Binomial P = %.2e, n = %d FOVs",
                          fov_res$binom_p, total),
       x = NULL, y = "Number of FOVs") +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) +
  theme(legend.position = "none") +
  coord_cartesian(ylim = c(0, max(fov_df$N) * 1.15))

ggsave("figures/Figure2/Fig2b_FOV_consistency.pdf", p2b, width = 5, height = 4)
print(p2b)

theme_pub <- function(base = 9) {
  theme_classic(base_size = base) +
    theme(plot.title = element_text(size = base + 1, face = "bold"),
          plot.subtitle = element_text(size = base - 1, color = "grey30"),
          plot.tag = element_text(size = base + 2, face = "bold"))
}

# ============================================================
# Figure 1

cat("\n[Figure 1]\n")

cosmx1 <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
col1 <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
celltype_vec1 <- cosmx1@meta.data[[col1]]
coords1 <- cosmx1@meta.data[, c("x_slide_mm","y_slide_mm","fov")]

# Panel a
ct_df <- data.frame(
  CellType = names(table(celltype_vec1)),
  N        = as.numeric(table(celltype_vec1))
) %>%
  mutate(Pct = N / sum(N) * 100) %>%
  arrange(desc(N)) %>% head(15)

p1a <- ggplot(ct_df, aes(reorder(CellType, N), N, fill = CellType)) +
  geom_col(alpha = 0.85) +
  coord_flip() +
  labs(title = "Cell type composition",
       subtitle = sprintf("CosMx Slide1 (n = %s cells)",
                          format(ncol(cosmx1), big.mark = ",")),
       x = NULL, y = "Cell count") +
  theme_pub(9) +
  theme(legend.position = "none")

# Panel b
plot_df <- coords1
plot_df$is_stellate <- celltype_vec1 == "Stellate.cells"

p1b <- ggplot() +
  geom_point(data = plot_df[!plot_df$is_stellate, ],
             aes(x_slide_mm, y_slide_mm),
             size = 0.05, alpha = 0.1, color = "grey80") +
  geom_point(data = plot_df[plot_df$is_stellate, ],
             aes(x_slide_mm, y_slide_mm),
             size = 0.3, alpha = 0.6, color = "#C73E1D") +
  coord_fixed() +
  labs(title = "Stellate cells spatial distribution",
       subtitle = sprintf("n = %s (%.1f%%)",
                          format(sum(plot_df$is_stellate), big.mark = ","),
                          100 * mean(plot_df$is_stellate)),
       x = "x_slide_mm", y = "y_slide_mm") +
  theme_pub(9)

# Panel c —— 关键修复：x 轴下限改成 1.0，让 vline(x=1) 可见
or_val <- 1.537
ci_val <- c(1.435, 1.647)

p1c <- ggplot() +
  geom_vline(xintercept = 1, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey40") +
  geom_point(aes(x = or_val, y = 1), size = 5, color = "#C73E1D") +
  geom_errorbarh(aes(xmin = ci_val[1], xmax = ci_val[2], y = 1),
                 height = 0.15, color = "#C73E1D", linewidth = 1) +
  scale_x_log10(limits = c(0.9, 2.0),            # ← 改成 0.9-2.0
                breaks = c(1.0, 1.2, 1.4, 1.6, 1.8, 2.0)) +
  labs(title = "Stellate enrichment in fibrotic region",
       subtitle = sprintf("OR = %.2f (95%% CI: %.2f-%.2f)\nHSC-independent signature",
                          or_val, ci_val[1], ci_val[2]),
       x = "Odds ratio (log scale)", y = NULL) +
  theme_pub(9) +
  theme(axis.text.y = element_blank(),
        axis.ticks.y = element_blank())

suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(Seurat); library(tidyverse); library(ggplot2)
  library(patchwork); library(ggrepel); library(pROC); library(dcurves)
  library(clusterProfiler); library(org.Hs.eg.db); library(dorothea)
})

OUT <- "figures/Figure2"
dir.create(OUT, showWarnings = FALSE, recursive = TRUE)

# ---- 稳健保存函数 ----
safe_save <- function(plot_obj, name, w, h, dpi = 300) {
  # 先尝试 PDF
  pdf_path <- file.path(OUT, paste0(name, ".pdf"))
  png_path <- file.path(OUT, paste0(name, ".png"))
  
  ok_pdf <- tryCatch({
    pdf(pdf_path, width = w, height = h)
    print(plot_obj)
    dev.off()
    TRUE
  }, error = function(e) {
    if (dev.cur() > 1) try(dev.off(), silent = TRUE)
    cat(sprintf("  ⚠️ %s.pdf 失败: %s\n", name, conditionMessage(e)))
    FALSE
  })
  
  ok_png <- tryCatch({
    png(png_path, width = w, height = h, units = "in", res = dpi)
    print(plot_obj)
    dev.off()
    TRUE
  }, error = function(e) {
    if (dev.cur() > 1) try(dev.off(), silent = TRUE)
    cat(sprintf("  ⚠️ %s.png 失败: %s\n", name, conditionMessage(e)))
    FALSE
  })
  
  if (ok_pdf && ok_png) cat(sprintf("  ✅ %s saved\n", name))
  else if (ok_pdf) cat(sprintf("  ⚠️ %s 只有 PDF\n", name))
  else if (ok_png) cat(sprintf("  ⚠️ %s 只有 PNG\n", name))
  invisible(ok_pdf || ok_png)
}

theme_pub <- function(base = 9) {
  theme_classic(base_size = base) +
    theme(plot.title = element_text(size = base + 1, face = "bold"),
          plot.subtitle = element_text(size = base - 1, color = "grey30"),
          plot.tag = element_text(size = base + 2, face = "bold"))
}

n_rev  <- nrow(fov_res$reverse_fovs)
total  <- n_cons + n_rev

fov_df <- data.frame(
  Direction = factor(c("HGF+ closer to Hep.4", "Other"),
                     levels = c("HGF+ closer to Hep.4", "Other")),
  N = c(n_cons, n_rev)
)
fov_df$Pct <- fov_df$N / total * 100

p2b <- ggplot(fov_df, aes(Direction, N, fill = Direction)) +
  geom_col(alpha = 0.9, width = 0.65) +
  geom_text(aes(label = sprintf("%d (%.1f%%)", N, Pct)),
            vjust = -0.5, size = 3.5) +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c("HGF+ closer to Hep.4" = "#C73E1D",
                               "Other" = "grey70")) +
  labs(title = "FOV-level direction consistency",
       subtitle = sprintf("Binomial P = %.2e, n = %d FOVs",
                          fov_res$binom_p, total),
       x = NULL, y = "Number of FOVs") +
  theme_pub(9) +
  theme(legend.position = "none") +
  coord_cartesian(ylim = c(0, max(fov_df$N) * 1.15))

merged <- read.csv("results/niche_scan_cross_slide.csv")
merged_clean <- merged %>%
  dplyr::filter(grepl("[A-Z]", CellType),
                grepl("\\.", CellType) | grepl("[A-Z][a-z]", CellType),
                !CellType %in% c("h","j","i","e","c","a","b","d","f","g"))

ct_2c <- cor.test(merged_clean$log2_R1, merged_clean$log2_R2)

p2c <- ggplot(merged_clean, aes(log2_R1, log2_R2)) +
  geom_hline(yintercept = 0, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey60") +
  geom_vline(xintercept = 0, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey60") +
  geom_abline(slope = 1, intercept = 0, color = "grey80", linetype = "dotted") +
  geom_point(size = 3, alpha = 0.85, color = "#C73E1D") +
  geom_smooth(method = "lm", se = TRUE, color = "black", fill = "grey80") +
  ggrepel::geom_text_repel(aes(label = CellType), size = 2.3, max.overlaps = 20) +
  labs(title = "Cross-slide niche consistency",
       subtitle = sprintf("Pearson r = %.3f, P = %.2e",
                          ct_2c$estimate, ct_2c$p.value),
       x = "log2 ratio Slide1", y = "log2 ratio Slide2") +
  coord_fixed() + theme_pub(9)

sens <- read.csv("results/HGF_threshold_sensitivity.csv")
sens_df <- sens %>%
  filter(CellType %in% c("Hep.4", "Periportal.LSECs")) %>%
  mutate(Threshold = factor(Threshold, levels = c("HGF>0", "HGF>1", "HGF>2")))

p2d <- ggplot(sens_df, aes(Threshold, Ratio, fill = CellType)) +
  geom_col(position = position_dodge(0.8), alpha = 0.9, width = 0.7) +
  geom_hline(yintercept = 1, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey40") +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c("Hep.4" = "#C73E1D",
                               "Periportal.LSECs" = "#2E86AB")) +
  labs(title = "Threshold sensitivity (dose-response)",
       x = "HGF threshold", y = "Enrichment ratio", fill = NULL) +
  theme_pub(9) + theme(legend.position = "top")

counts_tb <- data.frame(
  count = 0:12,
  pct = c(58.9, 19.0, 9.8, 4.9, 2.9, 2.1, 1.2, 0.5, 0.4, 0.3, 0.1, 0.0, 0.0)
)
p2e <- ggplot(counts_tb, aes(factor(count), pct)) +
  geom_col(fill = "#C73E1D", alpha = 0.85, width = 0.7) +
  geom_text(aes(label = sprintf("%.1f", pct)),
            vjust = -0.3, size = 2.3, color = "grey30") +
  labs(title = "HGF transcript count in HSCs",
       subtitle = "Zero-inflated long-tail (n = 3,559)",
       x = "HGF count per cell", y = "% of HSCs") +
  coord_cartesian(ylim = c(0, 65)) +
  theme_pub(9)

spi <- read.csv("results/SPI_summary_all_HSC_subsets.csv")
spi_df <- spi %>%
  mutate(Subset = factor(Subset, levels = c("HGF+", "HGF-", "PDGFRB+", "PDGFRB-")))

p2f <- ggplot(spi_df, aes(Subset, Median_SPI, fill = Subset)) +
  geom_col(alpha = 0.9, width = 0.65) +
  geom_hline(yintercept = 0, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey40") +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c("HGF+" = "#C73E1D", "HGF-" = "#2E86AB",
                               "PDGFRB+" = "#F18F01", "PDGFRB-" = "#3B8EA5")) +
  labs(title = "Selective Proximity Index (SPI)",
       subtitle = "Median SPI per HSC subset",
       x = NULL, y = "Median SPI") +
  theme_pub(9) + theme(legend.position = "none")

fig2 <- wrap_plots(p2a, p2b, p2c, p2d, p2e, p2f, ncol = 3) +
  plot_annotation(tag_levels = "a")

safe_save(fig2, "Figure2", 15, 10)

merged_pb <- inner_join(
  read.csv("results/DEG_pseudobulk_Slide1.csv") %>%
    dplyr::select(gene, log2FC_1 = logFC),
  read.csv("results/DEG_pseudobulk_Slide2.csv") %>%
    dplyr::select(gene, log2FC_2 = logFC),
  by = "gene"
)
sig16 <- read.csv("results/signature_HGFpos_up.csv")$gene
merged_pb$is_sig <- merged_pb$gene %in% sig16

  geom_hline(yintercept = 0, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey60") +
  geom_vline(xintercept = 0, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey60") +
  geom_point(data = subset(merged_pb, !is_sig),
             alpha = 0.2, size = 0.8, color = "grey60") +
  geom_point(data = subset(merged_pb, is_sig),
             alpha = 0.9, size = 2, color = "#C73E1D") +

                           aes(label = gene), size = 2.3, max.overlaps = 20) +

       subtitle = "16-gene signature (red)",

  theme_pub(9)

res_val <- read.csv("results/signature_up_single_cell_validation.csv")
res_val$gene <- factor(res_val$gene,
                       levels = res_val$gene[order(res_val$delta_pos)])

p3b <- ggplot(res_val, aes(gene, delta_pos, fill = delta_pos)) +
  geom_col(alpha = 0.9) +

       subtitle = "Δ positive rate (HGF+ minus HGF−)",
       x = NULL, y = "Δ positive rate") +

go <- read.csv("results/sig16_GO_BP.csv") %>% head(10)

cat("\n[4/6] Figure 4 ...\n")

pos_df <- read.csv("results/LIFR_OSMR_posrate_check.csv")
pos_long <- pos_df %>%
  dplyr::select(Slide, Gene, HGFpos_posrate, HGFneg_posrate) %>%
  pivot_longer(c(HGFpos_posrate, HGFneg_posrate),
               names_to = "Group", values_to = "PosRate") %>%
  mutate(Group = ifelse(Group == "HGFpos_posrate",
                        "HGF-expressing", "HGF-negative"))

p4a <- ggplot(pos_long, aes(Gene, PosRate, fill = Group)) +
  geom_col(position = position_dodge(0.8), alpha = 0.9) +
  facet_wrap(~ Slide) +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c("HGF-expressing" = "#C73E1D",
                               "HGF-negative" = "#2E86AB")) +
  labs(title = "IL6-family receptor positive rates",
       x = NULL, y = "Positive rate") +
  theme_pub(9) + theme(legend.position = "top")

tf <- read.csv("results/TF_significant_final.csv")
tf_long <- tf %>%
  dplyr::select(TF, Slide1 = delta1, Slide2 = delta2) %>%
  pivot_longer(-TF, names_to = "Slide", values_to = "delta")
tf_long$TF <- factor(tf_long$TF, levels = tf$TF[order(tf$delta1)])

p4b <- ggplot(tf_long, aes(delta, TF, fill = delta)) +
  geom_col(position = position_dodge(0.8), alpha = 0.9, width = 0.7) +
  scale_fill_gradient2(low = "#2E86AB", mid = "white", high = "#C73E1D",
                       midpoint = 0) +
  geom_vline(xintercept = 0, color = "grey40") +
  labs(title = "TF activity: HGF-expressing vs HGF-low HSC",
       subtitle = "DoRothEA A/B/C regulons, |delta| > 0.3",
       x = "TF activity delta score", y = NULL) +
  theme_pub(9) + theme(legend.position = "none")

if (file.exists("results/Fig4c_SP3_GO.csv")) {
  sp3_go <- read.csv("results/Fig4c_SP3_GO.csv") %>% head(8)
} else {
  data("dorothea_hs", package = "dorothea")
  sp3_targets <- dorothea_hs %>%
    dplyr::filter(tf == "SP3", confidence %in% c("A","B","C")) %>% pull(target)
  gene_ids <- bitr(sp3_targets, fromType = "SYMBOL", toType = "ENTREZID",
                   OrgDb = org.Hs.eg.db)
  ego <- enrichGO(gene = gene_ids$ENTREZID, OrgDb = org.Hs.eg.db,
                  ont = "BP", pAdjustMethod = "BH",
                  pvalueCutoff = 0.1, readable = TRUE)
  sp3_go <- as.data.frame(ego) %>% head(8)
  write.csv(as.data.frame(ego), "results/Fig4c_SP3_GO.csv", row.names = FALSE)
}
sp3_go$Description <- reorder(sp3_go$Description, -log10(sp3_go$p.adjust))

p4c <- ggplot(sp3_go, aes(Description, -log10(p.adjust),
                          fill = -log10(p.adjust))) +
  geom_col(alpha = 0.9) +
  scale_fill_gradient(low = "#F4A582", high = "#C73E1D") +
  coord_flip() +
  labs(title = "SP3 target gene enrichment (GO BP)",
       x = NULL, y = "-log10(adj.P)") +
  theme_pub(9) +
  theme(legend.position = "none", axis.text.y = element_text(size = 7))

fig4 <- (p4a | p4b) / (p4c | plot_spacer()) + plot_annotation(tag_levels = "a")
safe_save(fig4, "Figure4", 12, 9)


# ============================================================
# Figure 5
# ============================================================
cat("\n[5/6] Figure 5 ...\n")

m <- readRDS("results/Module1_ROC_results_zscored.rds")
roc_cv <- pROC::roc(m$y, m$cv_pred, quiet = TRUE)
auc_cv <- as.numeric(pROC::auc(roc_cv))
ci_cv  <- as.numeric(pROC::ci.auc(roc_cv))

roc_cv_df <- data.frame(FPR = 1 - roc_cv$specificities,
                        TPR = roc_cv$sensitivities)

p5a <- ggplot(roc_cv_df, aes(FPR, TPR)) +
  geom_line(color = "#C73E1D", linewidth = 1.2) +
  geom_abline(intercept = 1, slope = 1, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey60") +
  annotate("text", x = 0.62, y = 0.12,
           label = sprintf("CV AUC = %.3f\n95%% CI: %.3f-%.3f",
                           auc_cv, ci_cv[1], ci_cv[3]),
           size = 3.2, color = "#C73E1D", hjust = 0) +
  labs(title = "Training performance",
       subtitle = "GSE135251, 10-fold CV",
       x = "1 - Specificity", y = "Sensitivity") +
  coord_fixed(xlim = c(0, 1), ylim = c(0, 1)) +
  theme_pub(10)

val <- readRDS("results/GSE49541_validation_unified_zscored.rds")
roc_val <- val$roc
auc_val <- as.numeric(val$auc)
ci_val  <- as.numeric(val$ci)

roc_val_df <- data.frame(FPR = 1 - roc_val$specificities,
                         TPR = roc_val$sensitivities)

df_dca <- data.frame(outcome = as.integer(val$group),
                     pred_prob = as.numeric(val$pred_prob))
df_dca <- df_dca[!is.na(df_dca$outcome) & !is.na(df_dca$pred_prob), ]

p5b <- ggplot(roc_val_df, aes(FPR, TPR)) +
  geom_line(color = "#C73E1D", linewidth = 1.2) +
  geom_abline(intercept = 1, slope = 1, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey60") +
  annotate("text", x = 0.62, y = 0.12,
           label = sprintf("AUC = %.3f\n95%% CI: %.3f-%.3f",
                           auc_val, ci_val[1], ci_val[3]),
           size = 3.2, color = "#C73E1D", hjust = 0) +
  labs(title = "External validation",
       subtitle = sprintf("GSE49541, N = %d (%d events)",
                          nrow(df_dca), sum(df_dca$outcome)),
       x = "1 - Specificity", y = "Sensitivity") +
  coord_fixed(xlim = c(0, 1), ylim = c(0, 1)) +
  theme_pub(10)

dca_obj <- dcurves::dca(outcome ~ pred_prob, data = df_dca)
dca_df  <- dca_obj$dca
dca_long <- dca_df %>%
  dplyr::select(threshold, variable, net_benefit) %>%
  dplyr::filter(variable %in% c("pred_prob", "all", "none")) %>%
  dplyr::mutate(variable = dplyr::recode(variable,
                                         "pred_prob" = "11-gene signature",
                                         "all" = "Treat all",
                                         "none" = "Treat none"))

p5c <- ggplot(dca_long, aes(threshold, net_benefit, color = variable)) +
  geom_line(linewidth = 1.1) +
  scale_color_manual(values = c("11-gene signature" = "#C73E1D",
                                "Treat all" = "grey40",
                                "Treat none" = "black")) +
  geom_hline(yintercept = 0, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey70") +
  coord_cartesian(xlim = c(0, 0.6), ylim = c(-0.05, 0.45)) +
  labs(title = "Decision curve analysis",
       subtitle = "External validation cohort",
       x = "Threshold probability", y = "Net benefit", color = NULL) +
  theme_pub(10) +
  theme(legend.position = c(0.75, 0.85),
        legend.background = element_rect(fill = "white", color = NA),
        legend.key.size = unit(0.4, "cm"),
        legend.text = element_text(size = 8))

fig5 <- (p5a | p5b | p5c) + plot_annotation(tag_levels = "a")
safe_save(fig5, "Figure5", 13, 4.5)

drugs <- read.csv("results/DGIdb_signature_drugs.csv")
target_drugs <- c("TRETINOIN", "BEXAROTENE", "NINTEDANIB ESYLATE",
                  "PEMIGATINIB", "ERDAFITINIB", "ALITRETINOIN")

plot_df <- drugs %>%
  dplyr::filter(approved, drug %in% target_drugs) %>%
  dplyr::mutate(Class = case_when(
    grepl("RETIN|BEXAR|ALITRET", drug) ~ "RXR/retinoid agonist",
    grepl("NINTED|PEMIG|ERDAFIT", drug) ~ "FGFR inhibitor",
    TRUE ~ "Other"
  ))

p6a <- ggplot(plot_df, aes(reorder(drug, score), score, fill = Class)) +
  geom_col(alpha = 0.9) +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c("RXR/retinoid agonist" = "#C73E1D",
                               "FGFR inhibitor" = "#2E86AB")) +
  coord_flip() +
  labs(title = "Candidate drugs targeting HGF+ HSC signature (DGIdb)",
       subtitle = "Approved compounds with pathway relevance",
       x = NULL, y = "DGIdb interaction score", fill = NULL) +
  theme_pub(10) + theme(legend.position = "top")

# ============================================================
# Figure S1: WGCNA module–trait correlation
# ============================================================
cat("[S1] WGCNA module–trait correlation ...\n")

wgcna <- readRDS("results/WGCNA_results.rds")
cor_mat <- wgcna$moduleTraitCor
pval_mat <- wgcna$moduleTraitPvalue

s1_df <- data.frame(
  Module = rownames(cor_mat),
  r = as.numeric(cor_mat[, 1]),
  P = as.numeric(pval_mat[, 1])
) %>%
  mutate(
    Sig = ifelse(P < 0.05, "Significant (P < 0.05)", "n.s.")
  ) %>%
  arrange(r)

pS1 <- ggplot(s1_df, aes(reorder(Module, r), r, fill = Sig)) +
  geom_col(alpha = 0.9, width = 0.7) +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c("Significant (P < 0.05)" = "#C73E1D",
                               "n.s." = "grey70")) +
  geom_hline(yintercept = 0, color = "grey40") +
  coord_flip() +
  labs(title = "WGCNA module–trait correlation",
       subtitle = "Pearson correlation between module eigengenes and fibrosis group",
       x = NULL, y = "Pearson r", fill = NULL) +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) +
  theme(legend.position = "top",
        plot.title = element_text(face = "bold"),
        plot.subtitle = element_text(size = 9, color = "grey30"))

pdf(file.path(OUT, "FigureS1.pdf"), width = 5, height = 5)
print(pS1)
dev.off()

png(file.path(OUT, "FigureS1.png"), width = 5, height = 5,
    units = "in", res = 300)
print(pS1)
dev.off()

cat("  ✅ FigureS1 saved\n")

# ============================================================
# Figure S2: Consensus feature selection (bar chart)
# ============================================================
cat("\n[S2] Consensus feature selection ...\n")

ml <- readRDS("results/Module1_ML_results.rds")

n_lasso <- length(ml$lasso_genes)
n_rf    <- length(ml$rf_genes)
n_svm   <- length(ml$svm_genes)
n_xgb   <- length(ml$xgb_genes)
n_core  <- length(ml$core_genes)

cat(sprintf("LASSO: %d, RF: %d, SVM: %d, XGB: %d\n",
            n_lasso, n_rf, n_svm, n_xgb))
cat(sprintf("Intersection: %d genes\n", n_core))

s2_df <- data.frame(
  Method = factor(c("LASSO", "Random forest", "SVM-RFE", "XGBoost",
                    "Intersection (11-gene)"),
                  levels = c("LASSO", "Random forest", "SVM-RFE", "XGBoost",
                             "Intersection (11-gene)")),
  N = c(n_lasso, n_rf, n_svm, n_xgb, n_core),
  Type = c("Individual", "Individual", "Individual", "Individual", "Consensus")
)

pS2 <- ggplot(s2_df, aes(reorder(Method, N), N, fill = Type)) +
  geom_col(alpha = 0.9, width = 0.7) +
  geom_text(aes(label = N), hjust = -0.3, size = 3.5) +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c("Individual" = "#92C5DE",
                               "Consensus" = "#C73E1D")) +
  coord_flip() +
  labs(title = "Consensus feature selection",
       subtitle = "11-gene signature = intersection of four algorithms",
       x = NULL, y = "Number of genes selected", fill = NULL) +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) +
  theme(legend.position = "top",
        plot.title = element_text(face = "bold"),
        plot.subtitle = element_text(size = 9, color = "grey30")) +
  coord_cartesian(ylim = c(0, max(s2_df$N) * 1.15))

pdf(file.path(OUT, "FigureS2.pdf"), width = 6, height = 4)
print(pS2)
dev.off()

    units = "in", res = 300)
print(pS2)
dev.off()

suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(tidyverse); library(org.Hs.eg.db)

# ---- 1. 找到文件 ----
# 先确认 sjfx4 路径，你自己电脑上的实际位置可能需要调整
sjfx4_dir <- "~/Desktop/sjfx4"          # ← 如果路径不同，改这一行
destfile <- file.path(sjfx4_dir, "GSE130970_all_sample_salmon_tximport_TPM_entrez_gene_ID.csv.gz")

# 如果上面路径不对，用下面这行先搜索文件在哪：
# list.files("~/Desktop", pattern = "GSE130970", recursive = TRUE, full.names = TRUE)

cat(sprintf("File exists: %s\n", file.exists(destfile)))
cat(sprintf("File size: %.1f MB\n", file.info(destfile)$size / 1024^2))

# ---- 2. 读取 ----
expr_raw <- read.csv(gzfile(destfile), row.names = 1, check.names = FALSE)
cat(sprintf("Matrix: %d genes x %d samples\n", nrow(expr_raw), ncol(expr_raw)))
cat(sprintf("First 5 row names: %s\n",
            paste(head(rownames(expr_raw), 5), collapse = ", ")))
cat(sprintf("First 3 col names: %s\n",
            paste(head(colnames(expr_raw), 3), collapse = ", ")))

# ---- 3. Entrez -> Symbol ----
entrez_ids <- rownames(expr_raw)
gene_symbols <- mapIds(org.Hs.eg.db,
                       keys = entrez_ids,
                       column = "SYMBOL",
                       keytype = "ENTREZID",
                       multiVals = "first")

expr_df <- as.data.frame(expr_raw)
expr_df$Symbol <- gene_symbols
expr_df <- expr_df[!is.na(expr_df$Symbol), ]
cat(sprintf("After removing NA symbols: %d rows\n", nrow(expr_df)))

# 去重：保留平均表达最高的行
expr_df$mean_expr <- rowMeans(expr_df[, -ncol(expr_df)])
expr_df <- expr_df[order(-expr_df$mean_expr), ]
expr_df <- expr_df[!duplicated(expr_df$Symbol), ]
expr_df$mean_expr <- NULL
rownames(expr_df) <- expr_df$Symbol
expr_df$Symbol <- NULL
cat(sprintf("After deduplication: %d genes\n", nrow(expr_df)))

# ---- 4. 11 基因覆盖 ----
ml <- readRDS("results/Module1_ML_results.rds")
core_genes <- ml$core_genes

present <- core_genes[core_genes %in% rownames(expr_df)]
missing <- setdiff(core_genes, rownames(expr_df))
cat(sprintf("\n=== 11-gene coverage in GSE130970 ===\n"))
cat(sprintf("Present: %d / 11\n", length(present)))
cat(sprintf("Present: %s\n", paste(present, collapse = ", ")))
cat(sprintf("Missing: %s\n", paste(missing, collapse = ", ")))

# ---- 5. 保存 ----
saveRDS(list(expr = expr_df, present = present, missing = missing),
        "results/GSE130970_TPM.rds")
cat("\n✅ Saved: results/GSE130970_TPM.rds\n")

# ============================================================
# GSE130970 外部验证（用 pheno$title 对齐）
# ============================================================
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(tidyverse); library(pROC); library(dcurves); library(GEOquery)
})

# ---- 1. 表达矩阵 ----
d <- readRDS("results/GSE130970_TPM.rds")
expr_df <- d$expr
cat(sprintf("Expression: %d genes x %d samples\n",
            nrow(expr_df), ncol(expr_df)))

# ---- 2. pheno ----
gse <- getGEO("GSE130970", GSEMatrix = TRUE, getGPL = FALSE)
pheno <- pData(gse[[1]])
cat(sprintf("Pheno: %d samples\n", nrow(pheno)))

# ---- 3. 用 title 对齐 ----
pheno$sample_id <- pheno$title
common <- intersect(colnames(expr_df), pheno$sample_id)
cat(sprintf("Matched samples: %d / %d\n", length(common), ncol(expr_df)))

# 对齐
idx_expr <- match(common, colnames(expr_df))
idx_pheno <- match(common, pheno$sample_id)
expr_aligned <- expr_df[, idx_expr, drop = FALSE]
pheno_aligned <- pheno[idx_pheno, ]

# ---- 4. 提取 fibrosis stage ----
# characteristics_ch1.7 是 fibrosis stage
fs_col <- grep("fibrosis stage", colnames(pheno), ignore.case = TRUE,
               value = TRUE)[1]
cat(sprintf("\nUsing column: %s\n", fs_col))

fs_raw <- pheno_aligned[[fs_col]]
fs_num <- as.numeric(gsub("[^0-9]", "", as.character(fs_raw)))
cat("Fibrosis stage distribution:\n"); print(table(fs_num, useNA = "ifany"))

group <- ifelse(fs_num >= 2, 1,
                ifelse(fs_num <= 1, 0, NA))
cat(sprintf("Low (F0-F1): %d, High (F2-F4): %d, NA: %d\n",
            sum(group == 0, na.rm = TRUE),
            sum(group == 1, na.rm = TRUE),
            sum(is.na(group))))

# ---- 5. 预测 ----
ml <- readRDS("results/Module1_ROC_results_zscored.rds")
lr_model <- ml$lr_model
core_genes <- ml$core_genes

expr_11 <- expr_aligned[core_genes, , drop = FALSE]
expr_z <- t(scale(t(expr_11)))
expr_z[is.na(expr_z)] <- 0

df_val <- as.data.frame(t(expr_z))
colnames(df_val) <- core_genes

cat(sprintf("\nDim df_val: %d x %d\n", nrow(df_val), ncol(df_val)))

pred_prob <- predict(lr_model, newdata = df_val, type = "response")

# ---- 6. AUC + Brier + DCA ----
keep <- !is.na(group) & !is.na(pred_prob)
roc_obj <- roc(group[keep], pred_prob[keep], quiet = TRUE)
auc_val <- as.numeric(auc(roc_obj))
ci_val  <- as.numeric(ci.auc(roc_obj))

cat(sprintf("\n========================================\n"))
cat(sprintf("=== GSE130970 EXTERNAL VALIDATION ===\n"))
cat(sprintf("========================================\n"))
cat(sprintf("N = %d (events = %d)\n", sum(keep), sum(group[keep] == 1)))
cat(sprintf("AUC = %.3f (95%% CI: %.3f-%.3f)\n", auc_val, ci_val[1], ci_val[3]))

df_brier <- data.frame(outcome = group[keep], pred_prob = pred_prob[keep])
brier <- mean((df_brier$pred_prob - df_brier$outcome)^2)
cat(sprintf("Brier score = %.3f\n", brier))

dca_obj <- dcurves::dca(outcome ~ pred_prob, data = df_brier)
dca_df  <- dca_obj$dca
sel <- dca_df %>%
  filter(variable == "pred_prob",
         round(threshold, 3) %in% c(0.2, 0.3, 0.4))
cat("\n=== DCA net benefit ===\n")
print(sel[, c("threshold", "net_benefit")])

# ---- 7. 保存 ----
saveRDS(list(
  roc = roc_obj, auc = auc_val, ci = ci_val,
  pred_prob = pred_prob, group = group,
  brier = brier, dca = dca_obj,
  n = sum(keep), events = sum(group[keep] == 1)
), "results/GSE130970_validation.rds")

cat("\n✅ Saved: results/GSE130970_validation.rds\n")

# ---- 3. 按真实分期看预测均值 ----

# ---- 4. 关键：用 z-score 平均法（不依赖 logistic）重算 AUC ----
d <- readRDS("results/GSE130970_TPM.rds")
expr_df <- d$expr
ml <- readRDS("results/Module1_ML_results.rds")
core_genes <- ml$core_genes

# 直接对 11 基因做 mean z-score
expr_11 <- expr_df[core_genes, , drop = FALSE]
expr_z <- t(scale(t(expr_11)))
expr_z[is.na(expr_z)] <- 0
sig_score <- colMeans(expr_z)

# 对齐分期
suppressPackageStartupMessages(library(GEOquery))
gse <- getGEO("GSE130970", GSEMatrix = TRUE, getGPL = FALSE)
pheno <- pData(gse[[1]])
pheno$sample_id <- pheno$title
common <- intersect(names(sig_score), pheno$sample_id)
idx_pheno <- match(common, pheno$sample_id)
fs_num <- as.numeric(gsub("[^0-9]", "",
                          pheno$`fibrosis stage:ch1`[idx_pheno]))
group <- as.numeric(fs_num >= 2)

roc_avg <- roc(group, sig_score[common], quiet = TRUE)
cat(sprintf("\n=== z-score 平均法 AUC = %.3f ===\n",
            as.numeric(auc(roc_avg))))


#####======Bug1:Z-score 信息泄露   修复 1：重跑主 CV，折内 z-score====####
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(tidyverse); library(caret); library(pROC)
})

data <- readRDS("results/GSE135251_DEG_results.rds")
ml   <- readRDS("results/Module1_ML_results.rds")
core_genes <- ml$core_genes
X <- ml$X
y <- ml$y

# ---- 修正版主 CV：折内 z-score ----
set.seed(123)
folds <- createFolds(y, k = 10, list = TRUE)
cv_pred_fixed <- numeric(length(y))

for (i in seq_along(folds)) {
  test_idx  <- folds[[i]]
  train_idx <- setdiff(seq_along(y), test_idx)
  
  X_tr <- X[train_idx, core_genes, drop = FALSE]
  X_te <- X[test_idx,  core_genes, drop = FALSE]
  
  # 折内 z-score
  X_tr_z <- scale(X_tr)
  mu  <- attr(X_tr_z, "scaled:center")
  sdv <- attr(X_tr_z, "scaled:scale")
  X_tr_z[is.na(X_tr_z)] <- 0
  
  X_te_z <- scale(X_te, center = mu, scale = sdv)
  X_te_z[is.na(X_te_z)] <- 0
  
  # 训练 logistic
  df_tr <- data.frame(X_tr_z, y = y[train_idx])
  m <- glm(y ~ ., data = df_tr, family = binomial)
  
  # 预测
  df_te <- data.frame(X_te_z)
  colnames(df_te) <- core_genes
  cv_pred_fixed[test_idx] <- predict(m, newdata = df_te, type = "response")
}

roc_fixed <- roc(y, cv_pred_fixed, quiet = TRUE)
ci_fixed  <- ci.auc(roc_fixed)
cat(sprintf("修正版 CV AUC = %.3f (95%% CI: %.3f-%.3f)\n",
            as.numeric(auc(roc_fixed)), ci_fixed[1], ci_fixed[3]))

# 保存
saveRDS(list(cv_pred = cv_pred_fixed, roc = roc_fixed,
             auc = as.numeric(auc(roc_fixed)), ci = ci_fixed),
        "results/Module1_cv_fixed.rds")

#####=======Bug 2：嵌套 CV 仍有 2 个问题 修复 2：重跑嵌套 CV，检查 fallback====####
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(tidyverse); library(caret); library(pROC)
  library(glmnet); library(randomForest); library(e1071); library(xgboost)
})

ml <- readRDS("results/Module1_ML_results.rds")
X <- ml$X
y <- ml$y

# SVM-RFE 用线性核
svm_rfe_fast <- function(X, y, n_features_target = 20) {
  X <- as.data.frame(X)
  features <- colnames(X)
  y_factor <- as.factor(y)
  while (length(features) > n_features_target) {
    fit <- svm(x = X[, features, drop = FALSE], y = y_factor, kernel = "linear")
    w <- t(fit$coefs) %*% fit$SV
    w <- abs(as.numeric(w))
    if (length(w) != length(features)) break
    names(w) <- features
    features <- features[-which.min(w)]
  }
  features
}

set.seed(123)
folds <- createFolds(y, k = 10, list = TRUE)
cv_pred_nested <- numeric(length(y))
gene_count_per_fold <- integer(length(folds))
fallback_count <- integer(length(folds))

for (i in seq_along(folds)) {
  test_idx  <- folds[[i]]
  train_idx <- setdiff(seq_along(y), test_idx)
  
  X_tr <- X[train_idx, , drop = FALSE]
  y_tr <- y[train_idx]
  
  # 折内 z-score
  X_tr_z <- scale(X_tr)
  mu  <- attr(X_tr_z, "scaled:center")
  sdv <- attr(X_tr_z, "scaled:scale")
  X_tr_z[is.na(X_tr_z)] <- 0
  
  # LASSO
  set.seed(100 + i)
  cv_l <- cv.glmnet(X_tr_z, y_tr, family = "binomial", alpha = 1, nfolds = 5)
  coef_l <- coef(cv_l, s = "lambda.min")
  g_lasso <- setdiff(rownames(coef_l)[which(coef_l[, 1] != 0)], "(Intercept)")
  
  # RF
  set.seed(200 + i)
  rf <- randomForest(x = X_tr_z, y = as.factor(y_tr), ntree = 500, importance = TRUE)
  imp <- importance(rf)
  g_rf <- rownames(imp)[order(imp[, "MeanDecreaseGini"], decreasing = TRUE)][1:min(30, ncol(X_tr_z))]
  
  # SVM-RFE
  set.seed(300 + i)
  g_svm <- tryCatch(svm_rfe_fast(X_tr_z, y_tr, 20), error = function(e) g_rf)
  
  # XGBoost
  set.seed(400 + i)
  dtrain <- xgb.DMatrix(data = X_tr_z, label = y_tr)
  xgbm <- xgb.train(
    params = list(objective = "binary:logistic", eval_metric = "auc",
                  max_depth = 3, eta = 0.1),
    data = dtrain, nrounds = 100, verbose = 0)
  imp_x <- xgb.importance(model = xgbm)
  g_xgb <- imp_x$Feature[1:min(30, ncol(X_tr_z))]
  
  # 交集
  core_i <- Reduce(intersect, list(g_lasso, g_rf, g_svm, g_xgb))
  
  if (length(core_i) < 3) {
    fallback_count[i] <- 1
    all_g <- unique(c(g_lasso, g_rf, g_svm, g_xgb))
    core_i <- all_g[1:min(15, length(all_g))]
  }
  gene_count_per_fold[i] <- length(core_i)
  
  # 训练 logistic
  df_tr <- data.frame(X_tr_z[, core_i, drop = FALSE], y = y_tr)
  m <- glm(y ~ ., data = df_tr, family = binomial)
  
  # 测试折标准化
  X_te <- X[test_idx, , drop = FALSE]
  X_te_z <- scale(X_te, center = mu, scale = sdv)
  X_te_z[is.na(X_te_z)] <- 0
  
  for (g in core_i) {
    if (!(g %in% colnames(X_te_z))) {
      X_te_z <- cbind(X_te_z, 0)
      colnames(X_te_z)[ncol(X_te_z)] <- g
    }
  }
  
  df_te <- data.frame(X_te_z[, core_i, drop = FALSE])
  cv_pred_nested[test_idx] <- predict(m, newdata = df_te, type = "response")
}

roc_nested <- roc(y, cv_pred_nested, quiet = TRUE)
ci_nested  <- ci.auc(roc_nested)

cat(sprintf("嵌套 CV AUC = %.3f (95%% CI: %.3f-%.3f)\n",
            as.numeric(auc(roc_nested)), ci_nested[1], ci_nested[3]))
cat(sprintf("每折基因数: %s\n", paste(gene_count_per_fold, collapse = ", ")))
cat(sprintf("Fallback 触发次数: %d / 10\n", sum(fallback_count)))

saveRDS(list(
  cv_pred = cv_pred_nested,
  roc = roc_nested,
  auc = as.numeric(auc(roc_nested)),
  ci = ci_nested,
  gene_count_per_fold = gene_count_per_fold,
  fallback_count = fallback_count
), "results/Module1_nested_cv_fixed.rds")

m <- readRDS("results/Module1_ROC_results_zscored.rds")$lr_model
coef_df <- data.frame(
  Gene = names(coef(m))[-1],
  Coefficient = round(coef(m)[-1], 4)
)
write.csv(coef_df, "results/model_coefficients.csv", row.names = FALSE)
print(coef_df)

####补充校准分析（审稿人可能会需要）#####
# 校准曲线
v <- readRDS("results/GSE49541_validation_unified_zscored.rds")
df <- data.frame(outcome = as.integer(v$group),
                 pred = as.numeric(v$pred_prob))
df <- df[!is.na(df$outcome), ]

df$bin <- cut(df$pred, breaks = seq(0, 1, 0.2), include.lowest = TRUE)
cal_df <- df %>%
  group_by(bin) %>%
  summarise(mean_pred = mean(pred),
            obs_rate  = mean(outcome),
            n = n(),
            .groups = "drop") %>%
  filter(n >= 3)

cat("=== Calibration ===\n")
print(cal_df)
# 校准截距/斜率
cal_model <- glm(outcome ~ qlogis(pred), data = df, family = binomial)
cat(sprintf("Calibration slope = %.3f\n", coef(cal_model)[2]))


#####Supplementary Figure S3：校准曲线####
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({library(tidyverse); library(ggplot2)})

v <- readRDS("results/GSE49541_validation_unified_zscored.rds")
df <- data.frame(outcome = as.integer(v$group), pred = as.numeric(v$pred_prob))
df <- df[!is.na(df$outcome), ]

df$bin <- cut(df$pred, breaks = seq(0, 1, 0.2), include.lowest = TRUE)
cal_df <- df %>%
  group_by(bin) %>%
  summarise(mean_pred = mean(pred), obs_rate = mean(outcome),
            n = n(), .groups = "drop") %>%
  filter(n >= 3)

pS3 <- ggplot(cal_df, aes(mean_pred, obs_rate)) +
  geom_abline(intercept = 0, slope = 1, linetype = "dashed", color = "grey60") +
  geom_point(aes(size = n), color = "#C73E1D", alpha = 0.85) +
  geom_line(color = "#C73E1D") +
  labs(title = "Calibration plot: 11-gene signature (GSE49541)",
       subtitle = sprintf("Brier score = %.3f; calibration slope = 0.685",
                          mean((df$pred - df$outcome)^2)),
       x = "Predicted probability", y = "Observed rate", size = "N") +
  coord_fixed(xlim = c(0, 1), ylim = c(0, 1)) +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) +
  theme(plot.title = element_text(face = "bold"))

pdf("figures/Figure2/FigureS3_calibration.pdf", width = 5, height = 5)
print(pS3)
dev.off()

png("figures/Figure2/FigureS3_calibration.png", width = 5, height = 5,
    units = "in", res = 300)
print(pS3)
dev.off()

cat("✅ Saved: FigureS3_calibration\n")

  cat(sprintf("  %s\n", f))
}



####Bug修复：完整嵌套 CV####
# ============================================================
# 完整嵌套 CV：折内重新跑 DEG + WGCNA + 4 算法 + logistic
# ============================================================
# ============================================================
# 完整嵌套 CV（修正版：解决 WGCNA cor() 冲突）
# ============================================================
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")

# ---- 关键：WGCNA 先加载 ----
suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(WGCNA)
})
enableWGCNAThreads()

suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(tidyverse); library(caret); library(pROC)
  library(limma); library(glmnet)
  library(randomForest); library(e1071); library(xgboost)
  library(org.Hs.eg.db)
})

# ---- 强制 WGCNA 的 cor 生效 ----
cor <- WGCNA::cor
assignInNamespace("cor", WGCNA::cor, ns = "WGCNA")

# ---- 加载数据 ----
data <- readRDS("results/GSE135251_DEG_results.rds")
log_expr <- data$log_expr
y <- as.numeric(data$fibro_stage >= 2)
cat(sprintf("Expression matrix: %d genes × %d samples\n",
            nrow(log_expr), ncol(log_expr)))

wgcna_saved <- readRDS("results/WGCNA_results.rds")
power_use <- wgcna_saved$power
cat(sprintf("Using pre-selected power = %d\n", power_use))

svm_rfe_fast <- function(X, y, n_target = 20) {
  X <- as.data.frame(X); features <- colnames(X); y_f <- as.factor(y)
  while (length(features) > n_target) {
    fit <- svm(x = X[, features, drop = FALSE], y = y_f, kernel = "linear")
    w <- abs(as.numeric(t(fit$coefs) %*% fit$SV))
    if (length(w) != length(features)) break
    names(w) <- features; features <- features[-which.min(w)]
  }
  features
}

set.seed(123)
folds <- createFolds(y, k = 10, list = TRUE)
cv_pred_full <- numeric(length(y))
gene_count   <- integer(length(folds))
candidates_per_fold <- vector("list", length(folds))
core_signatures     <- vector("list", length(folds))
for (i in seq_along(folds)) {
  cat(sprintf("\n=== Fold %d / 10 ===\n", i))
  test_idx  <- folds[[i]]
  train_idx <- setdiff(seq_along(y), test_idx)
  
  log_tr <- log_expr[, train_idx]
  y_tr   <- y[train_idx]
  
  # ---- 4.1 折内 DEG ----
  design <- model.matrix(~ y_tr)
  fit <- lmFit(log_tr, design)
  fit <- eBayes(fit)
  deg <- topTable(fit, coef = 2, number = Inf, sort.by = "P")
  deg_sig <- deg[abs(deg$logFC) > 0.5 & deg$adj.P.Val < 0.05, ]
  cat(sprintf("  In-fold DEGs: %d\n", nrow(deg_sig)))
  
  # ---- 4.2 折内 WGCNA ----
  gene_var <- apply(log_tr, 1, var)
  keep_genes <- names(sort(gene_var, decreasing = TRUE))[1:min(5000, length(gene_var))]
  log_tr_sub <- log_tr[keep_genes, ]
  
  datExpr <- t(log_tr_sub)
  gsg <- goodSamplesGenes(datExpr, verbose = 0)
  if (!gsg$allOK) datExpr <- datExpr[gsg$goodSamples, gsg$goodGenes]
  
  net <- blockwiseModules(
    datExpr, power = power_use,
    networkType = "signed", TOMType = "signed",
    minModuleSize = 30, mergeCutHeight = 0.25,
    numericLabels = TRUE, pamRespectsDendro = FALSE,
    saveTOMs = FALSE, verbose = 0
  )
  
  MEs <- net$MEs
  
  # ---- 关键修复：显式 WGCNA::cor ----
  cor_trait <- WGCNA::cor(MEs, y_tr)
  
  top_module <- colnames(MEs)[which.max(abs(cor_trait))]
  module_label <- as.numeric(gsub("ME", "", top_module))
  module_genes <- colnames(datExpr)[net$colors == module_label]
  cat(sprintf("  In-fold top module: %s (%d genes, r = %.3f)\n",
              top_module, length(module_genes),
              cor_trait[which.max(abs(cor_trait))]))
  
  # ---- 4.3 候选基因 ----
  candidates <- intersect(rownames(deg_sig), module_genes)
  if (length(candidates) < 20) {
    fallback <- rownames(deg_sig)[1:min(50, nrow(deg_sig))]
    candidates <- unique(c(candidates, fallback))
    candidates <- candidates[candidates %in% rownames(log_tr)]
    cat(sprintf("  Fallback: candidates expanded to %d\n", length(candidates)))
  }
  candidates_per_fold[[i]] <- candidates
  cat(sprintf("  In-fold candidates: %d\n", length(candidates)))
  
  # ---- 4.4 折内 z-score ----
  X_tr <- t(log_tr[candidates, , drop = FALSE])
  X_tr_z <- scale(X_tr)
  mu  <- attr(X_tr_z, "scaled:center")
  sdv <- attr(X_tr_z, "scaled:scale")
  X_tr_z[is.na(X_tr_z)] <- 0
  
  # ---- 4.5 四算法 ----
  set.seed(100 + i)
  cv_l <- cv.glmnet(X_tr_z, y_tr, family = "binomial", alpha = 1, nfolds = 5)
  coef_l <- coef(cv_l, s = "lambda.min")
  g_lasso <- setdiff(rownames(coef_l)[which(coef_l[,1] != 0)], "(Intercept)")
  
  set.seed(200 + i)
  rf <- randomForest(x = X_tr_z, y = as.factor(y_tr),
                     ntree = 500, importance = TRUE)
  imp <- importance(rf)
  g_rf <- rownames(imp)[order(imp[,"MeanDecreaseGini"],
                              decreasing = TRUE)][1:min(30, ncol(X_tr_z))]
  
  set.seed(300 + i)
  g_svm <- tryCatch(svm_rfe_fast(X_tr_z, y_tr, 20), error = function(e) g_rf)
  
  set.seed(400 + i)
  dtrain <- xgb.DMatrix(data = X_tr_z, label = y_tr)
  xgbm <- xgb.train(
    params = list(objective = "binary:logistic", eval_metric = "auc",
                  max_depth = 3, eta = 0.1),
    data = dtrain, nrounds = 100, verbose = 0)
  g_xgb <- xgb.importance(model = xgbm)$Feature[1:min(30, ncol(X_tr_z))]
  
  core_i <- Reduce(intersect, list(g_lasso, g_rf, g_svm, g_xgb))
  if (length(core_i) < 3) {
    all_g <- unique(c(g_lasso, g_rf, g_svm, g_xgb))
    core_i <- all_g[1:min(15, length(all_g))]
  }
  gene_count[i] <- length(core_i)
  core_signatures[[i]] <- core_i   # ← 新增
  cat(sprintf("  In-fold core signature: %d genes\n", length(core_i)))
  
  # ---- 4.6 Logistic ----
  df_tr <- data.frame(X_tr_z[, core_i, drop = FALSE], y = y_tr)
  m <- glm(y ~ ., data = df_tr, family = binomial)
  
  # ---- 4.7 测试折预测 ----
  X_te <- t(log_expr[candidates, test_idx, drop = FALSE])
  for (g in core_i) {
    if (!(g %in% colnames(X_te))) {
      X_te <- cbind(X_te, 0); colnames(X_te)[ncol(X_te)] <- g
    }
  }
  X_te_z <- scale(X_te, center = mu, scale = sdv)
  X_te_z[is.na(X_te_z)] <- 0
  
  df_te <- data.frame(X_te_z[, core_i, drop = FALSE])
  cv_pred_full[test_idx] <- predict(m, newdata = df_te, type = "response")
}

roc_full <- roc(y, cv_pred_full, quiet = TRUE)
ci_full  <- ci.auc(roc_full)
auc_full <- as.numeric(auc(roc_full))

cat(sprintf("\n========================================\n"))
cat(sprintf("=== FULLY NESTED CV AUC = %.3f (95%% CI: %.3f-%.3f) ===\n",
            auc_full, ci_full[1], ci_full[3]))
cat(sprintf("Per-fold signature size: %s\n",
            paste(gene_count, collapse = ", ")))
cat(sprintf("Median signature size: %d\n", median(gene_count)))

saveRDS(list(
  cv_pred = cv_pred_full, roc = roc_full, auc = auc_full, ci = ci_full,
  gene_count = gene_count, candidates_per_fold = candidates_per_fold,
  core_signatures = core_signatures,
  design = "full nested CV (DEG + WGCNA + 4-algorithm within each fold)"
), "results/Module1_nested_cv_FULL.rds")

cat("\n✅ Saved: results/Module1_nested_cv_FULL.rds\n")

         label = sprintf("Fixed CV AUC = %.3f\nFully nested CV AUC = %.3f",
                         as.numeric(auc(roc_fixed)),

         size = 3.5) +
  labs(title = "Comparison of fixed-signature and fully nested CV",
       subtitle = "GSE135251 training cohort",
       x = "1 - Specificity", y = "Sensitivity", color = NULL) +
  coord_fixed(xlim = c(0, 1), ylim = c(0, 1)) +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) +
  theme(legend.position = "top",
        plot.title = element_text(face = "bold"))

####Bug修复 ：TMM归一化敏感性分析####
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(tidyverse); library(edgeR); library(limma)
  library(org.Hs.eg.db); library(caret); library(pROC)
})

# 加载原始 count
data <- readRDS("results/GSE135251_aligned_data.rds")
count_matrix <- data$count_matrix
group <- data$group
y <- as.numeric(group == "High")

# ---- TMM 归一化 ----
dge <- DGEList(counts = count_matrix)
dge <- calcNormFactors(dge, method = "TMM")
log_expr_tmm <- log2(cpm(dge, log = FALSE) + 1)

# 保留原始流程的基因过滤
keep <- rowSums(count_matrix >= 10) >= 3
log_expr_tmm <- log_expr_tmm[keep, ]

# ---- 用 TMM 数据跑 WGCNA + DEG + 4 算法（简化：只验证 11 基因）----
suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(glmnet); library(randomForest); library(e1071); library(xgboost)
})

ml <- readRDS("results/Module1_ML_results.rds")
core_genes <- ml$core_genes

# 检查 11 基因在 TMM 数据中可用
sym <- mapIds(org.Hs.eg.db, keys = rownames(log_expr_tmm),
              column = "SYMBOL", keytype = "ENSEMBL", multiVals = "first")
valid <- !is.na(sym) & !duplicated(sym)
log_expr_sym <- log_expr_tmm[valid, ]
rownames(log_expr_sym) <- sym[valid]

present <- core_genes[core_genes %in% rownames(log_expr_sym)]
cat(sprintf("TMM 数据中 11 基因可用: %d / 11\n", length(present)))

# ---- 用 TMM 数据重跑固定 signature CV ----
set.seed(123)
folds <- createFolds(y, k = 10, list = TRUE)
cv_pred_tmm <- numeric(length(y))

for (i in seq_along(folds)) {
  test_idx <- folds[[i]]; train_idx <- setdiff(seq_along(y), test_idx)
  X_tr <- t(log_expr_sym[present, train_idx, drop = FALSE])
  X_te <- t(log_expr_sym[present, test_idx, drop = FALSE])
  X_tr_z <- scale(X_tr)
  mu <- attr(X_tr_z, "scaled:center")
  sdv <- attr(X_tr_z, "scaled:scale")
  X_tr_z[is.na(X_tr_z)] <- 0
  X_te_z <- scale(X_te, center = mu, scale = sdv)
  X_te_z[is.na(X_te_z)] <- 0
  df_tr <- data.frame(X_tr_z, y = y[train_idx])
  m <- glm(y ~ ., data = df_tr, family = binomial)
  df_te <- data.frame(X_te_z); colnames(df_te) <- present
  cv_pred_tmm[test_idx] <- predict(m, newdata = df_te, type = "response")
}

roc_tmm <- roc(y, cv_pred_tmm, quiet = TRUE)
ci_tmm  <- ci.auc(roc_tmm)
cat(sprintf("\nTMM-based CV AUC = %.3f (95%% CI: %.3f-%.3f)\n",
            as.numeric(auc(roc_tmm)), ci_tmm[1], ci_tmm[3]))

saveRDS(list(roc = roc_tmm, auc = as.numeric(auc(roc_tmm)), ci = ci_tmm),
        "results/Module1_cv_TMM.rds")


######跑稳定性分析（Table S12）#####
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages(library(tidyverse))

full <- readRDS("results/Module1_nested_cv_FULL.rds")
core_sigs <- full$core_signatures
gene_count <- full$gene_count

stopifnot(length(core_sigs) == 10)
stopifnot(all(sapply(core_sigs, length) > 0))

# ---- 标记哪些折触发了 fallback ----
fallback_fold <- which(gene_count == 15)   # 15 = fallback 特征值
cat(sprintf("Fallback folds: %s\n", paste(fallback_fold, collapse = ", ")))

# ---- 汇总 ----
all_genes <- sort(unique(unlist(core_sigs)))
stab_df <- data.frame(Gene = all_genes, stringsAsFactors = FALSE)
for (i in seq_along(core_sigs)) {
  stab_df[[paste0("Fold_", i)]] <- as.integer(all_genes %in% core_sigs[[i]])
}
stab_df$N_folds <- rowSums(stab_df[, grep("^Fold_", colnames(stab_df))])

# 只在"真交集折"中统计（排除 fallback 折）
real_folds <- setdiff(seq_along(core_sigs), fallback_fold)
stab_df$N_folds_real <- rowSums(stab_df[, paste0("Fold_", real_folds)])

stab_df <- stab_df[order(-stab_df$N_folds, -stab_df$N_folds_real, stab_df$Gene), ]
stab_df$Stable_80pct <- stab_df$N_folds >= 8

dir.create("results/supplementary", showWarnings = FALSE, recursive = TRUE)
write.csv(stab_df,
          "results/supplementary/TableS12_nested_CV_stability.csv",
          row.names = FALSE)

cat(sprintf("总基因数（出现在任一折）: %d\n", nrow(stab_df)))
cat(sprintf("在 >=8/10 折中选中的基因: %d\n", sum(stab_df$Stable_80pct)))
cat(sprintf("仅在真交集折中 >=5/7 的基因: %d\n", sum(stab_df$N_folds_real >= 5)))

cat("\n--- 稳定基因 (N_folds >= 8) ---\n")
print(stab_df[stab_df$Stable_80pct, c("Gene", "N_folds", "N_folds_real")])

####验证： OR = 1.54 vs 1.49 来源混乱####
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")

# 检查两个 RDS 到底存了哪个 OR
f1 <- "results/GSE292268_Stellate_enrichment_independent.rds"
if (file.exists(f1)) {
  e <- readRDS(f1)

####验证： NAFLD-only CV AUC = 0.821 未对齐###
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(tidyverse); library(caret); library(pROC); library(GEOquery)
  library(org.Hs.eg.db)
})

# 1. 重新从 GEO 拉对齐信息
gse <- getGEO("GSE135251", GSEMatrix = TRUE, getGPL = FALSE)
pheno_geo <- pData(gse[[1]])

# 2. 检查 stage 顺序是否和 RDS 一致（关键）
data <- readRDS("results/GSE135251_DEG_results.rds")
stage_rds <- data$fibro_stage
stage_geo <- as.numeric(as.character(pheno_geo[["fibrosis stage:ch1"]]))

cat(sprintf("stage 顺序完全一致: %s\n",
            all(stage_rds == stage_geo, na.rm = TRUE)))
cat(sprintf("不一致位置数: %d\n", sum(stage_rds != stage_geo, na.rm = TRUE)))

# 3. 如果一致 → control 行号才可信
ctrl_rows <- which(pheno_geo[["disease:ch1"]] == "Control")
cat(sprintf("Control 样本数（GEO）: %d\n", length(ctrl_rows)))

# 4. 用正确对齐重跑
log_expr <- data$log_expr
ml <- readRDS("results/Module1_ML_results.rds")
core_genes <- ml$core_genes

sym <- mapIds(org.Hs.eg.db, keys = rownames(log_expr),
              column = "SYMBOL", keytype = "ENSEMBL", multiVals = "first")
valid <- !is.na(sym) & !duplicated(sym)
log_expr_sym <- log_expr[valid, ]
rownames(log_expr_sym) <- sym[valid]

X_all <- t(log_expr_sym[core_genes, ])
y_all <- as.numeric(stage_rds >= 2)

run_cv <- function(X, y, k = 10, seed = 123) {
  set.seed(seed)
  X_z <- scale(X); X_z[is.na(X_z)] <- 0
  folds <- createFolds(y, k = k, list = TRUE)
  pred <- numeric(length(y))
  for (i in seq_along(folds)) {
    te <- folds[[i]]; tr <- setdiff(seq_along(y), te)
    m <- glm(y[tr] ~ ., data = data.frame(X_z[tr, , drop = FALSE]),
             family = binomial)
    pred[te] <- predict(m, newdata = data.frame(X_z[te, , drop = FALSE]),
                        type = "response")
  }
  as.numeric(auc(roc(y, pred, quiet = TRUE)))
}

keep <- !(seq_len(nrow(X_all)) %in% ctrl_rows)
auc_nafld <- run_cv(X_all[keep, ], y_all[keep])
cat(sprintf("\nNAFLD-only CV AUC = %.3f\n", auc_nafld))

theme_pub <- function(base = 9) {
  theme_classic(base_size = base) +
    theme(plot.title = element_text(size = base + 1, face = "bold"),
          plot.subtitle = element_text(size = base - 1, color = "grey30"),
          plot.tag = element_text(size = base + 2, face = "bold"))
}

       subtitle = sprintf("Orange = fibrotic region, Red = Stellate (n = %s, %.1f%%)",

  theme_pub(9)

cat(sprintf("[17] Brier = %.3f  (手稿: 0.206)\n", brier))

# ---- 2.5 DCA ----
suppressPackageStartupMessages(library(dcurves))
df_dca <- data.frame(outcome = val$group, pred_prob = val$pred_prob)

dca <- dcurves::dca(outcome ~ pred_prob, data = df_dca)$dca
sel <- dca[dca$variable == "pred_prob" &
             round(dca$threshold, 3) %in% c(0.2, 0.3, 0.4), ]
cat(sprintf("[18] DCA NB @20/30/40%% = %.3f / %.3f / %.3f  (手稿: 0.333, 0.272, 0.190)\n",
            sel$net_benefit[sel$threshold == 0.2],
            sel$net_benefit[sel$threshold == 0.3],
            sel$net_benefit[sel$threshold == 0.4]))

# ---- 2.6 校准斜率 ----
cal_model <- glm(outcome ~ qlogis(pred_prob), data = df_dca, family = binomial)
cat(sprintf("[19] Calibration slope = %.3f  (手稿: 0.685)\n",
            coef(cal_model)[2]))

# ---- 4.2 CHI3L1/ILDR1 在 nested CV 中被选中折数 ----
nested <- readRDS("results/Module1_nested_cv_FULL.rds")
core_sigs <- nested$core_signatures

# 11 基因符号 → Ensembl
sig11 <- c("EYA2","FERMT1","PYROXD2","EPB41L4A","CHI3L1",
           "CNKSR1","ILDR1","GSN","TMPRSS3","ESRRG","SULF2")
sig11_ens <- mapIds(org.Hs.eg.db, keys = sig11,
                    keytype = "SYMBOL", column = "ENSEMBL")

fold_hit <- sapply(sig11_ens, function(g)
  sum(sapply(core_sigs, function(s) g %in% s)))

cat(sprintf("[32] CHI3L1 折数 = %d / 10  (手稿: 9/10)\n",
            fold_hit["ENSG00000133048"]))
cat(sprintf("[33] ILDR1 折数 = %d / 10  (手稿: 1/10)\n",
            fold_hit["ENSG00000145103"]))

  dplyr::filter(grepl("[A-Z]", CellType),
                grepl("\\.", CellType) | grepl("[A-Z][a-z]", CellType),
                !CellType %in% c("h","j","i","e","c","a","b","d","f","g"))

cat(sprintf("清洗前 N = %d\n", nrow(merged)))
cat(sprintf("清洗后 N = %d\n", nrow(merged_clean)))

            ct$estimate, ct$p.value))

# ---- 2. CHI3L1 / ILDR1 修正 ----

sig11 <- c("EYA2","FERMT1","PYROXD2","EPB41L4A","CHI3L1",
           "CNKSR1","ILDR1","GSN","TMPRSS3","ESRRG","SULF2")
sig11_ens <- mapIds(org.Hs.eg.db, keys = sig11,
                    keytype = "SYMBOL", column = "ENSEMBL")

  sum(sapply(core_sigs, function(s) sig11_ens[g] %in% s)))

####空间生态位的生物学定义####
###1、Hep.4身份定义
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(Seurat); library(tidyverse); library(ggplot2)
})

# ---- 加载 CosMx ----
cosmx1 <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
col1 <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
ct <- cosmx1@meta.data[[col1]]
expr <- cosmx1[["RNA"]]$data
panel <- rownames(expr)

# ---- 定义 zone marker ----
zone_markers <- list(
  Zone1_Periportal = c("PCK1", "SDS", "CPS1", "ASS1", "G6PC",
                       "CYP2E1", "HAL", "FBP1"),
  Zone2_Midzonal   = c("HNF4A", "ALB", "APOA1", "APOB", "TTR"),
  Zone3_Central    = c("GLUL", "CYP1A2", "OAT", "CYP2E1", "AXIN2"),
  General_Hep      = c("ALB", "APOA1", "TTR", "TF", "HP", "FGA", "FGB")
)

# 检查 panel 覆盖
for (nm in names(zone_markers)) {
  present <- zone_markers[[nm]][zone_markers[[nm]] %in% panel]
  cat(sprintf("%s: %d/%d in panel: %s\n",
              nm, length(present), length(zone_markers[[nm]]),
              paste(present, collapse = ", ")))
}

# ---- Hep 亚型平均表达 ----
hep_types <- c("Hep.1", "Hep.2", "Hep.3", "Hep.4", "Hep.5", "Hep.6")
hep_types <- hep_types[hep_types %in% unique(ct)]

all_markers <- unique(unlist(zone_markers))
all_markers <- all_markers[all_markers %in% panel]

avg_by_type <- sapply(all_markers, function(g) {
  tapply(as.numeric(expr[g, ]), ct, mean, na.rm = TRUE)
})

cat("\n=== Hep 亚型 marker 平均表达 ===\n")
print(round(avg_by_type[hep_types, ], 3))

# ---- Zone score per Hep subtype ----
zone_scores <- sapply(names(zone_markers), function(z) {
  g <- zone_markers[[z]][zone_markers[[z]] %in% panel]
  if (length(g) == 0) return(rep(NA, length(hep_types)))
  sapply(hep_types, function(h) {
    idx <- which(ct == h)
    mean(colMeans(expr[g, idx, drop = FALSE]))
  })
})
rownames(zone_scores) <- hep_types

cat("\n=== Hep 亚型 zone 评分 ===\n")
print(round(zone_scores, 3))

# ---- 可视化 ----
zone_df <- as.data.frame(zone_scores) %>%
  tibble::rownames_to_column("HepType") %>%
  pivot_longer(-HepType, names_to = "Zone", values_to = "Score")

p_zone <- ggplot(zone_df, aes(HepType, Score, fill = Zone)) +
  geom_col(position = "dodge", alpha = 0.9) +
  labs(title = "Hep subtype zone signature scores",
       x = NULL, y = "Mean zone marker expression") +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) +
  theme(legend.position = "top")

ggsave("figures/Figure2/Fig_Hep_zone_scores.pdf", p_zone,
       width = 8, height = 4)
print(p_zone)

# ---- 保存 ----
saveRDS(list(
  avg_by_type = avg_by_type,
  zone_scores = zone_scores,
  zone_markers = zone_markers
), "results/Hep4_zone_identity.rds")

cat("\n✅ Saved: results/Hep4_zone_identity.rds\n")

# ============================================================
# 第一段：CosMx Hep.4 DEG

suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(Seurat); library(tidyverse)
})

setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")

# ---- 加载 CosMx ----
cat("加载 CosMx...\n")
cosmx1 <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
cat(sprintf("CosMx: %d cells\n", ncol(cosmx1)))

col1 <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
ct <- cosmx1@meta.data[[col1]]

# ---- 保留高分化肝细胞 ----
hep_keep <- c("Hep.1", "Hep.3", "Hep.4", "Hep.5")
hep_idx <- which(ct %in% hep_keep)
cat(sprintf("Hep 亚型细胞数: %d\n", length(hep_idx)))

hep_obj <- subset(cosmx1, cells = colnames(cosmx1)[hep_idx])
hep_obj$hep_subtype <- ct[hep_idx]

# 立刻释放原对象
rm(cosmx1); gc()

# ---- DEG ----
Idents(hep_obj) <- hep_obj$hep_subtype
cat("跑 DEG...\n")

deg <- FindMarkers(hep_obj, ident.1 = "Hep.4",
                   ident.2 = c("Hep.1", "Hep.3", "Hep.5"),
                   logfc.threshold = 0.3,
                   min.pct = 0.1,
                   test.use = "wilcox")

deg$gene <- rownames(deg)
deg_up   <- deg[deg$p_val_adj < 0.05 & deg$avg_log2FC > 0.3, ]
deg_down <- deg[deg$p_val_adj < 0.05 & deg$avg_log2FC < -0.3, ]

cat(sprintf("\nHep.4 上调基因: %d\n", nrow(deg_up)))
cat(sprintf("Hep.4 下调基因: %d\n", nrow(deg_down)))

cat("\n=== Hep.4 上调 top 20 ===\n")
print(head(deg_up[order(-deg_up$avg_log2FC),
                  c("gene", "avg_log2FC", "pct.1", "pct.2", "p_val_adj")], 20))

# ---- 保存 ----
saveRDS(list(
  deg_up = deg_up,
  deg_down = deg_down,
  deg_all = deg
), "results/Hep4_CosMx_DEG.rds")

cat(sprintf("\n✅ Saved: results/Hep4_CosMx_DEG.rds (%.1f MB)\n",
            file.info("results/Hep4_CosMx_DEG.rds")$size / 1024^2))

# 清掉 CosMx 中间对象
rm(hep_obj, deg); gc()

  library(Seurat); library(tidyverse)

# 重新加载 CosMx 的 hep_obj
cosmx1 <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
col1 <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"

            min(data_mat), max(data_mat), median(data_mat)))

# ---- 检查 counts layer ----
if ("counts" %in% Layers(hep_obj[["RNA"]])) {
  cat("\n=== counts layer ===\n")

  cat(sprintf("Min: %.2f, Max: %.2f, Median: %.2f\n",
              min(counts_mat), max(counts_mat), median(counts_mat)))

cat("\n=== 关键基因（前 5 个细胞）===\n")
for (g in c("MALAT1", "APOA1", "TTR", "GLUL")) {
  if (g %in% rownames(hep_obj)) {
    cat(sprintf("\n--- %s ---\n", g))
    cat(sprintf("  data:   %s\n",
                paste(round(head(data_mat[g, ], 5), 2), collapse = ", ")))
    if (exists("counts_mat")) {
      cat(sprintf("  counts: %s\n",
                  paste(head(counts_mat[g, ], 5), collapse = ", ")))
    }
  }
}

# ============================================================
# 第一段修复版：先 NormalizeData，再 FindMarkers
# ============================================================
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(Seurat); library(tidyverse)
})

# ---- 加载 CosMx ----
cosmx1 <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
col1 <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
ct <- cosmx1@meta.data[[col1]]

hep_idx <- which(ct %in% c("Hep.1", "Hep.3", "Hep.4", "Hep.5"))
hep_obj <- subset(cosmx1, cells = colnames(cosmx1)[hep_idx])
hep_obj$hep_subtype <- ct[hep_idx]

rm(cosmx1); gc()

# ---- 关键修复：NormalizeData ----
cat("NormalizeData...\n")
hep_obj <- NormalizeData(hep_obj, normalization.method = "LogNormalize",
                         scale.factor = 10000, verbose = FALSE)

# ---- 检查标准化后数值 ----
cat("\n=== 标准化后 data layer ===\n")
data_norm <- hep_obj[["RNA"]]$data
cat(sprintf("Min: %.2f, Max: %.2f, Median: %.2f\n",
            min(data_norm), max(data_norm), median(data_norm)))

# ---- 跑 DEG ----
Idents(hep_obj) <- hep_obj$hep_subtype
cat("\n跑 DEG...\n")

deg <- FindMarkers(hep_obj, ident.1 = "Hep.4",
                   ident.2 = c("Hep.1", "Hep.3", "Hep.5"),
                   logfc.threshold = 0.25,
                   min.pct = 0.1,
                   test.use = "wilcox")

deg$gene <- rownames(deg)
deg_up   <- deg[deg$p_val_adj < 0.05 & deg$avg_log2FC > 0.25, ]
deg_down <- deg[deg$p_val_adj < 0.05 & deg$avg_log2FC < -0.25, ]

cat(sprintf("\nHep.4 上调基因: %d\n", nrow(deg_up)))
cat(sprintf("Hep.4 下调基因: %d\n", nrow(deg_down)))

cat("\n=== Hep.4 上调 top 20 ===\n")
print(head(deg_up[order(-deg_up$avg_log2FC),
                  c("gene","avg_log2FC","pct.1","pct.2","p_val_adj")], 20))

cat("\n=== Hep.4 下调 top 20 ===\n")
print(head(deg_down[order(deg_down$avg_log2FC),
                    c("gene","avg_log2FC","pct.1","pct.2","p_val_adj")], 20))

# ---- 保存（覆盖旧文件）----
saveRDS(list(deg_up = deg_up, deg_down = deg_down, deg_all = deg),
        "results/Hep4_CosMx_DEG.rds")
cat(sprintf("\n✅ Saved: results/Hep4_CosMx_DEG.rds (%.1f MB)\n",
            file.info("results/Hep4_CosMx_DEG.rds")$size / 1024^2))

rm(hep_obj, deg); gc()

# ---- 载入修复后的 DEG ----
deg_res <- readRDS("results/Hep4_CosMx_DEG.rds")
deg_up   <- deg_res$deg_up
deg_down <- deg_res$deg_down

cat(sprintf("deg_up: %d, deg_down: %d\n", nrow(deg_up), nrow(deg_down)))

# ---- signature 交集 ----
hep4_markers <- deg_up$gene[1:min(30, nrow(deg_up))]
hep4_markers_in_sc <- hep4_markers[hep4_markers %in% rownames(hep_sc)]
cat(sprintf("Hep.4 signature 交集: %d / %d\n",
            length(hep4_markers_in_sc), length(hep4_markers)))
cat("交集基因:\n"); print(hep4_markers_in_sc)

# ---- 标准化 ----
hep_sc <- NormalizeData(hep_sc, verbose = FALSE)

# ---- signature 打分 ----
hep_sc <- AddModuleScore(hep_sc, features = list(hep4_markers_in_sc),
                         name = "Hep4_sig", nbin = 24)

# ---- 聚类 ----
hep_sc <- FindVariableFeatures(hep_sc, verbose = FALSE)
hep_sc <- ScaleData(hep_sc, verbose = FALSE)
hep_sc <- RunPCA(hep_sc, verbose = FALSE)
hep_sc <- FindNeighbors(hep_sc, dims = 1:15, verbose = FALSE)
hep_sc <- FindClusters(hep_sc, resolution = 0.3, verbose = FALSE)

# ---- cluster score ----
cluster_scores <- hep_sc@meta.data %>%
  group_by(seurat_clusters) %>%
  summarise(N = n(),
            Hep4_score = mean(Hep4_sig1),
            .groups = "drop") %>%
  arrange(desc(Hep4_score))

cat("\n=== scRNA cluster Hep.4 signature score ===\n")
print(cluster_scores)

# ---- zone 评分 ----
zone_markers_sc <- list(
  Zone1 = c("PCK1", "SDS", "CPS1", "ASS1", "G6PC", "FBP1", "HAL"),
  Zone2 = c("HNF4A", "ALB", "APOA1", "APOB", "TTR"),
  Zone3 = c("GLUL", "CYP1A2", "OAT", "CYP2E1", "AXIN2")
)

zone_by_cluster <- sapply(names(zone_markers_sc), function(z) {
  g <- zone_markers_sc[[z]][zone_markers_sc[[z]] %in% rownames(hep_sc)]
  if (length(g) == 0) return(rep(NA, nlevels(hep_sc$seurat_clusters)))
  sapply(levels(hep_sc$seurat_clusters), function(cl) {
    idx <- which(hep_sc$seurat_clusters == cl)
    mean(colMeans(hep_sc[["RNA"]]$data[g, idx, drop = FALSE]))
  })
})
rownames(zone_by_cluster) <- levels(hep_sc$seurat_clusters)

cat("\n=== scRNA cluster zone 评分 ===\n")
print(round(zone_by_cluster, 3))

# ---- 综合 + 保存 ----
final_df <- data.frame(
  Cluster = rownames(zone_by_cluster),
  N = as.numeric(table(hep_sc$seurat_clusters)),
  Hep4_score = cluster_scores$Hep4_score[match(rownames(zone_by_cluster),
                                               cluster_scores$seurat_clusters)],
  zone_by_cluster
)
cat("\n=== 综合表 ===\n"); print(final_df)

top_cluster <- final_df$Cluster[which.max(final_df$Hep4_score)]
cat(sprintf("\n🎯 Hep.4 在 scRNA 里对应 cluster: %s\n", top_cluster))
cat(sprintf("   该 cluster 的 Zone1/2/3 评分: %s\n",
            paste(round(zone_by_cluster[top_cluster, ], 3), collapse = " / ")))

write.csv(final_df, "results/Hep4_scRNA_identity_mapping.csv", row.names = FALSE)
saveRDS(list(
  cluster_scores = cluster_scores,
  zone_by_cluster = zone_by_cluster,
  final_df = final_df,
  top_cluster = top_cluster,
  hep4_markers_in_sc = hep4_markers_in_sc
), "results/Hep4_scRNA_identity.rds")
cat("\n✅ Saved\n")

rm(list = ls()); gc()

suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(tidyverse); library(ggplot2); library(patchwork)
})

OUT <- "figures/Figure2"
dir.create(OUT, showWarnings = FALSE, recursive = TRUE)

# ---- 载入数据 ----
res <- readRDS("results/Hep4_scRNA_identity.rds")
final_df <- res$final_df
top_cluster <- res$top_cluster

cat(sprintf("Top cluster: %s\n", top_cluster))
print(final_df)

# ---- 标记 top cluster ----
final_df$Is_Top <- ifelse(final_df$Cluster == top_cluster, "Hep.4-mapped", "Other")

# ============================================================
# Panel a: Hep.4 signature score
# ============================================================
final_df$a_ord <- reorder(final_df$Cluster, final_df$Hep4_score)

p_a <- ggplot(final_df, aes(a_ord, Hep4_score, fill = Is_Top)) +
  geom_col(alpha = 0.9, width = 0.75) +
  geom_text(aes(label = sprintf("n=%d", N)),
            hjust = -0.15, size = 2.5, color = "grey30") +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c("Hep.4-mapped" = "#C73E1D",
                               "Other" = "grey70")) +
  coord_flip() +
  labs(title = "Hep.4 signature score across scRNA hepatocyte clusters",
       subtitle = sprintf("Top cluster: %s (score = %.3f)",
                          top_cluster,
                          final_df$Hep4_score[final_df$Cluster == top_cluster]),
       x = "scRNA cluster", y = "Mean Hep.4 signature score") +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) +
  theme(legend.position = "none",
        plot.title = element_text(face = "bold", size = 11),
        plot.subtitle = element_text(size = 9, color = "grey30")) +
  coord_cartesian(xlim = c(NA, NA))

# ============================================================
# Panel b: Zone 1/2/3 scores per cluster
# ============================================================
zone_long <- final_df %>%
  dplyr::select(Cluster, Zone1, Zone2, Zone3, Is_Top) %>%
  pivot_longer(cols = c(Zone1, Zone2, Zone3),
               names_to = "Zone", values_to = "Score")

# 排序：按 zone 平均分
zone_long$Cluster <- factor(zone_long$Cluster, levels = final_df$Cluster)

p_b <- ggplot(zone_long, aes(Cluster, Score, fill = Zone)) +
  geom_col(position = position_dodge(0.8), alpha = 0.9, width = 0.75) +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c("Zone1" = "#2E86AB",
                               "Zone2" = "#A23B72",
                               "Zone3" = "#F18F01"),
                    labels = c("Zone1" = "Zone 1 (periportal)",
                               "Zone2" = "Zone 2 (midzonal)",
                               "Zone3" = "Zone 3 (central)")) +
  # 高亮 top cluster
  geom_vline(xintercept = which(final_df$Cluster == top_cluster),
             linetype = "dashed", color = "#C73E1D", linewidth = 0.7) +
  labs(title = "Zone signature scores per scRNA cluster",
       subtitle = sprintf("Dashed line: Hep.4-mapped cluster %s", top_cluster),
       x = "scRNA cluster", y = "Mean zone marker expression",
       fill = NULL) +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) +
  theme(legend.position = "top",
        legend.key.size = unit(0.4, "cm"),
        legend.text = element_text(size = 8),
        plot.title = element_text(face = "bold", size = 11),
        plot.subtitle = element_text(size = 9, color = "grey30"),
        axis.text.x = element_text(angle = 45, hjust = 1))

  plot_annotation(tag_levels = "a") &
  theme(plot.tag = element_text(size = 12, face = "bold"))

# ============================================================

       width = 8, height = 9, device = "pdf")

       width = 8, height = 9, dpi = 300, device = "png")
