####MET 通路激活验证####
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(Seurat); library(tidyverse)
})

cosmx1 <- readRDS("data/GSM8855042_Slide1_SeuratObject.RDS")
panel <- rownames(cosmx1[["RNA"]])

# MET 通路关键基因
met_pathway <- list(
  Receptor   = c("MET"),
  MAPK_ERK   = c("MAPK1","MAPK3","FOS","JUN","EGR1","DUSP1","DUSP6","SPRY2"),
  PI3K_AKT   = c("PIK3CA","PIK3CB","AKT1","MTOR","RPS6KB1","FOXO1","FOXO3"),
  STAT3      = c("STAT3","SOCS3","PIM1","MYC","BCL2","MCL1"),
  MET_targets= c("MMP2","MMP9","MMP14","SPP1","VIM","CDH2","SNAI1")
)

cat("=== MET 通路基因 panel 覆盖 ===\n")
for (nm in names(met_pathway)) {
  g <- met_pathway[[nm]]
  present <- g[g %in% panel]
  cat(sprintf("%s: %d/%d → %s\n",
              nm, length(present), length(g),
              paste(present, collapse = ", ")))
}
# ============================================================
# #2 MET 通路激活：HGF+ HSC 邻近 vs 远离的 Hep.4
# ============================================================
suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(Seurat); library(tidyverse)
})

col1 <- "RNA_nbclust_8b0a3ae1.8754.4ba9.8269.45e2cf2e855a_1_clusters"
ct   <- cosmx1@meta.data[[col1]]
expr <- cosmx1[["RNA"]]$data
coords <- cosmx1@meta.data[, c("x_slide_mm", "y_slide_mm", "fov")]

stellate_idx <- which(ct == "Stellate.cells")
hgf_expr     <- as.numeric(expr["HGF", ])
hgf_pos      <- stellate_idx[hgf_expr[stellate_idx] > 0]
hep4_idx     <- which(ct == "Hep.4")

cat(sprintf("HGF+ HSC: %d\n", length(hgf_pos)))
cat(sprintf("Hep.4: %d\n", length(hep4_idx)))

# 计算每个 Hep.4 到最近 HGF+ HSC 的距离
compute_nn_dist <- function(src_idx, tgt_idx, coords) {
  out <- rep(NA_real_, length(src_idx))
  for (f in unique(coords$fov)) {
    s <- src_idx[coords$fov[src_idx] == f]
    t <- tgt_idx[coords$fov[tgt_idx] == f]
    if (length(s) == 0 || length(t) == 0) next
    sc <- coords[s, ]; tc <- coords[t, ]
    for (i in seq_along(s)) {
      d <- sqrt((tc$x_slide_mm - sc$x_slide_mm[i])^2 +
                  (tc$y_slide_mm - sc$y_slide_mm[i])^2)
      out[which(src_idx == s[i])] <- min(d) * 1000
    }
  }
  out
}

dist_to_hgf <- compute_nn_dist(hep4_idx, hgf_pos, coords)
cat(sprintf("距离: median = %.1f µm, range = %.1f-%.1f\n",
            median(dist_to_hgf, na.rm = TRUE),
            min(dist_to_hgf, na.rm = TRUE),
            max(dist_to_hgf, na.rm = TRUE)))

is_near <- dist_to_hgf < 50
is_far  <- dist_to_hgf > 100

cat(sprintf("Near (<50 µm): %d\n", sum(is_near, na.rm = TRUE)))
cat(sprintf("Far  (>100 µm): %d\n", sum(is_far, na.rm = TRUE)))

hep4_expr <- as.matrix(expr[, hep4_idx])

# MET 表达
met_expr <- as.numeric(hep4_expr["MET", ])
near_met <- met_expr[is_near & !is.na(is_near)]
far_met  <- met_expr[is_far  & !is.na(is_far)]

cat(sprintf("\n=== MET 表达 ===\n"))
cat(sprintf("Near: mean = %.3f, pos = %.3f\n", mean(near_met), mean(near_met > 0)))
cat(sprintf("Far:  mean = %.3f, pos = %.3f\n", mean(far_met),  mean(far_met > 0)))
w_met <- wilcox.test(near_met, far_met)
cat(sprintf("Wilcoxon p = %.2e\n", w_met$p.value))

# 通路评分
pathway_genes <- list(
  MAPK_ERK  = c("MAPK1","MAPK3","FOS","JUN","EGR1","DUSP1"),
  PI3K_AKT  = c("PIK3CA","PIK3CB","AKT1","MTOR","RPS6KB1","FOXO1"),
  STAT3     = c("STAT3","SOCS3","PIM1","MYC"),
  MET_target= c("MMP2","MMP9","MMP14","SPP1","VIM","CDH2","SNAI1")
)

cat("\n=== 通路评分 ===\n")
score_results <- data.frame()
for (pw in names(pathway_genes)) {
  g <- pathway_genes[[pw]][pathway_genes[[pw]] %in% rownames(hep4_expr)]
  if (length(g) < 3) { cat(sprintf("%s: 基因太少\n", pw)); next }
  score_pw <- colMeans(hep4_expr[g, , drop = FALSE])
  near_s <- score_pw[is_near & !is.na(is_near)]
  far_s  <- score_pw[is_far  & !is.na(is_far)]
  p_val  <- wilcox.test(near_s, far_s)$p.value
  cat(sprintf("%s (%d): Near=%.3f, Far=%.3f, Delta=%+.3f, p=%.2e\n",
              pw, length(g), mean(near_s), mean(far_s),
              mean(near_s) - mean(far_s), p_val))
  score_results <- rbind(score_results, data.frame(
    Pathway = pw, Near = round(mean(near_s), 3),
    Far = round(mean(far_s), 3),
    Delta = round(mean(near_s) - mean(far_s), 3),
    P = p_val))
}

saveRDS(list(dist_to_hgf = dist_to_hgf, is_near = is_near, is_far = is_far,
             met_expr = met_expr, w_met = w_met, score_results = score_results),
        "results/MET_pathway_activation.rds")

cat("\n✅ Saved\n")

##Near/Far 细胞数不对称 补充分析###
# ============================================================
# 敏感性分析 1：多个距离阈值
# ============================================================
suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(Seurat); library(tidyverse)
})

# 已加载 cosmx1, expr, hep4_idx, dist_to_hgf, met_expr

thresholds <- list(
  c(30, 150),
  c(50, 100),
  c(75, 100),
  c(50, 150)
)

cat("=== 多阈值敏感性分析 ===\n")
sens <- data.frame()
for (th in thresholds) {
  near_i <- dist_to_hgf < th[1]
  far_i  <- dist_to_hgf > th[2]
  
  if (sum(near_i, na.rm=TRUE) < 30 | sum(far_i, na.rm=TRUE) < 30) next
  
  nm <- met_expr[near_i & !is.na(near_i)]
  fm <- met_expr[far_i  & !is.na(far_i)]
  p  <- wilcox.test(nm, fm)$p.value
  
  cat(sprintf("Near<%d vs Far>%d: n=%d/%d, MET=%.3f/%.3f, p=%.3f\n",
              th[1], th[2], length(nm), length(fm),
              mean(nm), mean(fm), p))
  
  sens <- rbind(sens, data.frame(
    Near_um = th[1], Far_um = th[2],
    N_near = length(nm), N_far = length(fm),
    MET_near = round(mean(nm), 3),
    MET_far = round(mean(fm), 3),
    P = p
  ))
}
print(sens)
write.csv(sens, "results/MET_sensitivity_thresholds.csv", row.names = FALSE)

# ============================================================

               method = "spearman")
cat(sprintf("\n=== 连续分析 ===\n"))
cat(sprintf("Spearman rho (distance vs MET) = %.3f, p = %.2e\n",
            ct$estimate, ct$p.value))
cat("预期：rho < 0（距离越近，MET 越高）\n")

cosmx2 <- readRDS("data/GSM8855043_Slide2_SeuratObject.RDS")

###补充分析 效应量偏小（Delta 0.07–0.10）补法：Cohen's d + permutation test
# Cohen's d
cohens_d <- function(x, y) {
  (mean(x) - mean(y)) / sqrt((var(x) + var(y)) / 2)
}

cat(sprintf("MET Cohen's d: %.3f\n",
            cohens_d(near_met, far_met)))

# Permutation test（打乱距离标签，看 MET 差异是否随机）
set.seed(123)
n_perm <- 1000
perm_delta <- numeric(n_perm)
for (i in 1:n_perm) {
  shuffled <- sample(c(near_met, far_met))
  perm_delta[i] <- mean(shuffled[1:length(near_met)]) -
    mean(shuffled[(length(near_met)+1):length(shuffled)])
}
obs_delta <- mean(near_met) - mean(far_met)
p_perm <- mean(abs(perm_delta) >= abs(obs_delta))

cat(sprintf("Observed delta: %.3f\n", obs_delta))
cat(sprintf("Null delta mean: %.3f, SD: %.3f\n",
            mean(perm_delta), sd(perm_delta)))
cat(sprintf("Permutation p = %.4f\n", p_perm))

##补充分析 Hep.4 异质性分析###
# 比较 near vs far 的 Hep.4 是否在 UMAP 上分开
hep4_near <- hep4_idx[is_near & !is.na(is_near)]
hep4_far  <- hep4_idx[is_far & !is.na(is_far)]

# 简单：比较两组的基础肝细胞 marker
basic_markers <- c("ALB","APOA1","TTR","APOB")
for (g in basic_markers) {
  if (g %in% rownames(expr)) {
    x <- as.numeric(expr[g, ])
    cat(sprintf("%s: Near=%.3f, Far=%.3f, p=%.3f\n",
                g,
                mean(x[hep4_near]),
                mean(x[hep4_far]),
                wilcox.test(x[hep4_near], x[hep4_far])$p.value))
  }
}

            mean(nFeature[far_idx]), median(nFeature[far_idx])))

###深度匹配验证##
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(Seurat); library(tidyverse)
})

# ---- 深度匹配（按 nCount 匹配）----
set.seed(123)
nCount_near <- nCount[near_idx]
nCount_far  <- nCount[far_idx]

# 对每个 near 细胞，在 far 中找一个 nCount 相近的
matched_far <- c()
for (nc in nCount_near) {
  cand <- which(abs(nCount_far - nc) < 50)  # ±50 转录本
  if (length(cand) > 0) {
    matched_far <- c(matched_far, sample(cand, 1))
  }
}

# 匹配后重新比较 MET
met_near_matched <- met_expr[near_idx][1:length(matched_far)]
met_far_matched  <- met_expr[far_idx][matched_far]

cat(sprintf("匹配后: Near n=%d, Far n=%d\n",
            length(met_near_matched), length(met_far_matched)))
cat(sprintf("nCount 匹配后: Near=%.0f, Far=%.0f\n",
            mean(nCount[near_idx][1:length(matched_far)]),
            mean(nCount[far_idx][matched_far])))
cat(sprintf("MET: Near=%.3f, Far=%.3f\n",
            mean(met_near_matched), mean(met_far_matched)))
cat(sprintf("Wilcoxon p = %.3f\n",
            wilcox.test(met_near_matched, met_far_matched)$p.value))

# 同样匹配后比较 MAPK 通路
mapk_genes <- c("FOS","JUN","DUSP1","DUSP6","SPRY2")
mapk_genes <- mapk_genes[mapk_genes %in% rownames(hep4_expr)]
mapk_score <- colMeans(hep4_expr[mapk_genes, , drop = FALSE])

mapk_near_matched <- mapk_score[near_idx][1:length(matched_far)]
mapk_far_matched  <- mapk_score[far_idx][matched_far]

cat(sprintf("\nMAPK: Near=%.3f, Far=%.3f, p=%.3f\n",
            mean(mapk_near_matched), mean(mapk_far_matched),
            wilcox.test(mapk_near_matched, mapk_far_matched)$p.value))

###HGF 上游 TF — 诊断###
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")
suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(tidyverse); library(dorothea)

data("dorothea_hs", package = "dorothea")

# ============================================================
# 1. DoRothEA 里，谁是 HGF 的上游 TF？
# ============================================================
hgf_upstream <- dorothea_hs %>%
  dplyr::filter(target == "HGF") %>%
  dplyr::select(tf, confidence, mor)

cat("=== DoRothEA 里 HGF 的上游 TF ===\n")
print(hgf_upstream)
cat(sprintf("总数: %d\n", nrow(hgf_upstream)))

# 按 confidence 筛选
hgf_upstream_sig <- hgf_upstream %>% dplyr::filter(confidence %in% c("A","B","C"))
cat(sprintf("A/B/C confidence: %d\n", nrow(hgf_upstream_sig)))
print(hgf_upstream_sig)

# ============================================================
# 2. 这 5 个共识 TF 是否在 HGF 上游列表里？
# ============================================================
consensus_tfs <- c("TFAP2A", "STAT1", "TP53", "AR", "SP3")
cat("\n=== 5 个共识 TF 与 HGF 的关系 ===\n")
for (tf in consensus_tfs) {
  in_dorothea <- tf %in% hgf_upstream$tf
  conf <- hgf_upstream$confidence[hgf_upstream$tf == tf]
  mor <- hgf_upstream$mor[hgf_upstream$tf == tf]
  cat(sprintf("%-8s: %s", tf,
              ifelse(in_dorothea, "✅ 是 HGF 上游", "❌ 不是 HGF 上游")))
  if (in_dorothea) cat(sprintf(" (conf=%s, mor=%s)",
                               paste(conf, collapse=","), paste(mor, collapse=",")))
  cat("\n")
}

# ============================================================
# 3. 用 ChEA3 思路：DoRothEA 里所有能调控 HGF 的 TF
#    看这些 TF 在 HGF+/- HSC 里的活性
# ============================================================
cat("\n=== HGF 上游 TF 完整列表（A/B/C）===\n")
print(hgf_upstream_sig)

###进一步确认方向和显著性###
setwd("~/Desktop/sjfx3")

  library(tidyverse)
})

# ---- 载入 TF 活性结果 ----
tf_res <- read.csv("results/TF_significant_final.csv")
cat("=== 5 个共识 TF 的活性 (delta1=Slide1, delta2=Slide2) ===\n")
print(tf_res)

# ---- 看 TFAP2A 和 TP53 的具体数值 ----
cat("\n=== HGF 上游 TF 的方向验证 ===\n")

for (tf_name in c("TFAP2A", "TP53")) {
  row <- tf_res[tf_res$TF == tf_name, ]
  if (nrow(row) == 0) {
    cat(sprintf("%s: 不在最终列表中\n", tf_name))
    next
  }
  cat(sprintf("\n%s:\n", tf_name))
  cat(sprintf("  Slide1 delta = %.3f\n", row$delta1))
  cat(sprintf("  Slide2 delta = %.3f\n", row$delta2))
  cat(sprintf("  方向一致性: %s\n",
              ifelse(sign(row$delta1) == sign(row$delta2), "✅ 一致", "❌ 不一致")))
}

# ---- 与 DoRothEA mor 对照 ----
cat("\n=== 与 DoRothEA mor 对照 ===\n")
cat("TFAP2A (mor=-1, 抑制 HGF):\n")
tfap2a <- tf_res[tf_res$TF == "TFAP2A", ]
if (tfap2a$delta1 < 0 && tfap2a$delta2 < 0) {
  cat("  ✅ TFAP2A 在 HGF+ HSC 中下调 → 解除对 HGF 的抑制 → HGF 上调\n")
  cat("  ✅ 与 DoRothEA 预测一致，支持调控关系\n")
} else {
  cat("  ❌ 方向与 DoRothEA 预测不一致\n")
}

cat("\nTP53 (mor=+1, 激活 HGF):\n")
tp53 <- tf_res[tf_res$TF == "TP53", ]
if (nrow(tp53) > 0) {
  if (tp53$delta1 > 0 && tp53$delta2 > 0) {
    cat("  ✅ TP53 在 HGF+ HSC 中上调 → 激活 HGF\n")
    cat("  ✅ 与 DoRothEA 预测一致\n")
  } else if (tp53$delta1 < 0 && tp53$delta2 < 0) {
    cat("  ❌ TP53 下调，与激活 HGF 的预测不一致\n")
  } else {
    cat("  ⚠️ 方向不一致\n")
  }
} else {
  cat("  TP53 不在最终 TF 列表中\n")
}

p_b <- ggplot(tf_long, aes(delta, TF_label, fill = delta)) +
  geom_col(position = position_dodge(0.8), alpha = 0.9, width = 0.75) +
  scale_fill_gradient2(low = "#2E86AB", mid = "white", high = "#C73E1D",
                       midpoint = 0) +
  geom_vline(xintercept = 0, color = "grey40") +
  labs(title = "TF activity: HGF-expressing vs HGF-low HSC",
       subtitle = "DoRothEA A/B/C regulons, |delta| > 0.3",
       x = "TF activity delta score", y = NULL) +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) +
  theme(legend.position = "none",
        plot.title = element_text(face = "bold", size = 11),
        plot.subtitle = element_text(size = 9, color = "grey30"),
        axis.text.y = element_text(size = 9),
        plot.margin = ggplot2::margin(t = 5, r = 5, b = 5, l = 5))

# ============================================================
# Panel c: 保持不变
# ============================================================
sp3_go <- read.csv("results/Fig4c_SP3_GO.csv") %>% head(8)
sp3_go$Description <- reorder(sp3_go$Description, -log10(sp3_go$p.adjust))

p_c <- ggplot(sp3_go, aes(Description, -log10(p.adjust),
                          fill = -log10(p.adjust))) +
  geom_col(alpha = 0.9) +
  scale_fill_gradient(low = "#F4A582", high = "#C73E1D") +
  coord_flip() +
  labs(title = "SP3 target gene enrichment (GO BP)",
       x = NULL, y = "-log10(adj.P)") +
  theme_classic(base_size = 10) +
  theme(legend.position = "none",
        plot.title = element_text(face = "bold", size = 11),
        axis.text.y = element_text(size = 8),
        plot.margin = ggplot2::margin(t = 5, r = 5, b = 5, l = 5))

# ============================================================
# 拼接 + 保存
# ============================================================
fig4 <- (p_a | p_b) / (p_c | plot_spacer()) +
  plot_layout(widths = c(1, 1), heights = c(1, 1)) +
  plot_annotation(tag_levels = "a") &
  theme(plot.tag = element_text(size = 12, face = "bold"))

ggsave(file.path(OUT, "Figure4.pdf"), fig4,
       width = 12, height = 9, device = "pdf")
ggsave(file.path(OUT, "Figure4.png"), fig4,
       width = 12, height = 9, dpi = 300, device = "png")

cat("✅ Figure4 重画完成\n")
